### [1] [Beyond Correct Answers: How Compositional Cues Shape Visual Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28843)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Zheyuan Zhang,Kaiwen Shi,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过受控实验拆解结构化提示中各字段标签的作用：字段标签存在模型与任务依赖的差异，保序提示相对基线增益有限（Qwen3的优势主要来自换序提示引发的错误），更清晰的措辞只改善文本匹配而非视觉判断，直接选择关系则能部分降低错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉问答需要模型把属性和关系正确绑定到对象（视觉绑定），结构化提示通过带标签字段使这种绑定显式化；但"完整提示带来提升"无法说明究竟哪个标签有用、标签之间如何交互，因此需要逐项拆解。

Method: 三项受控实验：(1) 固定图像与陈述，在10个模型、6个任务上隔离单个字段标签的影响；(2) 以无提示为基线，比较保留或交换问题角色分配的辅助提示，并考察字段顺序对危害的影响；(3) 测试更清晰的提示措辞与直接选择图中所示关系两种干预。

Result: 存在关系标签时，加入对象角色标签对Qwen3在Spatial任务上有帮助、却损害Qwen2；Qwen3使用保序提示的优势主要源于换序提示导致的错误，保序提示仅接近基线，且字段顺序影响危害程度；更清晰的措辞改善文本匹配但不稳定改善视觉判断；关系选择在Qwen3与GPT-4.1-mini上对部分提示部分减少错误。

Conclusion: 整体准确率会掩盖字段标签的差异化与交互效应；结构化提示的收益需要按标签、顺序和模型逐一评估，措辞清晰化不等于视觉判断提升，而显式的关系选择是更有前景的改进方向。

Abstract: Visual question answering requires models to assign attributes and relations to the correct objects, a problem known as visual binding. Structured prompts make these assignments explicit through labeled fields. Yet an improvement from the full prompt does not reveal which labels help or how they interact. First, we isolate field-label effects across ten models and six tasks by holding the image and claim fixed. These comparisons reveal differences that overall accuracy can hide. With a relation label present, adding object-role labels helps Qwen3 but hurts Qwen2 on Spatial. Second, we compare auxiliary hints that preserve or swap the question's role assignments against a no-hint baseline. Across two image sets, Qwen3's advantage with preserving hints mainly reflects errors caused by swapped hints. Preserving hints remain near baseline. Field order further affects the harm. Third, we test clearer hint wording and direct selection of the relation shown in the image. Clearer hints improve text matching but do not consistently improve visual judgments. In contrast, relation selection partly reduces errors for some hints in Qwen3 and GPT-4.1-mini.

</details>


### [2] [OACap: Caption-Guided Active Observation for Detailed Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29327)
*Hao Shi,Hong Yan,Xiaoyang Zhang,Qing Cui,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以字幕为中心的智能体 OACap，将缺失信息转化为定向观测请求并迭代验证修订字幕；同时构建 FSCap-Bench 及 CSFS、CSRS 两项指标诊断字幕的事实与语义保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式详细字幕生成不同于查询式问答，需要自主决定获取哪些证据以及如何整合为连贯的全局描述；现有评测也难以诊断字幕究竟保留了多少事实与语义信息。

Method: OACap 作为字幕中心智能体，识别未支撑或缺失的信息并生成定向观测请求，调用时空工具获取证据，迭代校验与修订字幕；并引入 FSCap-Bench，其中 CSFS 结合断言精度与重要性感知的事实覆盖度，CSRS 通过视频—字幕—视频—字幕往返中原子事实匹配衡量语义保持。

Result: 在固定纯文本阅读器下，OACap 字幕在通用视频理解基准上稳定提升性能；CSFS 在 93.0–98.3% 的有效字幕对上按预期响应四类受控扰动。

Conclusion: 以观测自适应方式生成的字幕可提升下游文本记忆效用，两项指标将下游效用与事实、语义保真度联系起来，为字幕质量诊断提供统一框架。

Abstract: Unlike question answering, where a query specifies a perceptual target, open-ended detailed captioning must determine what evidence to acquire and how to integrate it into a coherent global description. We propose Observation-Adaptive Video Captioner (OACap), a caption-centered agent that turns unsupported or missing information into targeted observation requests, uses spatiotemporal tools to acquire evidence, and iteratively verifies and revises the caption. OACap captions consistently improve performance across general video-understanding benchmarks using a fixed text-only reader. To better diagnose the information they preserve, we introduce Factual-Semantic Captioning Bench (FSCap-Bench). Its Core-Support Factuality Score (CSFS) combines claim precision with importance-aware fact coverage and responds as expected to four controlled perturbation types on 93.0-98.3% of valid caption pairs. Its Cross-Modal Semantic Round-Trip Score (CSRS) measures semantic preservation through a video-caption-video-caption round trip by matching atomic facts from source and recovered captions. Together, they connect downstream textual-memory utility with factual and semantic preservation.

</details>


### [3] [Computer Vision and Knowledge-Grounded Language Models for Intelligent Beekeeper Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29283)
*Asaduz Zaman,Mohaimenul Azam Khan Raiaan,Malika Nisal Ratnayake*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可部署在边缘设备上的蜂箱巢框图像智能检查框架，将巢框照片经目标检测、结构化事实抽取、受约束文本生成与触发式知识库，转化为可解释、可行动的检查报告，并发布FAIRHiveText-1K数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜜蜂与野生蜂支撑全球约三分之一食品产出，但蜂群面临气候变化带来的极端天气、寄生虫与疾病模式变化等压力；及时评估蜂群强度、子脾面积、蜂蜜与花粉储量、巢脾使用率等状态对适应性管理至关重要，而人工巢框检查缓慢、昂贵且依赖稀缺专家经验。

Method: 构建面向边缘部署的流水线：目标检测器识别具有生物学意义的巢房单元类别；检测结果蒸馏为结构化事实模式（fact schema）；事实转为受约束的事实性文本；文本触发有依据的知识库以生成解释与建议，且知识库条目须可由事实模式中的证据触发。基于开源FAIRHiveFrames-1K数据集，优先采用可复现、边缘可部署模型而非大型通用前沿模型，并发布FAIRHiveText-1K数据集。

Result: 形成从巢框图像到可解释、可行动检查报告的端到端系统；发布FAIRHiveText-1K，将FAIRHiveFrames-1K转化为可互换的事实与文本解释对，便于后续基于LLM的研究；并提出构建有依据知识库的路径。

Conclusion: 该工作以轻量、可复现的边缘智能流水线替代依赖专家的人工检查，通过结构化事实与证据触发式知识库保证报告的可解释性与可行动性，并为该领域开放数据与后续LLM研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Managed and wild bees sustain roughly a third of the global food products, yet colonies face mounting stress from climate-change including extreme weather patterns, altered parasite and disease patterns. Timely assessment of colony condition such as understanding colony strength, brood area, honey and pollen storage, comb usage, is essential to adaptive management. However, manual hive frame inspection is slow, costly, and quality depends on scarce expertise. We present an artificial targeted intelligent system framework focusing on edge deployable pipeline that turns hive frame photographs into interpretable and action-oriented inspection report. The pipeline includes an object detector to detect biologically-meaningful comb-cell classes; detections are distilled into structured fact schema; facts are converted into constrained factual text; the text triggers a grounded knowledge-base for interpretations and recommendations. Knowledge-base entries are required to be triggerable by evidence expressed in the fact schema. We built our system based on open source FAIRHiveFrames-1K dataset and favour reproducible, edge-deployable models over large general purpose frontier models. We additionally release FAIRHiveText-1K dataset that turns FAIRHiveFrames-1K dataset into interchangeable facts and text interpretation of the facts, making it easy for further LLM-based research into this area. We also propose a pathway to develop the grounded knowledge-base that will be helpful in providing interpretable and actionable reports from the facts.

</details>


### [4] [CCG Diffusion: Combinatory Categorial Grammar-Driven Compositional and Syntactically Disambiguated Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29415)
*Ryosuke Yamaki,Daichi Mochihashi,Nobutaka Shimada,Seitaro Shinagawa,Tadahiro Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法CCG Diffusion，用组合范畴文法(CCG)控制文本到图像的生成，统一处理属性绑定、空间关系、计数与介词短语(PP)附着歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型不显式接收提示词的句法结构，难以正确完成属性绑定、空间关系与计数，也难以按意图解释句法歧义提示。现有基于句法的控制方法多仅解决属性绑定，且依赖短语结构或依存文法，需额外规则或树结构约束来提取修饰关系或指定句法解释。

Method: CCG Diffusion为免训练方法：利用CCG中每个词的范畴携带丰富句法信息（规定其与何组合、构成何短语），修饰关系可直接从CCG剖析结果读取；固定介词范畴即可消解PP附着歧义。将同一套基于CCG的两种机制——提示重写与注意力引导——同时用于控制属性绑定、空间关系和计数，以及消解PP歧义。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion与Sana两个架构不同的骨干上，属性绑定与二维空间关系指标均取得对比方法中最高分，在PP歧义消解上显著优于所有对比方法。

Conclusion: CCG范畴的句法信息可直接、无需额外规则地支撑修饰关系提取与歧义指定，使单一免训练框架统一实现多类句法级图文控制，并在不同骨干上具备有效性。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation models do not explicitly receive the syntactic structure of the prompt, i.e., which word modifies which and where each phrase attaches. Consequently, they often struggle to bind attributes to the correct objects, to depict spatial relations and counts correctly, and to render syntactically ambiguous prompts in the intended interpretation. Existing syntax-based control methods mainly address attribute binding and have limited ability to control spatial relations and counts or to specify the interpretation of syntactically ambiguous prompts. They rely on phrase-structure or dependency grammars and require additional rules or constraints on the tree structure to extract modification relations between words or to specify a syntactic interpretation. We propose CCG Diffusion, a training-free method that controls image generation using Combinatory Categorial Grammar (CCG). In CCG, the category of each word carries rich syntactic information: it specifies what the word combines with and what phrase it forms. Modification relations between words can therefore be read directly from a CCG parse. Moreover, prepositional phrase (PP) attachment ambiguity, a representative syntactic ambiguity, can be resolved simply by fixing the preposition's category. CCG Diffusion applies the same two CCG-based mechanisms, prompt rewriting and attention guidance, both to controlling attribute binding, spatial relations, and counts and to resolving PP ambiguity. On two architecturally different backbones, Stable Diffusion and Sana, CCG Diffusion achieves the highest scores among the compared methods in attribute binding and 2D spatial relations and significantly outperforms all of them in PP ambiguity resolution.

</details>


### [5] [Latent space configuration for neural network finetuning, fast ranking, and embedding dimensionality reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29295)
*Nikita Gabdullin,Anton Raskovalov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出针对神经网络结构化隐空间（LS）的检索算法，利用O(1)候选子集搜索加速top-K相似元素检索，在微调模型上实现最多3倍GPU、40%CPU加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型神经网络嵌入库中检索查询的K个最相似元素在计算机视觉与AI工业应用中至关重要，但穷举搜索计算代价高。

Method: 采用Latent Space Configuration（LSC）方法微调ArcFace预训练的IResNet50模型，获得具有期望几何性质的嵌入分布；在此基础上设计利用O(1)复杂度小候选库子集搜索的top-K检索算法。

Result: 相比基线穷举搜索，GPU上最高3倍加速、CPU上40%加速（相对基线GPU速度）；在原始与降维LS维度下均有效，且降维可进一步加速并改善rank-N指标。

Conclusion: 结构化隐空间结合微调可获得几何友好的嵌入分布，从而显著加速近似检索，并可通过降维进一步提速与提升排序质量。

Abstract: Finding K most similar elements for a query in a large gallery of neural network (NN) embeddings is essential in various areas of computer science and industrial applications of artificial intelligence. This paper proposes search algorithms for structured latent spaces (LS) of NNs which allow to speed up top K item retrieval owing to O(1) complexity small candidate gallery subset search. ArcFace-pretrained IResNet50 models are finetuned using Latent Space Configuration (LSC) methodology to obtain new distribution of embeddings with desired geometric properties. Applying the proposed algorithms to embeddings obtained from finetuned models leads to several times speed-up over baseline exhaustive search. Specifically, we report up to 3 times speed-up on GPU and 40% speed-up on CPU compared to the baseline method GPU speed. These results are obtained for the original and reduced numbers of LS dimensions. The results show that LS dimensionality reduction allows to further accelerate the search and even improve rank-N metrics.

</details>


### [6] [From Augmentation to Synthetic Evidence: Evaluating Generated Chest Radiographs at Dataset Intake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29152)
*Shaikh Akib Shahriyar,Zinedine Sehili,Adam Arthur,Christopher Schwartz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文区分“自动化质量检查、来源分类器检测、可信记录核验”三类不同问题，实验表明质量筛选与来源分类均无法验证图像的真实采集声明，主张医学数据集准入须单独记录并核查图像来源与用途。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式胸片用于数据增强是合理的，但同一图像若被当作真实检查记录提交则成为无依据证据。现有像素级自动检查常被误认为能验证“采集真实性”，作者希望厘清质量检查、来源识别与采集可信性三者的边界。

Method: 将问题拆为三问：是否通过自动质量检查、来源分类器是否标记为生成、是否有可信记录支持所声明的采集。实验由Qwen从1,000张Cheff-identified或SynthCheX候选中选至多20张；接收方用MedGemma评质量、用基于冻结DINOv3特征的分类器预测来源；比较均匀选择、质量选择、质量约束代理选择三种策略，并分析JPEG压缩影响。

Result: 质量选择使Cheff-identified图像质量通过数由12/20升至19/20，SynthCheX由8升至17；但无证据表明选择能提升双重通过率，所选Cheff-identified图像无一双通过，均匀/质量/质量约束代理选择下SynthCheX分别有2、1、2张通过。未处理测试集上来源分类器标记5,000张SynthCheX中的4,677张，但误判NIH真实图像110/2,297、另一集合真实图像568/957为假；JPEG压缩使SynthCheX-vs-NIH AUROC由0.987降至0.841；即便未处理仍有152张SynthCheX通过两项像素检查。

Conclusion: 像素级质量与来源检查可过滤提交，但无法验证采集声明。作者强烈建议在医学数据集纳入胸片时，必须分别记录并核查图像来源与预期用途。

Abstract: A generated chest radiograph can be useful as declared augmentation, but the same image becomes unsupported evidence when submitted as the record of a real examination. We study this problem by separating three questions: whether an image passes an automated quality check, whether an origin classifier flags it as generated, and whether a trusted record supports the claimed acquisition. In our experiments, Qwen selects up to 20 images from pools of 1,000 existing Cheff-identified or SynthCheX candidates. A receiver evaluates quality with MedGemma and predicted origin with a classifier on frozen DINOv3 features. Quality-based selection raises quality passes from 12 to 19 of 20 for Cheff-identified images and from 8 to 17 for SynthCheX. We find no evidence that selection improves passage through both checks: no selected Cheff-identified image passes both, while uniform, quality, and quality-constrained surrogate selection produce 2, 1, and 2 SynthCheX passes. On unprocessed test images, the origin classifier flags 4,677 of 5,000 SynthCheX images, but it also misclassify 110 of 2,297 NIH (real) images and 568 of 957 genuine images from another collection as fake. Our results further show that JPEG compression reduces SynthCheX-versus-NIH AUROC from 0.987 to 0.841. Even without processing, 152 SynthCheX images pass both pixel checks. These checks can filter submissions, but they cannot verify an acquisition claim. We strongly advocate that source and intended use must be recorded and checked separately for admission of chest radiographs in medical datasets.

</details>


### [7] [OccJEPA: Occlusion as Masking for Amodal Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29061)
*Genpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 OccJEPA，将遮挡视为掩码，利用预训练 V-JEPA 2.1 预测器补全被遮挡区域的表征，再经轻量解码头生成非模态掩码，在速度与精度上均优于基于扩散模型的 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优的非模态视频分割方法依赖微调的视频扩散模型，需大量去噪步数与额外深度网络，且每次采样结果不确定（非确定性）。作者希望利用 JEPA 系列自监督视频世界模型所学的"从可见区域预测被掩码时空区域表征"能力，以更高效、确定性的方式解决遮挡补全问题。

Method: 核心思路是"遮挡即掩码"：丢弃物体可见掩码未覆盖的 token，由预训练 V-JEPA 2.1 预测器补全这些 token 的表征，再用轻量解码头将观测特征与预测特征解码为非模态掩码，形成 OccJEPA 模型。

Result: 模型为确定性输出，比扩散类 SOTA 快 33.5 倍；在 SAIL-VOS、TAO-Amodal（含留出测试集 +4.00 AP50，及 SAM2 跟踪的可见掩码）与 Kubric 上精度更高。控制实验表明增益来源于预测器预训练权重而非模型容量，覆盖 87M 至 1.85B 参数的世界模型。

Conclusion: 将自监督视频世界模型的掩码表征预测能力迁移到非模态分割是有效且有原则的替代方案，兼顾效率与精度；性能提升主要归因于预训练表征知识。

Abstract: Amodal video segmentation asks for the complete mask of an object in every frame, including the parts hidden behind other objects. The best current methods generate these masks with fine-tuned video diffusion models, which need many denoising steps and an extra depth network, and give a different answer for every sample. Self-supervised video world models of the JEPA family are trained on a closely related task: predicting the representation of masked space-time regions from the visible ones. We therefore treat occlusion as masking. The tokens that the object's visible mask does not cover are dropped, the pre-trained V-JEPA 2.1 predictor fills them in, and a light head decodes the observed and predicted features into amodal masks. The resulting model, OccJEPA (Occlusion-as-masking JEPA), is deterministic and 33.5x faster than the diffusion-based state of the art, and more accurate on SAIL-VOS, on TAO-Amodal, including a held-out test split (+4.00 AP50) and visible masks tracked by SAM2, and on Kubric. Controlled experiments trace the gain to the predictor's pre-trained weights rather than to its capacity, for world models from 87M to 1.85B parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/zgp110/occjepa.

</details>


### [8] [MedBOE: Optimization-Based Estimation of Complete Patient Body Models from Whole-Body Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28985)
*Hoijoon Jung,Yuhao Wei,Mingxiao Tu,Younhyun Jung,Michael Fulham,Jinman Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedBOE：一种无需训练、基于SMPL模板与属性引导优化的自动框架，从存在缺失区域的全身影像中估计完整患者人体模型，BHMs误差<2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身影像因扫描仪几何（如孔径）、患者体型与摆位等因素常缺失部分体区（侧向手臂/躯干、手、前臂、肘），导致患者人体模型不完整，进而限制体态学指标（体体积、表面积等）的获取，影响放射性示踪剂剂量方案、心血管代谢风险评估及计算解剖建模与辐射剂量学等下游分析。

Method: 提出MedBOE：将SMPL参数化人体网格模板引入医学影像，通过属性引导的优化将模板对齐到不完整的患者人体模型，并以几何与体积约束的形变估计缺失体区；整体流程自动化且无需训练。

Result: 在AutoPET（91例全身PET-CT）与HIT（40例MRI全身分割）两套公开数据上评估，MedBOE在体积与几何性能上均为最优；由其导出的体态学指标误差在2%以内。

Conclusion: MedBOE为存在缺失体区的全身影像提供了一种自动化、免训练的完整人体模型估计方案，可为下游医学分析提供低误差的体态学指标。

Abstract: True whole-body medical imaging captures a patient from head to toe and provides comprehensive anatomical and/or functional information. This coverage enables reconstruction of the external body surface, yielding patient body models. However, scanner geometry (e.g., bore diameter), patient characteristics, and positioning can leave body regions missing (e.g., lateral arms/torso, hands, forearms, elbows), producing incomplete patient body models. Such incompleteness limits downstream medical analyses, including derivation of body-habitus metrics (BHMs), such as body volume and surface area, for injected radiotracer activity regimens, cardiometabolic risk assessment, and computational anatomical modeling for medical imaging simulation and radiation dosimetry. We present the Medical Body model Optimization-based Estimator (MedBOE), an automated, training-free framework for estimating complete patient body models from whole-body images with missing body regions. Our innovation is to leverage the Skinned Multi-Person Linear (SMPL) template, a parametric body mesh representing a broad range of body shapes and poses, in medical imaging. MedBOE aligns the SMPL template to the incomplete patient body models via attribute-guided optimization and estimates missing body regions through geometrically and volumetrically constrained deformation. We evaluated MedBOE using two public datasets: AutoPET (91 whole-body PET-CT images) and Human Implicit Tissues (HIT; 40 whole-body segmentations from MRI images). MedBOE achieved the best overall volumetric and geometric performance among evaluated methods. BHMs derived from MedBOE were within 2% error, suggesting low percentage errors for downstream analyses. MedBOE offers an automated, training-free method for estimating complete patient body models from whole-body images with missing body regions.

</details>


### [9] [CineEvolve: An Agentic Self-Evolution Framework for Cinematic Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29590)
*Jingzhe Zhang,Peixuan Zhang,Genying Li,Bing Ma,Shuchen Weng,Si Li,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CineEvolve——将导演与评论家统一在单一视觉-语言策略中的智能体自进化框架，用于电影级多镜头视频生成；通过交替强化学习与Evolutionary GRPO实现协同进化，并贡献CineTraj数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影级多镜头视频生成需要叙事规划与迭代精修，但现有方法要么缺乏叙事推理能力，要么采用无策略更新的前馈式流程，无法通过反馈持续改进。

Method: 1) 在单一视觉-语言策略中统一Director（导演）与Critic（评论家）两个角色；2) 采用交替强化学习训练：导演暴露缺陷以强化评论家，更新后的评论家指导导演定向修改；3) 提出Evolutionary GRPO（E-GRPO），利用历史锚定与修订保护机制缓解优势膨胀，稳定协同进化；4) 构建CineTraj数据集，含40K条覆盖创作、评论、修改的电影化轨迹。

Result: 大量实验表明CineEvolve优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过模仿真实电影制作中"指导—批判"耦合的迭代过程，自进化智能体框架可有效提升多镜头视频生成的叙事质量与精修能力；代码将开源。

Abstract: Cinematic multi-shot video generation requires both narrative planning and iterative refinement. Existing methods either lack narrative reasoning or rely on feed-forward workflows without policy updates. In this paper, we propose CineEvolve, an agentic self-evolution framework for cinematic multi-shot video generation that unifies a Director and a Critic within a single vision-language policy. Emulating real-world filmmaking that couples iterative direction with critical feedback, we train CineEvolve via alternating reinforcement learning, where the Director surfaces flaws to sharpen the Critic, and the updated Critic guides the Director's targeted revisions. To stabilize co-evolution, we introduce Evolutionary GRPO (E-GRPO), leveraging historical anchoring and revision safeguarding to mitigate advantage inflation. Additionally, we contribute CineTraj, a 40K cinematic trajectory dataset spanning creation, critique, and revision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CineEvolve outperforms existing approaches. Our code will be available at https://github.com/JingZhe-8/CineEvolve.

</details>


### [10] [Rethinking Underwater Image Enhancement: Learning through Text-Guided Degradation Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29569)
*Hongri Tian,Wenxue Li,Tian Ye,Sixiang Chen,Lujia Wang,Xianying Wang,Xinhu Zheng,Gang Hua,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的水下图像增强退化感知训练框架，通过文本条件扩散生成可控退化配对数据，并结合渐进式退化课程学习与透射引导的一步扩散模型，在多个基准上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成对干净-退化数据集规模有限或难以获取，难以覆盖真实水下环境的多样化退化，导致增强模型泛化性受限，因此需要真实且可控的退化建模。

Method: 1) 文本条件退化增强流水线：用文本引导扩散模型合成多样且语义一致的退化样本，提供配对数据；2) 渐进式退化课程：按多属性、多难度组织训练以促进均衡学习；3) 透射引导一步扩散模型：借助该渐进过程应对宽范围的退化程度与复原歧义。

Result: 在多个水下基准上的大量实验表明所提方法性能优越。

Conclusion: 退化感知的统一训练框架结合可控数据合成与课程式扩散复原，可有效提升水下图像增强的鲁棒性与泛化能力；代码与模型将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Existing paired clean-degraded datasets have advanced learning-based underwater image enhancement, but they remain either limited in scale or difficult to acquire, and still fail to adequately cover the diverse degradations encountered in real-world underwater environments. Such scarce and insufficient data ultimately restrict the generalization of underwater image enhancement models, highlighting the need for realistic and controllable degradation modeling to better capture real underwater conditions and improve enhancement robustness. To this end, we propose a unified degradation-aware training framework for the underwater image enhancement task. A Text-Conditioned Degradation Augmentation Pipeline uses a text-guided diffusion model to synthesize diverse and semantically consistent degraded samples, providing paired data to support the subsequent curriculum learning strategy. The proposed Progressive Degradation Curriculum organizes training across multiple attributes and difficulty levels to promote balanced learning, and a Transmission-Guided One-Step Diffusion Model leverages this progressive process to better address the wide range of degradation severity and restoration ambiguity in underwater images. Extensive experiments on multiple underwater benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed method achieves superior performance. Our code and models will be released upon the publication of this work.

</details>


### [11] [FM-Occ: Feature-Diverse Upsampling and Memory-Aware Decoding for 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29388)
*Yujia Zhai,Guoming Huang,Zhuanghuai Wang,Bangquan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FM-Occ，包含 3D Sparse Subvoxel Shuffle（3S）与 Entropy-Regulated Layer-wise Memory（ER-LM），解决 3D 语义占用预测中稀疏上采样特征同质化与解码层语义退化问题，在 SemanticKITTI 上以极小开销提升 mIoU，并可迁移至 BEV 架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验发现两个问题：(1) 传统稀疏转置卷积上采样产生的体素响应区分度低，特征趋于同质化；(2) 解码器逐层会丢弃此前正确的语义预测，出现语义退化。

Method: 3S 在通道维度构造候选子体素特征，联合变换与归一化后重排到更细的稀疏网格，建立显式特征—坐标对应以缓解特征同质化；ER-LM 为每个 query 维护逐层记忆，通过门控循环更新，并用预测熵调节传入下一层的历史信息；训练时引入类加权熵损失 Lcwe 对匹配的语义 query 做标签引导正则。

Result: SemanticKITTI 上，3S 将归一化体素级 RMS 响应的空间变异系数由 0.41% 提升至 4.03%；ER-LM 减轻语义退化并改善跨层误差纠正；FM-Occ 将 mIoU 从 12.16% 提升至 12.73%，内存开销可忽略；ER-LM 在 BEV 基的 DISC 框架中将 mIoU 从 16.78% 提升至 17.17%。

Conclusion: 稀疏上采样与解码记忆是可独立改进的瓶颈；所提模块轻量、通用，可同时适用于 3D 体素与 BEV 架构，但整体 mIoU 增益幅度仍较小。

Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction estimates voxel-wise occupancy and semantic labels throughout a scene. Our experiments reveal that conventional sparse transposed-convolution upsampling yields poorly differentiated voxel responses and that successive decoder layers discard previously correct semantic predictions. To address these issues, we present Feature-Diverse Upsampling and Memory-Aware Decoding for 3D Occupancy Prediction (FM-Occ), comprising two components. First, 3D Sparse Subvoxel Shuffle (3S) mitigates feature homogenization by constructing candidate child features in the channel dimension, jointly transforming and normalizing them, and then rearranging them onto a finer sparse grid with explicit feature-coordinate correspondence. Second, Entropy-Regulated Layer-wise Memory (ER-LM) retains semantic information across decoder layers by maintaining a layer-wise memory for each query through gated recurrent updates and using prediction entropy to regulate the historical information propagated to the next layer. During training, a class-weighted entropy loss Lcwe provides label-guided regularization for matched semantic queries. On SemanticKITTI, 3S increases the spatial coefficient of variation of normalized voxel-wise root-mean-square (RMS) responses from 0.41% to 4.03%, while ER-LM reduces semantic degradation and improves error correction across decoder layers. The combined FM-Occ improves mIoU from 12.16% to 12.73% with negligible memory overhead. ER-LM further improves mIoU in the BEV-based DISC framework from 16.78% to 17.17%, supporting its applicability across 3D voxel-based and BEV-based architectures.

</details>


### [12] [HorizonComposer: Spatiotemporally Consistent Driving Video Editing with Enriched Traffic Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28829)
*Mauricio Soroco,Yuqiu Liu,Zaid Tasneem,Francesco Pittaluga,Abhishek Aich,Wuyang Chen,Manmohan Chandraker,Ziyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在指令引导视频编辑中保护和蒸馏交通语义的三层方法：构建真实与合成混合数据集、借鉴离线强化学习的密集优势条件对齐、引入"思考帧"过渡机制以缓解时间冲击，从而在自动驾驶场景全局条件编辑中保持多主体动态结构，取得最优时空一致性并提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令引导视频生成模型可为自动驾驶系统提供可扩展的压力测试手段，但驾驶场景处于由严格交通语义约束的稠密ODD中，通用视频编辑器在进行全局驾驶条件编辑时常破坏这些语义，产生结构性幻觉与"时间冲击"问题。

Method: 分三层实现：(1) 构建时间对齐的真实视频与合成编辑相结合的混合数据集，兼顾基础场景结构与明确外观信号；(2) 训练时迁移离线强化学习中的优势条件方法，从混合数据中提取像素级密集奖励作为局部信号，主动引导生成偏向结构可靠的像素；(3) 引入"思考帧"过渡机制，无缝衔接静态图像条件与动态视频上下文，缓解时间冲击。

Result: 实验表明方法可防止多主体动态被破坏，时空一致性达到SOTA；用户偏好一致提升（内容保持相对提升39%，时间一致性提升12%）；下游道路线分割提升5%，多目标检测提升7%。

Conclusion: 通过在数据、训练与推理三个层面蒸馏并保护交通语义，可有效解决指令引导视频编辑在驾驶场景中的语义破坏与时间冲击问题，兼具生成质量与下游任务收益。

Abstract: Instruction-guided video generative models offer a scalable solution for stress-testing autonomous driving systems by simulating diverse environmental conditions. However, driving scenes operate within a dense Operational Design Domain (ODD) governed by strict traffic semantics. When applied to global driving-condition edits, general-purpose instruction-guided video editors frequently corrupt these semantics, leading to structural hallucinations and ``temporal shock.'' This work distills and protects traffic semantics during video editing at three levels. First, we construct a hybrid dataset by combining temporally aligned real-world videos with synthetic edits, balancing foundational scene structure with unambiguous appearance signals. Second, during training, we adapt advantage conditioning from offline reinforcement learning to align the model using dense rewards. We extract dense, pixel-level rewards from the hybrid data to serve as a localized signal and actively steer generation toward structurally reliable pixels. Finally, our model mitigates temporal shock by introducing thinking frames, a transition mechanism that seamlessly reconciles static image conditioning with dynamic video context. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method prevents the corruption of multi-actor dynamics, establishing state-of-the-art spatiotemporal consistency, yielding consistent user preference (39% relative improvement in content preservation and 12% in temporal consistency), and achieving a significant 5% gain in downstream road line segmentation and 7% gain in downstream multi-object detection.

</details>


### [13] [WorldStream: Streaming Interactive 3D Worlds with Hierarchical Planning and Persistent State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28827)
*Xiaoxiao Ma,Weiyang Jin,Yanwen Ma,Haoran Li,Zhixiang Wei,Songchun Zhang,Yaowei Li,Junhao Zhuang,Zeyue Xue,Yuming Li,Zheming Fu,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldStream 是一个开放式 3D 世界模型：可无限延展可玩地形、响应当前世界中的开放式语言指令、并在返回时恢复含编辑的已访问区域。核心是以层级规划器（Macro/Micro）产出的类型化决策状态驱动确定性本地代码编译，并通过流式调度与单一世界记录实现连贯、低成本、可交互的世界扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 世界生成多局限于预定义地图范围，难以同时满足三类需求：地形可无边界延展、能以开放式语言在运行世界中即时操控、以及返回旧地点时保留此前的编辑。作者希望用一个统一框架同时解决"扩展性、连贯性、可交互性"三者，并在有限算力预算下维持。

Method: 1) 层级规划器基于类型化决策模型输出结构化决策状态：Macro 层在一组 tile 上确定聚落、生态、交通与水文结构；Micro 层在实测地形与已接受邻接承诺约束下，将该契约特化为单个 tile 的语义。2) 确定性本地代码将接受的计划编译为地形、道路、聚落、植被与实体。3) 流式机制分离各层作用范围：规划沿预测走廊提前运行，编译仅发生在即将需要移动的区域，用户身后几何体释放。4) 单一世界记录保存计划、种子、对象身份、可变状态、边界承诺与按属性记录的编辑，并随用户前进与编辑而更新；重访 tile 由该记录重编译。5) 同一记录支撑交互：模型作用于既有实体（含随行为更新的有状态 NPC）、编辑当前区域，或将意图附加到未规划区域。

Result: 所得世界在有限预算下持续扩张，跨独立规划的 tile 保持连通，并支持交互式操作；编辑与对象状态在重访时可被精确恢复。

Conclusion: WorldStream 通过"层级规划 + 确定性编译 + 流式调度 + 单一世界记录"的组合，将开放式地形延展、开放式语言交互与可持久化编辑统一于一个世界模型中，为可扩展、连贯且可交互的 3D 世界生成提供了一种可行性路径。

Abstract: We present WorldStream, an open-ended 3D world model with three simultaneous capabilities: it extends playable terrain beyond any pre-specified map, acts on open-ended language in the running world, and restores visited places with their edits. A hierarchical planner over a typed decision model emits structured decision state: Macro fixes settlement, ecology, transport, and hydrological structure across a group of tiles; Micro specializes that contract into tile semantics under measured terrain and accepted neighbor commitments. Deterministic local code compiles accepted plans into terrain, roads, settlements, vegetation, and entities. Streaming separates their reach: plans run ahead along the predicted corridor, compilation only where movement will soon need it, geometry behind the user released. One world record retains plans, seeds, object identities and mutable state, boundary commitments, and edits by attribute, revised as the user advances and edits; returning tiles recompile from it. The same record grounds interaction: the model acts on existing entities, including stateful NPCs that update as they act, edits the present region, or attaches intent to unplanned regions. Together they yield a world expanding under bounded budgets, connected across independently planned tiles, and interactive.

</details>


### [14] [Complexity-Aware Surgical Video Reasoning Segmentation with Digital Twin and Context Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28776)
*Yiqing Shen,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SurgReasonSeg，一种面向手术视频的复杂度感知推理分割方法，基于数字孪生表示的双LoRA+RL路由器，并构建EndoVis-ReasonSeg基准，实时性能达18.8 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手术分割依赖闭集类别或显式文本查询，无法处理需要推理工作流程、器械功能与时序关系的隐式查询。

Method: 基于手术基础模型构建数字孪生（DT）表示；用强化学习训练同一大语言模型的两个LoRA适配器，分别针对不同查询复杂度；再用RL训练轻量路由器在两适配器间选择以平衡延迟与精度；采用随查询复杂度与工作流阶段自适应调整的滑动窗口设定时序上下文；通过工作流相似性与手术相关性从手术上下文记忆中检索历史病例。

Result: 在自建EndoVis-ReasonSeg（1,460训练/961测试，覆盖语义、空间、时序三类）上，J&F较最强基线ReSurgSAM2分别提升0.178、0.237、0.297；在Ref-EndoVis17与Ref-EndoVis18指代分割基准上取得最优结果；采用骨干匹配的归因协议区分上游分割器与推理模块的贡献；平均18.8 FPS。

Conclusion: 复杂度感知的推理分割结合DT表示、双LoRA+RL路由与自适应时序上下文，可在手术视频中实现实时、高精度的隐式查询分割，并提供了新的评测基准与归因分析框架。

Abstract: Conventional surgical segmentation relies on closed-set categories or explicit text queries, which cannot resolve implicit queries that require reasoning about workflows, instrument functions, and temporal relationships. To address these limitations, we propose SurgReasonSeg, a complexity-aware method that brings reasoning segmentation over digital twin (DT) representations to surgical video. Specifically, SurgReasonSeg constructs DT representations from surgical foundation models and trains two low-rank adapters (LoRA) of one large language model with reinforcement learning (RL), each specialized for a different query complexity. A lightweight router, also trained with RL, selects between the two adapters to balance latency against accuracy. SurgReasonSeg also sets the temporal context with an adaptive sliding window conditioned on query complexity and workflow phase, and retrieves relevant historical cases from a surgical context memory by workflow similarity and procedural relevance. We further introduce EndoVis-ReasonSeg, a surgical reasoning segmentation benchmark with 1,460 training and 961 test samples across semantic, spatial, and temporal categories. On EndoVis-ReasonSeg, SurgReasonSeg improves J&F over the strongest baseline, ReSurgSAM2, by 0.178, 0.237, and 0.297 on semantic, spatial, and temporal queries, and it also achieves the best results on the Ref-EndoVis17 and Ref-EndoVis18 referring segmentation benchmarks. A backbone-matched attribution protocol separates the contribution of the upstream segmenter from that of the reasoning modules. Our method runs at 18.8 FPS averaged over the query distribution, enabling real-time reasoning segmentation in surgical videos.

</details>


### [15] [Rethinking Post-hoc Debiasing of Large Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29688)
*Divyaansh Mertia,Chirag Bhutra,Himanshu Shekhar,Shubham Bagwari,Pratik Mazumder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLIP等冻结特征提取器的嵌入继承训练数据的伪相关；现有事后去偏方法依赖在文本中识别偏差特征，在双偏差属性场景（如UrbanCars）下性能甚至不如朴素分类器。论文发现冻结几何中"跨类最近训练对"几乎总共享伪属性，据此提出无需偏差标注、无需LLM的新方法：识别此类对并学习单一线性映射完成去偏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练模型（如CLIP）被广泛用作冻结特征提取器，但其嵌入继承训练数据中的伪相关，给下游带来偏差。现有事后去偏研究多保持骨干不变、保留偏差特征，然而作者发现这些方法虽机制各异，都要求在文本中识别并显式表达偏差特征，这一依赖并非无害：在多个偏差属性共现的场景下会带来未被察觉的性能惩罚。

Method: （1）在UrbanCars等基准上做实证实验，量化"需以文本表达偏差"这一依赖的代价，包括提供真实偏差身份的对照；（2）分析冻结特征的几何结构，发现被冻结编码器置于最接近位置的跨类训练对几乎总共享某一伪属性，且不依赖偏差标签；（3）据此提出方法：识别此类最近跨类对，并学习单一线性映射对特征进行变换，实现事后去偏，全程不使用偏差属性标注与任何LLM。

Result: 在UrbanCars（含两个共现偏差属性）上，近期事后去偏方法在带偏置的冻结骨干特征上表现不如朴素分类器，即使给出真实偏差身份也无法弥补差距；而所提方法在所有数据集上取得有竞争力的性能，且无需偏差属性标注或LLM。

Conclusion: 事后去偏对"文本化偏差识别"的依赖在复杂偏差场景下构成实质瓶颈；冻结几何本身已隐含偏差结构，利用最近跨类训练对即可在无偏差标注、无LLM条件下以单一线性映射实现有效的事后去偏。

Abstract: Large pretrained models such as CLIP are widely used as frozen feature extractors. However, their embeddings inherit spurious correlations from training data, introducing bias into the features. A growing body of research has sought to address this issue through post hoc debiasing, leaving the backbone untouched. We discover that despite differing mechanisms, these methods require the bias feature to be identified and expressed in text. This dependency is not innocuous. In this work, we experimentally demonstrate that this requirement incurs an unforeseen penalty in certain scenarios. For example, on the UrbanCars benchmark, having two co-occurring bias attributes, the recent methods underperform a vanilla classifier on biased frozenbackbone features, and even providing ground-truth bias identities does not close the gap. We show that the pattern that such methods seek is readily observable in the frozen geometry. Specifically, cross-class training pairs placed closest together by the frozen encoder almost always share a spurious attribute, regardless of the bias type, without requiring access to the bias labels. Based on this, we propose a novel approach to identify such pairs and learn a single linear map that transforms the features to perform post-hoc debiasing. The proposed approach achieves competitive performance across all datasets without access to bias attribute annotations or any LLMs.

</details>


### [16] [No Frames, Just Frequencies: Bandwidth-Efficient Deployment-Time Adaptation for Smart Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29607)
*Francesco Corti,Moritz Becker,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"仅频率、无帧"的带宽高效源端辅助自适应框架：智能眼镜端计算紧凑频率描述子替代传输图像帧，主机端据此生成分布偏移算子并适配模型副本，在多种视觉模型与任务上恢复93%–95%的有监督oracle增益，并支持213×–784×带宽压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署在智能眼镜上的深度网络需适应输入数据分布变化，但受严格隐私与设备（带宽、算力）约束，无法将相机帧上传到主机进行自适应。

Method: 眼镜端在设备上计算紧凑频率描述子以刻画分布偏移；仅将频率描述子发送至持有模型副本的主机；主机将描述子映射为分布偏移算子，合成OOD数据并按预定义适配协议更新模型副本。评估覆盖ImageNet-C混合损坏（ConvNeXtV2-Femto、DeiT-Tiny、TinyViT-5M）、Visual Wake Words人物分类与MS-COCO目标检测。

Result: 仅依赖传输描述子的自适应在三种架构上恢复93%–95%的有监督oracle增益，Visual Wake Words上82%，MS-COCO上94%–98%；描述子分辨率从224像素降至28像素时适配质量平缓退化，带宽可降低213×–784×。

Conclusion: 紧凑频率描述子足以驱动有效的分布偏移自适应，无需传输原始图像帧，从而在满足隐私与带宽约束的同时保持接近有监督oracle的适配性能，并具备良好的分辨率—带宽可扩展性。

Abstract: Deep neural networks deployed on smart glasses must adapt to changes in the distribution of incoming data while adhering to strict privacy and device constraints. We present No Frames, Just Frequencies, a bandwidth-efficient source-assisted adaptation framework that characterizes distribution shifts using compact frequency descriptors computed on-device. Rather than transmitting camera frames, the glasses compute and send frequency descriptors to a host device that contains a replica of the model. The host device maps the received frequency descriptor to a distribution shift operator that synthesizes out-of-distribution data and adapts a model replica using a pre-defined adaptation protocol. We evaluate the framework on mixtures of ImageNet-C corruptions using convolution-based (ConvNeXtV2-Femto), self-attention-based (DeiT-Tiny), and hybrid (TinyViT-5M) vision models, on person classification (Visual Wake Words), and on object detection (MS-COCO). Our results reveal that adaptation driven solely by the transmitted descriptors recovers $93-95\%$ of the supervised oracle adaptation gain on all three architectures, $82\%$ on Visual Wake Words and $94$--$98\%$ on MS-COCO. Notably, adaptation quality degrades gracefully as the descriptor resolution decreases from $224 $ to $28$ pixels, consistently across architectures, enabling bandwidth reductions from $213\times$ to $784\times$ across the frequency descriptors.

</details>


### [17] [Compositionality with Primitives: A Unified View from Euclidean to Hyperbolic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29811)
*Puru Vaish,Meelad Dashti,Christoph Brune,Pascal Mettes,Tatiana Tommasi,Nicola Strisciuglio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视组合零样本学习（CZSL）中"需显式重组属性-对象对"的假设，提出直接使用GDE分解出的原语表示即可识别未见组合，并分析嵌入流形几何（欧氏/球面/双曲）与数据集结构对组合推理的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（GDE）通过在嵌入分布Fréchet均值处切空间中识别编码原始视觉概念的方向，并经由向量加和合成未见属性-对象组合。作者怀疑组合泛化并不必然依赖显式的属性-对象对重组，因此检验"直接使用分解原语"是否足以完成未见组合识别。

Method: 引入新的组合评分函数（compositional scoring functions）并开展大量评测；在CLIP、DINOv2、MERU、HyCoCLIP等模型上，比较欧氏、球面与双曲三种嵌入流形的几何特性，考察原语表示对组合推理的作用。

Result: 在MIT-States、VAW-CZSL、UT-Zappos50K、Clothing16K等CZSL基准上，原语表示可直接实现未见组合的推理；同时在Waterbirds与CelebA上表现出群组鲁棒性。跨模型结果显示，组合结构稳定地以原语表示形式涌现；数据集结构与流形几何显著影响组合推理效果。

Conclusion: 显式的属性-对象对再组合并非组合泛化的必要条件，分解原语本身已足以支撑未见组合识别；嵌入流形几何与数据集结构是影响组合推理的关键因素，为CZSL及群组鲁棒性提供统一视角。

Abstract: Compositional zero-shot learning (CZSL) aims to recognize novel combinations of previously observed objects and attributes. Prior work showed that large-scale vision-language models encode decomposable semantic structures that can be recomposed to enable generalization. The Geodesically Decomposable Embeddings (GDE) framework formalizes this by identifying directions in the tangent space at the Frechet mean of the embedding distribution, which encode primitive visual concepts. Unseen attribute-object compositions can be synthesized through vector addition of these primitives. We revisit the assumption that compositional generalization requires explicit attribute-object pair re-composition. By means of new compositional scoring functions and extensive evaluations, we show that directly using the decomposed primitives suffices for recognizing unseen compositions. In that, we gain insights on how the geometry of the embedding manifold (Euclidean, spherical, or hyperbolic) and dataset structure affect compositional reasoning. Across models (CLIP, DINOv2, MERU, HyCoCLIP), we show that compositional structure consistently emerges as primitive representations, enabling inference on CZSL benchmarks (MIT-States, VAW-CZSL, UT-Zappos50K, Clothing16K) and group robustness on Waterbirds and CelebA. Code: https://github.com/nis-research/Compositionality-with-Primitives.

</details>


### [18] [Unlocking Spatial Grounding in Flow-Matching Models via SNR Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29581)
*Martin Thißen,Elke Hergenröther*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出流匹配模型的空间布局遵循能力由训练时的信噪比(SNR)分布决定，将训练聚焦于高噪声阶段即可在不改架构的情况下显著提升细粒度空间控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型图像合成能力强，但细粒度空间控制通常依赖额外结构适配器或复杂推理时引导。作者发现现代视觉-语言文本编码器已具备编码空间坐标的潜在能力，而生成模型未能加以利用，并假设SNR分布是空间定位的关键决定因素。

Method: 构建几何图元合成基准，隔离结构机制与纹理复杂性；提出严格的颜色感知布局度量来量化空间保真度；对比居中式SNR分布与偏向高噪声阶段的训练策略；并考察分辨率与长宽比方差的影响，在COCO真实数据上验证泛化性。

Result: 居中式SNR训练布局遵循率仅25.9%；偏向高噪声阶段训练提升至80.8%，接近VAE重建上限83.6%；该偏移的必要性随分辨率与长宽比方差增大；FLUX.1等模型采用的保守噪声调度不足以实现精确布局；COCO上平均精度从1.0提升至57.3（Average Precision）。

Conclusion: 高噪声阶段是空间定位的"关键学习阶段"；仅通过训练分布调优（无需架构修改）即可释放最先进的布局遵循能力，说明SNR分布是流匹配模型空间grounding的核心杠杆。

Abstract: Despite the success of foundation models in image synthesis, achieving fine-grained spatial control remains a significant challenge, typically requiring auxiliary structural adapters or complex inference-time steering. While modern vision-language (VLM) text encoders possess the latent capacity to represent spatial coordinates, generative models often fail to leverage this information. We demonstrate that the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) distribution is a critical determinant of spatial grounding in flow-matching models. Using a novel synthetic benchmark of geometric primitives, we isolate structural mechanics from textural complexity inherent in natural imagery and introduce a rigorous color-aware layout metric to quantify spatial fidelity. We show that training on a centered SNR distribution results in poor layout adherence (25.9%). Instead, we identify high-noise regimes as a "critical learning phase" for spatial grounding. Shifting the training focus toward these stages increases layout adherence to 80.8%, effectively reaching the 83.6% reconstruction ceiling of the underlying variational autoencoder. We find that the necessity for this shift scales with resolution and aspect ratio variance, and that the conservative noise schedules employed by models like FLUX.1 are insufficient for precise layout adherence. Finally, we show that these findings translate to real-world imagery via the COCO dataset (1.0 vs. 57.3 Average Precision), where state-of-the-art layout adherence is unlocked through distribution tuning alone, without architectural modifications.

</details>


### [19] [AutoPhyX: Automatic Text-Condition Physics Property Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29517)
*Bei Huang,Yixin Chen,Hongbin Zha,Yuru Pei,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出文本条件驱动的AutoPhyX框架，通过部分可控数据生成流水线与跨模态调制机制，在单次前向中预测空间变化的物理参数，兼容多种3D表示并具备良好泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真4D物理仿真需要与材质属性匹配的准确物理参数赋值。现有方法存在瓶颈：一类依赖计算昂贵的逐场景优化，另一类仅从纯视觉输入预测参数，而视觉本身存在固有歧义（如仅凭外观无法区分橡胶的软硬）。因此需要引入文本条件来消解歧义。

Method: 1) 构建部分可控的数据生成流水线：将复杂3D资产分解为语义独立的部件，并配对物理合理且多样的参数及对应文本描述，以提供细粒度监督；2) AutoPhyX采用跨模态调制机制，用文本特征动态调制视觉特征，实现精确的物理接地；3) 将属性预测表述在体素场中，从而兼容网格、点云、Gaussian Splatting与NeRF等多种3D表示；4) 采用前馈方式单次前向预测空间变化物理属性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法可在单次前向传播中实现物理合理、文本驱动的参数赋值，具有较高准确率，并对野外（in-the-wild）物体表现出稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: AutoPhyX通过文本条件与体素场表述有效缓解视觉歧义与逐场景优化开销，为物理密集型具身智能与机器人应用奠定基础。

Abstract: High-fidelity 4D physical simulation requires accurate physical parameter assignments that align with diverse material properties. Previous methods struggle with critical bottlenecks: they either rely on computationally intensive per-scene optimization or predict parameters from purely visual inputs. The latter suffers from inherent ambiguity, e.g., the inability to distinguish the stiffness of rubber from its appearance alone. To bridge this gap, we propose AutoPhyX, a text-conditioned framework for predicting spatially-varying physical properties. Since training robust feed-forward models requires fine-grained supervision, we first introduce a novel part-controllable data generation pipeline that decomposes complex 3D assets into semantically distinct components and pairs them with physically plausible, diverse parameters and corresponding text descriptions. Leveraging this dataset, AutoPhyX employs a cross-modal modulation mechanism where text features dynamically modulate visual features for precise physical grounding. By formulating property prediction within a voxel field, AutoPhyX ensures compatibility with diverse 3D representations, including meshes, point clouds, Gaussian Splatting, and NeRF. Experiments demonstrate that our method enables physically plausible, text-driven parameter assignment in a single forward pass. It achieves high accuracy and robust generalization to in-the-wild objects, paving the way for future physics-intensive embodied and robotic applications.

</details>


### [20] [Beyond Shortcut Learning: Hierarchical Spatial Prototype Regularization for Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29442)
*Chao Shuai,Shaojing Fan,Kun Pan,Jinwu Hu,Weichen Lian,Ziqi Yang,Zhongjie Ba,Zhenguang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出层次化空间原型（HSP）正则化，通过结构化原型子空间建模伪造流形，缓解深度伪造检测器的表征坍塌，提升跨数据集与跨篡改泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型使伪造人脸高度逼真，检测器对未见篡改流程的泛化仍困难。作者指出关键但少被研究的问题：微调视觉模型出现"取证表征坍塌"，提取线索与不稳定的数据集特有伪影纠缠，表征结构脆弱、跨域泛化差。

Method: HSP正则化：在空间特征上学习结构化原型子空间，显式建模伪造流形；引入原型正交性约束保持潜在伪造因子的几何独立性，并设计二阶分配平衡促进取证表征的高有效秩利用，减少对数据集特有捷径的依赖。

Result: 跨数据集、跨篡改设置下帧级AUC平均提升5.6%与5.3%；作为插件模块可将现有SOTA检测器视频级平均AUC提升至95.3%（+1.8%）。

Conclusion: HSP是一种易于采用的正则化模块，通过缓解表征坍塌有效增强深度伪造检测器的实际泛化性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models have enabled highly realistic face forgeries, yet generalizing deepfake detectors to unseen manipulation pipelines remains a major challenge. One critical yet underexplored issue is that the learned forensic representation itself may fail to remain stable across different manipulation sources. In this paper, we provide new evidence that naively fine-tuned vision models suffer from severe \textit{forensic representation collapse}: their extracted cues are often entangled with unstable, dataset-specific artifacts, resulting in structurally fragile representations and poor cross-domain generalization. Leveraging this insight, we propose \textbf{H}ierarchical \textbf{S}patial \textbf{P}rototype (\textbf{HSP}) regularization, which explicitly models the forgery manifold by learning a structured prototype subspace over spatial features. We further introduce prototype orthogonality and second-order assignment balance, which jointly preserve the geometric independence of latent forgery factors and promote high-effective-rank utilization of the forensic representation, thereby reducing reliance on dataset-specific shortcuts. Empirical results demonstrate that our method substantially improves generalizability, yielding average frame-level AUC gains of 5.6\% and 5.3\% in the cross-dataset and cross-manipulation settings, respectively. Moreover, when used as a plug-in module, HSP boosts the average video-level AUC of existing state-of-the-art detectors to 95.3\% (+1.8\%). These results demonstrate the practical value of HSP as an easy-to-adopt regularization module for enhancing deepfake detectors.

</details>


### [21] [AdvFidelity: Unifying Preference Alignment and Few-Step Image Generation within Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29380)
*Sicong Kang,Fangao Zeng,Yuhan Li,Hao Zhou,Mengfei Xu,Wenxiang Shang,Wei Li,Bingbing Ni,Pipei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdvFidelity，将伪影敏感的保真度监督以在线对抗奖励形式整合进少步强化学习，在单一RL阶段同时实现人类偏好对齐与高保真少步生成，无需DMD等显式蒸馏目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中将RL与蒸馏结合往往需要多阶段流程，且可能侵蚀RL收益。作者观察到：少步RL能逐步改善结构连贯性（即便使用专门设计的奖励），但会残留低级伪影，而这类奖励往往无法惩罚这些伪影。

Method: 训练判别器将高质量参考（正样本）排在由当前生成器产生的、内容匹配的重建（负样本）之上；重建中等噪声水平的参考可在基本保留风格与场景布局的同时暴露低级退化，从而促使判别器关注伪影而非内容差异。负样本在线重建，交替更新使保真监督适配生成器不断演化的失败模式。参考可来自目标域数据或全步模型采样。基于LeapAlign，在推理所用的少步采样轨迹上同时回传两种奖励的梯度。

Result: 在两个模型骨干、三种任务设置下，AdvFidelity在仅用4或8步且不使用CFG的情况下，在对齐度、任务表现和整体生成质量上与全步LeapAlign相当，并与其他全步RL方法保持高度竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过在线对抗奖励引入保真度监督，可在单一RL阶段内统一奖励对齐与高保真少步生成，避免显式蒸馏带来的多阶段复杂性与RL收益损失，为实用的少步图像生成提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Practical image generation requires both human preference alignment and few-step efficiency, yet combining reinforcement learning (RL) with distillation is often multi-stage and may erode RL gains We observe that few-step RL progressively improves structural coherence, even with specialized rewards, but leaves low-level artifacts that these rewards may fail to penalize. To address this gap, we propose AdvFidelity, which integrates artifact-sensitive fidelity supervision into RL through an online adversarial reward. This enables joint reward alignment and high-fidelity few-step generation within a single RL stage, without distribution matching distillation (DMD) or other explicit distillation objectives. We train a discriminator to rank high-quality references (positives) above content-matched reconstructions (negatives) produced by the current generator. Reconstructing moderately noised references largely preserves style and scene layout while exposing low-level degradations. This encourages the discriminator to assess artifacts rather than content differences. With negatives reconstructed online, alternating updates adapt fidelity supervision to the generator's evolving failure modes. References can come from existing target-domain data or full-step model samples. Built on LeapAlign, AdvFidelity backpropagates gradients from both rewards through the few-step sampling trajectories used at inference. Across two model backbones and three task settings, AdvFidelity matches full-step LeapAlign in alignment, task performance, and overall generation quality and remains highly competitive with other evaluated full-step RL methods, using only 4 or 8 steps without CFG.

</details>


### [22] [Reading the Mask: Descriptor-Grounded BI-RADS Rationale Generation for Breast MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29358)
*Hrishikesh Singh,Aarti Sharma,Divyanshu Kumar,Millie Pant*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出从DCE-MRI分割掩膜及其动态增强序列中推导符合BI-RADS的临床描述符，并用大语言模型生成可复核的影像学推理文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习分割模型虽能准确勾画肿瘤，但掩膜本身无法提供放射科医生评估病灶可疑度所用的理由与推理，限制了可解释性与临床整合。

Method: 以BI-RADS指南为结构化框架，设计含八个节点的BI-RADS决策树，节点由8项影像组学定量测量、病灶大小与成分计数决定；据此生成描述符，并约束大语言模型仅表达决策树导出的记录以生成自然语言推理。应用于MAMA-MIA数据集全部1,506例的专家与自动掩膜。

Result: 生成3,012条可复现的BI-RADS依据性推理；忠实性得分99.98%，反事实准确率99.3%；专家与自动掩膜描述符一致性随掩膜质量由Good降至Missed而从0.832降至0.549。

Conclusion: 分割掩膜可支撑结构化、可追溯的BI-RADS描述与推理生成；生成文本定位为供放射科医生审阅与签核的草稿，而非自主临床报告。

Abstract: Deep learning based models accurately delineate tumours on dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI (DCE-MRI), but segmentation masks alone do not provide the rationales or reasoning used by radiologists to assess the lesion suspicion. This limits the explainability as well the clinical integration. We investigate whether clinically relevant descriptors can instead be derived from the segmentation masks and their dynamic contrast-enhancement series. We have used Breast Imaging Reporting and Data System (BI-RADS) guidelines as a structured framework. Using these guidelines we designed a BI-RADS decision tree with eight quantitative measurements using radiomics, together with the lesion size and component count, that determine the descriptors at eight nodes. Using these descriptors we generate natural-language rationale using a large language model. The model was constrained to express only the derived records from the decision tree. After applying this framework to the expert and automatic masks of each of the 1,506 cases in the MAMA-MIA dataset, we generated a reproducible corpus of 3,012 BI-RADS grounded rationales. We obtained a faithfulness score of 99.98% and a counterfactual accuracy of 99.3%, indicating that the generated rationales faithfully verbalises the measured descriptors and correctly reflects changes in the underlying record. Agreement between the descriptors from expert and automatic masks falls from 0.832 to 0.549 as mask quality falls from Good to Missed. The generated rationales are intended as draft representations for radiologist review and final sign-off rather than autonomous clinical reports.

</details>


### [23] [Diff-IrisPAD: Modeling Bona Fide Iris Distributions for Unsupervised Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29611)
*Geetanjali Sharma,Harsh Yadav,Tanmay Deshpande,Shubham Ashwani,Aditya Nigam,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无监督的Diff-IrisPAD框架，基于布朗桥扩散建模真实虹膜分布，通过重建不一致性检测呈现攻击，在多传感器大规模数据集上ACER为30.52，优于扩散基线并对未见攻击有强泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虹膜呈现攻击检测（PAD）依赖带攻击数据的监督学习，导致对未见攻击类型和新传感器泛化能力受限。

Method: Diff-IrisPAD采用布朗桥扩散（Brownian Bridge Diffusion）仅对真实（bona fide）虹膜分布建模，不使用攻击样本；从退化输入重建干净虹膜纹理，并利用ViT评分与像素级度量捕捉重建不一致性来判别攻击。

Result: 在大规模多传感器数据集（12.5万+图像，NIR与VIS，8种攻击类型）上，整体ACER达30.52，显著优于扩散类基线，并对未见攻击表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于扩散的真实虹膜建模对鲁棒、可扩展的虹膜PAD有效，为无需攻击样本的无监督检测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Iris recognition systems are vulnerable to presentation attacks (PAs), including textured contact lenses, print replays, and synthetic irises. Existing PAD methods rely on supervised learning with attack data, limiting generalization to unseen attacks and new sensors. We propose Diff-IrisPAD, an unsupervised framework based on Brownian Bridge Diffusion that models bona fide iris distributions without using attack samples. The method reconstructs clean iris textures from degraded inputs and detects attacks via reconstruction inconsistency using ViT scoring and pixel-level measures. Experiments on a large-scale multi-sensor dataset (125K+ images, NIR and VIS, 8 attack types) show that Diff-IrisPAD achieves an overall ACER of 30.52, significantly outperforming diffusion-based baselines while demonstrating strong generalization to unseen attacks. These results highlight the effectiveness of diffusion-based bona fide modeling for robust and scalable iris PAD.

</details>


### [24] [LEAF-Mamba: Feature Adaptation and Fusion for Whole Slide Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29746)
*Lubin Gan,Jing Zhang,Mingjun Ma,Linhao Qu,Dachun Kai,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LEAF-Mamba 是一个面向全切片图像（WSI）分析的框架，联合进行隐证据自适应与融合：用重建正则化适配器学习紧凑的任务相关表示并重建输入特征，再由并行的 Mamba 与卷积流分别建模长程依赖和序列局部交互，经输入相关门控调制后做切片级池化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI 为千兆像素级且标注有限，现有基于冻结预训练编码器特征的 MIL 方法存在两点不足：仅依赖任务监督，未显式约束特征自适应过程中的信息保留；Mamba 将局部处理与长程传播耦合，无法为融合保留独立表示。

Method: 提出 LEAF-Mamba：1）重建正则化适配器，在重建输入特征的同时学习紧凑、任务特定的表示；2）并行 Mamba 流与卷积流，分别捕获长程序列依赖与局部交互；3）输入相关门控在切片级池化前调制二者联合表示。

Result: 在多种特征提取器下，诊断分类与生存预测任务上的平均性能均优于强 MIL 基线；消融/组件分析进一步支持自适应与融合两个模块的贡献。

Conclusion: 联合设计特征变换与上下文聚合，可将预训练表示有效转化为切片级预测，凸显二者协同设计的价值。

Abstract: Whole slide image (WSI) analysis enables computational diagnosis and prognosis, but gigapixel images and limited annotations make slide-level learning challenging. Multiple instance learning addresses this task by aggregating patch representations from frozen pretrained encoders. Recently, Mamba has enabled efficient modeling of long patch sequences. However, task supervision alone does not explicitly constrain information recovery during feature adaptation, while Mamba couples local processing with long-range propagation rather than retaining separate representations for fusion. Motivated by these challenges, we introduce LEAF-Mamba, a framework that jointly addresses latent evidence adaptation and fusion for frozen-feature WSI analysis. A reconstruction-regularized adapter learns compact, task-specific representations while reconstructing the input features. Parallel Mamba and convolutional streams then capture long-range dependencies and sequence-local interactions, with an input-dependent gate modulating their joint representation before slide-level pooling. Experiments across feature extractors demonstrate improved average performance over strong MIL baselines in both diagnostic classification and survival prediction. Component analyses further support the contributions of adaptation and fusion. These findings highlight the value of jointly designing feature transformation and contextual aggregation to translate pretrained representations into effective slide-level predictions.

</details>


### [25] [APED-CXR: Adaptive Privileged Evidence Distillation for Chest X-Ray Grounding with Image-Only Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29740)
*Md Shihabul Islam Shovo,Nishi Kanta Paul,Jamil Fayyad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: APED-CXR 将报告知情多模态教师的空间证据蒸馏到 11.1M 参数的学生模型，测试时仅需图像即可同时预测疾病与边界框，刷新学生提议使定位 F1 提升 0.036。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑的 CXR 定位模型适合部署，但边界框标注稀缺，而报告感知的多模态模型推理成本高。

Method: 冻结多模态教师（输入图像、报告、查询类别）评估由学生当前证据图生成的区域提议，并将其支持分数作为学生区域池化证据分数的训练目标；学生从共享证据图同时输出疾病与框，测试仅用图像。教师提议需随学生证据图刷新。

Result: PadChest 训练、PadChest-GR 测试：刷新提议使 IoU≥0.5 的框级定位 F1 从 0.302 升至 0.338（增益 0.036，95% CI [0.022, 0.049]），诊断 AUC 保持；随机框、固定稠密框集、教师生成框或报告规则替代均无此增益，两次刷新前的提议仅恢复部分增益。NIH ChestX-ray14 上框召回从 0.423 升至 0.447。

Conclusion: 教师对"当前"学生提议的动态验证是定位增益的关键来源，该蒸馏范式在图像-only 推理下实现了高效的空间证据学习。

Abstract: Compact chest X-ray (CXR) models that localize findings suit deployment, but box annotations are limited and report-aware multimodal models are expensive at inference. We introduce APED-CXR, which distills report-informed spatial evidence from a frozen multimodal teacher into a student with 11.1M parameters that predicts diseases and boxes from shared evidence maps and requires the image alone at test time. During training, the teacher uses the radiograph, report, and queried class to evaluate regions proposed from the student's current evidence maps, and its support scores serve as training targets for the student's pooled evidence scores within these regions. Trained on PadChest and evaluated on held-out PadChest-GR, refreshing the student's proposals for teacher evaluation improves box-level localization F1 from 0.302 with fixed warm-up proposals to 0.338 (IoU >= 0.5; gain 0.036, 95% CI [0.022, 0.049]), while maintaining diagnostic AUC. This gain depends on the teacher checking current proposals: with equally many random boxes, a fixed dense box set, teacher-generated boxes, or report rules instead of the teacher, F1 stays near the fixed-proposal level, and proposals two refreshes old recover only part of the gain. On NIH ChestX-ray14, a secondary test set, box recall also rises from 0.423 to 0.447.

</details>


### [26] [Efficient Generative Image Compression with Quantized Latents Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29691)
*Hao Cao,Yinhuan Huang,Wenqi Guo,Zhijin Qin,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GR-GIC：以Guided Refinement提升低码率重建，以重组的P-RVQ加速推理，兼顾感知质量与低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高感知质量GIC依赖预训练扩散模型、延迟高；轻量GIC虽快但感知质量差。作者观察到轻量GIC在低码率下粗粒度确定性量化会把对应不同细节的邻近encoder潜变量坍缩到同一点，导致细节难以恢复。

Method: GR-GIC包含两部分：(1) Guided Refinement（GR）——由熵上下文引导的潜变量精炼，结合自适应扰动作为训练正则，联合改善低码率重建；(2) 重组的投影残差向量量化（P-RVQ）——将残差更新等价重组为低维查表，暴露更多并行性、减少串行计算，实现快速推理。

Result: Kodak上相比OneDC，LPIPS/DISTS/PSNR/FID的BD-rate分别节省23.19%、37.71%、22.30%、22.14%；A100上编解码为4.60/5.98 ms；重组P-RVQ在FP32下平均加速3.14×；Jetson Orin NX上延迟与轻量GIC相当。

Conclusion: GR-GIC在保持快速推理的同时取得具竞争力的感知重建性能，验证了潜变量精炼与量化重组的有效性。

Abstract: Generative image compression (GIC) delivers strong perceptual quality at low bitrates. However, GIC methods with strong perceptual quality often rely on pretrained diffusion models with high latency, while recent lightweight GIC methods reduce latency but deliver lower perceptual quality. We observe that, for lightweight GIC methods at low bitrates, coarse deterministic quantization can collapse nearby encoder latents corresponding to different fine details to the same point, making these details difficult to recover. Motivated by this, we propose GR-GIC, an efficient GIC that delivers competitive reconstruction performance with fast inference. For better performance, we introduce Guided Refinement (GR), which combines latent refinement guided by entropy context with adaptive perturbations for training regularization, jointly improving reconstruction at low bitrates. For fast inference, we introduce reorganized projected residual vector quantization (P-RVQ), which equivalently reorganizes residual updates into low-dimensional lookups to expose greater parallelism and reduce sequential computation. Experiments on Kodak show that for performance, compared with OneDC, GR-GIC achieves BD-rate savings of 23.19%, 37.71%, 22.30%, and 22.14% for LPIPS, DISTS, PSNR, and FID, respectively. For fast inference, the highly optimized implementation of GR-GIC encodes and decodes in 4.60 and 5.98 ms on an NVIDIA A100. Specifically, the reorganized P-RVQ implementation achieves a $3.14\times$ average speedup over the original implementation under the same FP32 setting. On an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX, GR-GIC achieves latency comparable to lightweight GIC methods.

</details>


### [27] [Learning to Reason Like a Cytopathologist:Clinically Grounded Multiscale Evidential Learning for Cervical Cytology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29665)
*Ajeet Kumar Singh,Nehal Mishra,Anurag Tripathi,Santanu Chaudhury,Dorien Herremans*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多尺度证据框架：用序数软锚点建模宫颈细胞学连续谱，三个生物空间上下文经共享冻结DINOv2独立编码，经校准后转为Dempster–Shafer信任质量并带可靠性折扣融合，使冲突与证据不足显式保留为"未决"，驱动可解释分流；在RIVA与CRIC Cervix上泛化一致，错误预测的不确定性为正确预测的1.94–4.13倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫颈细胞学存在定义性的形态学灰区（如ASC-US），诊断本质上具模糊性；临床决策支持不仅需给出确定标签，还应区分"证据充分可自信决策"与"需进一步检查"的样本，而常规分类模型无法显式表征这一区别。此外，细胞核形态、胞质特征与邻域组织结构等多尺度信息可能在早期融合中被掩盖其相互分歧。

Method: 1）以临床依据的软锚点（soft anchors）将宫颈细胞学建模为从良性反应性改变到癌前病变及恶性的序数疾病连续谱；2）对三个生物学驱动的空间上下文（核形态、胞质特征、邻域细胞架构）独立编码，共享一个冻结的DINOv2骨干，保留尺度间分歧；3）将校准后的预测转换为Dempster–Shafer信任质量，并采用可靠性折扣进行证据融合，使冲突证据与支持不足保持"未承诺"而非强制判定；4）由融合信念状态驱动可解释路由，将高置信样本与存在不可约证据不足的样本分离，实现临床有意义的分流。

Result: 在两个独立公开常规细胞学数据集RIVA与CRIC Cervix上表现一致泛化；不确定性强度与诊断难度显著相关，错误预测所携带的不确定性为正确预测的1.94–4.13倍。

Conclusion: 将序数连续谱建模与多尺度证据融合相结合，可在宫颈细胞学中显式表征并量化认知不确定性，实现置信病例与证据不足病例的可解释分流，为临床分诊提供可靠依据；不确定性可作为诊断难度的有效指示信号。

Abstract: Cervical cytology presents a fundamentally ambiguous diagnostic landscape, where equivocal categories such as Atypical Squamous Cells of Undetermined Significance (ASC-US) occupy a morphological grey zone that is definitional. For clinical decision support, the goal extends beyond assigning a definitive label to every sample: cases must also be distinguished by whether the available evidence supports a confident decision or warrants further consideration, a distinction conventional classification models are not designed to explicitly represent. We propose a multiscale evidential framework that models cervical cytology along an ordinal disease continuum using clinically grounded soft anchors, representing progression from benign reactive changes to precancerous lesions and malignancy. Three biologically motivated spatial contexts--nuclear morphology, cytoplasmic characteristics, and neighbourhood cellular architecture--are independently encoded using a shared frozen DINOv2 backbone, preserving inter-scale disagreement that early fusion may obscure. Calibrated predictions are transformed into Dempster--Shafer belief masses and fused with reliability discounting, allowing conflicting evidence and insufficient support to remain explicitly uncommitted rather than forcing a decision. The fused belief state drives interpretable routing that separates confidently classified cases from those with irreducible evidential insufficiency, enabling clinically meaningful triage. Across two independent publicly available conventional cytology datasets, RIVA and CRIC Cervix, the framework generalises consistently, with uncertainty strongly associated with diagnostic difficulty: incorrect predictions carry 1.94x-4.13x higher uncertainty than correct ones.

</details>


### [28] [Joint Semantic-Channel Coding and Modulation for Generative Image Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29643)
*Hao Cao,Jingkai Ying,Yinhuan Huang,Zhijin Qin,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SemQAM——面向生成式图像传输的联合语义信道编码与调制方案，将潜在特征经VQ映射到QAM符号，使信道损伤直接影响码本学习，并用接收码字条件期望抑制自回归误差传播；单模型支持多SNR/CBR，并兼容传统源编码（SemQAM-C）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字图像传输通常将源编码、信道编码与调制分开优化，限制了源表示对信道损伤的适应性；同时信道损伤会破坏接收端潜在表示，并在自回归编码中逐级累积，导致重建质量下降。

Method: 用向量量化(VQ)表示潜在特征，将每个VQ索引映射为一个QAM符号；训练时依据检测到的符号更新对应码字，使信道损伤直接参与码本学习；推理时以接收码字的条件期望近似接收端重建，减少自回归编码中的误差传播。统一模型支持多种SNR与CBR，并保留传统源编码路径SemQAM-C。

Result: 在相同LDPC与QAM设置下，SemQAM-C在LPIPS与DISTS上优于OneDC且复杂度显著更低；相比分离式方案，在LPIPS相当时信道使用数减少18.57%，并优于所对比的JSCC方法。

Conclusion: 联合语义-信道编码与调制相较分离式设计具有明确增益：统一模型既能保持有竞争力的源编码能力，又能提升信道效率与重建保真度。

Abstract: Digital image transmission typically optimizes source coding, channel coding, and modulation separately, limiting how source representations adapt to channel impairments. We propose SemQAM, a joint semantic-channel coding and modulation scheme for generative image transmission converting source to modulated symbols, such as standard QAM symbols. Channel impairments create two challenges: they can corrupt the latent representation at the receiver, and this corruption can compound through autoregressive coding, degrading reconstruction quality. First, SemQAM represents latent features with vector quantization (VQ), maps each VQ index to a QAM symbol, and updates the codeword corresponding to the detected symbol so that channel impairments directly affect codebook learning. Moreover, SemQAM uses the conditional expectation of received codewords to approximate the receiver reconstruction and reduce error propagation during autoregressive coding. SemQAM supports multiple SNRs and CBRs in a unified model, while also supporting conventional source coding through SemQAM-C. Experiments show that (i) under the same LDPC and QAM settings, SemQAM-C outperforms OneDC in LPIPS and DISTS with substantially lower complexity, showing that the unified model retains competitive capability for source coding. (ii) Compared with this separated counterpart, SemQAM reduces channel uses by 18.57% at matched LPIPS and outperforms the compared JSCC methods, demonstrating the benefit of joint coding.

</details>


### [29] [Region-Aware Multi-View Inspection Using Deep Learning and Domain-Informed Decision Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29398)
*Srivishnu Ethirajulu Krishnaraj,Rishikesh R. Chopade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出区域感知双模型检测框架，结合实例分割与图像级分类，并用确定性的决策融合层形式化工业SOP，将多视角帧级输出转化为部件级判定，使燃油喷射器针阀误拒率从57.6%降至10.9%，同时保持100%不良品召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密零件微小表面缺陷的自动光学检测中，同一零件的不同区域缺陷特征与验收标准不同，单一模型或简单的模型输出聚合难以兼顾灵敏度与实用判定。

Method: 区域感知双模型框架：对局部座面区域缺陷做实例分割，对全局轴杆区域异常做图像级分类；引入形式化工业SOP的确定性决策融合层对模型输出做部件级判定；PLC同步成像系统在360°旋转中采集18张座面与18张轴杆图像实现多视角周向覆盖。

Result: 在380件汽车燃油喷射器针阀上评估（所有配置使用相同模型预测）：简单并集逻辑识别出全部104件不合格品，但部件级误拒率达57.6%；加入完整决策层后，NOK召回保持100%，误拒率降至10.9%，误正部件决策减少81.1%。

Conclusion: 形式化的制造验收准则可与敏感视觉模型互补，将帧级检测转化为实用且可追溯的部件级决策。

Abstract: Automated optical inspection of precision components with tiny surface defects remains challenging when different regions of the same component exhibit different defect characteristics and, therefore, different acceptance criteria. This paper presents a region-aware, dual-model inspection framework that combines instance segmentation of localized seat-region defects with image-level classification of global shaft-region anomalies. Rather than treating these two model detections as sufficient evidence for component rejection, a deterministic decision fusion layer that formalizes industrial Standard Operating Procedures is applied to the model outputs to make the final component level decision. A PLC-synchronized imaging system acquires 18 seat and 18 shaft images over a full 360-degree rotation to provide multi-view circumferential coverage. The framework was evaluated on 380 automotive fuel injector needles, covering representative operational defect types, using identical model predictions in all configurations. Direct model-output aggregation via simple union logic (where any single-view detection flags a rejection) identified all 104 non-conforming components but produced a component level false rejection rate of 57.6%. Inclusion of a complete decision layer maintained the observed non-conforming (NOK) recall of 100% while reducing the false rejection rate to 10.9%, corresponding to an 81.1% reduction in false-positive component decisions. The results demonstrate that formalized manufacturing acceptance criteria can complement sensitive visual models by translating frame-level detections into practical and traceable component-level decisions.

</details>


### [30] [Failure Modes of Vision-Language Models for Human Activity Understanding in Thermal HRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29244)
*Han Hu,He Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统评估了7个可本地部署的视觉语言模型（含面向具身智能与机器人的模型）在两人本中心热红外数据集上的人类活动理解失效模式，涵盖细粒度动作识别、活动时间线重建与拒答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热成像相机可在暗光下感知人类活动，且相较RGB相机泄露的可识别外观信息与场景细节更少，具备隐私友好优势；但VLM原本针对RGB图像训练，其在热红外人类活动理解与HRI场景中的失效模式尚未被充分研究。

Method: 对7个可本地部署的VLM（含物理AI/机器人专用模型）在两个以人为中心的热红外数据集上进行诊断性评测；任务包括细粒度动作识别、活动时间线重建与"无依据时拒答"；以配对的RGB录像作为性能参照，并控制帧采样、热输入呈现方式与推理过程的变体，检验误差是否可被缓解。

Result: 模型能识别粗粒度活动类别，但在咳嗽、打喷嚏、打哈欠等细粒度动作上表现不佳；时间线重建不准确，并出现涉及物体的动作幻觉；简单的热输入预处理与额外推理仅带来有限且依赖具体模型的增益；即便给出明确指令，各模型的拒答行为差异显著。

Conclusion: 现有VLM在热红外人类活动理解上存在细粒度识别弱、时序重建差、幻觉与拒答不稳定等系统性缺陷，限制了其在真实机器人应用中的可靠性；需针对性改进热模态适配与不确定性表达能力。

Abstract: Thermal cameras enable robots to perceive human activities in darkness while revealing less personally identifiable appearance information and fewer details of the surrounding environment than RGB cameras. As vision-language models (VLMs), originally developed for RGB imagery, are increasingly integrated into embodied systems for scene understanding and high-level planning, thermal sensing offers a promising way to extend activity perception to low-light and privacy-sensitive settings. However, the failure modes of VLMs in thermal human activity understanding for human-robot interaction (HRI) remain underexplored. We fill this gap by evaluating seven locally deployable VLMs, including models developed for physical AI and robotics, on two person-centric thermal datasets. Our diagnostic tasks examine fine-grained action recognition, activity timeline reconstruction, and the ability to withhold unsupported answers. Paired RGB recordings serve as a performance reference, while controlled variations in frame sampling, thermal input presentation, and reasoning are used to assess whether the observed errors can be mitigated. Our results show that models can recognize broad activity categories but struggle with fine-grained actions such as coughing, sneezing, and yawning. They also produce inaccurate event timelines and hallucinate actions involving objects. Simple thermal input preprocessing and additional reasoning offer only limited, model-dependent gains. Abstention behavior also varies across models even under explicit instructions, limiting their reliability for practical robotic use.

</details>


### [31] [DisasterRoute: Evidence-Guided Dual-System Routing for Visual Disaster Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29231)
*Tan Zhang,Xing Deng,Lu Zhang,Xiaofeng Zhu,Ping Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向灾害无人机影像的快速视觉问答系统：Disaster-S1 在单次多模态前向传播中输出选项概率分布以实现快速直答，DisasterRoute 结合预测置信度与灾害证据进行双系统路由，按需调用 DisasterVL 推理模型复核，兼顾低延迟与高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无人机影像的灾害视觉问答需在边缘算力受限下及时给出判断。常规问题可直答，难题需逐步推理；但现有研究多聚焦视觉理解与生成推理能力提升，对高效直答及其与生成推理的自适应协同探索不足。

Method: 基于适配灾害领域的视觉语言模型构建 Disaster-S1，以选项判别方式训练，在单次多模态前向中输出答案选项上的概率分布以快速决策；并提出 DisasterRoute 双系统路由框架，融合预测置信度与灾害证据，决定接受初始答案或调用灾害推理模型 DisasterVL 复核，实现按需算力分配。

Result: 在 DisasterBench 上，Disaster-S1 以低延迟取得更优准确率；DisasterRoute 通过选择性复核进一步提升准确率，且延迟显著低于完整推理。

Conclusion: 直答与生成推理的自适应协同可在边缘资源约束下有效平衡灾害视觉问答的准确率与效率。

Abstract: Visual question answering from low-altitude UAV imagery must deliver timely and accurate judgments to support disaster response under limited edge computing resources. Routine questions may be answered through direct decisions, whereas more challenging questions may benefit from step-by-step reasoning. However, existing research primarily focuses on improving models' visual understanding and generative reasoning capabilities, with limited exploration of efficient direct decision-making and its adaptive coordination with generative reasoning. We develop Disaster-S1 based on a vision-language model adapted to the disaster domain. Trained for option discrimination, it outputs a probability distribution over answer options in a single multimodal forward pass for fast decision-making. We further propose DisasterRoute, a dual-system routing framework that combines prediction confidence with disaster evidence to decide whether to accept the initial answer or invoke the disaster reasoning model DisasterVL for review. By allocating inference computation on demand, the framework retains the efficiency of direct decision-making and improves overall question-answering accuracy through selective review. Experiments on DisasterBench show that Disaster-S1 achieves superior accuracy with low latency, while DisasterRoute further improves accuracy through selective review at substantially lower latency than full reasoning.

</details>


### [32] [Cache Locally, Recompute Selectively for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29229)
*Junhyeok Lee,Sangyoon Lee,Jaeho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FrameBlend：一种免训练、即插即用的方法，通过在查询时对选定视频token进行选择性KV重计算，解决流式视频理解中预填充阶段上下文受限导致的上下文不匹配问题，在降低预填充成本的同时提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解（SVU）要求视频-LLM在持续接收视频的同时以低延迟回答查询。现有方法利用空闲时间预填充帧到KV缓存以降低查询时开销，但为约束每帧计算量而限制了预填充阶段的上下文，造成"上下文不匹配"：受限上下文可能遗漏正确上下文化视频token所需的信息，却仍包含与最终查询（预填充时未知）无关的信息。

Method: FrameBlend为即插即用方法，无需额外训练。查询时，使用与查询相关的上下文对选定的视频token重新进行预填充（re-prefill），并用重算得到的KV替换其缓存KV。该重计算机制使得空闲期预填充可采用更短的注意力窗口，从而显著降低预填充开销。

Result: 在三个SVU框架和五个流式及离线基准上，准确率最高提升3.9个百分点。在RVS-Ego上，每帧预填充FLOPs降低超过20%，基于检索的框架总处理时间减少14–59%（已计入查询时重计算成本）。

Conclusion: 通过在空闲时间与查询时间之间重新分配上下文化工作，仅做简单改动即可改善SVU的准确率-效率权衡。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding (SVU) requires video-LLMs to process incoming video continuously while answering user queries with low latency. Recent methods reduce query-time cost by prefilling frames into KV caches during idle time and reusing the cached KVs at query time. To bound per-frame computation over unbounded video streams, these methods restrict the context available during prefill. This creates a fundamental context mismatch: the restricted context may omit information needed to properly contextualize video tokens, yet, still contain information irrelevant to the eventual query, which is unknown during prefill. We propose FrameBlend, a simple yet effective plug-and-play method to address this mismatch through selective KV recomputation, without requiring any additional training. At query time, FrameBlend re-prefills selected video tokens using query-relevant context and replaces their cached KVs with recomputed ones. Crucially, this recomputation enables shorter attention windows during idle-time prefill, substantially reducing prefill cost while improving accuracy. Experiments across three SVU frameworks and five streaming and offline benchmarks demonstrate accuracy gains of up to 3.9 pp. On RVS-Ego, FrameBlend reduces per-frame prefill FLOPs by over 20% and total processing time by 14-59% for retrieval-based frameworks, including the cost of query-time recomputation. These results demonstrate that a simple change in how contextualization is distributed between idle time and query time can improve the accuracy-efficiency trade-off for SVU.

</details>


### [33] [NeuralSRender: G-Buffer Guided Video Diffusion for Sim-to-Real Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29200)
*Yipeng Lu,Youqi Liao,Zhenyu Wang,Yuning Peng,Zhen Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuralSRender：基于预训练视频DiT的仿真到真实视频翻译框架，通过G-buffer几何/材质先验注入与两阶段LoRA适配提升合成视频照片真实感并保持几何一致性，参数开销仅1.45%，并发布GenReal数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知缺乏高质量标注视觉数据；模拟器可规模化生成数据，但合成视频真实感不足，导致真实场景感知性能下降。需要在保持源场景几何一致性的前提下提升合成视频的照片真实感，实现仿真到真实的视频翻译。

Method: 提出NeuralSRender，构建于预训练视频扩散Transformer（DiT）之上：用轻量projector将G-buffer中的几何与材质先验注入模型，并用LoRA实现参数高效适配。两阶段训练：第一阶段在具有精确G-buffer的合成视频上学习G-buffer条件控制；第二阶段在有估计G-buffer的真实视频上适配真实外观，此时新增一个LoRA模块训练，而projector与第一阶段LoRA冻结，以降低估计噪声干扰。同时构建GenReal大规模驾驶视频数据集（合成与真实视频及对应G-buffer）。

Result: 仅引入1.45%参数开销，可在24 GB显存的消费级GPU上训练；生成视频照片真实感提升且几何一致性具竞争力。用翻译视频替代原始模拟器渲染训练感知模型，nuImages检测AP50由35.1提升至40.6，Cityscapes分割mIoU由32.4提升至40.8。

Conclusion: NeuralSRender以低参数开销和低成本硬件实现了兼顾真实感与几何一致性的sim-to-real视频翻译，显著提升下游检测与分割性能，并通过GenReal数据集与开源代码/权重支持后续研究。

Abstract: The scarcity of high-quality annotated visual data remains a critical bottleneck for autonomous-driving perception. Simulators provide training data at scale, but the limited realism of synthetic videos compromises perception performance in real-world applications. To address this limitation, we propose NeuralSRender, a sim-to-real video translation framework that enhances the photorealism of synthetic videos while maintaining geometric consistency with the source scenes. Built on a pretrained video diffusion transformer (DiT), NeuralSRender uses a lightweight projector to inject geometric and material priors from G-buffers, and LoRA enables parameter-efficient adaptation. The model is trained in two stages: it first learns G-buffer conditioning from synthetic videos with accurate G-buffers, then adapts to real-world appearance using real videos with estimated G-buffers. During the second stage, an additional LoRA module is trained while the projector and first-stage LoRA remain frozen to reduce interference from estimation noise. NeuralSRender introduces only 1.45\% parameter overhead and can be trained on consumer-grade GPUs with 24 GB of memory. To support training and evaluation, we introduce GenReal, a large-scale driving video dataset comprising synthetic and real-world videos with corresponding G-buffers. Experiments demonstrate improved photorealism with competitive geometric consistency. Training perception models on our translated videos instead of raw simulator renders improves detection $\mathrm{AP}_{50}$ on nuImages from 35.1 to 40.6 and segmentation mIoU on Cityscapes from 32.4 to 40.8. Our code, data, and model weights are available at: https://github.com/WHU-USI3DV/NeuralSRender.

</details>


### [34] [Multi-Scale Latent Temporal Compression Attention and Video-Text Cross Attention Fusion for Video Action Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29176)
*Yicheng Qiu,Keiji Yanai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多尺度潜在时间压缩注意力的视频时间注意力模块，以及视频-文本交叉注意力融合模块，用于视频动作检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频流中时序依赖建模至关重要，但现有深度网络方法难以刻画动态时序模式，存在帧信息冗余、全局与局部特征难以平衡、视觉与文本特征融合不足等问题。

Method: 提出多尺度潜在时间压缩注意力（Multi-Scale Latent Temporal Compression Attention）模块，将连续时序序列聚合为多时间尺度的特征表示并降低冗余；同时引入视频-文本交叉注意力融合（Video-Text Cross Attention Fusion）模块，在潜在特征与标准特征两个层级使用交叉注意力进行细粒度融合，增强语义对齐与特征交互。

Result: 在多个视频识别基准上取得优于现有方法的性能，验证了方法的有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 多尺度时间压缩注意力与双层交叉注意力融合可有效缓解时序建模中的冗余与融合不足问题，提升视频动作检测性能。

Abstract: In video action detection, modeling the temporal dependencies in continuous video streams is crucial. Previous methods based on deep neural networks can perform frame-level video recognition tasks. However, these methods struggle to model dynamic temporal patterns, facing challenges such as redundant frame information, difficulty balancing global and local features, and insufficient fusion of visual and textual features. To address these issues, we propose a video temporal attention module based on the Multi-Scale Latent Temporal Compression Attention, which aggregates continuous temporal sequences into feature representations across diverse temporal scales while efficiently reducing redundancy. We also introduce a Video-Text Cross Attention Fusion module that leverages cross-attention at both latent and standard feature levels, achieving fine-grained fusion between video and textual features to enhance semantic alignment and feature interaction. Experimental results across multiple video recognition benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed methods achieve superior performance compared to existing methods, validating the effectiveness and generalization capability.

</details>


### [35] [LocalAgent: Collaborative Agentic Verification for Fine-Grained Instance-Level Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29174)
*Jiachen Guo,Xinshan Zhu,Boyun Wang,Yanyan Liang,Jun Wan,Feng Lin,Ajian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LocalAgent 是一个协作式 agentic 框架，用通用 MLLM 做全局推理并定位可疑局部区域，再交由轻量专家验证器工具包 LEV 做细粒度匹配，配合辅助项限幅的 RFT 策略，在实例级一致性验证上平均准确率提升 4.9%，且仅在 12.13% 样本上调用验证器。


<details>
