  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在通用视觉理解上进展迅速，但在细粒度实例级一致性验证（判断两图是否同一物理对象，且视角、光照、背景变化）上仍不足。该任务要求同时具备全局语义感知与对细微局部证据的精确比对，因为语义相似的对象可能仅在小块可疑区域上存在差异；纯 MLLM 难以可靠完成这种局部精确检验。

Method: 提出 LocalAgent 协作式 agentic 框架：1）通用 MLLM 先进行全局推理，定位可疑局部区域；2）将区域委派给 Local Evidence Verifier（LEV）工具包做细粒度外观与几何匹配，LEV 由 Local Appearance Consistency（LAC，在光照与颜色变化下估计局部外观相似度）和 Local Geometric Correspondence（LGC，在视角变化下估计空间对应关系）组成；3）LEV 返回结构化标量证据，由 MLLM 整合后作出最终判定。训练上提出 auxiliary-capped reinforcement fine-tuning（RFT），令答案正确性主导辅助的工具使用与格式信号，使策略学会何时需要验证。

Result: 相比最强的 CoT-RFT 基线，平均准确率提升 4.9%；仅在 12.13% 的样本上调用验证器；同时保留了底层 MLLM 的通用多模态能力。

Conclusion: 将通用 MLLM 的全局语义推理与轻量专家验证器的局部精确匹配相结合，并以"答案优先、辅助信号限幅"的 RFT 训练调度工具调用，是提升实例级一致性验证的有效且高效的范式；按需触发的验证机制在显著提升精度的同时控制开销，并避免通用能力退化。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made rapid progress in general visual understanding, yet they still struggle with fine-grained instance-level consistency verification, where the goal is to determine whether two images depict the exact same physical object under varying viewpoints, illumination, and backgrounds. This task requires both global semantic perception and precise examination of subtle local evidence, since semantically similar objects may differ only in small suspicious regions. To address this challenge, we propose LocalAgent, a collaborative agentic framework that integrates the complementary strengths of a generalist MLLM and lightweight specialist verifiers. The MLLM first reasons globally to identify suspicious local regions, which are then delegated to a Local Evidence Verifier (LEV) toolkit for fine-grained appearance and geometric matching. LEV comprises Local Appearance Consistency (LAC), which estimates appearance similarity under illumination and color changes, and Local Geometric Correspondence (LGC), which estimates spatial correspondence under viewpoint changes. LEV returns structured scalar evidence that the MLLM integrates for the final decision. We further introduce an auxiliary-capped reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) strategy, in which answer correctness dominates auxiliary tool-use and format signals, enabling the policy to learn when verification is necessary. Experiments show that LocalAgent improves mean accuracy by 4.9% over the strongest CoT-RFT baseline while invoking a verifier on only 12.13% of samples and preserving the general multimodal capabilities of the underlying MLLM. The open-source repository is available at https://github.com/Firefly-zoo/LocalAgent-main.

</details>


### [36] [Weakly Supervised Fish Segmentation through Point-Supervised Tracking and Motion-Informed Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29047)
*Vedanth Kallakuri,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅需点标注（每条鱼单击一次）的弱监督鱼类分割流水线，通过点跟踪、运动信息驱动的语言表达引导语言条件分割模型（LMPM++）并结合光流与背景减除后处理，在DeepFish数据集上前景mIoU优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鱼类种群属性分析（长度、宽度、质量、计数）对水产养殖与海洋保护至关重要，而基于深度学习的分割模型可规模化获取这些信息，但其训练依赖逐像素分割标注，单条鱼人工标注耗时可达两分钟，成本高、效率低。

Method: 四阶段弱监督分割流水线：（1）点监督跟踪器为每帧预测点标注；（2）将点转换为含运动信息的语言表达；（3）以该表达引导语言条件分割模型LMPM++，捕捉时序一致性并生成候选分割掩码；（4）以光流与背景减除作为后处理滤波，抑制错误分割噪声。

Result: 在DeepFish数据集上，前景平均交并比（mIoU）相较基线模型有所提升。

Conclusion: 点级弱监督结合运动信息语言引导与光流/背景减除后处理，可在大幅降低标注成本的前提下获得精确的鱼体分割掩码，为鱼类种群监测提供可扩展方案。

Abstract: Understanding the properties of fish populations is increasingly important. Analyzing fish length, width, and mass is integral to maintaining healthy stock in the aquaculture industry, while counting wild fish populations supports marine conservation and coastal reef restoration efforts. Deep learning-based segmentation models offer a promising means of obtaining such information at scale. Most of these models, however, require costly and time-consuming per-pixel segmentation labels for training, which can take up to two minutes per fish to annotate manually. To address this inefficiency, we propose a weakly supervised segmentation pipeline that requires only point-labeled data (one click per fish) to produce accurate segmentation masks. Our pipeline consists of four stages. First, a point-supervised tracker predicts point annotations for each frame. These points are then converted into motion-informed language expressions, which guide a language-conditioned segmentation model (LMPM++) to capture temporal consistency and produce candidate segmentation masks. Finally, optical flow and background subtraction are applied as post-processing filters to suppress noise from incorrect segmentations. Experiments on the DeepFish dataset show improvement in foreground mean intersection over union (mIoU) as compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [37] [Single-Image Food Mass Estimators Do Not Transfer Their Gram Scale Across Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29025)
*Yongsheng Lian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在Nutrition5k上微调的DINOv2-base单图食物质量估计在同域MAE为24.8 g，但对新相机和新食物的迁移能力差：外部数据集上不及零样本Qwen3-VL-32B，且不优于食物像素计数；其克度尺度既随取景范围变化，又因食物种类呈不同倍率偏差；需目标场景的少量标注照片才能可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像膳食评估的核心是从单张RGB照片估计食物质量，但现有模型能否迁移到新相机与新食物尚不清楚，且常见评测方式可能高估泛化性能。

Method: 在2,253份带称重的Nutrition5k菜品上微调DINOv2-base做单图质量回归，并与零样本视觉语言模型Qwen3-VL-32B对比；在Nutrition5k及三个外部数据集（ASA24 studio portions、ECUSTFD整食、Food Portion Benchmark餐盘）上评测。另做消融/诊断实验：加入少量目标域食物或餐盘训练、改用DINOv2-large主干、单张标注照片校准、分析预测区间覆盖率、检验随机划分下的近重复照片泄漏。

Result: 同域MAE 24.8 g，不足Qwen的一半；外部数据集上未能优于Qwen。对未见食物，模型能按大小排序份量但不如食物像素计数；克度尺度随取景占比变化，且每种食物偏差倍率不同。加入测试数据集中的其他食物或餐盘训练，外部误差降低38%–65%；DINOv2-large未降低外部误差；同食物一张标注照片将ASA24误差降至28.3 g，优于该照片单独估计；90%覆盖率预测区间在外部数据上覆盖率至多约50%；Nutrition5k同一餐盘多次拍摄导致随机划分与训练集近重复，高估精度。

Conclusion: 单图像质量估计器缺乏跨域泛化能力，性能被近重复泄漏的评测所高估，实际部署需来自目标使用场景的标注照片（或目标域数据）进行校准与微调。

Abstract: Estimating food mass from a photograph is central to image-based dietary assessment, but whether trained models transfer to new cameras and foods is unclear. We fine-tuned DINOv2-base on 2,253 weighed Nutrition5k dishes to predict mass from one RGB photograph and compared it with a zero-shot vision-language model, Qwen3-VL-32B, on Nutrition5k and three external datasets: ASA24 studio portions, ECUSTFD whole foods and Food Portion Benchmark meal trays. In domain, the fine-tuned model's mean absolute error was 24.8 g, less than half of Qwen's. On external datasets it did not beat Qwen. For a food it had never seen, it ranked portions by size, but no better than a count of food pixels. Its gram scale was wrong in two ways: it changed with how much of the scene the photograph showed, and it was off by a different factor for each food. Training on other foods or trays from the tested dataset cut the external error by 38 to 65%. DINOv2-large, a larger backbone trained on the same dishes, did not reduce the external error. One labeled photograph of the same food cut the model's ASA24 error to 28.3 g, better than that photograph achieved alone. Prediction intervals that held the true mass for 90% of Nutrition5k dishes held it for at most half of external photographs. Nutrition5k often photographed the same plate several times, so a random test split shared near-identical photographs with training and overstated accuracy. Single-image mass estimators therefore need labeled photographs from the setting where they are used.

</details>


### [38] [Enhancing Cross-Grade Generalization in Banana Variety Classification via Hybrid CNN-Transformers and Knowledge Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29008)
*Khush Kataruka,Mohammad Muzammila,Zahid Akhtar,Raghavendra Ramachandra,Praveen Kumar Chandaliya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双流Swin-ResNet混合框架并结合KL散度自蒸馏，在跨等级香蕉品种分类任务中取得81.25%的平均准确率，优于CNN与ViT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业中的自动水果分类在品质、成熟度、光照、纹理退化、域偏移等条件下仍面临挑战；本文针对"跨等级香蕉品种分类"这一真实场景，即模型需从已见品质等级泛化到未见等级，存在显著域偏移。

Method: 构建含31,678张图像（3个品种×3个品质等级）的大规模数据集，采用严格的Leave-One-Grade-Out评估协议，对CNN、ViT及双流混合架构进行系统对比；提出双流Swin-ResNet混合框架，并以KL散度自蒸馏对齐CNN流与Transformer流的概率特征表示。

Result: 混合框架结合KL散度自蒸馏取得最佳整体性能，平均准确率81.25%，显著超过CNN与Transformer基线；定性分析显示其提升了判别性特征的可迁移性、注意力定位质量并稳定了优化过程。

Conclusion: 双流混合架构配合自蒸馏可有效缓解跨等级域偏移，弥补单一范式框架的不足，为真实农业场景下的鲁棒水果分类提供可行方案。

Abstract: In recent years, automated fruit classification systems have become a more critical component in precision agriculture for efficient yield estimation, quality control, and supply chain optimization. However, they still face significant challenges under varying quality, fruit maturity, illumination, texture degradation, morphological attributes, domain shift, and post-harvest handling conditions. In this study, we address the challenging issue of cross-grade banana variety classification. This is a real-world setting where models that have been trained on specific quality grades must generalize to unseen grades to handle significant domain shift. Under a rigorous Leave-One-Grade-Out evaluation protocol, we present a thorough comparative analysis of CNNs, ViTs, and dual-stream hybrid architectures using a large-scale dataset of 31,678 banana images with three varieties and three quality grades. Specifically, to address the drawbacks of single-paradigm frameworks, we present a dual-stream Swin ResNet hybrid framework enhanced with KL-divergence-based self-distillation to align probabilistic feature representations across the CNN and Transformer streams. Comprehensive experimental analyzes show that the presented hybrid framework with KL-divergence based self-distillation achieves better overall performance, i.e., it attains a mean accuracy of 81.25% and significantly surpasses CNN and Transformer baseline models. Qualitative analysis further confirms that the hybrid framework in this study improves discriminative feature transferability, improves attention localization, and stabilizes optimization.

</details>


### [39] [TEMPL: A Resource-Efficient and Transparent Model-Free Framework for Pseudo-Label Generation in Material CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28974)
*Ashiqur Rahman,Venkata Devesh Reddy Seethi,Austin Yunker,Rajkumar Kettimuthu,Hamed Alhoori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需模型、无需训练权重的多阶段框架，直接从灰度CT图像生成伪标签：结合阈值化、形态学膨胀与自定义广度优先搜索区域生长算法得到多边形标注，并在枝晶与硅砂数据上优于零样本SAM，可作为标注引导与微观结构量化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料CT分析中大规模标注数据稀缺制约深度学习分割；基于DL的伪标签方法计算开销大且可解释性差，因此需要一种无需预训练权重、标注数据与GPU的透明伪标签生成方案。

Method: 多阶段、模型无关框架：阈值化 + 形态学膨胀 + 自定义广度优先搜索（BFS）区域生长算法，直接由灰度CT扫描生成多边形标注。对照实验对象为零样本Segment Anything Model（SAM）、全监督3D U-Net及经典基线；并以YOLO在两类标签上训练作定性比较，另做阈值敏感性分析。

Result: 枝晶数据集上有真值时IoU为63.19%，零样本SAM仅10.74%；IoU=0.5时实例召回率为33.3% vs 8.3%（约4倍）。硅砂样本上YOLO定性表现与SAM标签模型相当；1,053颗颗粒的中位等效直径为163.8 μm，接近筛分范围106–150 μm，而SAM为219.5 μm且存在粘连合并与虚假检测。敏感性分析显示关键阈值存在稳定工作区间，并存在与相位对比度相关的陡峭可预测失效点。耗时：CPU上15 s/图像，SAM在GPU上28 s/图像。

Conclusion: 该透明、无模型的方法在资源受限场景下可作为标注引导（bootstrapping）与微观结构量化的有效工具，but其依赖相位对比度、存在明确失效边界。

Abstract: The scarcity of large annotated datasets limits deep learning (DL) segmentation in material CT analysis. DL-based pseudo-labeling can help, but it is often computationally expensive and hard to interpret. We introduce a multi-stage, model-free framework that generates pseudo-labels directly from grayscale CT scans. It combines thresholding, morphological dilation, and a custom breadth-first search region-growing algorithm to produce polygonal annotations without pre-trained weights, labeled data, or a GPU. We compare it against the zero-shot Segment Anything Model (SAM), a fully supervised 3D U-Net, and classical baselines. On a dendrite dataset with ground truth, our method reaches 63.19% IoU versus 10.74% for zero-shot SAM, with four times the instance recall at 0.5 IoU (33.3% versus 8.3%). The supervised 3D U-Net serves as a reference ceiling that requires expert labels. On a silica sand specimen, YOLO models trained on our labels performed comparably to models trained on SAM labels in a qualitative comparison. Across 1,053 extracted grains, the median equivalent diameter was 163.8 micrometers, near the specimen's known 106 to 150 micrometers sieve range, compared with 219.5 micrometers for SAM, whose labels merge adjacent particles and add spurious detections. A sensitivity analysis shows a stable operating range for the key threshold and a sharp, predictable failure point linked to phase contrast. Our method needs 15s per image on a standard CPU versus 28s for SAM on a GPU. These results support our transparent approach as a tool for annotation bootstrapping and microstructural quantification in resource-limited settings.

</details>


### [40] [RoPT: Robust 6DoF Object Pose Tracking in Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28962)
*Yongqing Liang,Yuzhong Chen,Sihang Li,Junsuk Kim,Chase Kidder,Hui-ping Wang,Yunzhi Xu,Raihan Haque,Yubiao Zhang,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoPT是一个紧耦合闭环的6-DoF物体姿态跟踪框架，统一视觉感知、时序运动学与全局重定位，显著提升快速运动下的跟踪存活率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目6-DoF姿态跟踪在机器人交互中至关重要，但传统单目流水线在物体快速运动时易发生灾难性发散，现有跟踪器多为开环结构，缺乏失效感知与自恢复能力。

Method: 三部分紧耦合：1）视觉感知模块跟踪物体掩码，鲁棒应对部分遮挡与严重遮挡；2）运动学因子图正则化帧间更新，进行前向姿态预测，并驱动并行的多假设验证以跳出局部最优；3）主动重定位模块将流水线转为闭环，实现失效感知与确定性自我恢复。

Result: 在多项挑战性基准及真实机器人演示中验证有效，快速运动场景下跟踪存活率显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉感知、时序运动学与全局重定位统一为紧耦合闭环，RoPT克服了开环单目跟踪在快速运动下的发散问题，具备失效感知与自恢复能力。

Abstract: Accurate 6-DoF object pose tracking is essential for robotic interaction, yet conventional monocular pipelines frequently suffer from catastrophic divergence under rapid object motion. We present RoPT, a robust tracking framework engineered to overcome this limitation by unifying visual perception, temporal kinematics, and global re-localization into a tightly coupled closed loop. Unlike existing open-loop trackers, RoPT delivers three critical capabilities that fundamentally alter system resilience. First, the visual perception component tracks object masks to robustly handle partial and severe occlusions. Second, a kinematic factor graph regularizes frame-to-frame updates, forecasts forward pose predictions, and drives a parallel multi-hypothesis verification scheme to bypass local minima. Finally, an active re-localization module transforms the pipeline into a closed-loop system, making it failure-aware and capable of deterministic self-recovery. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework across challenging benchmarks alongside real-world robotic demonstrations, achieving significantly higher tracking survivability under rapid motion.

</details>


### [41] [DualHeads: Reward-tilted few-step diffusion made simple](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28858)
*Yuanzhi Zhu,Eleftherios Tsonis,Simo Alami Chehboune,Xi Wang,Stéphane Lathuilière,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DualHeads：在冻结扩散骨干上接两个轻量二分类头（奖励头+参考头），仅用外部奖励评估与参考样本即可把少步蒸馏生成器对齐到任务奖励，无额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏扩散模型能在少步内生成高质量图像，但难以与任务要求和人类偏好对齐；现有面向多步扩散的对齐方法依赖可微奖励、score 场或随机轨迹，而这些正是少步蒸馏生成器所缺乏的。因此作者追问：能否只用外部奖励评估与参考样本适配少步生成器。

Method: DualHeads 在冻结扩散骨干上实现两个轻量分类头：奖励头学习同一 prompt 下哪些样本得分更高；参考头区分参考图像与生成图像；生成器最大化二者 logits 的加权和。当头最优时，该目标等价于对学得奖励做 KL 正则化最大化，两个头的权重比即控制正则强度。

Result: 在规则型与模型型奖励下，少步 DualHeads 生成器取得接近 40 步强化学习方法的任务奖励，同时保持图像质量，且推理阶段无额外成本。

Conclusion: 双头形式简单优雅，仅需二元奖励信号与参考样本即可实现少步奖励倾斜生成，有望推动少步奖励对齐生成方向的发展。

Abstract: Distilled diffusion models generate high-quality images in a few sampling steps, but aligning them with task requirements and human preferences is difficult. Methods developed for multi-step diffusion models rely on differentiable rewards, or on score fields and stochastic trajectories that distilled generators may lack. We ask whether a few-step generator can be adapted using only external reward evaluations and reference samples, and show that two binary classifiers suffice. Our method, DualHeads, implements them as lightweight heads on a frozen diffusion backbone. The reward head learns which samples of the same prompt score higher, the reference head separates reference images from generated ones, and the generator maximizes a weighted sum of their logits. When the heads are optimal, this objective is KL-regularized maximization of the learned reward, and the ratio of head weights sets the regularization strength. Empirically, with both rule-based and model-based rewards, a few-step DualHeads generator reaches task rewards close to those of 40-step reinforcement learning methods while preserving image quality, at no extra inference cost. We hope that this simple and elegant dual-head formulation will help advance few-step reward-tilted generation.

</details>


### [42] [Conditional Imaging for Privacy-Preserving Visual Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28835)
*Hengyu Lian,Debabrata Mandal,Anzhou Wen,Yujie Wang,Xi Peng,Praneeth Chakravarthula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“条件成像”(conditional imaging)：让每帧采集时相机光学状态变化，使测量本身隐藏场景，而掌握对应物理状态者才能重建，从信息论上突破静态光学系统中隐私与可恢复性不可兼得的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉感知面临两难：今天应保密的信息明天可能需要恢复。相机日益普及使该问题愈发重要。现有防护要么仅在成像后保护内容，要么在采集时抑制信息以致无法事后恢复，二者都无法兼顾隐私与可恢复性。

Method: 先通过信息论分析证明静态光学系统中隐私与可恢复性不能同时实现（为重建保留在测量中的信息同样可被观测者利用）。据此提出条件成像：每采集一帧即改变光学状态，测量本身隐去场景，唯有已知对应物理系统状态才能重建；分析光学参数如何决定可恢复信息量，并据此设计相机原型。在人脸数据集上从直接可视化、计算反演到学习式重建与身份识别的递增强度攻击进行评估。

Result: 实验表明条件成像在授权重建中保持身份信息，而对无系统状态访问权的观察者隐匿视觉信息；即使采用学习式重建与身份识别等强恢复手段亦如此。

Conclusion: 将相机物理状态纳入重建所需信息，使条件成像能在采集端保护视觉信息，同时保留未来授权使用的可恢复性。

Abstract: Conventional visual sensing faces a fundamental dilemma: information that should remain private today may need to be recovered tomorrow. This challenge is becoming increasingly important as cameras become pervasive in our daily surroundings. Existing safeguards either protect visual content only after an image is formed or suppress information during capture in ways that prevent its later recovery. Here, we show through an information-theoretic analysis that privacy and recoverability cannot be achieved simultaneously in static optical systems. Information preserved in a measurement for later reconstruction also remains available to an observer of that measurement, limiting the privacy such systems can provide. To overcome this limitation, we introduce conditional imaging, an imaging approach that changes the optical state for every captured frame. The measurement alone conceals the scene, while knowledge of the corresponding physical system state enables its reconstruction. We analyze how the optical parameters determine the information that can be recovered and use this analysis to guide the design of a camera prototype. We evaluate the system on a captured dataset of human faces using increasingly powerful attempts to recover the scene, from direct visualization and computational inversion to learned reconstruction and identity recognition. Our experiments show that conditional imaging preserves identity in authorized reconstructions while withholding visual information from observers without access to the system state. By making the physical state of the camera part of the information needed for reconstruction, conditional imaging enables visual information to be protected at the point of capture while remaining recoverable for future authorized use.

</details>


### [43] [SAIL: A Learnable Generalisation of Spatial Autocorrelation over Graph-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28806)
*Piotr Keller,Fayyaz Minhas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出SAIL，一个可微分框架，将空间自相关从固定的单变量统计量推广为可学习算子，通过联合学习特征投影与节点间邻域亲和度生成可解释的局部/全局空间一致性描述符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典空间自相关统计（Moran's I、Geary's C）局限于单一标量变量与固定邻域权重，难以适用于高维、图状的空间数据。

Method: SAIL联合学习定义候选标量场的特征投影，以及节点间邻域亲和度，从而将空间自相关构造为可学习、可微分的算子，输出局部与全局空间一致性描述符，支持有监督预测与无监督的模式/热点发现。

Result: 在适当参数化下SAIL可精确还原经典空间统计量；对由潜在空间自相关驱动的任务，其归纳偏置与任务匹配，可逼近贝叶斯最优预测器；在合成数据与大规模生物医学图上验证了统计正确性与空间一致性发现能力。

Conclusion: SAIL将空间自相关统一为可学习算子，兼具可解释性与理论保证，可应用于视觉、地理空间分析与生物医学等领域。

Abstract: In vision and scientific domains involving spatial data, a key question is whether node feature values vary independently across space or form coherent spatial structures. Classical spatial autocorrelation statistics such as Moran's $I$ and Geary's $C$ quantify this phenomenon but operate on a single scalar variable with fixed neighbourhood weights, limiting their use for high-dimensional graph-like data. We introduce SAIL (Spatial Autocorrelation Index Learner), a differentiable framework that generalises spatial autocorrelation into a learnable operator by jointly learning feature projections defining candidate scalar fields and inter-node neighbourhood affinities. SAIL produces interpretable local and global spatial coherence descriptors, enabling supervised prediction and unsupervised discovery of spatial autocorrelation modes and hotspots. We show that SAIL exactly recovers classical spatial statistics under appropriate parametrisations and, for tasks driven by latent spatial autocorrelation, can approximate the Bayes-optimal predictor with an inductive bias matched to such tasks. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale biomedical graphs demonstrate statistical correctness and discovery of spatial coherence with applications across vision, geospatial analysis, and biomedicine. The code repository is available at: https://github.com/pkeller00/SAIL.

</details>


### [44] [Encoded but Unused: Bounding the Sources of VLM Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29241)
*Anat Veikherman,Mor Ventura,Nitay Calderon,Lihi Zelnik-Manor,Roi Reichart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将VLM错误分解为"视觉表征失败"与"视觉利用失败"，提出基于轻量探针的VELens方法估计视觉利用失败率并给出理论界，发现视觉利用是VLM的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作观察到VLM频繁出错，但不清楚错误究竟源于视觉信息未被编码，还是已编码却未被使用；厘清这一来源对确定改进方向至关重要。

Method: 提出VELens（Visual Evidence Lens），一种基于排序（rank-based）的探针，跨VLM各层追踪目标物体及其真实属性（颜色、材质、形状）的视觉证据；用轻量探针估计视觉利用失败率，并推导考虑探针不完美性的真值率理论界。在6000余张图像上用LLaVA-1.6-7B与Qwen2.5-VL-7B评估。

Result: VELens分别取得ROC-AUC 0.95（LLaVA）与0.91（Qwen），在低资源与未见属性设置下均优于对比探针。错误诊断显示：LLaVA错误中84.4%源于视觉利用失败（保守下界77.5%），Qwen为70.0%（下界61.0%）。

Conclusion: 视觉利用而非视觉表征是VLM的主导瓶颈；改进机会在于构建更好的机制来定位、路由并使用模型已编码的视觉信息。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate why vision-language models (VLMs) produce errors by decomposing them into two sources: visual-representation failures, in which the visual evidence required for the correct answer is not encoded, and visual-utilization failures, in which this evidence is encoded but not successfully used. We propose to estimate the visual-utilization failure rate using lightweight probes and derive theoretical bounds on the true rate that account for probe imperfections. We introduce VELens (Visual Evidence Lens), a rank-based probe that traces visual evidence for both a target object and its ground-truth attribute, such as color, material, or shape, across VLM layers. We evaluate VELens on over 6,000 images using LLaVA-1.6-7B and Qwen2.5-VL-7B. Our method achieves ROC-AUC scores of 0.95 and 0.91, respectively, and outperforms alternative probes in both low-resource and unseen-attribute settings. Using it to diagnose errors, we estimate that visual-utilization failures account for 84.4% of LLaVA errors and 70.0% of Qwen errors, with conservative lower bounds of 77.5% and 61.0%, respectively. Overall, our study identifies visual utilization as the dominant bottleneck in VLMs and suggests that a central opportunity for improvement lies in developing better mechanisms to locate, route, and use the visual information they already encode.

</details>


### [45] [SICAF: Time-Varying Focus Bottleneck for Self-Supervised Event-Based Optical Flow with Spiking Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29158)
*Shuangming Yang,Qing He,Shangqi Guo,Badong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出SICAF框架，将自监督脉冲神经网络的光流学习建模为带时间调制的约束优化，通过"紧凑压缩、时变聚焦"策略提取紧凑潜在表征并避免过度压缩，配合可学习时间注意力估计器实现跨时间上下文感知，在自监督SNN中取得SOTA，MVSEC上AEE提升8.14%，白盒对抗攻击鲁棒性提升13.49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机光流估计具有高时间分辨率与大动态范围，自监督可缓解真值标签缺失问题，SNN的异步脉冲特性与事件数据天然契合。但事件流的时间不规则性阻碍紧凑的时空特征提取；同时缺乏显式标签易导致伪相关，使潜在表征冗余度上升。因此需要兼顾表征紧凑性与时间显著性的学习策略。

Method: 提出"Compactly Compress, Time-varying Focus"训练策略与SICAF（Spikes Information Compression And Focus）框架：把学习过程表述为带时间调制的约束优化，促使自监督SNN提取紧凑潜在表征，同时抑制会丢弃时间显著性的过度压缩；并设计带可学习时间注意力的估计器，实现跨时间依赖的上下文感知，增强不同时间步的特征选择性。

Result: 在多个数据集上实验表明，SICAF在自监督SNN中达到SOTA：MVSEC数据集AEE提升8.14%，白盒对抗攻击下的鲁棒性提升13.49%。

Conclusion: 通过约束优化式的时间调制训练与可学习时间注意力，SICAF在自监督脉冲光流估计中同时改善了精度与鲁棒性，验证了紧凑表征学习与时间聚焦机制结合的有效性。

Abstract: Event-based optical flow estimation achieves high temporal resolution and dynamic range by exploiting asynchronous events, while self-supervised learning provides an effective solution to the absence of ground-truth labels. Spiking neural networks (SNNs), as brain-inspired models, process information through asynchronous spikes, naturally aligning with optical flow events. However, the temporal irregularity across event streams hinders the compact extraction of spatiotemporal features, while the absence of explicit labels leads to spurious correlations, thereby increasing redundancy within latent representations. In this paper, we propose a novel 'Compactly Compress, Time-varying Focus' training strategy. We present SICAF (Spikes Information Compression And Focus) framework that formulates the learning process as a constrained optimization with temporal modulation. It encourages self-supervised SNNs to extract compact latent representations while preventing over-compression that would discard temporal saliency. To better enable feature selectivity across time steps, we design an estimator with learnable temporal attention, achieving context awareness with cross-time dependencies. Experimental results on several datasets demonstrate that SICAF achieves state-of-the-art performance in self-supervised SNNs, with a 8.14% improvement in AEE on the MVSEC dataset and a 13.49% increase in robustness under white-box adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [46] [DGCM-SSFA: Dual-Granularity Cross-Modulation and Sparse Strip Frequency Attention for Efficient 3T-to-7T MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29141)
*Zhichun Fan,Zhiqiang Chen,Lanling Liu,Lingjun Liu,Zhonghua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅11M参数的紧凑多域注意力框架DGCM-SSFA，在3T到7T MRI合成任务上接近最强基准的结构保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3T→7T MRI合成方法依赖超45M参数的大模型或高计算量集成策略，难以在资源受限临床环境部署。

Method: 在U-Net Transformer骨干中嵌入两级模块：多层级DGCM（Sobel引导空间注意力+深度可分离频率感知通道注意力+跨分支门控）增强局部边缘纹理；瓶颈处SSFA（条带池化近线性复杂度全局注意力+FFT频域调制）捕获全局上下文；与骨干原生通道注意力构成三重粒度特征精炼。

Result: UNC与BNU数据集T1w/T2w实验显示强结构保真度：BNU T2w SSIM 0.8431（最佳基准0.8624），BNU T1w SSIM 0.8100；参数量显著低于ResViT(123.4M)与FS-RWKV(45.4M)。

Conclusion: 紧凑多域注意力设计可在大幅降低参数量的同时实现有竞争力的7T MRI合成质量，适合资源受限临床场景。

Abstract: Synthesizing high-fidelity 7T MRI from routinely acquired 3T scans offers a practical pathway to enhanced neuroimaging without the prohibitive cost of ultra-high-field scanners. While existing deep learning approaches have achieved promising results, state-of-the-art methods typically rely on either large-scale architectures exceeding 45 million parameters or computationally intensive ensemble strategies, limiting their practicality in resource-constrained clinical environments. In this work, we propose a compact multi-domain attention framework, termed DGCM-SSFA, that achieves competitive synthesis quality with only 11M parameters. The proposed architecture integrates two complementary modules into a U-Net Transformer backbone. First, a Dual-Granularity Cross-Modulated Feature Modulator (DGCM) is embedded at multiple hierarchical levels to enhance local edge and texture details through Sobel-guided spatial attention and depthwise frequency-aware channel attention with cross-branch gating. Second, a Sparse Strip Frequency Attention (SSFA) module at the bottleneck captures global spatial context via strip-pooled attention with near-linear complexity, while simultaneously performing FFT-based spectral modulation in the frequency domain. These two modules operate synergistically with the backbone's native channel attention, forming a triple-granularity feature refinement pipeline. Extensive experiments on the UNC and BNU 3T-to-7T MRI datasets across T1w and T2w modalities demonstrate that DGCM-SSFA achieves strong structural fidelity, attaining an SSIM of 0.8431 on BNU T2w---approaching the best benchmark value of 0.8624---and 0.8100 on BNU T1w, while using substantially fewer parameters than Transformer-based alternatives such as ResViT (123.4M) and FS-RWKV (45.4M).

</details>


### [47] [Diffusion Posterior Sampling with Uncertain Forward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29102)
*Wentao Shangguan,Vivek K Goyal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知似然（UAL），将前向模型参数不确定性边缘化为结构化测量协方差，嵌入现有扩散后验采样器，在六类逆问题上提升重建与集成预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散后验采样器通常将模糊核、探测器响应等前向模型参数固定为估计值，参数不确定性未进入似然，其误差被吸收进重建结果，导致重建偏差与过度自信。

Method: 构造未确定参数边缘似然的高斯近似（UAL）：对每个候选信号，将参数不确定性传播为结构化测量协方差，从而在参数变化可解释的残差方向上降低二次惩罚；可直接替换现有采样器的数据更新项，保留预训练扩散先验与采样调度。并在固定高斯冗余参数模型下给出线性化似然误差与精确边缘后验/UAL后验平均散度的界。

Result: 在包含高斯去模糊、运动去模糊、CT、MRI、超声与荧光显微的六个逆问题上均优于无UAL基线：主高斯去模糊场景几何平均MSE降低9%–20%；非盲采样器使用总体均值核时运动去模糊降低51%–92%；运动去模糊中DAPS与PnP-DM+UAL的集成均值PSNR比BlindDPS和Fast Diffusion EM高6.2–6.4 dB；消融中优于迹匹配的各向同性膨胀，并分别以3.3 dB（PnP-DM）和4.2 dB（DAPS）超过最佳固定标量数据加权。

Conclusion: 参数不确定性应显式进入似然而非固定点估计；UAL以低成本、模型无关方式改进多种扩散后验采样器的单点重建与不确定性量化。

Abstract: Diffusion posterior samplers reconstruct signals by combining a pretrained diffusion prior with a measurement likelihood specified by a forward model and a noise model. Calibration and reference data can characterize uncertainty in the forward model's parameters, such as blur kernels or detector responses. Fixing these parameters at estimated values leaves their uncertainty out of the likelihood, and parameter errors are thus absorbed into the reconstruction. We introduce an uncertainty-aware likelihood (UAL), a Gaussian approximation to the marginal likelihood obtained by integrating over the uncertain parameters. For each candidate signal, UAL propagates parameter uncertainty into a structured measurement covariance, reducing the quadratic penalty along residual directions that parameter variation can explain. Existing samplers can use UAL in their data updates while retaining their pretrained diffusion priors and sampling schedules. Under a fixed Gaussian nuisance model, we bound likelihood error from linearization and average divergence between the exact marginal and UAL posteriors. We compare samplers with and without UAL across six inverse problems spanning Gaussian and motion deblurring, CT, MRI, ultrasound, and fluorescence microscopy, and find improvements in reconstruction and ensemble prediction. UAL reduces geometric mean MSE by 9% to 20% across samplers in the main Gaussian-deblurring setting and by 51% to 92% in motion deblurring when non-blind samplers use the population-mean kernel. In motion deblurring, DAPS and PnP-DM with UAL also give 6.2 to 6.4 dB higher ensemble-mean PSNR than published BlindDPS and Fast Diffusion EM. On the ablation set, UAL outperforms trace-matched isotropic inflation for PnP-DM in both deblurring tasks; in motion deblurring, it also exceeds the best-tested fixed scalar data weighting by 3.3 dB for PnP-DM and 4.2 dB for DAPS in average PSNR.

</details>


### [48] [Look Beyond the Question: Preserve-Focus-Fuse for Whole-Slide Image Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29431)
*Xiangyu Li,Jincheng Li,Tianle Du,Yanheng Li,Haoxuan Che,Yuxin Sun,Long Bai,Liangliang Liu,Ran Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEYONDPATH：面向全切片图像问答的“保留-聚焦-融合”框架，兼顾问题无关的形态保真与问题定向证据检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像问答中，单个问题很少点明所有必要形态学线索。仅按问题引导选取图像块会遗漏支撑性形态，而整片压缩又会稀释局灶证据，二者难以兼顾。

Method: 提出Preserve-Focus-Fuse三阶段框架：Preserve通过针对每张训练切片多问题的答案监督，学习问题无关的Morphology Bank；Focus在前景块（含Bank之外区域）中检索问题特定证据；Fuse通过Morphology-on-Visual-Evidence Fusion将保留的形态信息整合到检索证据位置，生成固定96 token的视觉前缀。

Result: WSI-Bench上Avg-9达0.769，同一检查点在全部八项报告生成指标上领先；SlideBench-BCNB零样本总体准确率0.639。消融实验验证形态保留与问题定向检索的互补增益，并支持跨问题监督及在证据位置进行融合。

Conclusion: 应超越问题显式线索：在保留全局切片形态的同时，保持针对每个问题主动检索证据的能力。

Abstract: A question about a whole-slide image rarely names every morphological finding needed to answer it. Question-guided patch selection can therefore overlook supporting morphology, while slide-level compression can dilute focal evidence. Inspired by how pathologists combine broad slide review, question-directed examination, and integration of their findings, we introduce BEYONDPATH, a Preserve-Focus-Fuse framework for whole-slide image question answering. Preserve learns a question-agnostic Morphology Bank through answer supervision across multiple questions about each training slide. Focus retrieves questionspecific evidence from all foreground patches, including regions outside the Bank. Fuse integrates the preserved morphology at the retrieved evidence positions through Morphology-on-Visual-Evidence Fusion, producing a fixed 96-token visual prefix. On WSI-Bench, BEYONDPATH achieves an Avg-9 of 0.769 and leads all eight reported report-generation metrics using the same checkpoint. It also achieves 0.639 zero-shot overall accuracy on SlideBench-BCNB. Controlled ablations show complementary benefits from morphology preservation and question-directed retrieval, and support cross-question supervision and fusion at the evidence positions. These results support looking beyond the question's explicit cues: preserving broader slide morphology while retaining the ability to seek evidence for each question.

</details>


### [49] [LLMSeg: Training-Free Text-Prompted Image Segmentation with B-Splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29409)
*Abdullah Hamdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的LLMSeg流程，将通用多模态大模型直接转化为文本提示图像分割器，无需分割头、掩码解码器或微调，在多个分割基准上与专用模型SAM 3相当甚至更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可提示分割模型（如SAM 3）虽具备优秀的开放词表能力，但依赖专用架构、大规模标注引擎和任务特定训练。作者提出反向问题：通用多模态大模型能否不做任何附加分割模块、不训练，直接完成图像分割。

Method: LLMSeg为三阶段LLM驱动流水线：(1) detect：定位匹配自由文本查询的所有实例并描述其粗略形态；(2) segment：在检测框内用闭合周期B样条描绘实例，样条定义外边界及可选孔洞环；(3) refine：针对残余误差迭代提出聚焦框并重描受限样条，直至掩码稳定或迭代预算耗尽。输出为二值掩码，中间几何可检视。

Result: 在十个指代与推理分割划分（ReasonSeg、RefCOCO/+/g）上评估，并覆盖开放词表实例分割（LVIS、COCO、SA-Co）、语义分割与零样本检测（ODinW13、RF-100VL）。以现成Gemini-3.7-Flash为后端，LLMSeg在同一条目上与SAM 3相当且常优于该专用模型；ReasonSeg val上达71.5 cIoU，且无需任何分割专项训练。

Conclusion: 通用MLLM经合理流程编排即可胜任分割任务，摆脱分割专用架构与训练；后端模型升级可直接提升精度而无需重训流水线，体现了流水线解耦与可迁移性。

Abstract: Promptable segmentation models such as SAM 3 achieve remarkable open-vocabulary performance but require dedicated architectures, large-scale annotation engines, and task-specific training. We ask a different question: can a general-purpose multimodal large language model (MLLM) segment images directly, without an added segmentation head, mask decoder, or training? We present LLMSeg, a training-free pipeline that turns a vision-capable LLM into a text-prompted image segmenter. LLMSeg decomposes segmentation into three LLM-driven stages. First, detect localizes all instances matching a free-form text query and describes their coarse morphology. Next, segment traces each instance using closed periodic B-splines confined to its detected box. These splines define an outer boundary and optional hole loops. Finally, refine iteratively proposes focus boxes around residual errors and retraces confined splines. The process ends when the mask stabilizes or the pass budget is exhausted. The output is a binary mask with inspectable intermediate geometry. We evaluate LLMSeg across ten referring and reasoning segmentation splits (ReasonSeg and RefCOCO/+/g). We also evaluate open-vocabulary instance segmentation (LVIS, COCO, and SA-Co), semantic segmentation, and zero-shot detection (ODinW13 and RF-100VL). With an off-the-shelf Gemini-3.7-Flash backend, LLMSeg is competitive with and often surpasses the specialist SAM 3 model on identical items. It reaches 71.5 cIoU on ReasonSeg val without additional segmentation-specific training. Backend model updates can improve accuracy without retraining the pipeline. The code is available at https://abdullahamdi.com/llmseg .

</details>


### [50] [Seeing through Denoising: Frequency-guided One-step Denoising for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29361)
*Xuelin Qian,Zixuan Wang,Chang Xu,Dingwen Zhang,Junwei Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiffIRSTD，将红外小目标检测重构为背景重建问题，用扩散模型一步去噪恢复无目标背景，以残差提示候选目标区域，并以频域感知损失保障细节，作为即插即用框架在三个数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法多采用判别式范式，直接将图像映射为目标掩码，忽视了生成式建模的潜力；而IRSTD任务本身面临目标极小、信噪比低、背景杂波强的困难。

Method: 将小目标重新解释为类噪声信号，用扩散模型从受扰图像中重建干净、无目标的背景；以重建背景与原图之差作为残差提示候选目标区域；引入一步去噪策略降低扩散采样开销；设计频域引导感知损失，保留背景高频细节并强化目标区域重建质量；整体为即插即用框架，不增加检测器计算复杂度。

Result: 在IRSTD-1K、NUAA-SIRST、NUDT-SIRST三个数据集上实验，均达到SOTA性能，验证了方法优越性。

Conclusion: 以生成式背景重建替代直接掩码判别是IRSTD的有效新范式，配合一步去噪与频域损失可在保证推理效率的同时提升检测性能，且具备良好兼容性。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection (IRSTD) is a challenging task due to the extremely small size of targets, low signal-to-noise ratios, and heavy background clutter in infrared imagery. While the progress of deep learning has advanced this field, existing methods primarily adopt discriminative paradigms that directly map images to target masks, overlooking the potential of generative modeling. In this paper, we propose DiffIRSTD, a novel generative framework that reformulates infrared small target detection as a background reconstruction problem. Instead of explicitly predicting target regions, we reinterpret small targets as noise-like signals and leverage a diffusion-based model to recover a clean, target-free background from a perturbed input image. The residual between the reconstructed background and the original image is then exploited to highlight candidate target regions, providing explicit cues for subsequent detection. To address the computational cost typically associated with diffusion sampling, we introduce a one-step denoising strategy that enables efficient background reconstruction while maintaining practical inference speed. Furthermore, we present a frequency-guided perception loss to preserve high-frequency background details and focus more on the reconstruction quality of target regions. More importantly, our DiffIRSTD operates as a plug-and-play framework, compatible with existing IRSTD models without increasing the computational complexity of detectors. Extensive experiments on IRSTD-1K, NUAA-SIRST, and NUDT-SIRST datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method, achieving state-of-the-art performance across three benchmarks.

</details>


### [51] [ReSeg: Retraining-free segmentation of abdominal organs via generalizable semantic representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29090)
*Derong Yu,Guoyan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReSeg——一种两阶段、免重训练（retraining-free）的域无关零样本腹部器官分割框架，完全基于域随机化合成图像训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习分割方法依赖大规模真实图像，存在泛化性与隐私风险；已有基于域随机化合成的范式在腹部器官上分割性能有限，或需要针对数据集进行微调。

Method: 两阶段：第一阶段用专用数据引擎生成语义与视觉多样的合成图像，预训练器官无关的通用语义表征网络（GSRNet），学习对解剖结构、成像对比度、分辨率与空间上下文变化鲁棒的语义特征；第二阶段用另一数据引擎生成保留腹部器官固有空间布局的多样化视觉图像，以 GSRNet 权重初始化编码器，训练器官特定编解码分割网络（OESNet）。

Result: 在覆盖 CT、MRI、PET 三种模态的七个腹部器官分割数据集上评估，ReSeg 持续优于现有 SOTA 方法，且无需任何真实训练图像。

Conclusion: 域随机化合成数据驱动的两阶段预训练范式可实现免微调、域无关的零样本腹部器官分割，为规避真实数据依赖与隐私问题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Abdominal organ segmentation is essential in modern medical applications. While existing deep learning approaches demonstrate promising performance, their heavy reliance on large-scale image data raises concerns over generalization capability and privacy risks. This motivates the development of generalizable segmentation training paradigms based on domain-randomized image synthesis, facilitating generalization across unseen real image domains. However, existing paradigms either suffer from limited segmentation performance when applied to abdominal organs, or require dataset-specific finetuning. To address these limitations, we propose ReSeg, a novel two-stage retraining-free framework for domain-agnostic zero-shot abdominal organ segmentation. At the first stage, our organ-agnostic synthetic data-driven pretraining approach exploits semantically and visually diverse synthetic images generated by a dedicated data engine to optimize a Generalizable Semantic Representation Network (GSRNet). The network is thereby capable of learning semantic features robust against variations in anatomical structure, imaging contrast, resolution and spatial context, which provide universal representation capabilities that serve as a foundation for segmentation learning. Subsequently, in the second stage, we continue to train an Organ-specific Encoder-decoder Segmentation Network (OESNet), whose encoder is initialized with the pretrained weights. This training process is facilitated by another dedicated data engine, which generates diversified visual images while strictly retaining the inherent spatial layout of abdominal organs. Comprehensive evaluation on seven abdominal organ segmentation datasets across three different modalities including CT, MRI, and PET demonstrates that ReSeg consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods while eliminating the need for real training images.

</details>


### [52] [HumanCentric-Scenes-AI: A Human-Curated Multimodal Dataset of AI-Generated Human-Centric Scenes Across Surveillance, Occupational Portraits, AI-Edited Images, and Short-Form Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29045)
*Nima Kamali,Sana Nasiri,Negar Kamali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布 HumanCentric-Scenes-AI：一个由人工多轮筛选、包含 296 个 AI 生成刺激物的数据集，覆盖监控、职业肖像、AI 编辑真实照片与短视频四个场景，并附带生成提示与逐条元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 生成媒体研究缺乏面向"人类遭遇 AI 生成内容"真实场景、经人工筛选且具备可复现元数据的高逼真刺激物集合，难以支撑人类感知研究与检测器校准、生成模型偏差分析。

Method: 构建四子集：(1) Surveillance 85 张 AI 生成 CCTV 风格图像；(2) Occupations 128 张职业肖像，64 职业×性别配对平衡设计；(3) AI-edited 27 张真实照片，其中某一区域被生成模型替换/修改；(4) Video 56 段 AI 生成短视频。所有素材经多轮人工筛选以最大化照片真实感，并发布文件、生成提示（如记录）与逐刺激元数据。分析包括：134 次未指定性别生成的性别分布与 BLS 职业性别占比比较、编辑区域大小与零样本检测难度的关联、Surveillance 图像质量分布、Video 主题类别覆盖。

Result: 给出未指定性别生成中的呈现性别分布及其与 U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics 职业性别份额的对照结果；报告 AI 编辑子集中编辑区域尺寸与零样本检测难度的关联；提供 Surveillance 子集图像质量分布与 Video 子集主体类别覆盖情况。

Conclusion: HumanCentric-Scenes-AI 主要面向人类感知研究，同时可用于 AI 检测分类器的校准与生成模型的偏差分析，具备人工筛选的高真实感与可复现元数据优势。

Abstract: We introduce HumanCentric-Scenes-AI, a human-curated dataset of 296 AI-generated stimuli that depict people across four contexts in which people encounter AI-generated media. The Surveillance subset contains 85 AI-generated CCTV-style images. The Occupations subset contains 128 AI-generated occupational portraits in a balanced paired design covering 64 occupations with one female and one male version of each. The AI-edited subset contains 27 real photographs in which a region was altered or replaced by a generative model. The Video subset contains 56 AI-generated short videos. We curated the images and videos manually over multiple rounds to make them as photorealistic as possible. For each stimulus we release the file, the generation prompt where recorded, and per-stimulus metadata. We report depicted-gender distributions from an initial set of 134 generations without a specified gender and compare them with U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics occupational gender shares. We report edit-region size and its association with zero-shot detection difficulty for the AI-edited subset. We report image-quality distributions for the Surveillance subset and subject-category coverage for the Video subset. HumanCentric-Scenes-AI is intended first for human-perception research, and also for calibration of AI-detection classifiers and bias analysis of generative models.

</details>


### [53] [Policy-Mask: Budget-Aware Adaptation of Diffusion Features to Discriminative Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28889)
*Ivan Listopadov,Maksim Nakhodnov,Aibek Alanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Policy-Mask：一种预算条件策略，在冻结扩散模型上联合选择时间步、网络块与通道子集，用于下游判别任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结扩散模型中有用信号分布在去噪轨迹、网络深度与通道上，现有方法多依赖手工固定或仅优化时间步等少数超参，并跨输入复用同一规则，难以适应不同输入与预算。

Method: 提出 Policy-Mask，以下游目标训练预算条件策略，在给定通道预算下联合选择时间步、块与通道；按子集基数归一化通道分数贡献，使三个策略头量级可比、稳定联合训练；并扩展到扩散Transformer（PixArt-Σ、SD3.5 MMDiT），以图像token隐藏维度替代U-Net通道。

Result: 同预算下在分割、分类、语义对应上优于固定提取与特征筛选基线：ADE20K 38.9 mIoU，CIFAR-100在128维下75.4% top-1，SPair-71k 76.3 PCK@0.1；消融显示块选择显著影响精度，最高通道预算得分出现在保留256通道处。

Conclusion: 联合、输入与预算自适应的特征提取选择优于固定规则；块选择是关键因素，256通道为紧凑扩散表征的实用工作点，方法可迁移至DiT架构。

Abstract: Selecting intermediate features from a frozen diffusion model for discriminative tasks is non-trivial because the useful signal is distributed across the denoising trajectory, network depth, and channels. In practice, this requires choosing a timestep, a block, and a channel subset. Existing methods either fix these choices by hand or optimize only a few extraction hyperparameters, most often the timestep, then reuse the same rule across inputs and budgets. We introduce Policy-Mask, a budget-conditioned policy trained on the downstream objective that jointly selects all three under a prescribed channel budget. Without normalization, the channel-score contribution grows with the number of selected channels and dominates the timestep and block updates. We normalize it by subset cardinality, keeping the three policy heads on comparable scales and stabilizing joint training. At matched budgets, Policy-Mask improves over fixed extraction and feature-filter baselines across segmentation, classification, and semantic correspondence, reaching 38.9 mIoU on ADE20K, 75.4% top-1 on CIFAR-100 at 128 dimensions, and 76.3 PCK@0.1 on SPair-71k. Component-wise ablations show that block selection materially affects downstream accuracy, and the highest recorded channel-budget score occurs at 256 retained channels, identifying a practical operating point for compact diffusion representations. We further adapt Policy-Mask to diffusion transformers, including PixArt-$Σ$ and SD3.5 MMDiT, by replacing U-Net channels with image-token hidden dimensions.

</details>


### [54] [Depth-guided Geometric Adaptation for Few-shot Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28856)
*Zhiqi Miao,Estefanía Talavera,Weichuan Zhang,Jiapan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DAFIC框架，将单目深度引入小样本图像分类的表示学习、episodic适配与稠密支持-查询匹配，以缓解视觉歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本图像分类（FSIC）在数据稀缺条件下易受视觉歧义影响：背景杂乱与RGB外观相似性削弱判别性特征匹配，需要引入几何线索增强区分度。

Method: DAFIC为几何引导框架：(1) 通过RGB-D自监督预训练学习几何感知的token表示；(2) 深度引导几何适配（DGA）模块利用深度几何以几何增强的类上下文细化patch表示并抑制背景干扰；(3) 空间双向patch匹配（SBiM）在细化特征上进行双向稠密匹配，降低对虚假对应与背景杂乱的敏感性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上相较现有方法取得较强性能；消融实验与鲁棒性分析支持“几何条件化episodic适配 + 双向匹配”耦合的有效性。

Conclusion: 将单目深度几何贯穿表示学习与匹配流程，可有效缓解小样本视觉歧义，为小样本视觉识别提供几何条件化的适配与匹配范式。

Abstract: Few-shot image classification (FSIC) frequently suffers from visual ambiguity, where background clutter and RGB appearance similarities hinder discriminative feature matching in data-scarce settings. To mitigate this, we propose DAFIC, a geometry-guided framework that incorporates monocular depth into representation learning, episodic adaptation, and dense support-query matching. DAFIC first learns geometry-aware token representations through RGB-D self-supervised pre-training. Subsequently, the Depth-guided Geometric Adaptation (DGA) module uses depth-derived geometry to refine patch representations with geometry-enriched class context while suppressing background interference. Spatial Bidirectional Patch Matching (SBiM) then performs bidirectional dense matching on the refined features, reducing sensitivity to spurious correspondences and background clutter. Extensive experiments on four benchmarks show that DAFIC achieves strong performance against existing methods, with ablation and robustness analyses supporting the effectiveness of coupling geometry-conditioned episodic adaptation with bidirectional matching for few-shot visual recognition.

</details>


### [55] [Early versus Late RGB-Infrared Fusion for Counter-UAV Detection: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29828)
*Alberto De Zan,Denis Tavaris,Gian Luca Foresti,Ivan Scagnetto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了RGB-IR无人机检测的早期（图像级）与晚期（决策级）融合策略，在多种亮度、背景、距离、高度与变焦条件下测试，早期融合原始性能更优，晚期融合在传感器或帧错位时更具实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升恶劣环境与天气条件下无人机检测的精度与可靠性，探索可见光与红外多光谱融合的有效方式。

Method: 实现并对比两种相反策略：图像级（早期）融合将两光谱合并为单图后由单一检测器处理；决策级（晚期）融合将两条检测流程的输出边界框融合为可同时适用于两光谱的公共结果。在专门环境中测试，涵盖高低亮度交替场景、天空与地面背景、5/25/60 m距离、不同高度及1X/2X/4X变焦，并在对齐图像集上进行评估比较。

Result: 早期融合因从训练开始即引入热红外信息，取得更优的原始检测性能；晚期融合则提供了适用于传感器或帧错位情形的创新且有用的检测工作流。论文同时讨论了两种方法的优缺点。

Conclusion: 两种融合策略各有取舍，选择取决于运行优先级；论文给出实施考量与实用建议，为RGB-IR融合模态研究与无人机检测能力提升提供参考。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel strategy for UAV detection through visible (RGB) and infrared (IR) fusion, in order to improve accuracy and precision in harsh environments and weather conditions. In this research, two opposite approaches have been studied and implemented: the first concerns image-level (early) fusion, which merges two different spectra into a single image to which one detector is applied. On the other hand, the second approach, called decision-level (late) fusion, focuses on blending together the output bounding boxes of two different detection pipelines, aiming to create a common result that can be applied to both spectra simultaneously. Both strategies have been extensively tested in a dedicated environment, which included alternating low and high brightness scenes, different backgrounds (sky and ground), distances (5, 25 and 60 m), altitudes and zoom levels (1X, 2X and 4X). These tests allowed for the assessment of actual detection capabilities and system reliability in real-world applications. Furthermore, the two fusion strategies were evaluated and compared over a set of aligned images in order to highlight the better approach of the two. The results indicate that early fusion achieves better raw performance, since it incorporates the thermal information from the beginning of the training process. However, late fusion presents an innovative and useful detection workflow that can be applied in cases of sensor or frame misalignment. Both positive and negative aspects have also been pointed out and discussed, in order to give a more comprehensive overview. Finally, the paper outlines implementation considerations and practical recommendations based on operational priorities. Overall, the findings contribute to the research in RGB-IR fusion modalities, with the goal of enhancing UAV detection capabilities.

</details>


### [56] [JointLight: Human-Centric Video Relighting with Asynchronous RGB-Irradiance Joint Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29780)
*Henglei Lv,Bailin Deng,Xiaoqiang Liu,Yichen Wang,Haoxian Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Lin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出异步联合建模范式，让模型在分离时间步下联合去噪RGB与真值辐照度帧，以实现细粒度可控的人像重打光，并发布基于游戏引擎构建的多光照高质量合成视频数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人像场景光传输建模困难；现有扩散重打光方法隐式建模光传输导致可控性差，而以路径追踪辐照度提示作为显式蓝图的方法易引入伪影、损害生成质量；同时缺乏多样、高质量、精确标注的视频重打光数据集。

Method: 关键观察：噪声破坏可去除高频伪影而保留低频光传输信息。据此设计两阶段训练的异步联合建模：以不同时间步调度联合去噪RGB与真值辐照度帧；第二阶段引入域匹配策略弥合辐照度提示与真值辐照度图之间的域差异；推理时采用“异步劫持”生成流水线实现灵活光照控制。数据方面用游戏引擎构建带配对辐照度图的多光照高质量合成视频数据集。

Result: 仅在该合成数据集上训练，模型即可稳健泛化到真实场景，在光照保真度与生成质量上均优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 以真值辐照度为显式控制信号并结合异步联合去噪，可在保持生成质量的同时获得细粒度、可灵活操控的人像重打光能力；合成数据训练亦可实现良好的真实场景迁移。代码与数据集将公开。

Abstract: Understanding and modeling light transport in human-centric scenes is a longstanding challenge. While recent progress in diffusion models has boosted research in relighting, precise illumination control still lags, primarily due to the scarcity of diverse, high-quality, precisely labeled video relighting datasets. Existing end-to-end relighting approaches model light transport implicitly through neural networks, which substantially complicates controllability. In contrast, some works resort to path-traced irradiance cues as an explicit blueprint for light transport. However, such constructed irradiance cues are prone to artifacts and hinder generative quality when directly used as control signals. With the key observation that noise corruption removes high-frequency artifacts and preserves low-frequency light transport, we introduce an asynchronous joint-modeling paradigm that facilitates fine-grained illumination control. During two-stage training, we force the model to jointly denoise RGB and ground-truth irradiance frames with separate timesteps scheduling. A domain matching strategy is applied to the second stage to bridge the domain gap between irradiance cues and ground-truth irradiance maps. At inference time, we introduce an asynchronous hijacked generation pipeline that facilitates flexible illumination control. For training data, we develop a multi-illumination high-quality synthetic video dataset with paired irradiance maps using a powerful game engine. Trained exclusively on this dataset, our model exhibits robust generalization to real- world scenarios and outperforms prior approaches in both lighting fidelity and generative quality. The code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [57] [GeoMem-VLA: Internalizing 3D Scene Understanding into Temporal Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29866)
*Qinfan Zhang,Yifan Luo,Haoyuan Shi,Guanlin Liu,Han Dong,Haozhe Shan,Yong Dai,Xiangcong Ren,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMem-VLA 将显式 3D 几何监督内化为可学习的 GeoPlan token，并作为时序记忆维护，用于 VLA 动作生成；无需推理时调用 3D 教师模型或显式重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作与 3D 世界理解依赖几何结构，但多数 VLA 模型仅用 2D 观测表示，几何感知方法又需额外 3D 输入；且单帧推断出的几何往往被丢弃，未累积为持久任务表示。作者认为动作生成需要紧凑、任务相关且可持续更新的几何抽象，而非每步完整可渲染的 3D 重建。

Method: 提出 GeoMem-VLA 框架：训练时用冻结的几何基础模型，以几何 flow-matching 目标对当前场景点图与未来末端执行器轨迹监督可学习的 GeoPlan token，且不与 VLM 原生视觉嵌入做教师特征空间对齐；这些 token 与观测表示记忆集成，历史信息经检索、与当前观测自适应融合并整合，随任务推进保留事件帧。推理时策略检索历史表示并与当前工作表示融合预测动作，不调用 3D 教师、不显式重建场景。

Result: 经基准特定下游适配后，LIBERO 平均成功率 98.9%；RoboTwin 干净/随机化设置分别为 91.10% / 91.24%；五个真实世界任务平均成功率 78.0%。

Conclusion: 将 3D 场景理解内化并记忆是机器人控制的一条有前景路径。

Abstract: Geometric structure is essential for robotic manipulation and understanding the 3D world. Most VLA models rely on 2D representations of current observations, while geometry-aware approaches often require additional 3D information. However, geometry inferred independently from individual observations is often discarded rather than accumulated into a persistent task representation. Our key insight is that action generation needs a compact, task-relevant geometric abstraction that can be continuously updated, rather than a complete, renderable 3D reconstruction at every step. We introduce GeoMem-VLA, a VLA framework that internalizes explicit 3D supervision into dedicated geometric tokens and maintains them as temporal memory for action generation, without directly aligning the VLM's native visual embeddings with the teacher feature space. During training, a frozen geometry foundation model supervises learnable GeoPlan tokens through a geometric flow-matching objective over the current scene point map and future end-effector tracks. These tokens are integrated with the memory of observation representations, where historical information is retrieved, adaptively fused with current observations, and consolidated to retain event frames as the task progresses. At inference, the policy retrieves historical representations and fuses them with the current working representation to predict actions, without invoking the 3D teacher or explicitly reconstructing the scene. After benchmark-specific downstream adaptation, GeoMem-VLA achieves 98.9% average success on LIBERO and 91.10% / 91.24% on RoboTwin's clean/randomized settings. Across five real-world tasks, GeoMem-VLA achieves 78.0% average success. These results suggest that internalizing and remembering 3D scene understanding is a promising approach to robot control.

</details>


### [58] [GuidedSAM-Plume: An Expert-Guided Segmentation Framework for Volcanic Plumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29825)
*Taddeo D'Adamo,Simon Thivet,Catherine Pothier,Riccardo Simionato,Allan Fries,Jonathan Lemus,Costanza Bonadonna,Laure Tougne Rodet,Bertrand Kerautret*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出开源专家引导框架GuidedSAM-Plume，基于SAM2的零样本空间提示能力实现火山羽流与云的交互式分割，无需重训练即显著优于全自动方法并保持派生参数可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火山学中基于RGB图像的火山羽流与云几何表征受限于专用分割方案稀缺、标注部分依赖人工且不统一的问题。

Method: 利用SAM2的零样本、基于空间提示的分割能力，构建专家引导的交互式分割框架；在挑战性数据集上以专用分割指标和火山学描述符进行验证。

Result: 仅需极少用户输入即可显著提升分割性能，优于全自动专用方案，同时保持派生火山学参数的可靠性；无需重训练降低应用门槛。

Conclusion: 框架适用于复杂弥散羽流状现象，可推广至更广泛的环境科学领域；经专家引导重新标注的数据集更具标准化，框架与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Quantitative analysis of environmental phenomena increasingly relies on image-based measurements. In volcanology, the geometric characterization of volcanic plumes and clouds from RGB images remains hindered by the scarcity of dedicated segmentation solutions and partially handcrafted annotations remain widely prevalent. We present GuidedSAM-Plume, an open-source expert-guided framework tailored for the interactive segmentation of volcanic plumes and clouds. It leverages the zero-shot, spatial prompt-based capabilities of SAM2. The framework was thoroughly validated on a challenging dataset through dedicated segmentation metrics and field-relevant volcanological descriptors. From minimal user inputs, it is able to significantly increase performance compared to fully automatic dedicated solutions while preserving the reliability of derived volcanological parameters. The absence of retraining requirements lowers adoption barriers within the volcanological community, and the framework's demonstrated suitability for complex, diffuse plume-shaped phenomena extends its applicability to broader environmental science domains. The re-annotated dataset, produced under the guidance of expert volcanologists within the proposed framework, ultimately yields a more standardized release. The framework and accompanying dataset are made available at https://github.com/tad-damo/GuidedSAM-Plume.

</details>


### [59] [Key Findings for Small UAS for Post-Disaster Damage Assessment from the CURSE'26 Exercise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29777)
*Thomas Manzini,Stephen Johnson,Priyankari Perali,David Merrick,Robin Murphy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLARKE建筑与道路损伤评估AI分类器被纳入CURSE 2026演练，用未见过的正射影像评估其泛化性：建筑评估Micro F1达0.834且跨佛州、密苏里州可迁移，道路评估因配准与光照问题表现不佳，并总结五点对sUAS社区的经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLARKE虽已在两场飓风（Debby与Helene）中实际部署，但缺乏在大规模、未见数据上的系统评估；CURSE'26演练提供了用更多全新正射影像检验模型的机会，并借此考察sUAS与机器学习在灾害响应中的实际可用性。

Method: 将CLARKE建筑与道路损伤评估AI分类器集成进灾难性无人遥感演练CURSE 2026，训练佛州与密苏里州23支州/地方机构及非政府组织的sUAS队伍，为事件指挥遥感单元采集影像；在演练中收集多幅此前未见的正射影像，对机器学习模型进行评测，并记录运行时、精度与误差来源。

Result: 建筑与道路损伤评估合计平均运行3.5分钟；建筑损伤评估在未做空间错位人工校正的情况下Micro F1为0.834，且在佛州与密苏里州表现一致，显示良好地理迁移性；道路损伤评估表现较差，主要受道路地图与影像配准误差、城市环境光照与条件影响。

Conclusion: 分析还给出五点对sUAS社区有价值的发现：sUAS与机器学习的结合已走向常态化并被接受；外业队伍可通过选择飞行时段提升分类精度；演练是良好的运行测试，但用于评估学习模型的价值尚不明显；边缘计算是克服无线回传瓶颈的必要手段；sUAS与AI用于灾害响应给飞手和数据管理者带来伦理与法律层面的影响。

Abstract: The CLARKE building and road damage assessment AI classifier was incorporated into the Catastrophic Unmanned Remote Sensing Exercise (CURSE) 2026, which trained 23 small uncrewed aerial systems (sUAS) teams from state and local agencies and non-governmental organizations in Florida and Missouri on how to collect imagery for an incident command Remote Sensing Cell. Although CLARKE has been operationally deployed to two hurricanes (Debby and Helene), CURSE'26 presented an opportunity to evaluate the machine learning model with a larger set of never-before-seen orthomosaics. The results show that building and road damage assessment together ran in 3.5 minutes on average, and that building damage assessment achieved a Micro F1 of 0.834 with no manual adjustments to correct spatial misalignment and delivered that performance in both Florida and Missouri, demonstrating good geographic transfer. It also showed that road damage assessment performed poorly due to sensitivity to misalignment between road maps and the images, and to lighting and environmental conditions in an urban setting. The analysis of CURSE'26 presents five other findings of value to the sUAS community: that the use of sUAS and machine learning is now operationally normalized and accepted, sUAS teams in the field can improve classification accuracy by timing their flights, exercises are good operational tests but their value for evaluating learning models is less clear, edge computing is needed to overcome the wireless upload barrier, and the application of sUAS and AI to disaster response has ethical and legal ramifications for pilots and data managers.

</details>


### [60] [UniRAP: Towards Unified Part-level Physical Affordance Reasoning and Actionable Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29768)
*Linfei Li,Ruining Hu,Lin Zhang,Zhong Wang,Fengyi Zhang,Ying Shen,Binqiang Wang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniRAP统一模型，将语言指令映射到部件级物理可供性与可执行接触几何，通过条件多模态生成与统一可供性解码器联合完成检测、部件分割与4-DoF交互位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言感知在自然语言与视觉观测对齐上进展显著，但将高层语义落地为部件级物理交互仍困难，缺乏可执行的几何表征。

Method: 将问题建模为条件多模态生成任务，用统一接口token机制把视觉输入、文本指令与可选提示整合到共享时空表征空间；设计统一可供性解码器（UAD），利用中间分割特征联合执行目标检测、部件级可供性分割与4-DoF交互位姿估计；并采用课程式迁移训练策略，由通用视觉解析渐进过渡到交互感知。

Result: 在指代表达分割、可供性接地与交互位姿估计上达到SOTA，并在动态视频场景中保持较强时空一致性。

Conclusion: UniRAP可作为语言引导物理操作的有效统一感知框架，验证了统一建模部件级可供性与几何预测的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-language perception has achieved impressive progress in aligning natural language with visual observations, yet grounding high-level semantics into part-level physical interaction remains challenging. To address this gap, we propose UniRAP, a unified model for inferring part-level physical affordances and mapping language instructions to actionable geometric representations. UniRAP formulates this problem as a conditional multimodal generation task, integrating visual inputs, textual instructions, and optional prompts into a shared spatiotemporal representation space through a unified interface token mechanism. To predict executable contact geometry, we introduce a Unified Affordance Decoder (UAD), which jointly performs object detection, part-level affordance segmentation, and 4-DoF interaction pose estimation by leveraging intermediate segmentation features. In addition, we propose a curriculum-based transfer training strategy that progressively adapts the model from general visual parsing to interaction-aware perception, improving data efficiency under limited textual annotations. Experiments show that UniRAP achieves state-of-the-art performance on referring expression segmentation, affordance grounding, and interaction pose estimation, while maintaining strong spatiotemporal consistency in dynamic video scenarios. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of UniRAP as a unified perception framework for language-guided physical manipulation.

</details>


### [61] [Learning to Measure and Diagnose: A Clinical-Dictionary Radiomic Bottleneck Model for Thyroid Nodule Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29738)
*Guibin Zhang,Weier Luo,Lei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“临床字典放射组学瓶颈模型”，用于甲状腺结节超声分类，使其预测可追溯到临床可理解的放射组学量化证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声虽可评估结节良恶性，但分类预测普遍缺乏与临床熟悉描述相联系的证据；放射组学可提供明确定义的测量量并借助临床字典支撑解释，但要将其用作可解释证据，需从图像中可靠估计这些测量量，并在优化分类性能时保持测量保真度。

Method: 模型包含三部分：(1) Family-Aware Radiomic Learning (FARL)，在各特征族间进行均衡监督以估计放射组学测量量；(2) Fidelity-Guided Bottleneck Modeling (FGBM)，构建固定瓶颈，并在保真度引导下联合优化测量估计与分类；(3) 决策证据分解，通过线性分类器追溯特征贡献，并按临床字典类目组织。

Result: 在 ThyroidXL 与 TN3K 数据集上取得有竞争力的分类性能；案例研究展示了良性结节与恶性结节的特征贡献。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式引入临床字典放射组学瓶颈可实现可解释分类，并提供受临床知识约束、可检验的定量证据；代码已公开。

Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound supports the assessment of nodule malignancy, but classification predictions often lack evidence linked to familiar clinical descriptions. Radiomics provides explicitly defined measurements that can support such explanations through a clinical dictionary. However, using these measurements as interpretable evidence requires reliable estimation from ultrasound images and preservation of measurement fidelity when optimizing classification performance. We propose a clinical-dictionary radiomic bottleneck model for thyroid nodule classification. Within this model, Family-Aware Radiomic Learning (FARL) estimates measurements with balanced supervision across feature families. Fidelity-Guided Bottleneck Modeling (FGBM) constructs a fixed bottleneck, followed by joint optimization of measurement estimation and classification under fidelity guidance. Decision evidence decomposition then traces feature contributions through a linear classifier and organizes them by clinical dictionary categories. Experiments on ThyroidXL and TN3K show competitive classification performance, while case studies illustrate feature contributions for benign and malignant nodules. These findings indicate interpretable classification with inspectable quantitative evidence informed by clinical knowledge. Code is available online.

</details>


### [62] [Forward-Only Learning for Shared-Kernel Wavelet Neural Networks without Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29697)
*Yongfei Guo,Xuan Sun,Ang Li,Zhao Zhang,Yuanhao Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于CMA-ES的全局前向学习框架，用于共享核小波神经网络，实现无需反向传播的整网训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播依赖后向梯度，深层网络中易出现梯度消失/爆炸；Forward-Forward虽避开BP但主要为局部或逐层学习，缺乏整网前向学习方案。

Method: 针对共享核小波神经网络，构建CMA-ES驱动的全局前向学习框架，并引入结构感知参数分解与通道级信用分配，将搜索空间分解为三个子空间。

Result: BSDS500上测试MSE由4.1783降至4.0130，PSNR由42.621提升至42.787 dB（对比Adam/BP且无反向传播）；训练仅需6次训练集覆盖，而BP需1500个epoch。消融显示结构分解与对齐信用分配比增大种群规模更关键。

Conclusion: 验证了紧凑重建网络整网前向学习的可行性，为资源受限成像及边缘/近传感系统的前向优化提供潜在路径。

Abstract: Backpropagation remains the dominant training paradigm, using backward gradients and local updates that can suffer from vanishing or exploding gradients in deep networks. Although the Forward-Forward algorithm avoids conventional backpropagation, it remains mainly local or layer-wise, motivating global whole-network learning using forward evaluations only. For shared-kernel wavelet neural networks, we therefore propose a CMA-ES-based global forward-only learning framework. We further introduce structure-aware parameter decomposition and channel-specific credit assignment, factorizing the search into three subspaces. On BSDS500, our method reduces test MSE from 4.1783 to 4.0130 and improves PSNR from 42.621 to 42.787~dB over Adam/BP without backward propagation. Beyond these gains, training requires only 6 training-set coverages, compared with 1500 epochs for BP. Ablations show that structural decomposition and aligned credit assignment are more important than increasing population size, demonstrating the feasibility of whole-network forward-only learning for compact reconstruction networks. These results suggest a potential route toward forward-only optimization of compact reconstruction models for resource-constrained imaging and edge or near-sensor systems.

</details>


### [63] [Spatially Gated Demographic Steering for Debiasing Multi-Person Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29651)
*Venkatesh Thirugnana Sambandham,Torsten Schon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出空间门控引导（SGS），利用DiT内部注意力图提取逐人掩码，在关键去噪步后对每个人物独立施加不同人口统计向量，无需微调即可在多人场景中同时提升图内与总体多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型会复现社会刻板印象，而现有缓解方法对整个场景施加单一人口统计校正。在多人场景中这导致权衡：全局引导只靠"同质化"图内人物来改善总体平衡，Embedding Arithmetic等引导方法甚至使图内多样性低于未引导基线；文本与潜空间投影方法仅带来边际的图内多样性提升。

Method: SGS在关键去噪时间步从DiT内部注意力图中提取逐人掩码（用轻量分割器作用于冻结的DiT注意力特征，在早期时间步定位主体），随后在剩余去噪步中于每个掩码内施加不同的人口统计向量，实现逐人独立引导。方法无需微调、反演或布局指定。

Result: 在偏斜的职业提示上，SGS实现较高的图内种族与性别多样性，同时保持有竞争力的总体平衡与接近基线的图像质量；结果在FLUX.1-Dev与SD3.5-Large两种架构不同的扩散Transformer上得到验证。

Conclusion: 逐人空间门控引导可解耦"图内多样性"与"总体平衡"，避免全局引导的同质化代价；基于冻结注意力特征的轻量定位即可实现，具备跨架构可迁移性，代码与权重已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-Image models reproduce societal stereotypes, and existing mitigation applies one demographic correction to the whole scene. In multi-person scene generation this creates a trade-off: global steering improves aggregate balance only by homogenizing the people within an image. Steering-based methods like Embedding Arithmetic drive the intra-image diversity below the unsteered baseline. Text- and latent-projection approaches shift the aggregate skew with only marginal gains in intra-image diversity. We present Spatially Gated Steering (SGS), which steers the demographics of each person independently by extracting per-person masks from the Diffusion Transformer's (DiT) internal attention maps at a critical denoising timestep and applying a distinct demographic vector within each mask for the remaining steps. SGS needs no fine-tuning, inversion or layout specifications, and uses a lightweight segmentor over frozen DiT attention features to localize subjects at early timesteps. On skewed occupational prompts, SGS reaches high intra-image racial and gender diversity while keeping aggregate balance competitive and image quality near baseline, a result we validate on two architecturally distinct diffusion transformers, FLUX.1-Dev and SD3.5-Large. Codebase and model weights are available at https://github.com/cvims/spatially-gated-steering

</details>


### [64] [FogTTA: Online Test-Time Adaptation for Robust Transformer-based Object Detection in Foggy Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29618)
*Ali Abedi,Q. M. Jonathan Wu,Ning Zhang,Shiqi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FogTTA：面向雾天场景的在线测试时自适应框架，提升基于Transformer的目标检测器鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶目标检测模型在训练数据（晴天）与实际运行条件（雾天）之间存在域偏移，导致可见度与对比度下降时性能显著退化。现有方法需源数据或重训练，故需无源在线自适应方案。

Method: 基于RF-DETR检测器，采用师生架构：教师对弱增强目标输入生成伪标签，经NMS与置信度过滤精炼；学生对强增强样本以Varifocal损失学习以抑制伪标签噪声；教师通过指数移动平均（EMA）更新以稳定持续适应，无需源数据与重训练。

Result: 实验表明FogTTA优于已有基线，检测精度与稳定性提升，同时保持实时性能。

Conclusion: 无源在线测试时自适应可有效缓解雾天域偏移下的检测性能退化，兼顾精度、稳定性与实时性。

Abstract: Object detection models for autonomous driving commonly experience substantial performance drops when deployed under adverse weather due to the domain shift between training data and real-world operating conditions. This degradation is especially evident when models trained on clear-weather images encounter foggy environments with reduced visibility and contrast. To address this challenge, we introduce FogTTA, an online test-time adaptation framework designed to improve the robustness of Transformer-based object detectors in fog. Using RF-DETR as the underlying object detector, FogTTA enables real-time adaptation to the streaming target domain without requiring source data or retraining. The framework follows a teacher-student design, where the deployed model serves as the teacher and generates pseudo labels from weakly augmented target inputs. These predictions are subsequently refined through non-maximum suppression and confidence filtering. The student model then learns from strongly augmented target sample using the Varifocal loss to mitigate pseudo-label noise. The teacher is updated via exponential moving averaging to ensure stable and continuous adaptation. Experiments show that FogTTA outperforms prior baselines, delivering improved detection accuracy and stability while maintaining real-time performance.

</details>


### [65] [An interpretable deep learning method for early hepatocellular carcinoma diagnosis on multiphase CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29616)
*Emma Sarfati,Alexandre Bône,Pietro Gori,Sisi Yang,Marc-Michel Rohé,François Nicolas,Jeong-Min Lee,Jeong Hee Yoon,Maxime Ronot,Isabelle Bloch,Christophe Aubé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: dLI-RADS是一种可解释AI方法，通过融合神经网络预测与反映LI-RADS主要特征的定量影像测量指标，经逻辑回归整合，从多期CT手动分割病灶估计肝细胞癌概率；内部集准确率80%非劣于LI-RADS的74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝细胞癌早期诊断关乎根治性治疗机会；LI-RADS提供基于多期CT主要视觉特征的无创标准化诊断框架，但特征判读一致性不足，小病灶尤甚。

Method: 多中心回顾性研究：纳入有多期CT且至少一个肝脏病灶的患者，排除>40 mm病灶；金标准为组织学（HCC）及复合标准（非HCC）。dLI-RADS将神经网络预测与人工设计的定量影像测量（对应LI-RADS主要特征）经逻辑回归整合。内部评价集用10,000次bootstrap非劣性检验比较病灶级准确率（5%界值），外部测试集比较敏感度。

Result: 内部评价集上dLI-RADS对LI-RADS在病灶级准确率上非劣：80% vs 74%（p = 0.04）。

Conclusion: 可解释AI可在与LI-RADS概念对齐的前提下提升HCC无创诊断准确率，有望辅助小病灶等判读困难病例。

Abstract: Early diagnosis of hepatocellular carcinoma is critical to preserve access to curative treatment options. The Liver Imaging Reporting and Data System (LI-RADS) provides a standardized framework for non-invasive diagnosis on multiphase computed tomography, based on major visual imaging features. However, these features may be difficult to assess consistently, particularly in small liver lesions. We developed dLI-RADS, an interpretable artificial intelligence method designed to estimate the probability of hepatocellular carcinoma from multiphase computed tomography images of manually segmented liver lesions, while remaining aligned with LI-RADS-related imaging concepts. dLI-RADS combines neural network-derived predictions with handcrafted quantitative imaging measurements reflecting LI-RADS major features and integrates these inputs using logistic regression. In this multicenter retrospective study, patients with multiphase computed tomography and at least one liver lesion were included, while lesions larger than 40 mm were excluded. Histology served as the reference standard for hepatocellular carcinoma, and a composite reference standard was used for non-hepatocellular carcinoma lesions. The lesion-level accuracy of dLI-RADS and LI-RADS were compared on the internal evaluation set using 10,000-repetition bootstrap non-inferiority tests, with 5% margins and significance levels. On the external test set, sensitivities were similarly compared. dLI-RADS was non-inferior to LI-RADS for lesion-level accuracy in the internal evaluation set, achieving 80% accuracy compared with 74% for LI-RADS (p = 0.04)...

</details>


### [66] [JUICE: Unified Multimodal Modeling in Pretrained Representation Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28801)
*Jiahan Li,Huangyuan Su,Shengbang Tong,Shusheng Yang,Ruihan Guo,Suozhi Huang,Hexu Zhao,Pratyusha Sharma,Sherry Yang,Jiatao Gu,Rob Fergus,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 JUICE，一个在冻结的图像与文本编码器连续表示空间上联合建模多模态分布的单一 flow matching Transformer，用各模态独立噪声水平统一图像生成、描述、VQA 与联合生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型对图像和文本处理不对称（LM 出文本、外接扩散头出图像），且含双模态的输出按固定顺序生成，先输出的模态无法适应后者。作者希望两个模态由同一从头联合训练的生成过程产生。

Method: 在预训练编码器空间中直接建模联合分布：JUICE 为单一 flow matching Transformer，作用于冻结的图像和文本表示，每个模态有独立噪声水平；通过指定哪些模态干净、哪些被生成来确定任务（图像生成、captioning、VQA、联合生成）。并做受控预训练实验检验各设计选择。

Result: 同等引导下，文本以连续状态生成时图像生成基本不受损（GenEval 48.41 vs 纯 T2I 的 47.86），但描述在逐字措辞上有损失；联合去噪与条件去噪共同训练优于任一单独；语义编码器空间优于 VAE latents 和原始像素。2.1B 参数、无语言模型初始化、224×224 下 GenEval 达 0.88、VQAv2 达 73.1。耦合采样下迷宫多解问题联合成功率由独立采样的 54.6% 升至 93.7%，视觉空间规划在其训练规模下解出 99.8% 地图。

Conclusion: 多模态可由单一联合训练的连续空间生成过程统一完成，无需模态顺序或 LM 初始化；联合分布采样带来显著的任务级收益（如联合成功率与规划准确率），且不牺牲单模态生成质量。

Abstract: Most unified multimodal models treat images and text asymmetrically: a pretrained language model generates text, an attached diffusion head generates images, and outputs that contain both are produced in a fixed order, so the first output cannot adapt to the second. We ask whether both modalities can instead be generated by one process that is trained on them jointly from scratch. Pretrained encoder spaces hold both modalities in one continuous form and carry knowledge learned at scale, and recent work finds them effective as generative targets for vision and language. We therefore model the joint distribution directly in these spaces. We introduce JUICE (Joint Unified multimodal modeling In Continuous Encoder spaces), a single flow matching Transformer over frozen image and text representations, in which each modality has its own noise level. Which modalities are clean and which are generated then decides the task: image generation, captioning, visual question answering, or joint generation. Controlled pretraining runs test each design choice. At equal guidance, generating text as continuous states leaves image generation intact (48.41 GenEval, against 47.86 for a model trained only for text-to-image generation), while captions lose some exact wording. Training on joint and conditional denoising together beats either alone, and semantic encoder spaces beat VAE latents and raw pixels as targets. At 2.1B parameters, with no language model initialization and at 224x224, JUICE reaches up to 0.88 on GenEval and 73.1 on VQAv2. By sampling both modalities from the joint distribution (coupled sampling), it can also draw a route on a map while writing out the matching moves. On mazes with several valid solutions, joint success rises from 54.6% with independent sampling to 93.7%, and no modality has to go first. On visual spatial planning, JUICE solves 99.8% of maps at its training sizes.

</details>


### [67] [UNIVERS: Learning UNIversal Voxel-wise Embedding via Randomized Synthesis for generalizable one-shot landmark localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29098)
*Derong Yu,Qingsong Zhao,Yanbing Yue,Yikai Li,Zhi Zhang,Guoyan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UNIVERS——端到端可训练的通用体素级嵌入学习框架，通过随机合成伪数据+跨域判别式体素对比学习，实现任意FOV多模态体数据的单样本地标定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督方法依赖同一图像增强视图的对比学习，跨模态泛化性受限；而基于配对多模态图像的跨模态嵌入学习又受限于训练数据不足和多阶段迭代训练需求。

Method: 基于器官形状模板随机合成伪数据，对语义布局、视觉外观、FOV与空间位姿进行随机化；结合跨域判别式体素级对比学习，迫使网络忽略域特定偏置，学习对成像对比度、分辨率与空间上下文变化鲁棒的通用体素嵌入；端到端训练。

Result: 在三个具挑战性的肾上腺数据集上，UNIVERS持续优于SOTA单样本地标定位方法，在跨模态场景下优势尤为突出。

Conclusion: 域随机化合成+跨域体素对比学习可有效实现跨模态、任意FOV的单样本地标定位，代码已开源。

Abstract: One-shot landmark localization in volume scans with varying fields of view (FOVs) is challenging. While existing self-supervised learning methods have shown promising performance, they rely on contrastive learning built upon augmented views of the same image, which inherently limits their generalization in cross-modality scenarios. This motivates research on cross-modality embedding learning using paired multi-modality images. However, existing methods suffer from not only limited training data, but also the requirement of multi-stage iterative training. To address these limitations, we propose UNIVERS, an end-to-end trainable framework that learns UNIversal Voxel-wise Embedding via Randomized Synthesis. Our framework leverages diverse pseudo-data synthesized from organ shape templates, with randomization applied to semantic layout, visual appearance, FOV, and spatial pose. Coupled with cross-domain discriminative voxel-wise contrastive learning, this domain randomization strategy forces the network to disregard domain-specific biases and learn features that are robust against variations in imaging contrast, resolution, and spatial context. Consequently, the trained model enables one-shot landmark localization in multi-modality volume scans with arbitrary FOVs. In this study, the efficacy of the proposed method is demonstrated by applying it to the task of localizing the adrenal glands, which are paired, small, and irregular endocrine organs. In particular, comprehensive experiments are conducted on three challenging adrenal gland datasets. The experimental results demonstrate that UNIVERS consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art (SOTA) one-shot landmark localization approaches, with particularly prominent advantages in cross-modality scenarios. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Kendra9999/UNIVERS.

</details>


### [68] [T-DIG: Text-Conditioned Diversity for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29003)
*Simran Khanuja,Rohit Gandikota,Gaurav Parmar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T-DIG是一个文本条件多样性框架，通过LLM策略为冻结的图像生成/编辑器构造K个沿用户指定轴线的提示或编辑指令，并以GRPO+v多样性奖励训练，在保持原提示对齐的同时显著提升用户导向的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2I平台通常每个提示返回多张图以供用户探索，但模型多以单图质量为优化目标，牺牲了多图多样性；且多样性是"上下文相关"的——不同用户/时刻关注的变异轴（背景、风格、服饰等）不同。因此需要一种可控、按轴线定向的多样性机制。

Method: 提出T-DIG迭代式框架，用户输入提示、查看默认生成、用自然语言请求变化并反复精修。核心是一个LLM策略，为冻结的生成器或编辑器生成K个"轴条件化"提示或编辑指令；用GRPO训练该策略，奖励为文本条件化多样性奖励并辅以对齐惩罚项。因生成器冻结，方法可适用于开放权重、蒸馏及闭源模型。同时发布首个文本条件化提示多样性数据集：训练/验证集由人工撰写的轴线构成，测试集来自真实用户交互。

Result: 在多项指标与人工评估中，T-DIG在提升文本条件化多样性方面大幅超越基线，同时保持对基础提示的对齐。

Conclusion: 用户导向的多样性探索可作为外挂模块加到广泛的图像生成模型（开源与闭源）之上，无需访问模型权重。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) platforms typically return several images per prompt so users can explore different visual interpretations and choose one that matches their intent. However, T2I models are usually optimized for single-image quality which comes at a cost of multi-image diversity, limiting this exploration.
