Diversity is also contextual: one user wants variations in background, while another wants them in artistic style, and the same user may later want variations in clothing. The generated images should differ along whatever axis the user cares about. In this paper, we introduce T-DIG, a text-conditioned diversity framework for image generation and editing. T-DIG is an iterative framework comprising multiple modules that lets users type prompts, inspect default generations, request variations using natural language, and continue refining outputs until they're satisfied. At its core, T-DIG uses an LLM policy that produces K axis-conditioned prompts or edit instructions for a frozen generator or editor. We train this policy with GRPO using a text-conditioned diversity reward and alignment penalties. Because the image generator remains frozen, T-DIG applies to open-weight, distilled, and proprietary models. We also release the first dataset for text-conditioned prompt diversity in image generation, with the training and validation splits built from human-written axes and a test set drawn from real user interactions with T-DIG. T-DIG far exceeds baselines in improving text-conditioned diversity, while preserving base-prompt alignment across a variety of metrics and human judgements. We show that user-directed diversity exploration can be added to a wide range of image generation models, open and closed, without the need to access their weights. Website: https://image-study.org/t-dig/

</details>


### [69] [Pair-Free Coarse-to-Real Video Generation via Real-to-Coarse Image Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28954)
*Gonzalo Gómez-Nogales,Daohan Lu,Peiye Zhuang,Tinghui Zhou,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无配对数据的粗到真视频生成框架：用真实→粗糙图像对训练DINO特征空间的R2C模型，逐帧合成伪粗糙视频，再训练C2R#模型实现真实视频重建，推理时只需真实粗糙视频驱动，摆脱了对(粗糙,真实)视频对的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C2R视频生成（将粗动画/仿真/游戏渲染转为照片级真实视频并保持布局、相机运动与动态）需要对齐的(粗糙,真实)视频对，这类数据制作昂贵，使既有系统局限于狭窄的合成域。作者观察到一种非对称性：配对视频难获得，但真实→粗糙的配对图像可用现有编辑与3D重建工具轻易构造。

Method: 利用构造的真实→粗糙图像对，训练DINO-R2C模型在DINO特征空间中将真实图像转换为粗糙图像，并逐帧应用于普通真实视频以合成伪粗糙视频；随后训练C2R#模型学习由伪粗糙对应帧生成真实视频，从而无需(粗糙,真实)视频对。推理阶段丢弃DINO-R2C，直接由真实粗糙视频驱动C2R#。

Result: 训练不再依赖配对合成视频，可随任意真实视频语料扩展（pair-free training scales with any real-video corpora）；模型与项目页面将公开。

Conclusion: 通过图像级配对代理任务替代视频级配对监督，解除C2R视频生成的数据瓶颈，使训练可扩展到通用真实视频域而非狭窄合成域。

Abstract: Coarse-to-real (C2R) video generation converts coarse animation, simulation, or game-rendered videos into photorealistic videos while preserving scene layout, camera motion, and dynamics. Training such models requires aligned (coarse, realistic) video pairs, which are expensive to create and confine prior systems to narrow synthetic domains. We exploit a simple asymmetry: paired videos are hard to obtain, but paired real-to-coarse images are easy to construct with existing editing and 3D reconstruction tools. Using these image pairs, we train the DINO-R2C model to transfer realistic images to coarse images in DINO feature space, and apply it frame-wise to ordinary real videos to synthesize pseudo-coarse videos. The C2R# model then learns to generate each real video from its pseudo-coarse counterpart, free from needing (coarse, realistic) video pairs. At inference, the DINO-R2C model is discarded and actual coarse videos drive the C2R# model directly. Thus, our pair-free training scales with any real-video corpora rather than with paired synthetic videos. We will release the model and project webpage at https://gonzalognogales.github.io/coarse2real-sharp/.

</details>


### [70] [Data-Driven Glyph Positioning for Handwritten Music Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28899)
*Elona Shatri,Jiří Mayer,Jan Hajič*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文检验 Smashcima 手写乐谱合成中硬编码排版规则的经验依据，用 MUSCIMA++ 与 OmniOMR 的约 28.5 万条标注关系提取字形间分布，发现关键参数系统性偏离真实手写；以经验均值替换常数可将定位 RMSE 降低最多 60%，再用基于 Music Notation Graph 的 GNN 预测上下文相关偏移，在符干与符梁上再降 50%–85%。下游 YOLOv8 检测器在 GNN 定位的合成数据上预训练，仅用 200 行真实手写谱微调后，mAP50 较基线提升 30%、较经验均值提升 11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OMR 领域缺乏标注手写乐谱，合成数据成为主流对策；Smashcima 通过采样 MUSCIMA++ 字形并按硬编码规则定位来生成整页手写乐谱，但这些规则是否具有经验依据、以及上下文相关的学习式定位能否进一步提升合成质量，尚不清楚。

Method: 从 MUSCIMA++ 与 OmniOMR 的约 285,000 条字形间标注关系中统计经验分布，诊断 Smashcima 硬编码参数的偏差；将常数替换为经验均值；在 Music Notation Graph 结构上训练 GNN 预测上下文相关的字形偏移；并进行跨数据集风格/密度分析与下游检测评测。

Result: 多个关键定位参数系统性偏离真实手写；以经验均值替换常数使定位 RMSE 最多下降 60%；GNN 借助邻近字形的约束建模，使符干与符梁的 RMSE 进一步下降 50%–85%。跨数据集分析显示两语料间距差异由音符密度解释（系数 3.3）而非书写风格，且手写中不存在时长比例间距规律（R²=0.001）。下游 YOLOv8 在 GNN 定位合成数据上预训练、再以 200 行真实手写谱微调，mAP50 较基线提升 30%、较经验均值提升 11%。

Conclusion: Smashcima 的硬编码定位规则缺乏经验依据，经验化与学习式（GNN）上下文定位可显著降低定位误差，且定位精度对下游 OMR 性能有实质影响；手写乐谱的间距主要由音符密度而非书写风格或时长比例决定。

Abstract: Synthesised data is a common strategy to address the lack of annotated handwritten music scores in Optical Music Recognition (OMR). Smashcima is a recent synthesis framework that assembles pages of handwritten scores by sampling glyph images from MUSCIMA++ and positioning them with hard-coded rules. In this work we ask whether these rules are grounded empirically and whether a learned model for context-dependent positioning can further improve synthesis quality. We extract inter-glyph distributions from around 285 thousand annotated relationships across MUSCIMA++ and OmniOMR, finding that several key parameters deviate systematically from real handwriting. Replacing these constants with empirical means reduces positioning RMSE by up to 60%. We then train a Graph Neural Network (GNN) on the Music Notation Graph structure to predict context-dependent offsets, achieving an additional 50 to 85% RMSE reduction on stems and beams by modelling how neighbouring glyphs constrain placement. Cross-dataset analysis shows that spacing differences between the two corpora are explained by note density (3.3), not style, and that duration-proportional spacing does not hold in handwriting (R2 = 0.001). In downstream evaluation, a YOLOv8 detector pretrained on GNN-positioned synthetic data achieves +30% mAP50 over the baseline and +11% over empirical means when fine-tuned on 200 real handwritten staves, showing that positioning accuracy matters for downstream OMR.

</details>


### [71] [Supervision-Adaptive Curriculum and Guarded Anti-Curriculum Learning for Dense Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29519)
*Chunming He,Dingming Zhang,Fengyang Xiao,Zhiwen Cao,Sina Farsiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaceShift：先稳定后挑战的两阶段范式，将监督可靠性与时序更新价值分离，结合自适应课程选择与带保护的谱后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程学习仅以难度排序样本，无法保证密集预测中监督信号的可靠性：简单样本可能暴露捷径，困难样本或有信息量，噪声/模糊样本则会误导训练。

Method: 第一阶段从可用监督、增强一致性、模型分歧与预测确定性估计可靠性，用时序统计引导样本加权，并用像素不确定性权重降低早期高不确定区域影响；第二阶段对多个谱视图的预测按监督一致性、鲁棒性、参考一致性及惩罚项排序，以针对冻结的第一阶段参考的成对偏好目标加锚点细化预测。支持全监督、弱监督、半监督与模型生成监督。

Result: 在上下文纠缠内容分割（CECS）、更广泛的密集预测任务以及基础模型和跨模态适配实验中均取得精度提升；针对性诊断显示在评估设置下池化校准与腐蚀鲁棒性改善；保持部署架构不变且不增加推理计算。

Conclusion: PaceShift在保持推理零开销的前提下，通过可靠性感知的课程选择与谱后训练显著提升密集预测的精度与鲁棒性，并具备多监督范式的通用性。

Abstract: Curriculum learning orders samples by difficulty, but difficulty alone does not establish supervision reliability for dense prediction. Easy samples can expose shortcuts, hard samples can be informative, and noisy or ambiguous samples can mislead training. Building on our conference method CurriSeg, we present PaceShift, a stabilize-then-challenge paradigm combining supervision-adaptive curriculum selection with guarded spectral post-training. A learning-dynamics view motivates separating supervision reliability from temporal update value and applying spectral challenges after stabilization. The first phase estimates reliability from available supervision, augmentation consistency, model disagreement, and prediction certainty. Temporal statistics guide sample weighting, while pixel-uncertainty weights reduce the early influence of uncertain regions. The second phase ranks predictions from multiple spectral views using supervision agreement, robustness, reference consistency, and penalties. A pairwise preference objective measured against the frozen Phase-I reference, together with an anchor, refines the prediction. The framework supports full, weak, semi-supervised, and model-derived supervision. Experiments across context-entangled content segmentation (CECS), broader dense-prediction tasks, and foundation-model and cross-modal adaptations show accuracy gains. Targeted diagnostics indicate improved pooled calibration and corruption robustness in the evaluated settings. PaceShift preserves the deployed architecture and adds no inference computation. Code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [72] [Balancing Foundation Models and Trait-Specific Learning for Forehead-Crease Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29495)
*Geetanjali Sharma,Aryan Jain,Dev Nagaich,Aditya Nigam,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统评估额纹（forehead crease）作为部分遮挡下补充生物特征的识别性能，比较可解释零样本推理（FIRE/GPT-4o）、域自适应学习（Edge-Forehead）与特征专用建模三种范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别在面部部分可见（遮挡）时性能显著下降，而额头区域始终可见且具独特皱纹模式，可作为互补生物特征；同时需厘清多模态大模型零样本推理与专用学习在该任务上的相对优劣。

Method: 统一评测框架：FIRE 借助多模态大语言模型实现可解释零样本验证；同时将预训练人脸识别模型通过 margin-based（基于间隔）学习迁移至额头域（Edge-Forehead）；在开集设置下评估。

Result: 最优零样本模型 GPT-4o+FIRE 的 EER 为 18.22%；Edge-Forehead 达 8.30% EER 与 54.01% TMR@0.1% FMR。

Conclusion: MLLM 可提供可解释的零样本验证能力，但对稳健的额纹生物识别而言，特征专用学习（域自适应）仍更为有效。

Abstract: Biometric recognition often degrades under partial facial vis- ibility, where conventional facial cues are occluded. The forehead region remains consistently visible and contains distinctive crease patterns that can serve as a complementary biometric trait. We present a unified eval- uation of forehead-crease recognition across explainable zero-shot rea- soning, domain-adaptive learning, and trait-specific modeling. FIRE en- ables interpretable zero-shot verification using multimodal large language models, while pretrained face-recognition models are adapted to the fore- head domain through margin-based learning. Under open-set evaluation, the best zero-shot model GPT-4o+FIRE achieves 18.22% EER, while Edge-Forehead achieves 8.30% EER and 54.01% TMR@0.1% FMR. The results show that although MLLMs offer interpretable zero-shot verifi- cation, trait-specific learning remains more effective for robust forehead- crease biometric recognition.

</details>


### [73] [BlazeVGGT: 13x Faster VGGT at 2,000 Frames via Camera Token without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29454)
*Shujun Yao,Junhui Lin,Congying Han,Tiande Guo,Ziwen Liu,Bonan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BlazeVGGT：一种免训练、结构感知的帧级 KV 稀疏化框架，利用 VGGT 相机 token 的“先拓扑后几何”演化规律，用 DPP 选取互补视图作为 key/value，实现长序列高效多视图重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈视觉几何模型（如 VGGT）依赖稠密全局注意力，all-to-all 跨视图计算随视图数二次增长，长序列推理代价高昂；而高重叠观测中存在大量帧级冗余。作者发现 VGGT 相机 token 浅层捕获局部视图邻域、深层渐进编码全局几何关系，为稀疏化提供了结构依据。

Method: 构建 view-level 相似度核（基于相机 token 关系），在全局注意力块中采用贪心行列式点过程（DPP）推理选出互为互补的紧凑视图集合作为 KV 上下文，同时所有输入帧保留为 query，形成非对称注意力，保留逐帧稠密预测；无需参数更新，可作为插件直接嵌入预训练 VGGT。

Result: 在 2000 帧下推理速度约为 VGGT 的 13 倍，Chamfer Distance 由 0.41 降至 0.32；在点云重建与相机位姿估计上取得良好精度-效率权衡，并兼容 FastVGGT、LiteVGGT 等加速器，可迁移至 StreamVGGT、PAGE-4D 等 VGGT 系模型。

Conclusion: 通过结构感知的视图级稀疏化替代全对全跨视图注意力，可在免训练条件下显著降低长序列推理开销并提升精度，具备良好的通用性与即插即用特性。

Abstract: Feed-forward visual geometry models, exemplified by VGGT, have demonstrated strong multi-view reconstruction capabilities through dense global attention. However, the resulting all-to-all cross-view computation scales quadratically with the number of input views, making long-sequence inference particularly expensive due to substantial frame-level redundancy among highly overlapping observations. We uncover a topology-first, geometry-later evolution in VGGT camera tokens: shallow-layer tokens reliably capture local view neighborhoods, whereas deeper-layer tokens progressively encode globally organized inter-view geometry. Motivated by this observation, we introduce BlazeVGGT, a training-free framework for structure-aware frame-level key--value sparsification. At each global-attention block, BlazeVGGT constructs a view-level similarity kernel from camera-token relations and employs greedy determinantal point process inference to select a compact set of mutually complementary views. The selected views constitute the key--value context, while all input frames are retained as queries. This asymmetric attention design preserves dense per-frame predictions while eliminating redundant cross-view computation, allowing our method to be directly integrated into pretrained VGGT as a plug-and-play module without parameter updates. Its advantages become increasingly pronounced with sequence length: at 2,000 frames, BlazeVGGT achieves approximately 13$\times$ faster inference than VGGT while reducing the Chamfer Distance from 0.41 to 0.32. Extensive experiments demonstrate a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off in point-cloud reconstruction and camera-pose estimation, compatibility with existing VGGT accelerators such as FastVGGT and LiteVGGT, and transferability to VGGT-based models including StreamVGGT and PAGE-4D. Codes are publicly available at https://github.com/MagiciannnY/BlazeVGGT.git.

</details>


### [74] [Sparsity Should Be ALLOTted by Output: Training-Free Allocation of Block-Sparse Attention for Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29441)
*Tianze Xia,Xiaoling Zhou,Ruya Lan,Yiqiao Liao,Chengru Song,Shiming Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALLOT：一种免训练的分层-步骤稀疏注意力分配方法，通过输出误差探测与轨迹校准联合优化各 layer-step 单元的注意力密度，在实测延迟预算下提升视频 DiT 保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全 3D 注意力在视频 Diffusion Transformer 中开销高；块稀疏注意力虽降本，但现有方法忽略稀疏分配优化，或仅依赖启发式规则与局部指标。作者观察到不同层与不同去噪步的稀疏化对最终视频保真度影响差异显著，且最敏感的 layer-step 组合在不同输入间几乎不变。

Method: 将每个 layer-step 对视为一个 cell，通过 (i) cell probing 与 (ii) trajectory calibration 估计每个 cell 对最终视频误差的贡献，然后在实测延迟预算约束下联合优化各 cell 的密度；方法免训练，保留原有选择策略与 kernel，仅替换分配模块。

Result: 在相同或更短的墙钟时间下，ALLOT 在所有对比的分配策略中表现最佳：搭配 XAttention 时在 HunyuanVideo 上取得 11.8 dB PSNR 提升；在 Wan2.1、Wan2.2、HunyuanVideo 的四个已发表流水线共九个设置中均优于或持平默认配置。

Conclusion: 层-步敏感度的输入无关稳定性使全局分配优化可行；输出误差驱动的分配优于启发式与局部指标方法，可作为即插即用组件推广到多种视频生成流水线。

Abstract: Full 3D attention is costly in video Diffusion Transformers. Block-sparse attention reduces this cost by computing only selected attention blocks. However, existing methods either overlook the potential to optimize sparsity allocation or rely on heuristic rules or local metrics to guide it. In this work, we observe that sparsifying attention in different layers and at different denoising steps affects final-video fidelity to substantially different degrees. Yet the identities of the most sensitive layer-step pairs remain nearly unchanged across inputs. Building on these findings, we propose Allocating Layer-step Latency by Output Tests (ALLOT), a training-free sparsity allocation method. Treating each layer-step pair as a cell, ALLOT estimates each cell's contribution to final-video error through (i) cell probing and (ii) trajectory calibration, then jointly optimizes cell densities under a measured-latency budget. It preserves existing selection strategies and kernels, replacing only the allocation component. At equal or shorter wall-clock time, ALLOT outperforms every compared allocation strategy with XAttention, with an 11.8 dB PSNR gain on HunyuanVideo. Extensive experiments across four additional published pipelines on Wan2.1, Wan2.2, and HunyuanVideo show that ALLOT outperforms or ties the default in all nine settings.

</details>


### [75] [LiveAct: Towards Real-Time Multi-Agent World Models with Heterogeneous Action Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29408)
*Xinyu Wang,Huafeng Shi,Yue Ma,Zijie Meng,Teng Xi,Yuzhuo Chen,Yucheng Wang,Ruicheng Zhang,Guoxin Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Xiu Li,Fangneng Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiveAct多智能体视频世界模型，通过异构动作接口(HAI)实现多智能体独立控制与异构控制语义自适应，并借助教师强制与rollout感知分布匹配迁移到分块因果生成器，实现17.70 FPS实时流式生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型普遍假设单一受控智能体与任务专用动作接口，无法处理多智能体联合未来预测及从二值游戏按键到连续机器人指令的异构控制。核心挑战有二：(1) 每个按智能体索引的动作流须独立控制其对应视觉实体；(2) 同一张量维度在不同任务中语义不同（攻击/转向/机器人指令），模型须自适应控制变量的含义。

Method: 以异构动作接口(HAI)为核心，包含两个组件：Agent-Aware Spatial Modulation——用语言驱动的空间门控与智能体专属注意力，将各动作流绑定到目标视觉智能体，实现独立控制；Schema-Guided Action Adaptation——用语言描述每个控制变量，并将其语义token与同步数值调制融合，实现异构控制适配。随后通过教师强制与rollout感知分布匹配，将完整接口迁移至分块因果生成器，实现高效流式生成并保持智能体专属控制与动作语义。

Result: 在覆盖游戏与多机器人操作的六个多智能体数据源上，从视觉质量、动作跟随、智能体归属和延迟四方面评估。游戏与机器人操作任务中，在无智能体位置与身份裁剪条件下，智能体归属准确率分别达0.898与0.942；因果生成器在512×448分辨率下达到17.70 FPS。

Conclusion: LiveAct将多智能体独立控制与异构动作语义解耦为HAI的两个对应组件，成功统一了游戏按键与连续机器人指令等异构控制，并可高效迁移到因果流式生成器，在保持高归属准确率的同时实现实时推理，为多智能体视频世界建模提供了通用范式。

Abstract: Interactive video world models predict how an environment evolves under user actions, but most existing systems assume one controlled agent and one task-specific action interface. We study multi-agent video world modeling, where one model generates a joint visual future for multiple controlled agents while interpreting heterogeneous controls ranging from binary game buttons to continuous robot commands. This task presents two complementary challenges. First, every agent-indexed action stream must control its intended visual entity independently. Second, the model must adapt the meaning of each control variable across different tasks, where the same tensor dimension may denote an attack, steering command, or robot command. We introduce LiveAct, centered on a Heterogeneous Action Interface (HAI) whose two components mirror this decomposition. To support independent control, Agent-Aware Spatial Modulation uses language-driven spatial gates and agent-specific attention to bind each action stream to its intended visual agent. To accommodate heterogeneous controls, Schema-Guided Action Adaptation describes each control variable in language and modulates its semantic token with the synchronized numerical value. We further transfer the complete interface to a blockwise causal generator through teacher forcing and rollout-aware distribution matching, enabling efficient streaming generation while retaining agent-specific control and action semantics. We instantiate the formulation on six multi-agent sources spanning games and multi-robot manipulation, and evaluate visual quality, action following, agent attribution, and latency. Across games and robot manipulation, LiveAct reaches 0.898 and 0.942 agent-attribution accuracy without agent locations or identity crops, and the causal generator attains 17.70 FPS at $512\times 448$.

</details>


### [76] [VideoGen Model 1: Prompt Expansion Versus Formatting in Audiovisual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29407)
*David Grossman,Anton Koenig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在VideoGen Model 1上的受控试点实验中，创意式提示词扩展相比原始提示词提升了整体音视频质量评分；但提升主要来自提示词对齐维度，视觉质量几乎不变，且两名评判者（VA-Judger与Gemini）结论不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究“创意提示词扩展”是否真能提升生成视频的音视频质量，并将其效果与单纯的“格式重排”区分开，从而为提示词工程在视频生成中的应用提供可量化的证据。

Method: 受控试点对比实验：固定生成器（VideoGen Model 1，即MiniMax H3的后训练版本）、随机种子与采样设置，对30条提示词分别构造原始、重排、扩展三种版本；每条片段按五个维度独立评分，取均值形成1–10的整体分数；由VA-Judger与Gemini 3.8 Flash两名评判者评分，并给出95%区间。

Result: 相对仅重排，扩展使整体分提升0.63分（95%区间[+0.11, +1.15]）于VA-Judger；Gemini下仅+0.15（[-0.03, +0.35]），整体变化不确定。提示词对齐分在VA-Judger下+0.97、Gemini下+0.50。音频质量在VA-Judger下亦有提升；视觉质量在两评判者下均变化甚微。

Conclusion: 提示词扩展的收益主要体现在语义覆盖与提示词对齐（以及部分音频维度），而非视觉保真度；且效果量对评判模型敏感，结论具有不确定性，需以更大规模实验与人工评测进一步验证。

Abstract: Creative prompt expansion improved overall audiovisual quality scores over raw prompts in a controlled pilot of VideoGen Model 1, our post-trained version of MiniMax H3. We compared the raw, reformatted, and expanded versions of 30 prompts, holding the generator, seed, and sampling settings fixed. Each clip was rated separately on five dimensions. Their mean formed an overall score on a 1-10 scale. Compared with formatting alone, overall scores increased by 0.63 points (95% interval: [+0.11, +1.15]) under VA-Judger and 0.15 points (95% interval: [-0.03, +0.35]) under Gemini 3.8 Flash, whose overall change remained uncertain. Prompt-alignment scores increased by 0.97 points under VA-Judger and 0.50 under Gemini. VA-Judger also showed higher audio quality; visual-quality ratings changed little under either judge.

</details>


### [77] [AOV-GS: Active Open-Vocabulary Semantic Mapping with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29374)
*Rodion Shkokov,Tatiana Zemskova,Dmitry Yudin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AOV-GS：用冻结的SAM/CLIP引导活跃探索的3D高斯建图系统，实现开放词汇语义地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要同时支持几何导航与开放词汇语义查询的地图，难点在于不依赖人工分割掩码或闭集类别来赋予地图语义。现有几何驱动的主动建图只最大化覆盖、把语义当作轨迹的被动副产品，而主动语义建图又多依赖监督式语义分割。

Method: 在粗探索阶段，利用SAM实例分割得到掩码并经CLIP计算掩码嵌入新颖度，对几何上可行的候选视角重排序，使探索偏向语义信息丰富区域；全流程用冻结的SAM、CLIP提供实例掩码与语言对齐嵌入；离线用采集到的CLIP嵌入拟合轻量场景专用语言场（而非人工掩码），从而在新视角上支持开放词汇文本查询分割。

Result: 在与基线相同的SAM-CLIP感知栈下，语言场平均mIoU相比纯几何主动规划：Replica提升1.3个百分点（八场景34.6%），Matterport3D提升1.8个百分点（五场景23.1%）；使用CorrCLIP时Replica达35.6%，比匹配的Geometry+CorrCLIP基线高2.0个百分点；两个数据集的新视角PSNR与几何基线相当，Replica上深度L1更优。

Conclusion: 将冻结基础模型的语义新颖度信号注入主动规划的视角选择，可在不依赖人工标注的情况下提升开放词汇语义地图质量，且不牺牲重建保真度。

Abstract: Robots deployed in human-centered environments need maps that support both geometric navigation and open-vocabulary semantic queries beyond a fixed label set. A key challenge is enriching such maps with semantic information without relying on manually annotated segmentation masks or closed-set semantic categories. Existing geometry-driven active mapping maximizes coverage but treats semantics as a passive by-product of the collected trajectory, whereas active semantic mapping typically depends on supervised semantic segmentation. We present AOV-GS, an active 3D Gaussian mapping system that guides exploration using frozen foundation models. During coarse exploration, AOV-GS re-ranks geometrically promising candidate views according to CLIP mask-embedding novelty computed from SAM instance segmentation, biasing exploration toward semantically informative regions. Frozen SAM and CLIP provide instance masks and language-aligned embeddings throughout mapping, while a lightweight scene-specific language field is fitted offline directly to the collected CLIP embeddings rather than manually annotated masks, enabling open-vocabulary text-query segmentation on novel views. In a controlled comparison using the same SAM-CLIP perception stack, AOV-GS improves average language-field mIoU over geometry-only active planning by 1.3 percentage points on Replica (34.6% on eight scenes) and by 1.8 points on Matterport3D (23.1% on five scenes). With CorrCLIP, AOV-GS reaches 35.6% on Replica, 2.0 points above the matched Geometry-based + CorrCLIP baseline. Novel-view PSNR remains comparable to the geometry-only baseline on both datasets while depth L1 improves on Replica. Code is available at https://github.com/OptimusFaber/AOV-GS.

</details>


### [78] [Clinical radar heart sounds with echocardiographic phenotypes of ventricular dysfunction and valvular disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29267)
*Jinye Li,Qingchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 发布537段雷达心音与162例超声心动图报告配对的公开数据集，覆盖LVEF降低、室壁运动异常与瓣膜反流三种表型，并用信号-表型分析验证其价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达心音可无接触记录心脏机械活动，超声心动图刻画心室与瓣膜功能，二者配对可建立机械信号与结构性心脏病联系，但此类公开数据稀缺。

Method: 采集162例患者共537段雷达心音，每例配对一份超声心动图报告；提供原始复数信号、预处理心音与时序质量分段；进行技术验证（信号时长保留95.93%、波形重建可重复）；采用患者级五折交叉评估进行信号-表型建模。

Result: 队列含LVEF<50%（38例）、区域性室壁运动异常（43例）、至少中度瓣膜反流（39例）三种重叠表型；LVEF降低的平均外部测试AUROC为0.770±0.138。

Conclusion: 该资源支持将非接触心脏机械信号与超声心动图结构及功能表型关联的研究，并识别出与心室收缩功能障碍相关的机械信息。

Abstract: Radar heart sounds capture cardiac mechanical activity without contact, while echocardiography characterizes ventricular and valvular function. Paired measurements connect these signals to structural heart disease, yet such data remain sparsely represented in public resources. Here we present 537 radar heart-sound recordings paired with one echocardiographic report for each of 162 patients. The cohort covers three overlapping phenotypes: left ventricular ejection fraction (LVEF) below 50%, regional wall-motion abnormality and at least moderate valvular regurgitation, present in 38, 43 and 39 patients, respectively. Original complex signals, processed radar heart sounds and temporal quality segmentation support signal-to-phenotype analysis. Technical validation retains 95.93% of signal duration and confirms reproducible waveform reconstruction. Patient-level five-fold evaluation achieves a mean outer-test AUROC of $0.770\pm0.138$ for reduced LVEF, identifying mechanical information associated with ventricular systolic dysfunction. This resource supports research linking non-contact cardiac mechanical signals to echocardiographic structural and functional phenotypes.

</details>


### [79] [Think-Capture: Active 3D Geometry Acquisition for Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29232)
*Junjie Wang,Zhihao Yuan,Yunhan Tang,Sheng Xu,Jiaqi Mao,Xiaoyu Ye,Yueru Luo,Xu Yan,Chun-Mei Feng,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Think-Capture，一个查询条件驱动的框架：先定位任务关键物体，再按需获取其几何信息，并用分层GRPO训练VLM学会何时依赖工具证据、何时回退到视觉线索，从而缓解稀疏视角下的3D空间幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM擅长2D识别，但在稀疏视角下难以维持一致的3D空间关系，在遮挡与视角变化时产生空间幻觉。已有方法通常用稠密几何融合所有帧，带来巨大计算与上下文开销，并导致模型盲目依赖不完美的几何估计器。

Method: 1）查询条件化流程：先识别任务关键物体，再按需获取其几何；2）3D工具箱将零样本掩码提升到统一坐标系；3）符号化3D状态抽象（Symbolic 3D State Abstraction）把物体与相机状态压缩为结构化文本；4）分层组相对策略优化（Hierarchical GRPO）在"几何有帮助"与"几何误导"两类样本上训练VLM，使其学会何时采用工具证据、何时回退视觉线索。

Result: Think-Capture-4B在MindCube-Tiny达54.4%（较骨干+21.5），MMSI-Bench位置子集39.1%（+7.9），ViewSpatial 42.8%（+5.2）；在坐标与掩码扰动下保持鲁棒；完整系统可装入单张24GB GPU，并作为异步高层推理器部署于Agibot G1。

Conclusion: 按需获取几何配合符号化状态抽象与分层强化学习，可在低计算与上下文开销下显著提升VLM的3D空间推理一致性与鲁棒性，并具备实际机器人平台的可部署性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at 2D recognition but often fail to maintain consistent 3D relations across sparse viewpoints, leading to spatial hallucinations under occlusion and perspective changes. Existing methods commonly fuse all frames with dense geometry, incurring substantial computation and context overhead while encouraging blind reliance on imperfect estimators. We present Think-Capture, a query-conditioned framework that first identifies task-critical objects and then acquires their geometry on demand. A 3D toolkit lifts zero-shot masks into a common coordinate frame, and a Symbolic 3D State Abstraction compresses object and camera states into structured text. Hierarchical Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) trains the VLM on cases in which geometry either helps or misleads, teaching it when to use tool evidence and when to fall back on visual cues. Think-Capture-4B reaches 54.4% on MindCube-Tiny (+21.5 points over its backbone), 39.1% on the MMSI-Bench positional subset (+7.9), and 42.8% on ViewSpatial (+5.2). It also remains robust under coordinate and mask perturbations. The full stack fits one 24 GB GPU and serves as an asynchronous high-level reasoner on an Agibot G1. Our code is available at https://github.com/819346675/Think-Capture.

</details>


### [80] [RiO-DETRv2: Geometry-Aware Uncertainty Modeling Meets Real-Time Oriented Object Detection Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29159)
*Zhangchi Hu,Zilong He,Yifan Zhao,Xiangyu Yu,Jie Chen,Peixi Wu,Yansong Peng,Hebei Li,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RiO-DETRv2 提出几何感知的不确定性建模框架，通过对象对齐边界分布细化、循环到局部角度残差细化以及统一的 OBB 质量对齐学习，在几乎不增加计算开销的前提下提升实时旋转目标检测的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RiO-DETR 是首个实时端到端旋转目标检测 Transformer，但进一步提升定位精度需要更强的框表示。分布回归在水平 DETR 中已获成功，但直接扩展到旋转检测会带来两个问题：几何失配的边界建模，以及拓扑不一致的角度细化。

Method: 1) Object-Aligned Boundary Distribution Refinement：在对象对齐坐标系中将框边界表示为概率分布，并在解码器层间逐步细化，解决几何失配；2) Circular-to-Local Angular Residual Refinement：从全局周期搜索过渡到局部精细估计，提升角度拓扑一致性；3) Unified OBB Quality-Aligned Learning：使分类、边界与角度细化同完整 OBB 定位质量对齐。

Result: RiO-DETRv2-l/x 在 DOTA 上达到 53.7/54.1 mAP50-95，较 RiO-DETR-l/x 分别提升 1.2/1.5 个点，计算开销几乎不变。

Conclusion: 该框架在不牺牲实时性的前提下显著改善定位精度，实现了速度与精度更好的平衡，体现了分布回归与几何感知不确定性建模在实时旋转目标检测中的有效性。

Abstract: RiO-DETR is the first real-time end-to-end transformer for oriented object detection, yet further localization gains require a more expressive box representation. Although distributional regression has succeeded in horizontal DETRs, extending it to oriented detection introduces geometry-misaligned boundary modeling and topology-inconsistent orientation refinement. We present RiO-DETRv2, a geometry-aware uncertainty modeling framework for real-time oriented object detection. To resolve the geometry mismatch, Object-Aligned Boundary Distribution Refinement represents box boundaries as probability distributions in an object-aligned coordinate system and progressively refines them across decoder layers. For better orientation topology consistency, we introduce Circular-to-Local Angular Residual Refinement that transitions from global periodic search to local fine-grained estimation. Unified OBB Quality-Aligned Learning further aligns classification, boundary and angular refinement with complete-OBB localization quality. Together, these designs improve localization without compromising efficiency, achieving a better balance between speed and accuracy. Specifically, RiO-DETRv2-l/x achieves 53.7/54.1 mAP50-95 on DOTA, outperforming RiO-DETR-l/x by 1.2/1.5 points with nearly unchanged computational cost. Code is available at https://github.com/RicePasteM/RiO-DETRv2.

</details>


### [81] [Two for One: Privileged Learning for Resource-Constrained Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29126)
*Lisa Y. W. Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出特权学习（privileged learning）框架：教师模型在训练时使用双眼（bilateral）CFP图像，学生模型仅用单眼图像输入并借助教师迁移知识，推理阶段无需对侧图像，在AMD与DR检测上AUC一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统常在数据丰富条件下开发，但部署时信息不完整。CFP中常规采集双眼以获得互补信息，但双侧采集并非总可行；已有方法要么在单一模型中融合双眼，要么用知识蒸馏把双眼教师压缩为学生，但后者推理时仍依赖双眼。

Method: 构建特权学习框架：教师模型训练时可见双侧CFP图像，学习由双眼互补信息所塑造的表征；学生模型仅以单眼图像为输入，通过知识迁移从双眼教师中学习。推理时学生仅使用单眼输入，不访问对侧图像。

Result: 与常规单眼训练相比，AUC 一致提升；左眼图像增益最大：AMD 检测 0.931→0.966，DR 检测 0.789→0.836，且推理仅需单眼图像。

Conclusion: 训练期利用双眼信息、推理期仅需单眼图像，使模型在资源受限的临床与筛查环境中具备更可靠的部署潜力。

Abstract: Machine learning systems are often developed under data-rich conditions but deployed when only incomplete information is available. A natural example arises in colour fundus photography (CFP), where both eyes are commonly imaged because they can provide complementary information for ocular disease assessment. Prior approaches that exploit bilateral information either fuse both eyes in a single model or use knowledge distillation to compress a dual-eye teacher into a lightweight student that still requires both images at test time. However, bilateral acquisition may not always be feasible. We thus propose a privileged learning framework in which a teacher model has access to bilateral CFP images during training and learns representations informed by information from both eyes, while the student is trained to transfer this knowledge from the bilateral teacher using only a single-eye image as input. At inference, the student operates exclusively on this single-eye input and does not require the contralateral image. Experimental results show consistent improvements in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve over conventional monocular training, with the largest gains observed for left-eye images for the detection of age-related macular degeneration (0.931 vs. 0.966) and diabetic retinopathy (0.789 vs. 0.836).%, while requiring only a single-eye image at inference. By enabling models to benefit from bilateral information during training while requiring only a single-eye image at inference, our approach may support more reliable deployment in resource-constrained clinical and screening environments.

</details>


### [82] [Open-Vocabulary Models and Label Propagation for Uncertainty-aware Pseudo-Labeling of Object Detection Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29788)
*Jonathan Klees,Timo Heller,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出半监督不确定性感知标注框架，利用开放词汇检测器生成区域提议与对象表示，构建对象级相似图传播概率标签，仅用1%标注成本在COCO上达到40.5% AP（全手工标注为40.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测依赖大规模人工标注，而软标签需对每个目标多次标注，成本更高。作者希望大幅降低不确定性感知标注的成本。

Method: 半监督框架：用开放词汇检测器生成区域提议和语义对象表示；基于表示构建对象级相似图并传播概率标签信息；为未标注对象分配软标签，同时量化偶然不确定性与认知不确定性。

Result: 在4个带软标签的检测基准上，仅用少量人工标注对象即可获得高质量标注；半自动标注训练的检测器性能与全手工标注相当；COCO半监督检测达40.5% AP（全监督40.9%），标注成本仅1%，为SOTA。

Conclusion: 开放词汇模型与图标签传播结合，可实现高效、准确且不确定性感知的伪标签生成，显著降低软标签标注成本。

Abstract: Object detection models rely on large-scale annotated datasets that require substantial manual annotation effort. The labeling costs further increase when annotation uncertainty is captured through soft labels, as multiple annotations must be collected for each object. We address this challenge by introducing a semi-supervised framework for the uncertainty-aware annotation of object detection datasets. Our approach leverages open-vocabulary object detectors to generate region proposals and semantically meaningful object representations. Based on these representations, we construct an object-level similarity graph and propagate probabilistic label information. Our framework assigns soft labels to previously unlabeled objects, quantifying both aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty. We evaluate our method on four object detection benchmarks with soft-label annotations and demonstrate that high-quality annotations can be obtained with only a small fraction of manually labeled objects. Furthermore, we show that detectors trained on semi-automatically annotated data achieve performance comparable to models trained on fully manually annotated data. We achieve state-of-the-art downstream detection performance for the task of semi-supervised object detection on the COCO dataset, with 40.5% AP virtually matching the performance of the model trained on fully manually constructed data (40.9%) at only 1% of labeling costs. These results highlight the potential of open-vocabulary models and graph-based label propagation for efficient, accurate and uncertainty-aware pseudo-labeling.

</details>


### [83] [MS2CAST: A Video Anomaly Detection Framework via Multi-Scale Spatial Cross-Attention and Efficient Spatiotemporal Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29649)
*Ali Safaei,Mohammad Kazemi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MS2CAST：一种端到端半监督预测式视频异常检测框架，用多尺度交叉注意力编码器融合ViT与CNN特征，并以潜空间时空模块替代光流，取得有竞争力AUC且显存占用低于1GB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频异常检测面临异常事件稀缺且多样的问题；现有半监督方法依赖计算昂贵的光流网络建模运动，且CNN感受野高度局部化，限制空间表征能力。

Method: 构建端到端半监督预测式VAD框架：空间编码采用多尺度交叉注意力编码器，融合MViTv2-S（ViT）与ConvNeXt-T（CNN）的层级特征以结合不同感受野；在潜空间引入两个可互换时空模块（含Swin Transformer的SwinLSTM、含Vision Mamba块的VMRNN）以避免光流估计；目标函数结合L1损失、梯度差分损失GDL与多尺度结构相似性MS-SSIM。

Result: 在UCSD Ped2、CUHK Avenue、ShanghaiTech数据集上，帧级ROC-AUC与顶尖方法相当，同时峰值GPU显存占用低于1GB。

Conclusion: 多尺度跨注意力特征融合与潜空间时空建模可在不依赖光流的前提下实现高效、准确的视频异常检测，兼顾性能与资源效率。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) is a challenging task for surveillance systems due to the scarcity and diversity of anomalous events. While semi-supervised VAD approaches have advanced the field, many rely on computationally intensive optical flow networks to capture motion dynamics. Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are also widely used for spatial encoding, but their effective receptive field is highly local. To address these limitations, we introduce MS2CAST, an end-to-end semi-supervised predictive VAD framework. For spatial encoding, we employ a multi-scale cross-attention encoder that fuses the hierarchical feature representations from a vision transformer (MViTv2-S) and a CNN (ConvNeXt-T) to exploit their distinct receptive fields. To model spatiotemporal dynamics without optical flow estimation, we integrate two interchangeable spatiotemporal modules in the latent space. One module (SwinLSTM) incorporates a Swin Transformer, while the other (VMRNN) integrates Vision Mamba blocks. Our objective function combines L1 loss, Gradient Difference Loss (GDL), and Multi-Scale Structural Similarity Index Measure (MS-SSIM) to ensure pixel-wise accuracy, sharpness, and structural integrity. Evaluations across the UCSD Ped2, CUHK Avenue, and ShanghaiTech datasets demonstrate frame-level Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) Area Under the Curve (AUC) scores that are competitive with top-tier techniques while maintaining a peak GPU memory usage of under 1 GB.

</details>


### [84] [MINER: Modeling Inter- and Intra-Person Variance for Reidentification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28941)
*Oron Nir,Eliyahu Strugo,Ariel Shamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MINER：一种模型无关的换衣行人重识别框架，通过难样本对比抑制服装偏置并自适应平衡类内/类间方差，在四个数据集上达到SOTA，并在TVRID RGB-Depth挑战赛三个赛道中位居前列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换衣行人重识别（CC-ReID）中服装变化导致外观剧变。多数已有方法依赖分割掩码等生物特征信号提取启发式来增强类间方差，却忽视了对类内方差的控制，使服装引起的类内差异不受约束。

Method: 提出MINER，一个简单且模型无关的框架：通过难样本（hard examples）对比来抑制服装偏置，并依据数据集特性自适应地平衡类内方差与类间方差，不依赖特定骨干网络或分割先验。

Result: 大量实验表明MINER在多个CC-ReID基准上提升检索性能，在四个数据集上取得SOTA；在开放TVRID RGB-Depth ReID挑战赛的三个赛道中均位列领先梯队。

Conclusion: 相较于侧重类间方差的启发式方案，显式控制类内方差并结合难样本对比是应对换衣外观变化的有效途径；MINER的模型无关性使其具备良好的通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Clothes-Changing Person Re-Identification (CC-ReID) remains challenging due to drastic appearance shifts across outfits. Most prior works devise biometric signal-extraction heuristics to enhance inter-person variance, e.g., using segmentation masks, while leaving intra-person variance uncontrolled. We present MINER, a simple model-agnostic framework that suppresses garment bias by contrasting hard examples and adaptively balancing dataset-dependent intra- and inter-person variance. Extensive experiments show that MINER improves retrieval across CC-ReID benchmarks, achieving state-of-the-art performance on four datasets and ranking among the leaders in all three tracks of the open TVRID RGB-Depth ReID challenge.

</details>


### [85] [Register Anything Model for Generalizable and Robust Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28821)
*Zheng Qin,Junhua Xi,Yan Huang,Jiahong Lai,Anwen Huang,Qiong Li,Guangda Zhang,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一模型RAM，通过几何感知归一化与高效几何Transformer实现跨域泛化的3D点云配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有配准方法通常针对单一域定制架构与超参数，迁移到新域时性能显著下降；而跨域统一训练的核心障碍在于域间严重的几何差异（场景尺度、传感器噪声、点密度、结构模式），难以用统一的点基模型处理。

Method: 在室内、室外、物体多域数据上训练单一模型。引入几何感知归一化策略，联合体素化与归一化，依据点云几何属性自适应地将点云映射到标准化几何空间，在统一分辨率的同时保留显著局部结构。基于该归一化，RAM采用层次化由粗到细范式，并使用高效几何Transformer，仅建模局部几何一致性而非全局所有点。

Result: 在12个基准上取得最优的inlier ratio与配准召回率；并优于各单域单独训练的对应方法，展现出更强的泛化能力。该设计还缓解了体素化带来的离散化噪声并提升计算效率。

Conclusion: 几何感知归一化是弥合跨域几何差异、实现统一点云配准模型的关键；局部几何一致性建模在精度与效率间取得良好平衡，验证了统一多域训练的可行性与优越泛化性。

Abstract: We present Register Anything Model (RAM), a unified model for generalizable 3D point cloud registration that robustly estimates 6-DoF transformations across diverse domains spanning sensors and environments. Existing registration methods are typically tailored to a single domain, with carefully tuned architectures and hyperparameters, and thus degrade markedly when applied to novel domains. We bridge this gap by training a single model on diverse data from indoor, outdoor, and object domains. A central challenge is the severe geometric discrepancy across domains, i.e., differences in scene scale, sensor noise, point density, and structural patterns, which hinders processing with a unified point-based model. To address this, we introduce a geometry-aware normalization strategy that jointly voxelizes and normalizes point clouds into a standardized geometric space, adaptively based on their geometric properties to preserve salient local structures while standardizing resolution. Built on this normalization, RAM follows a hierarchical coarse-to-fine paradigm, and employs an efficient geometric transformer that models local geometric consistency instead of globally over all points. This design mitigates discretization noise introduced by voxelization, and improves computational efficiency. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in terms of inlier ratio and registration recall. Notably, our method outperforms the counterparts trained on each separate domain, showing superior generalization capability.

</details>


### [86] [Different Defects, Different Effects: A Controlled Audit of Face Recognition Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28766)
*Ana Dias,João Ribeiro Pinto,Hugo Proença,João C. Neves*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统审计MS1MV3人脸识别训练集中的数据缺陷，将其归为五类（精确/近似重复、身份切分、裁剪错误、多人脸歧义、无效内容），并在IResNet-50+ArcFace受控协议下评估修复效果，发现定向清洗可保持甚至提升识别性能且训练收敛更快的优化步数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别依赖大规模数据集（如MS-Celeb-1M），但数据规模大、异质性强导致标注错误与冗余样本。虽已有MS1MV2、MS1MV3等精炼版本，作者发现MS1MV3中仍残留多类训练数据缺陷，而数据质量因素在大规模人脸识别中相对欠研究。

Method: 提出互补的数据审计策略以识别五类缺陷：精确与近似重复图像、身份切分（split identities）、裁剪错误、多人脸歧义、无效内容；随后在基于IResNet-50与ArcFace的受控人脸识别训练协议下，对各类缺陷修复的效果进行对比评估。

Result: 定向干预可保持、部分情况下提升识别性能，同时以更少的优化步数达到原始训练集的性能水平。去重带来最显著的总体收益；身份切分合并与无效内容移除也提升训练效率。

Conclusion: 数据集清洗的效果高度依赖于所针对的缺陷类型；训练数据质量仍是大规模人脸识别中重要且相对被忽视的因素。训练清单与代码将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: The development of face recognition has been strongly supported by the availability of large-scale training datasets. Among them, MS-Celeb-1M has played an important role by providing millions of facial images from nearly 100K identities. However, the size and heterogeneity of such datasets increase the likelihood of annotation errors, redundant samples, and other forms of data inconsistency. This motivated the introduction of refined versions, such as MS1MV2 and MS1MV3, aimed at improving the quality of the training data. Despite these cleaning efforts, we show that several types of training-data defects remain in MS1MV3. By proposing complementary data-auditing strategies, we identify five defect categories: exact and near-duplicate images, split identities, crop errors, multiple-face ambiguity, and invalid content. We then assess the effect of correcting these defects under a controlled face-recognition training protocol based on IResNet-50 and ArcFace. The results show that targeted interventions can preserve, and in some cases improve, recognition performance while requiring fewer optimization steps to reach the performance of the original training set. Duplicate removal provides the clearest overall benefit, while split-identity merging and invalid-content removal also improve training efficiency. Overall, the results indicate that the effect of dataset cleaning depends strongly on the type of defect being addressed and provide further evidence that training-data quality remains an important and comparatively underexplored factor in large-scale face recognition. The training manifests and code used in this work will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [87] [OpenMAP-HIE: Pathology Informed Score-based Diffusion Model for Unsupervised Neonatal Hypoxic-Ischemic Encephalopathy Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29322)
*Pengyu Kan,Craig Jones,Kenichi Oishi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 OpenMAP-HIE：基于病理先验的 score-based 扩散模型，在无体素级标注下实现新生儿 HIE 病灶无监督定位，ADC-only 取得 Dice 0.283，Z-map 细化后提升至 0.602。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿 HIE 病灶在 ADC 图像上的精确定位具有临床意义，但受限于专家标注稀缺及病灶表现变异大，难以依赖有监督学习。

Method: 训练去噪扩散模型学习健康新生儿 ADC 分布，并用 ControlNet 条件分支保留患者特异性解剖结构；推理时直接比较条件与非条件噪声预测之差，在独立评估的多个扩散噪声水平上提取异常证据，不依赖最终伪健康重建或顺序反向扩散链；依据急性 HIE 的 ADC 降低特征仅保留符合病理方向的差异，用几何平均跨噪声水平聚合，并以空间均值滤波抑制孤立响应；可选群体 ADC Z-map 细化。

Result: 在 BONBID-HIE 数据集上，ADC-only 方法 Dice 0.283±0.244、AP 0.505、HD 25.45±15.63 mm、MASD 9.40±8.49 mm、NSD 0.355±0.236，在所评估的无监督方法中重叠与表面距离表现最佳，Dice 与有监督 ADC-only 模型相当；Z-map 细化后 Dice 提升至 0.602±0.269。

Conclusion: 病理知情（pathology-informed）的 score 差异可作为新生儿 HIE 病灶定位的有效信号，但小病灶与低对比病灶的检测仍是待解决挑战。

Abstract: Accurate localization of neonatal hypoxic--ischemic encephalopathy (HIE) lesions on apparent diffusion coefficient (ADC) images is clinically important but is limited by scarce expert annotations and substantial variation in lesion appearance. We propose OpenMAP-HIE, a pathology-informed score-based diffusion model for unsupervised HIE lesion localization without voxel-wise lesion supervision. A denoising diffusion model learns the healthy neonatal ADC distribution, while a ControlNet-based conditional branch preserves patient-specific anatomy. At inference, anomaly evidence is derived directly from differences between conditional and unconditioned noise predictions at independently evaluated diffusion noise levels, avoiding dependence on a final pseudo-healthy reconstruction or sequential reverse-diffusion chain. Based on the characteristic ADC reduction associated with acute HIE, only discrepancies consistent with the expected pathological direction are retained and aggregated across noise levels using a geometric mean. Spatial mean filtering further suppresses isolated responses. The framework was evaluated on the BONBID-HIE dataset. The ADC-only method achieved a Dice score of $0.283\pm0.244$, average precision of $0.505$, Hausdorff distance of $25.45\pm15.63$ mm, mean average surface distance of $9.40\pm8.49$ mm, and normalized surface distance of $0.355\pm0.236$, providing the best overlap and surface-distance performance among the evaluated unsupervised methods and Dice performance comparable to supervised ADC-only models. Optional population-derived ADC $Z$-map refinement further increased Dice to $0.602\pm0.269$. These findings support pathology-informed score discrepancies as a useful signal for neonatal HIE lesion localization, while highlighting remaining challenges in detecting small and low-contrast lesions.

</details>


### [88] [CRAB: Cuboid-based Representation for As-built BIM from Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29257)
*Haoran Xiong,Jing Wang,Shanshan Pan,Zihao Yan,Minglun Gong,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRAB：以“建筑立方体”为原语，从点云重建可直接编辑的BIM构件（墙、门、窗、梁、柱），结合ProposalNet与关系感知的Flow Matching精化网络AssemblyNet，实现结构关系预测与自动/人机协同修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有scan-to-BIM流程依赖手工规则、启发式优化或人工修正；学习型方法难以恢复墙厚、洞口几何与构件级BIM结构，输出多为2D布局或平面抽象，不满足语义化、体量化、参数化、可编辑的BIM需求。

Method: 提出CRAB框架，将构件编码为具IFC兼容语义与参数的紧凑立方体；ProposalNet预测粗粒度构件立方体，AssemblyNet（关系感知Flow Matching）精化几何、预测结构关系并支持自动结构修正与人工引导编辑；为缓解立方体级监督不足，扩展Structured3D的构件级立方体标注并构建基于立方体的程序化场景。

Result: 在构件完整性、几何精度与泛化性上优于平面图及平面基方法；内墙厚度估计平均误差2.80 cm，79.50%的匹配墙体误差在3 cm以内。

Conclusion: 以立方体为中心的表征可弥合点云与可编辑BIM之间的差距，兼顾参数化输出与结构一致性，并支持交互式编辑，为BIM-ready重建提供有效范式。

Abstract: As-built BIM reconstruction from point clouds demands semantic, volumetric, parametric, and editable building components, beyond 2D layouts or planar abstractions. Yet existing scan-to-BIM pipelines often rely on handcrafted rules, heuristic optimization, or manual correction, while learning-based methods fall short of recovering wall thickness, opening geometry, and component-level BIM structure. We introduce CRAB, a BIM-ready cuboid reconstruction framework that encodes walls, doors, windows, beams, and columns as compact architectural cuboids with IFC-compatible semantics and parameters. CRAB bridges point clouds and editable BIM by combining ProposalNet, which predicts coarse component cuboids, with AssemblyNet, a relation-aware Flow Matching model that refines geometry, predicts structural relationships, and enables automatic structural correction and human-guided editing. To address limited cuboid-level supervision, we extend Structured3D with component-wise cuboid annotations and construct cuboid-based procedural scenes. Experiments show CRAB surpasses floor plan and plane-based alternatives in component completeness, geometric accuracy, and generalization. For interior wall thickness estimation, CRAB achieves a mean error of 2.80 cm, with 79.50% of matched walls within 3 cm. The project page is available at https://vcc.tech/research/2026/CRAB.

</details>


### [89] [ER-Chord: Spatially Consistent One-Step Image Editing via Equal-Energy Reliability Redistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29248)
*Peize Li,Hanzhen Zhao,Xingguo Xu,Yilong Fan,Zhen Tao,Bo Yin,Zhifei Liu,Qingdong He,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、免反演的 ER-Chord 框架，利用相邻噪声层级编辑场的一致性作为局部可靠性信号，通过等能量优化实现编辑控制能量的空间重分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步生成模型实现快速文本引导图像编辑，但会对本应保持不变的区域引入非预期改动；现有时间聚合方法虽在噪声层级上稳定编辑场，却使空间分配对局部可靠性变化不敏感，故时间稳定性不能保证空间一致性。

Method: 将编辑强度分配建模为等能量优化：依据局部可靠性重分配控制能量，同时保持原始编辑方向与总控制能量不变；该形式具有唯一全局解，可直接复用已有场评估高效计算，无需额外去噪器调用。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 与 TEdBench 两个基准上优于最先进的一步编辑基线，在多个生成骨干上实现更优的源图像保持性并取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 局部可靠性引导的空间重分配可弥补时间聚合的不足，在免训练、免反演条件下显著提升一步编辑的空间一致性与源保持能力。

Abstract: One-step generative models enable fast text-guided image editing, yet they often introduce unintended changes to regions that should remain unchanged. Temporal aggregation stabilizes editing fields across noise levels, but leaves their spatial allocation insensitive to local variations in reliability. Consequently, temporal stability alone does not ensure spatially consistent editing. To address this lim- itation, we propose ER-Chord, a training-free and inversion-free framework for reliability-guided spatial redistribution. Our key insight is that agreement between editing fields at neighboring noise levels provides a local reliability signal for allocating editing strength. We formulate this allocation as an equal-energy op- timization that redistributes control energy according to local reliability while preserving the original editing directions and total control energy. This formu- lation admits a unique global solution that can be efficiently computed using existing field evaluations, requiring no additional denoiser calls. Experiments on two benchmarks, PIE-Bench and TEdBench, demonstrate improvements over state-of-the-art one-step editing baselines, achieving superior source preservation alongside consistent gains across multiple generative backbones.The project page is at https://github.com/Peizeli1/ER-Chord.

</details>


### [90] [DetM-Tracker: Deterministic and Memory-Efficient VIS for Autonomous Industrial Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29212)
*You-Rui Lin,Jen-Chieh Wu,Tzu-Ti Wei,Jen-Jee Chen,Yu-Chee Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DetM-Tracker，一个面向自主工业巡检的轻量视频实例分割（VIS）框架，通过三个协同组件消除架构冗余，在边缘平台上实现确定性实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业巡检需在严格内存与算力约束下进行确定性实时推理，但现有 VIS 模型存在内存墙：稠密时序记忆随空间分辨率与序列长度增长，固定容量解码器无法利用工业场景的稀疏性，导致在资源受限边缘平台难以部署。

Method: 三个协同组件：(1) Compact Scalar Memory，将高维实例特征压缩为四标量描述子，把时序记忆复杂度从 O(T·N·R) 降至 O(T·N̂)，将帧级表示压缩为活跃实例库（R 为分辨率，N/N̂ 为全局/窗口实例数，T 为时序窗口长度）；(2) Dynamic Instance Channels，按可见目标实际数量自适应分配查询容量；(3) Entropy-Gated Skip Connection，将算力集中于高不确定性空间区域，单次前向即可细化目标边界，无需辅助细化头。

Result: 相比最先进的稠密特征基线，推理延迟降低约 20 倍，显存（VRAM）消耗降低 6 倍。

Conclusion: DetM-Tracker 弥合了现代 VIS 模型与工业边缘部署实际需求之间的差距，适配视场受限下外观高度相似目标的“微”视场工业场景。

Abstract: Autonomous industrial inspection requires strictly deterministic real-time inference under tight memory and computational constraints. However, existing Video Instance Segmentation (VIS) models suffer from prohibitive scaling: dense temporal memory grows with spatial resolution and sequence length, while fixed-capacity decoders fail to exploit the inherent sparsity of industrial scenes, creating a practical memory wall on resource-constrained edge platforms. We present DetM-Tracker, a streamlined VIS framework for autonomous industrial inspection, where target objects often exhibit highly similar appearance under constrained "micro" fields of view. DetM-Tracker eliminates architectural redundancy through three synergistic components: (1) Compact Scalar Memory, which condenses high-dimensional instance features into four-scalar descriptors, reducing temporal memory complexity from $O(T \cdot N \cdot R)$ to $O(T \cdot \hat{N})$. This mechanism effectively compresses frame-level representations into a bank of active instances, where $R$ denotes resolution, $N$ and $\hat{N}$ represent global and window-specific instance counts, respectively and $T$ the temporal window size; and (2) Dynamic Instance Channels, which adaptively allocate query capacity according to the actual number of visible objects; and (3) Entropy-Gated Skip Connection, which concentrates computational budget on high-uncertainty spatial regions to refine object boundaries in a single forward pass without auxiliary refinement heads. Our approach effectively bridges the gap between modern VIS models and the practical demands of industrial edge deployment, reducing inference latency by approximately 20$\times$ and VRAM consumption by 6$\times$ compared to state-of-the-art dense-feature baselines.

</details>


### [91] [ROCOCO: Robust Outline Completion of Occluded Curvilinear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29177)
*Ken Zheng,Jaimyn Drake*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ROCOCO，一个基于flow matching的生成模型，用UNet掩码预测器实现无先验遮挡掩码条件下的曲线特征补全，性能显著优于现有inpainting基线且参数量更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境障碍物遮挡导致曲线特征（如道路、河流等）不完整，现有方法通常依赖先验的遮挡掩码（privileged information），实际应用中难以获得，因此需要一种仅凭遮挡观测即可自动识别补全区域的方案。

Method: 采用flow matching框架实现遮挡区域曲线特征的inpainting；额外训练一个UNet-based掩码预测模型，从遮挡观测中自动识别需补全区域，从而摆脱对先验遮挡掩码的依赖；使用程序化生成的测试用例验证各组件，并在大型公共曲线对象数据集上验证完整流程。

Result: 在无掩码条件下ROCOCO大幅领先现成inpainting基线；在有掩码监督条件下与基线相当——全图误差最优，遮挡区域内仅次于LaMa；补全器仅7.7M参数，远小于LaMa的51M和Stable Diffusion的860M。

Conclusion: ROCOCO以轻量化模型实现了无需先验遮挡掩码的曲线特征补全，在精度与效率间取得良好平衡，验证了flow matching+自动掩码预测这一pipeline的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a learning-based generative model for completing curvilinear features under occlusion by environmental obstacles. To this end, we adopt a flow matching-based approach that supports the inpainting of occluded regions with the appropriate curvilinear features. To eliminate the reliance on privileged information about the occlusion a priori, we also train a UNet-based mask prediction model, which can identify regions for inpainting given only the occluded observation. The components of our approach are validated via experiments using procedurally-generated test cases, and the complete pipeline is validated using large public datasets for curvilinear objects. Against off-the-shelf inpainting baselines, ROCOCO is the strongest method by a wide margin when no occlusion mask is given, and is competitive with mask-supervised inpainting when one is, achieving best on full-image error and second only to LaMa on the occluded region, while using a completer of $7.7$M parameters against LaMa's $51$M and Stable Diffusion's $860$M.

</details>


### [92] [WaFIR: Wavelet-Fourier Mixture-of-Experts with Subband-Adaptive Routing for Blind All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29108)
*Trong Nhan Nguyen,Vu Hoang Minh Doan,Thanh Dat Le,Truong Tien Vo,Thi Thu Ha Vu,Tan Hung Vo,Jaeyeop Choi,Junghwan Oh,Truong-Son Hy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WaFIR解码器模块，将特征分解为Haar子带，用紧凑傅里叶描述子表征各子带并路由至提示条件化的谱调制专家，实现子带级频率自适应，在盲一体化图像复原中平均PSNR较PromptIR提升0.48 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲一体化图像复原需在无任务标签的测试条件下，用共享模型处理异构甚至混合退化。现有提示学习多为图像级频率条件化，缺乏频率特异性处理与可解释性。

Method: WaFIR解码模块：对特征做Haar小波分解得到子带；以紧凑傅里叶描述子归纳每个子带；据描述子将子带路由至提示条件化的谱调制专家，从而在子带级做路由决策，并保留骨干网络已学习的任务上下文。配以去噪、去雨、去雾的统一训练与评测协议，并设置匹配容量与路由消融控制。

Result: 三任务平均PSNR较PromptIR提升0.48 dB，较Up-Restorer提升0.38 dB；匹配容量与路由控制实验表明增益并非仅由参数增加带来；在未见噪声强度上，基于描述子的路由比离散标签条件化方案更鲁棒，在复合退化下性能相当；路由分析显示专家使用随退化类型呈稳定的子带依赖性。

Conclusion: 子带频率结构可作为提示条件化的可解释补充，用于盲图像复原。

Abstract: Blind all-in-one image restoration must adapt a shared model to heterogeneous and sometimes mixed degradations without a task label at test time. We introduce WaFIR, a decoder module that decomposes features into Haar subbands, summarizes each subband with a compact Fourier descriptor, and routes it to prompt-conditioned spectral modulation experts. Unlike image-level frequency conditioning, WaFIR makes routing decisions at the subband level, enabling frequency-specific processing while retaining the task context learned by the backbone. Under a unified training and evaluation protocol for denoising, deraining, and dehazing, WaFIR improves average PSNR over the three tasks by 0.48 dB over the PromptIR baseline and by 0.38 dB over the recent Up-Restorer. Matched-capacity and routing controls show that the gain is not explained by additional parameters alone. On unseen noise intensities, descriptor-based routing is more robust than a discrete label-conditioned alternative and remains on par under compound degradations, while routing analyses show stable subband-dependent expert use across degradation types. These results support subband frequency structure as an interpretable complement to prompt-based conditioning for blind image restoration. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/WaFIR/README.md

</details>


### [93] [PhyMo: Learning Physical Dynamics with Accurate and Continuous Motion from Multi-View Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29107)
*Shangjia Liu,Jinxi Li,Siyuan Zhou,Bo Yang,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyMo从多视角视频中学习动态场景的演化：提出运动感知轨迹表示与连续运动演化模块，抑制“外观捷径”，实现几何忠实重建与连续运动外推，在四个动态场景基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠渲染监督时，模型可用颜色/不透明度变化来补偿不准确的形变，从而拟合观测图像而非恢复真实几何与运动；该问题在快速或局部复杂运动场景尤为严重，会在观测帧间产生伪合理图像，但重建不可靠、未来预测不连续。因此需要从形变中显式恢复运动，而非依赖外观捷径。

Method: 提出PhyMo框架：1）运动感知轨迹表示（Motion-Aware Trajectory Representation），将运动与形变解耦，驱动模型从形变中恢复准确运动；2）连续运动演化模块（Continuous Motion Evolution），配合双向运动学连续性约束，促进位移、速度、加速度在时间上的连续一致演化。

Result: 在四个动态场景基准上取得state-of-the-art性能，实现几何忠实的插值与连续的运动外推。

Conclusion: 显式建模运动轨迹并施加运动学连续性约束，可有效缓解外观-形变歧义，提升动态多视角重建的几何可信度与未来运动预测的连续性。

Abstract: Recovering dynamic 3D scene geometry, appearance, and the physical motion states that govern scene evolution from multi-view videos is important yet challenging. A key difficulty is that appearance can be deceptive. Under rendering supervision alone, a model may fit the observed images by changing color or opacity to compensate for inaccurate deformation, rather than recovering the true evolving geometry and motion. This issue is especially severe in scenes with fast or locally complex motion, where inaccurate trajectories can still produce plausible images over the observed frames, but lead to unreliable reconstruction and discontinuous future prediction. In this paper, we propose PhyMo, a framework for learning evolving dynamics from multi-view videos. We introduce a Motion-Aware Trajectory Representation that drives the model to recover accurate motion from deformation, rather than relying on appearance shortcuts to explain observed motion. On top of this, we propose a Continuous Motion Evolution module with bidirectional kinematic continuity constraint to promote temporally consistent evolution of displacement, velocity, and acceleration. Together, these designs yield geometrically faithful interpolation and continuous motion extrapolation. Experiments on four dynamic scene benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [94] [DriftNeRF: Cross-Ray Density Drifting for Sparse-View Neural Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29106)
*Juntang Wang,Yihan Wang,Sheng'en Li,Hao Wu,Dongmian Zou,Shixin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出NeRF在稀疏视角下退化的根源是渲染损失中缺少跨射线密度一致性约束，并据此提出DriftNeRF正则项，显式惩罚不同射线对同一空间区域密度预测的分歧，在多个数据集上显著提升3视图重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeRF依赖稠密视角才能合成逼真新视图，少视角时性能急剧下降。作者将这一缺陷归因于目标函数本身：渲染损失是对独立射线的求和，不含比较不同射线对同一空间区域所赋密度的项，因此相邻3D点可能收敛到相互矛盾的密度，而每个训练像素仍被正确重建。作者通过直接测量跨射线密度分歧来显式化这一量，发现其在原版NeRF中持续偏高，在基于频率的正则化下仅偶然下降，且在稀疏情形下与留出视图质量同步变化。

Method: 提出DriftNeRF——一种即插即用正则项，将漂移场（drifting-field）模板实例化于密度采样上：用掩码指数核从其他射线的邻近点聚合密度，形成带stop-gradient的共识目标；所得漂移场惩罚缺乏支持的密度分歧，同时保留已达成一致的边界。该正则项仅读取采样位置、射线标识与预测密度，无需预训练模型、外部几何或额外图像，并可跨场参数化迁移。

Result: 在NeRF-Synthetic上，残差分歧降低一个数量级；在NeRF-Synthetic、DTU与LLFF上，3视图PSNR较最强MLP-NeRF基线提升0.42–0.65 dB；在匹配协议下与稀疏视角高斯管线具有竞争力，且无需其深度、光流或共视预处理。

Conclusion: 稀疏视角NeRF的核心瓶颈在于跨射线密度不一致，而非单纯的欠约束像素拟合；显式度量并正则可消除该分歧，为稀疏视角重建提供轻量、通用且与场表示无关的改进方案。

Abstract: Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) synthesize photorealistic novel views from dense image collections, but degrade sharply when few views are available. We attribute a specific, measurable defect to the objective itself: the rendering loss is a sum over independent rays and contains no term comparing densities that different rays assign to the same region of space, so nearby 3D points can settle on contradictory densities while every training pixel is reproduced correctly. We make this quantity explicit by measuring cross-ray density disagreement directly during training, and show that it stays high in vanilla NeRF, falls only incidentally under frequency-based regularization, and moves together with held-out quality in the sparse regime. We then close the gap with DriftNeRF, a drop-in regularizer that instantiates the drifting-field template on density samples: a masked exponential kernel aggregates densities from nearby points on other rays into a stop-gradient consensus target, and the resulting drifting field penalizes unsupported disagreement while leaving agreed-upon boundaries intact. The regularizer reads only sampled positions, ray identifiers, and predicted densities, so it requires no pretrained model, external geometry, or additional images, and transfers across field parameterizations. On NeRF-Synthetic it reduces residual disagreement by an order of magnitude, and across NeRF-Synthetic, DTU, and LLFF it improves 3-view PSNR over the strongest MLP-NeRF baseline by 0.42-0.65 dB and remains competitive with sparse-view Gaussian pipelines under a matched protocol without their depth, flow, or covisibility preprocessing.

</details>


### [95] [From Canon to Nikon: Camera Domain Shift and Potential Equity Implications in Retinal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29104)
*Lisa Y. W. Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在巴西8,524例患者队列中，Nikon NF5050采集的视网膜图像质量不达标概率是Canon CR-2的两倍以上，尽管翻新Canon系统成本也高出一倍以上。基于冻结的预训练视网膜基础模型，作者在12个分类任务、4种输入分辨率和5种下游分类器上评估相机相关性能差异，发现差异随任务显著变化，提高分辨率或更换分类器均无法一致消除跨相机变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病视网膜病变是可预防性失明的主因，AI筛查具可扩展性，但其性能对图像质量与采集条件敏感。实际部署中不同相机平台的图像质量与成本差异可能被忽视，且成本与质量存在潜在权衡，需系统评估相机平台是否构成AI性能变异的重要来源。

Method: 采用单一巴西队列（8,524例患者）比较Nikon NF5050与Canon CR-2的图像质量不达标率及设备成本；使用冻结的预训练视网膜基础模型作为特征提取器，在12个视网膜疾病分类任务、4种输入分辨率和5种下游分类器组成的网格中进行系统的跨相机性能评估。

Result: Nikon NF5050图像质量不达标风险超过Canon CR-2的两倍，而翻新Canon系统价格也超过Nikon系统的两倍。相机相关性能差异在不同任务间变化显著；提高输入分辨率与更换下游分类器均不能一致地缓解跨相机变异。

Conclusion: 相机平台是视网膜AI性能变异的一个易被忽视来源，对异构成像系统部署下AI的安全性与可靠性具有重要影响；作者公开全部源代码、评估脚本与逐任务结果以支持可复现基准测试。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy is a leading cause of preventable blindness, yet access to regular screening remains limited worldwide. AI offers a scalable approach, but its performance can be sensitive to image quality and acquisition conditions. In a Brazilian cohort of 8,524 patients, retinal images acquired with the Nikon NF5050 were more than twice as likely to have inadequate image quality compared with those acquired with the Canon CR-2, despite refurbished Canon systems costing more than twice as much as Nikon systems. This potential cost-quality trade-off highlights an important consideration for the reliability of AI systems deployed across heterogeneous imaging systems. Using a frozen pretrained retinal foundation model, we systematically evaluate whether camera-associated performance differences persist across 12 retinal disease classification tasks, four input resolutions, and five downstream classifiers. We find that camera-associated performance differences vary substantially across tasks, and that neither higher input resolution nor classifier choice consistently mitigates cross-camera variation. These findings identify camera platform as a potentially overlooked source of performance variability, with implications for the safety and reliability of retinal AI when deployed across heterogeneous imaging systems. We release all source code, evaluation scripts, and per-task results on GitHub to support reproducible benchmarking.

</details>


### [96] [Generalized CycleGRPO for Fine-Grained Multimodal Understanding and Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29552)
*Xin Zhang,Haochen Wang,Beibei Lin,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Robby T. Tan,Jason Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Generalized CycleGRPO，首个统一强化学习框架，将区域理解与定位通过"区域-文本循环一致性"耦合到空间、时间、时空三种域，无需参考描述或外部语言模型评判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度多模态智能要求模型既能理解指定区域，又能从语言中定位该区域。现有方法以分离的目标和任务特定标注分别学习这两个方向，割裂了理解与定位的相互验证关系。

Method: 给定区域，MLLM 生成候选描述，再将每条描述定位回原区域以完成重构。重构重叠度作为可验证奖励用于描述生成，单条定位奖励改进逆向映射。统一的"区域空间"表述将该循环从图像掩码与框扩展到视频区间与带时间戳的空间证据；强化学习过程无需参考描述或外部 LLM 评判。

Result: CycleGRPO 将 SAMTok 在 GroundingSuite 上的总体 gIoU 从 57.5% 提升至 68.2%，在 GRES 上平均 gIoU 从 71.2% 提升至 78.7%。时间任务上，Charades 达 51.9% tIoU，DOVE 达 33.3% Temporal Evidence Coverage，而 Qwen3-VL 初始化分别为 45.6% 与 14.8%。

Conclusion: 区域-文本循环一致性可作为多模态理解与定位的通用自评估原则。此外提出 DOVE——经人工校验的对比式视频描述评测基准，用于衡量时间上的区分性。

Abstract: Fine-grained multimodal intelligence requires models to both understand a designated region and localize it from language. Existing methods learn these directions with separate objectives and task-specific annotations. We present Generalized CycleGRPO, the first unified reinforcement learning framework coupling region understanding and localization across spatial, temporal, and spatio-temporal domains. Given a region, a multimodal large language model generates candidate descriptions and then localizes each description to reconstruct the input region. Reconstruction overlap provides a verifiable reward for captioning, while individual localization rewards improve the inverse mapping. A common region-space formulation extends this cycle from image masks and boxes to video intervals and timestamped spatial evidence, without reference captions or an external language-model judge during reinforcement learning. In videos, the cycle encourages descriptions that distinguish a target event from similar recurrences. We introduce DOVE (Distinctive Occurrence-level Video-caption Evaluation), a human-verified contrastive benchmark for this temporal distinctiveness. CycleGRPO improves SAMTok's overall gIoU from 57.5% to 68.2% on GroundingSuite and average gIoU from 71.2% to 78.7% on GRES. For temporal tasks, it achieves 51.9% tIoU on Charades and 33.3% Temporal Evidence Coverage on DOVE, compared with 45.6% and 14.8% for the Qwen3-VL initialization. These results establish region-text cycle consistency as a general self-evaluation principle for multimodal understanding and localization. Models, code, and DOVE are available at https://devinxzhang.github.io/Generalized-CycleGRPO-Page/.

</details>


### [97] [Hyperspherical Simplex Representations for Backward-Compatible Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29550)
*Simone Ricci,Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于softmax输出与logits的"超球单纯形表示"，无需兼容性专用训练、映射函数或架构修改，即可实现独立训练模型间的向后兼容检索，并通过确定性线性变换解决新增类别导致的单纯形扩张错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型更新会改变内部特征表示，检索系统中每次更新常需对整个gallery重新建索引，代价高昂。现有向后兼容方法通常依赖兼容性专用训练、学习到的映射函数或架构修改，且显式依赖旧模型，限制了实用性。

Method: 利用one-hot标签下softmax交叉熵训练诱导的单纯形结构作为跨模型更新的共同几何参照，从softmax输出和logits构造超球单纯形表示；针对新增类别使单纯形扩张、旧类原型错位的问题，推导确定性线性变换恢复对齐。理论上在von Mises–Fisher类分布且更新间集中度递增的假设下，给出向后兼容性不等式期望成立的两个几何条件，并证明对齐的规则单纯形表示满足这些条件。

Result: 在标准兼容性基准上取得有竞争力的结果，覆盖扩展类别集、更强表达力架构以及来自公开仓库的独立训练模型；且不使用额外兼容性损失、学习映射或架构修改。

Conclusion: softmax交叉熵训练天然诱导的单纯形几何可作为独立训练模型间的通用兼容参照，配合确定性对齐变换即可实现免再索引的向后兼容检索，并在给定分布假设下具有理论支撑。

Abstract: Training modern AI models has become increasingly expensive, and updating deployed models can alter their internal feature representations, affecting applications built upon them. In retrieval systems, such updates often require complete gallery re-indexing, a computationally expensive process at scale. Existing backward-compatibility methods enable direct comparison of representations from updated and earlier models, avoiding re-indexing. However, they typically require compatibility-specific training, learned mapping functions, or architectural modifications, and often depend explicitly on the previous model. We propose hyperspherical simplex representations derived from softmax outputs and logits for backward-compatible retrieval across independently trained models. They leverage the simplex structure induced by softmax cross-entropy training with one-hot labels as a common geometric reference across model updates. Since this reference depends on the number of classes, adding new ones expands the simplex, misaligning existing class prototypes with their earlier counterparts. We therefore derive a deterministic linear transformation to restore their alignment. Our theoretical analysis of the formal backward-compatibility definition identifies two geometric conditions under which its inequalities hold in expectation, assuming von Mises--Fisher class distributions with increasing concentration across updates. Aligned regular-simplex representations satisfy these conditions, providing a theoretical basis for the proposed representations under the stated assumptions. Without using additional compatibility-specific losses, learned mappings, or architectural modifications, the proposed representations achieve competitive results on standard compatibility benchmarks, including expanded class sets, more expressive architectures, and independently trained models from public repositories.

</details>


### [98] [Melange: A Synthetic Visual Bias Benchmark for Multi-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29537)
*Razieh Rezaei,Ehsaneddin Asgary,Masoud Jalili Sabet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态社会偏见基准 Melange（7 类、合成视觉场景、双阶段验证），系统评测 23 个开源 MLLM 在纯文本/纯图像/图像+上下文三种匹配设置下的公平性，发现引入视觉使公平性平均下降 0.291，且差距取决于模型家族而非规模；模型能高准确识别属性（90–95%）却仍不弃权，偏置作答时约 3/4 选择人类刻板印象答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 已广泛部署，但其偏见研究远少于纯文本 LLM，存在现实社会风险。既有将 LLM 偏见分析扩展到多模态的工作存在两点不足：一是仅覆盖少数偏见类别；二是未保证被比较的群体确实由图像中的视觉线索清晰体现，导致评测效度不足。

Method: 构建 Melange 合成视觉基准：以 BigBench 的纯语言 BBQ 数据集为基础，通过受控生成视觉场景扩展其构造上下文，使目标属性在视觉上明确可见；图像经两阶段验证——VQA 自动筛查 + 人工标注复核。在纯文本、纯图像、图像+上下文三种匹配设置下系统评测 23 个开源 MLLM，以隔离输入模态对公平性的影响；并设置感知对照实验与极性感知的刻板印象对齐分析。

Result: 由文本切换到视觉平均使公平性下降 0.291；该差距随模型家族而非仅随规模变化。感知对照显示差距并非视觉理解缺陷所致：模型对 depicted 属性的识别准确率达 90–95%，却仍不能弃权。当模型给出有偏答案时，极性感知的刻板印象对齐分析表明其约四分之三的情形选择人类刻板印象式回答。

Conclusion: 多模态输入会系统性放大 MLLM 的社会偏见，且该效应无法用视觉感知能力不足解释，反映的是弃权决策与刻板印象倾向层面的失效；偏见水平更多与模型家族相关而非参数规模，构建组件化、广泛覆盖且视觉明确的合成基准对可靠诊断 MLLM 公平性具有必要性。

Abstract: Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have seen broad adoption, but their biases are still far less examined than those of standard LLMs, creating possible social harms when deployed in real-world settings. Prior efforts to extend bias analyses from LLMs into the multi-modal space either cover only a few bias categories or do not ensure that the groups being compared are clearly addressed by visual cues in the images. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Melange, a wide-ranging synthetic visual benchmark covering seven categories for assessing social biases in MLLMs. It is grounded in the widely used language-only BBQ dataset (Bias Benchmark for Question Answering) from BigBench, carefully extending its constructed contexts via controlled generation of visual scenarios so the target attributes appear visually and clearly. To maximize reliability, our generated images pass through two stages of validation: automated screening using visual question answering (VQA) and subsequent manual inspection by human annotators. By systematically evaluating 23 open-source MLLMs across three matched evaluation settings---text-only, image-only, and image-with-context---Melange isolates the fairness effect of the input modality. Switching from text to vision reduces fairness by 0.291 on average, a gap that varies by model family rather than by scale alone. A perception control confirms that this gap is not an artifact of visual comprehension: models identify the depicted attributes at 90--95\% accuracy yet still fail to abstain. When models do commit to a biased answer, polarity-aware stereotype alignment shows they choose the human-stereotypical response roughly three-quarters of the time.

</details>


### [99] [Extending Affordance Segmentation for Wearable Robots to Multi-Object Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29474)
*Simone Lugani,Matteo Montani,Paolo Gastaldo,Rodolfo Zunino,Edoardo Ragusa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文对现有基于视觉的可穿戴抓取部件识别流水线提出改进：在硬件中加入激光指示器用于多目标消歧，并配合混合现实程序化生成的带标注图像对深度CNN重训练，在保持计算成本的前提下将目标消歧准确率最高提升9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉流水线已能较好支持上肢假肢的自动抓取选择与预塑形，但在多物体共存的实际场景中，目标识别与消歧仍是难题。

Method: 1）对常规硬件配置做小幅修改，引入激光指示器作为目标消歧机制；2）据此对识别可抓取部件的深度卷积神经网络进行针对性重训练；3）采用混合现实策略，复用已有高质量分割掩码程序化生成逼真图像，扩展设备可覆盖的场景范围。

Result: 实验结果表明，与现有方案相比，所提流水线将目标消歧准确率最高提升9%，且未增加计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过硬件层面的激光指示与相应的网络重训练及混合现实数据生成，可在低成本条件下有效提升多物体场景下的抓取部件识别与目标消歧性能，适用于假肢装置的实用化部署。

Abstract: This paper proposes a refinement to existing vision-based portable pipelines for the recognition of graspable object parts. Such pipelines can effectively support automatic grasp selection and preshaping in upper-limb prosthetic devices. However, realistic scenarios in which multiple objects are present still represent a challenge. The proposed method exploits a minor modification to the conventional hardware setup and a custom training approach to extend the applicability of vision-based pipelines. A laser pointer is introduced in the setup as an effective mechanism for object disambiguation. Accordingly, a suitable retraining of the deep convolutional neural networks supporting recognition of graspable parts is implemented. A mixed reality strategy allows the reuse of available high-quality segmentation masks to generate realistic images procedurally, extending the range of scenarios supported by the final device. Experimental results confirm that the proposed pipeline improves target disambiguation accuracy by up to 9% compared with existing solutions, while preserving the computational cost.

</details>


### [100] [Revealing Hidden Failure Modes in Chest X-ray Classification via Spectral Domain Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29830)
*Samuel Halimi,Loic Themyr,Arnaud Abreu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以数据为中心的频域分析框架：将胸片投影到频域，提取刻画信号能量在各频带分布的紧凑频谱画像，并对频谱画像做无监督聚类，证明模型失败并非随机分布，而是高度集中于特定频谱簇，从而无需真实失败标注即可发现“盲点”与性能退化数据切片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片异常检测的深度学习模型对细微分布偏移（采集技术、患者相关因素、预处理等）敏感；传统误差分析依赖语义元数据或模型嵌入，容易掩盖导致性能下降的低层信号变异。

Method: 以数据为中心的自动化失效模式发现：图像变换至频域，提取概括各频带信号能量分布的紧凑频谱画像；对频谱画像进行无监督聚类，识别失败集中的频谱簇。

Result: 模型失败在频谱簇间呈强聚集性而非随机分布；该方法可有效隔离“盲点”，支持模型可靠性预测，并在无真实失败标注的情况下发现性能退化的数据切片。

Conclusion: 频域频谱画像聚类为胸片异常检测提供了一种轻量、免标注的可解释误差分析手段，可按低层信号特征定位失效数据切片并评估模型可靠性。

Abstract: Deep learning models for chest X-ray anomaly detection remain vulnerable to subtle distributional shifts (e.g., acquisition technique, patient-related factors, and preprocessing). Traditional error analysis often relies on semantic metadata or model embeddings, which can mask low-level signal variations that degrade performance. In this work, we propose a data-centric framework for automated failure mode discovery using spectral analysis. We project images into the frequency domain and extract a compact profile summarizing the distribution of signal energy across frequency bands. By performing unsupervised clustering on these spectral profiles, we demonstrate that model failures are not randomly distributed, but are strongly concentrated within specific spectral clusters. This method effectively isolates "blind spots", enabling the prediction of model reliability and the discovery of performance-degrading data slices without requiring ground-truth failure annotations.

</details>


### [101] [OpenEarth-OVS44: A Multimodal Remote Sensing Dataset for Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29642)
*Yimin Wei,Hongruixuan Chen,Hao Zheng,Hao Liu,Jian Song,Yuting Zhu,Junshi Xia,Naoto Yokoya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 OpenEarth-OVS44，一个亚米级分辨率、多模态（RGB+SAR）、统一44类细粒度分类体系的开放词汇遥感分割基准，含三条评测赛道，并证明在其上训练可显著提升细粒度与跨区域分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感开放词汇分割对跨区域地物理解与泛化至新类很重要，但现有数据集类别覆盖有限、分类体系过于粗糙，难以支撑城市功能区划分、碳核算、灾后评估等细粒度应用需求。

Method: 构建 OpenEarth-OVS44 基准：290,587 个专家核验分割实例，来自 2,832 张 RGB 影像与 1,341 对严格配准的 RGB-SAR 影像对，亚米级分辨率，覆盖法国、日本、美国共40个城市，采用9大基类下44类的统一分类体系；设置三条赛道——仅 RGB 细粒度基准、含多样合成云况的多模态鲁棒性赛道、留出跨区域赛道；并在代表性开放词汇分割模型上进行实验。

Result: 在 OpenEarth-OVS44 上训练后，相比最强免训练基线，细粒度分割性能显著提升：未见类别最高提升 7.1 mIoU，已见类别提升达 27.6 mIoU。

Conclusion: 细粒度、多模态、跨区域的开放词汇遥感分割基准可有效推动该领域发展；数据集与代码将公开。

Abstract: In remote sensing, open-vocabulary segmentation is particularly important for flexible and scalable land-cover understanding across diverse regions, as it improves generalization to novel categories without requiring exhaustive pixel-level annotations. Despite its great promise, progress in this area remains largely constrained by existing datasets with limited category coverage and overly coarse taxonomies, and therefore struggles to support fine-grained semantic understanding required in real-world applications such as urban functional zoning, carbon accounting, and post-disaster assessment. To address these challenges, we present OpenEarth-OVS44, a multimodal benchmark with 290,587 expert-verified segments from 2,832 RGB images and 1,341 strictly registered RGB-SAR pairs at sub-meter spatial resolution, covering 40 cities across France, Japan, and the United States under a unified 44-class taxonomy organized into 9 base categories. Three evaluation tracks are provided: an RGB-only fine-grained benchmark, a multimodal track with diverse synthetic cloudy conditions for weather-robust evaluation, and a held-out cross-region track. Extensive experiments on representative open-vocabulary segmentation models demonstrate that training on OpenEarth-OVS44 substantially improves fine-grained segmentation performance over the strongest training-free baseline, with improvements of up to 7.1 mIoU points on unseen categories and 27.6 mIoU points on seen categories. The dataset and code will be released.

</details>


### [102] [Certified Abstention for Provably-Unidentifiable Index-Phase Recovery in Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29272)
*Dinh Tuan Nguyen,Trong Nhan Nguyen,Vu Nguyen Minh Hung,Tien Minh Khoi Nguyen,Jaeyeop Choi,Sudip Mondal,Jae Sung Ahn,Junghwan Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出配准可恢复性的判别理论：在参考扰动采样成像中，位置保真与相位保真逻辑独立，仅凭位置信息无法保证相位保真；给出紧致分辨率阈值区分可恢复区与可证不可辨识区，并提出可认证恢复框架，在可辨识区精确恢复、在不可辨识区弃权而非幻觉；在扫描系统上验证了方向双峰偏移，并跨扫描、卷帘快门与冷冻电镜显示弃权机制的迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像配准是扫描、相机与显微成像测量的基础，但"样本到位置映射何时可恢复、何时任何估计器都必然虚构位置"这一根本问题很少被提出；现有经典与学习方法在不可辨识区仍会输出结果并虚构结构。

Method: 在参考扰动采样成像设定下，将位置保真（样本落点）与相位保真（哪一参考索引成为块原点）形式化为逻辑独立的两个量，证明仅依赖位置信息的策略无法保证相位保真；推导区分可恢复与可证不可辨识的紧致分辨率阈值；构建带证书的恢复框架，对可辨识区域精确恢复、对不可辨识区域主动弃权。

Result: 在部署的扫描系统上测得与理论一致的方向双峰配准偏移；与经典及学习式复原基线相比，无证书偏移估计器在不可辨识区虚构结构，而认证方法选择弃权；认证弃权机制可迁移至扫描、卷帘快门与冷冻电镜成像，但弃权完整度与保留区风险界紧致度因模态而异（极低信噪比下冷冻电镜证书仅部分弃权）。

Conclusion: 配准可靠性存在结构性—统计性边界：可恢复性由结构阈值界定，而弃权行为与风险刻画则依赖具体模态的统计特性；证书化弃权为配准提供了可验证的可靠性保障。

Abstract: Image registration underpins measurement across scanning, camera, and microscopy imaging, yet a fundamental question is rarely posed: when is the sample-to-position map recoverable at all, and when does any estimator that reports a position necessarily fabricate one? We show that in reference-perturbed sampled imaging, where samples are localized through a perturbed reference, position fidelity (where samples land) and phase fidelity (which reference index becomes each block's origin) are logically independent: no policy acting on position information alone can guarantee phase fidelity. We derive a tight resolution threshold separating recoverable from provably-unidentifiable registration, and a recovery framework that certifies identifiable regions--recovering them exactly--while abstaining on provably-unidentifiable regions rather than hallucinating structure. On a deployed scanning system we measure a direction-bimodal registration offset consistent with the theory. Against classical and learned restoration baselines, certificate-free offset estimators fabricate structure on the unidentifiable regime, whereas the certified method abstains rather than fabricating.
