Finally, the certified-abstention mechanism carries across scanning, rolling-shutter, and cryo-electron-microscopy imaging--declining rather than fabricating on the regime it flags--though how completely it abstains and how tightly it bounds kept-region risk stay modality-specific (at extreme SNR the cryo-EM certificate abstains only partially): a structural-versus-statistical boundary in registration reliability.

</details>


### [103] [How Do DiTs Denoise? An Aggregator-Predictor View of Attention and MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29854)
*Gyeonghun Kang,William Xingxu Chen,Xiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"聚合器—预测器"视角解释DiT：注意力充当输入/噪声自适应的局部上下文聚合器，MLP充当逐token的patch预测器，并给出GMRF理论支撑与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT如何表征图像内容、如何学到可复用的去噪规则并实现泛化，尚缺乏组件层面的功能解释；既有的"分块线性去噪器"解释不足以说明注意力与MLP各自的作用。

Method: 1) 将DiT刻画为输入自适应的局部去噪器，把上下文聚合与patch预测分别对应到注意力和MLP；2) 在GMRF先验下证明仅用部分上下文patch即可逼近全局贝叶斯去噪器，误差随上下文半径指数衰减；3) 证明在充分信息化的聚合下，分块线性去噪器可恢复经验局部去噪器；4) 将AdaLN调制注意力形式化为输入与噪声相关的聚合器，MLP形式化为学习到的key–value表示用于token级预测；5) 实验：真实图像训练DiT的噪声相关去噪上下文与注意力质量展宽、CelebA64上用共享记忆patch预测器替换MLP、跨数据集组件移植（QK/VO/MLP）。

Result: 真实图像上训练的DiT呈现与GMRF解析设定相似的噪声相关去噪上下文，注意力质量有定性的展宽趋势；CelebA64受控实验中用共享记忆patch预测器替代DiT MLP，去噪与生成性能具竞争力；跨数据集组件移植显示兼容性模式与QK负责空间加权、VO负责特征传输、MLP负责patch预测的角色一致。

Conclusion: 结果为DiT去噪提供了组件级解释，揭示了注意力与MLP的功能分工，并为架构特异的微调与受控干预提供依据。

Abstract: How do diffusion transformers (DiTs) represent image content and learn rules for reusing it to generalize? We develop an aggregator--predictor view of DiTs as input-adaptive local denoisers, in which context aggregation and patch prediction provide functional roles for interpreting attention and MLPs. We first establish that, for Gaussian Markov random field (GMRF) priors, the global Bayes denoiser can be approximated using only a subset of context patches, with error decaying exponentially in context radius. We further show that patchwise linear denoisers, often invoked to explain diffusion generalization, recover the empirical local denoiser with sufficiently informative aggregation. Within this view, AdaLN-modulated attention naturally parameterizes an input- and noise-dependent aggregator, while the MLP admits a learned key--value representation for tokenwise patch prediction. Our experiments support this view. DiTs trained on real images exhibit noise-dependent denoising contexts resembling those in the analytic GMRF setting, while attention mass exhibits a qualitatively corresponding broadening pattern. In a controlled CelebA64 study, shared-memory patch predictors used in place of DiT MLPs achieve competitive denoising and generative performance. Finally, cross-dataset component transplantation reveals compatibility patterns consistent with the proposed roles of QK in spatial weighting, VO in feature transport, and MLPs in patch prediction. Together, these results provide a component-level account of DiT denoising and motivate architecture-specific fine-tuning and controlled intervention.

</details>


### [104] [A Unified Evaluation Framework of Physical Adversarial Robustness in the Edge VLM Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29196)
*Eric Liang,Alan Luo,Kaiwen Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统评测了紧凑型grounding VLM与目标检测器在物理对抗补丁下的人身规避攻击鲁棒性，提出统一指标cASR并将失败模式划分为omission、substitution、displacement三类；发现VLM可与检测器同样脆弱，失败模式主要由输出格式而非架构族决定，且分布偏移下omission型失败会转向更危险的substitution/displacement。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM正被快速部署到边缘安全关键系统中，但其物理对抗鲁棒性缺乏系统研究；同时VLM的生成式输出与目标检测器输出存在本质差异，导致难以进行公平比较，需要一套统一评测方法来直接对比两类模型。

Method: 以"佩戴在行人身上的对抗补丁能否规避模型检测"为统一评测结果，提出条件攻击成功率cASR（仅在匹配灰色遮挡物下模型仍能定位的图像上计数），并将每次失败划分为omission（无框）、substitution（框落在补丁上）、displacement（框落在二者之外）三类；在紧凑grounding VLM与多种目标检测器上进行物理对抗基准测试，并考察分布偏移下的失败模式变化。

Result: grounding VLM可与检测器一样脆弱；失败模式主要由模型输出格式决定，而非架构族；三类失败中只有omission对下游系统可见，substitution与displacement会返回高置信度、格式良好的框，因而潜在更危险；在分布偏移下，原本以omission为主的VLM会转向更多substitution/displacement失败。

Conclusion: grounding VLM既可能继承检测器的脆弱性，又可能表现出更危险的失败模式，因此边缘部署需按输出格式与失败模式而非仅按平均攻击成功率评估鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable end-to-end visual grounding via language prompts and generative decoding, demonstrating largely improved zero-shot and open-vocabulary detection capabilities. As a result, VLMs are rapidly being deployed at the edge in safety-critical real-world systems. However, the physical adversarial robustness of edge VLMs has not been systematically studied, and the fundamental differences in output with modern object detectors create methodological challenges for fair evaluation. To compare the two families directly, we evaluate every selected representative model on one outcome: whether an adversarial patch worn on a person evades detection of the model. We report this by introducing a unified metric, conditional attack success rate (cASR), counted only on images the model still localizes under a matched gray occluder, and partition every failure into omission (no box), substitution (a box on the patch), and displacement (a box on neither). We benchmark the physical adversarial vulnerability of compact grounding VLMs against a range of object detectors, and find that the VLMs can be as vulnerable as the detectors. We disclose that failure mode is largely associated with the output format of a model rather than its architecture family. Of the three, only omission is visible to a downstream system; displacement and substitution return confident, well-formed boxes, making them potentially more dangerous failures. Further, we find that, under distribution shift, VLMs that primarily failed by omission instead shift to failing more by displacement/substitution. Thus, a grounding VLM can inherit the detector's vulnerability while showing the possibility to adopt more dangerous failure modes. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/ericliang1/physical-adversarial-robustness.

</details>


### [105] [Attn-Forcing: Learning Full-Context Attention Proxies for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30036)
*Junyao Gao,Sibo Liu,Jiaxing Li,Yanan Sun,Hanxin Zhu,Weidong Zhang,Jun Zhang,Cairong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Attn-Forcing 框架，用可学习的注意力代理（attention proxies）在有限内存与计算下保留流式视频生成的完整上下文注意力贡献，并通过 Attention Matching Loss 显式监督，实现 SOTA 流式视频生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增大注意力上下文长度可显著提升流式视频生成质量，但内存与计算开销随上下文增长而不可承受。现有基于注意力选择或压缩的方法受限于有界表示，且缺乏注意力层面的显式监督来保留历史上下文对当前查询的贡献。

Method: 提出 Attn-Forcing 框架：引入注意力代理（attention proxies）持续逼近历史上下文所诱导的注意力响应；将代理实现为可学习视觉 latent，并使用 Proxy Update Module 融合历史 latent 的视觉上下文；提出 Attention Matching Loss，以完整上下文注意力为监督，显式约束学习到的代理的注意力行为。

Result: 大量定性与定量实验表明，Attn-Forcing 稳定提升流式视频生成质量，并在现有基线方法上取得 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: 通过在有限内存与计算预算下近似完整上下文的注意力贡献并提供注意力级监督，Attn-Forcing 有效突破了现有选择/压缩方法的表示瓶颈，为长上下文流式视频生成提供了可行且高效的方案。

Abstract: Increasing the attention context length significantly improves streaming video generation quality, while incurring prohibitive memory and computational costs as the context grows. Existing methods attempt to capture this contextual information through attention selection or compression, but remain limited by bounded representations and lack explicit attention-level supervision to retain historical contributions to current queries. To address this, we introduce Attn-Forcing, a novel framework that preserves full-context attention contributions for streaming video generation under bounded memory and computation. We achieve this by introducing attention proxies that continuously approximate the attention responses induced by the historical context. Specifically, we implement the proxies as learnable visual latents and employ a Proxy Update Module to incorporate visual context from historical latents. Moreover, we propose an Attention Matching Loss to explicitly constrain the attention behavior of the learned proxies using full-context attention supervision. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experiments demonstrate that Attn-Forcing consistently improves streaming video generation quality and achieves state-of-the-art performance over existing baseline methods.

</details>


### [106] [DewHand: Direct Metric World-Space Hand Motion Reconstruction from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29936)
*Naru Suzuki,Satoshi Ikehata,Takehiko Ohkawa,Yoichi Sato,Shuhei Kurita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DewHand 是一个单阶段统一模型，同时联合重建世界坐标系下的手部与相机运动，通过结合 VGGT-Ω 场景几何特征与度量监督、并采用 flow matching 条件生成来处理遮挡与出视野问题，在精度、时序平滑性与零样本泛化上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自我中心视频恢复度量世界空间的手部运动对理解人类交互并迁移至具身智能体至关重要。现有方法面临三大挑战：多阶段流水线中手部与相机独立估计导致误差累积；单目观测使物理尺度存在歧义；遮挡与出视野的手部存在多个可能的合理轨迹。

Method: 提出 DewHand 统一模型：在单一阶段流水线中于共享世界坐标系内联合预测手部与相机运动以消除多阶段误差累积；融合 VGGT-Ω 的场景级几何特征与度量空间监督以消除尺度歧义，同时保留手部专用特征以维持细粒度关节结构；将重建表述为基于 flow matching 的条件运动生成，以应对遮挡与出视野情况，仅需两步采样。

Result: 在 HOT3D 与 ARCTIC 上，刚体对齐世界空间误差相对 StableHand 分别由 32.9 mm 降至 28.4 mm、由 76.2 mm 降至 36.2 mm；相机尺度在 HOT3D 三分之二片段上误差恢复至 10% 以内（SLAM 仅约四分之一）；同一架构下该生成式表述在精度与时序平滑性上优于直接回归。在 HoloAssist、H2O、TACO 的零样本评测中，各数据集均取得最低的刚体对齐世界空间误差（WA-MPJPE）。

Conclusion: 联合单阶段建模、度量几何融合与生成式运动建模相结合，可有效解决自我中心手部世界空间重建中的误差累积、尺度歧义与遮挡歧义问题，并展现出跨设备零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Recovering metric world-space hand motion from egocentric video supports understanding human interactions and transferring them to embodied agents. Three challenges complicate this task: multi-stage pipelines propagate errors from independently estimated hand and camera motion; monocular observations leave physical scale ambiguous; and occlusion and out-of-view hands admit multiple plausible motion trajectories. We present DewHand, a unified model for direct joint reconstruction of world-space hand and camera motion. To avoid compounding errors from multi-stage estimation, DewHand jointly predicts hand and camera motion in a shared world coordinate system within a single-stage pipeline. To resolve metric-scale ambiguity, it combines scene-level geometric features from VGGT-$Ω$ with metric-space supervision, while hand-specific features preserve fine-grained articulation. To handle occlusion and out-of-view hands, we formulate reconstruction as conditional motion generation with flow matching. Under the same architecture, this generative formulation outperforms direct regression in both accuracy and temporal smoothness with only two sampling steps. Experiments on HOT3D and ARCTIC demonstrate improved world-space accuracy and temporal consistency over state-of-the-art methods. DewHand reduces rigid-aligned world-space error relative to StableHand from 32.9 to 28.4 mm on HOT3D and from 76.2 to 36.2 mm on ARCTIC. It also recovers camera scale within 10% on two thirds of HOT3D clips, versus about a quarter for SLAM. Moreover, in zero-shot evaluations on HoloAssist, H2O, and TACO, captured with different devices, DewHand achieves the lowest rigid-aligned world-space error (WA-MPJPE) on each dataset.

</details>


### [107] [ProactEgoMem: Benchmarking Proactive Memory in Streaming Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30089)
*Ruotong Liao,Meikel Kokowski,Xiaoxiao Sun,Yongqin Xian,Laura Bravo Sánchez,Shiye Su,Federico Tombari,Serena Yeung-Levy,Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ProactEgoMem 基准，评估流式第一视角视频中多模态助手的"主动记忆"能力，包含 IC/ST/LT 三类任务与通知级、干预级两层协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴多模态助手依赖用户显式提示来解读视觉证据，需由用户承担跨长时间跨度的记忆负担。助手应主动采集并维护有用记忆，这要求识别关键信息、实时理解场景并在后台启动记忆维护，而现有基准缺乏对此能力的评测。

Method: 构建 ProactEgoMem 流式第一视角视频基准，围绕环境需求设计三项任务：信息捕获（IC，保存瞬态高密度视觉信息）、状态跟踪（ST，跟踪物体状态变化）、位置跟踪（LT，记录物体移动）。协议分两级主动性：通知级（给出一次及时通知满足意图）与干预级（通过 start-to-stop 工具生命周期主动维护记忆）。设 Daily 与 Sci 两条并行赛道，分别含 300/100 个片段，配套 ProactEgoMem-QA 的 1000/500 个问题以衡量所采集记忆的质量。

Result: 在闭源、开源、视频-语言及流式基线模型上的评测显示，即使最强模型也无法同时做到及时且准确地捕获记忆。

Conclusion: ProactEgoMem 为主动式多模态辅助及与用户共存的视频智能体提供了严谨的评测基础。

Abstract: Today's multimodal assistants are typically prompted to interpret the visual evidence presented to them. To offload the cognitive burden of remembering across long horizons, wearable assistants should instead proactively acquire and maintain useful memory as the user's surroundings evolve. Doing so on the user's behalf requires identifying what matters, understanding the scene in real time, and initiating memory maintenance in the background while the user stays focused on their activities. We introduce ProactEgoMem, a benchmark that evaluates proactive memory in streaming egocentric video. Three tasks ground the evaluation in ambient environmental needs: Information Capture (IC) preserves transient yet dense visual information, State Tracking (ST) follows changing object states, and Location Tracking (LT) records object movements. The benchmark defines its protocol at two levels of proactiveness: the Notification Level asks for a single timely notification that satisfies the user's intent, while the deeper Intervention Level asks the agent to maintain memory on the user's behalf through an active start-to-stop tool lifecycle. Parallel ProactEgoMem-Daily and ProactEgoMem-Sci tracks contain 300/100 clips covering daily and scientific use cases, paired with 1000/500 questions in ProactEgoMem-QA, which measures the quality of the proactively acquired memory. Across proprietary, open-source, video-language, and streaming baselines, even the strongest models fail to capture memory both timely and accurately. ProactEgoMem provides a rigorous evaluation foundation for proactive multimodal assistance and for video agents that co-exist with their users.

</details>


### [108] [Multiclass Boundary Refinement with Neural Cellular Automata for Remote Sensing Land Cover Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30007)
*Pascoassis Souza Santos Meira,Gilson Antonio Giraldi,Rafael Duarte Coelho dos Santos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出边界精化神经细胞自动机BRNCA，从冻结分割器概率出发逐格修正，多类BIoU平均提升2.36点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感影像语义分割的类别边界常有几像素的位置偏移，该误差集中于Boundary IoU度量的窄带区域，需针对性精化边界位置。

Method: 将边界精化视为小型可学习局部规则的迭代应用（BRNCA）；以冻结预训练分割器的类别概率为输入逐单元修正；将二值rNCA推广到多类：通过Aitchison（对数空间）更新使可见通道保持于概率单纯形上，并增加自由隐藏通道。

Result: 最优配置使平均BIoU较基分割器提升2.36点（46.63→48.99）；增益集中于不规则林地类（+8.5点BIoU）。

Conclusion: 边界位置误差与类别边界的分形维数相关；轻量局部规则迭代可有效精化多类分割边界。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of remote sensing imagery often places class boundaries a few pixels away from where they truly lie, and this position error is concentrated in the thin band measured by Boundary IoU (BIoU). We treat boundary refinement as the iterated application of a small learned local rule: the Boundary Refinement Neural Cellular Automaton (BRNCA), that starts from the class probabilities of a frozen, pre-trained segmenter and corrects them cell by cell. We generalise the binary rNCA to the multiclass case by keeping the visible channels on the probability simplex through an Aitchison (log-space) update and by adding free hidden channels. The best configuration improves the mean BIoU over the base segmenter by 2.36 points (from 46.63 to 48.99). The gain is concentrated on the irregular woodland class (+8.5 points BIoU); this suggests that position error is correlated with the fractal dimension of the class boundaries.

</details>


### [109] [VoxProg: 3D Spatial Reasoning through Voxel Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30127)
*Zhichao Zhai,Menglei Zhao,Guikun Chen,Zihao Yan,Kanzhi Wu,Wenguan Wang,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VoxProg，将 3D 物体放置形式化为"体素编程"：语言模型生成可执行程序，在场景表面的局部体素视图上进行几何推理并计算目标 SE(3) 位姿，配合执行反馈迭代修正代码，并构建 71 任务的 VoxProg-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型擅长理解任务与生成程序，但"在 3D 场景中遵循空间指令"要求程序构建语言所隐含的几何参照（如支撑面、边界、接触关系）。现有基于空间关系、关键点或网格的方法难以显式构造这类参照并验证放置。

Method: 核心为体素编程：(1) 语言模型将任务解释转写为在局部体素视图上运行的可执行程序；体素视图以规则网格上的显式坐标与邻接关系索引表面几何。(2) 程序分两段——证据程序从场景网格中直接隔离任务相关区域与边界，位姿程序测量所得几何以计算目标 SE(3) 位姿。(3) 利用执行得到的视觉与数值反馈，模型检查中间选择与候选放置，迭代修订代码。

Result: 提出 71 任务的 VoxProg-Bench 基准，覆盖功能可用性、几何契合与上下文布置三类开放式放置，并配套评测套件（感知层 Intent Alignment + 3D Physical Validity）。在 VoxProg-Bench 及外部基准上，VoxProg 稳定优于基于关系、基于关键点与基于网格的基线。

Conclusion: 将空间指令执行落到显式坐标化的体素表示与可执行程序上，可实现可检查、可迭代修正的 3D 放置推理，为该类任务提供了统一的表示、方法与评测框架。

Abstract: Language models' abilities to interpret tasks and generate programs offer a promising basis for 3D spatial reasoning. Yet following spatial instructions in 3D scenes requires programs to construct geometric references that language leaves implicit. In this work, we introduce VoxProg, which formulates 3D object placement through voxel programming: a language model translates task interpretations into executable programs over local voxel views of scene surfaces. Because these views index surface geometry on regular grids with explicit coordinates and neighborhood relations, an evidence program can isolate task-relevant regions and boundaries directly from scene meshes, and a downstream pose program can measure the resulting geometry to compute a target $SE(3)$ pose. Visual and numerical feedback from execution lets the model inspect intermediate selections and candidate placements to iteratively revise its code. To evaluate open-ended placement across functional use, geometric fit, and contextual arrangements, we introduce the 71-task VoxProg-Bench benchmark along with an evaluation suite pairing perceptual Intent Alignment and 3D Physical Validity. Across VoxProg-Bench and external benchmarks, VoxProg consistently outperforms relation-based, keypoint-based, and mesh-based baselines.

</details>


### [110] [Plausible but Unimplantable: A Trustworthiness Audit of Shape Completion Foundation Models for Cranial Defect Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30035)
*Marek Wodzinski,Kamil Kwarciak,Daria Hemmerling,Mateusz Daniol*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 审计三个公开预训练形状补全模型（含两个基础模型）在239例颅骨缺损上的零样本表现：无一个输出可植入，甚至无一优于"直接用患者剩余骨"的基线；形状相似不等于临床可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颅骨缺损植入体目前仍需专家在CAD中手工绘制，耗时且昂贵；若形状补全（含首批形状补全基础模型）能自动化该步骤，将显著降低成本与周期，因此需评估其临床可用性。

Method: 将任务建模为形状补全问题，对三个公开预训练模型（其中两个为基础模型）做零样本推理，覆盖239例颅骨缺损（210例来自公共颅骨数据集的人工造孔、29例真实去骨瓣术后病例）。将每个输出与"直接返回患者剩余骨"的平凡基线比较，并检验预测材料能否在不与残留骨相交的前提下放置；同时与一个针对该任务训练、并在这些病例上选型的专用体素模型对照。

Result: 210例人造缺损上三模型均未超越"什么都不做"的基线；29例真实病例中仅一个模型超过随机水平。任何模型、任何配置下均无可植入输出——即无法同时满足避开残留骨与补入多于基线的材料；专用体素模型除1例外全部可植入。视觉上最像颅骨的输出并不更具可放置性；在该点密度下无法从输出点云读取植入体厚度，这属于输出表示本身的属性而非单个模型的缺陷。

Conclusion: 现有可获得的形状补全基础模型不可信，按发布状态无法用于临床实践；实现可信的形状补全基础模型仍需大量研究。

Abstract: A patient who has lost part of the skull vault needs an implant that fits the defect exactly, and such implants are still drawn by hand in CAD by specialists, which makes each case slow and expensive. The task can be considered as a shape completion problem, so pretrained completion models, and now the first shape completion foundation models, look like a way to remove that manual step. We audited three publicly released pretrained shape completion models, two of them foundation models, applied zero-shot to 239 cranial defects, some carved from public skull datasets and some real post-craniectomy cases. Each output was compared with simply returning the patient's own remaining bone, and tested for whether the predicted material could be placed without intersecting the bone that is still there. On the 210 carved defects none of the three improved on doing nothing, and on the 29 real cases only one did so beyond chance. Yet not one output, from any model in any configuration, was implantable: none managed both to stay clear of the bone the patient still has and to place more material than doing nothing, where a task-specific volumetric model, trained for this task and selected on these cases, was implantable on all but one. The output that most convincingly resembles a skull is no more placeable than the rest, and at these point densities implant thickness cannot be read off the emitted points at all, which is a property of the output representation rather than of any one model. On this evidence the shape completion foundation models we could obtain cannot be trusted and, used as released, are not usable in clinical practice. Substantial research towards trustworthy foundation models for shape completion is still needed.

</details>


### [111] [RealMe: Immersive Volumetric Video from Minimal Captures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30023)
*Chenxu Zhou,Zhiyuan Yu,Yudong Jin,Yinji Shentu,Yu Zhang,Jiaming Sun,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealMe：以高分辨率视频扩散模型将稀疏低分辨率多视角采集增强为2K、4D一致的视频，用于沉浸式体积视频重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密多视角重建质量高但采集成本昂贵；基于生成模型的方法虽放宽采集要求，仍受限于分辨率低与4D不一致。

Method: 构建以高分辨率视频扩散模型为核心的统一框架：从采集视频生成辅助数据，与布局感知位置编码一同编码为隐变量；将分解式时空注意力适配到多视角采集布局，跨视角与时间对隐变量去噪并解码为增强视频以精化4D表示；并在隐空间引入潜频域损失，缓解生成视频与真实采集的数据域差异。

Result: 在公开DNA-Rendering基准与自采RealVV数据集上，可生成沉浸式体积视频，视觉保真度与4D一致性均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过将多视角布局融入视频扩散的时空注意力并结合隐空间频域监督，可在稀疏低分辨率输入下实现高质量、4D一致的体积视频重建。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of creating immersive volumetric videos from sparse and low-resolution capture resources. Conventional dense multi-view reconstruction methods can produce high-quality volumetric videos, but their reliance on costly capture setups makes them impractical in real-world scenarios. Recent approaches use generative models to relax capture requirements, but still suffer from limited resolution and 4D inconsistency. To overcome these constraints, we propose RealMe, a unified framework built around a high-resolution video diffusion model that enhances limited captures into high-resolution (2K), 4D-consistent videos for volumetric video reconstruction. Specifically, RealMe constructs auxiliary data from capture videos and encodes them into latents along with layout-aware positional embeddings. The video diffusion model adapts factorized spatio-temporal attention to the multi-view capture layout, denoises these latents across viewpoints and time, and decodes them into enhanced videos to refine the 4D representation. This adaptation helps the model capture temporal dynamics and multi-view relationships, leading to more 4D-consistent generation. To reduce the data gap between generated videos and real captures, we apply a latent frequency loss that provides frequency-domain supervision in latent space. Extensive experiments on the public DNA-Rendering benchmark and our self-captured RealVV dataset demonstrate that RealMe is able to create immersive volumetric videos and outperforms state-of-the-art methods in visual fidelity and 4D consistency.

</details>


### [112] [Looking Ahead: Modeling Future Evolution in Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29989)
*Ruopeng Gao,Yukun Huang,Jiarui Hu,Shuai Wang,Wenbo Hu,Ying Shan,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出辅助目标 Future Chunk Prediction (FCP)，与teacher forcing去噪联合优化，预测被时间间隔分离的chunk，以规避局部视觉连续性捷径，提升自回归视频扩散模型对场景演化的建模能力，且训练计算开销更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型逐chunk生成，在teacher forcing下强时间冗余使模型可用局部视觉连续性作为当前chunk去噪的捷径，从而降低了对视觉演化建模的压力；生成"看似合理"的续帧并不等于捕捉到场景如何演化。

Method: 引入Future Chunk Prediction（FCP）作为与teacher-forced去噪联合优化的辅助目标：预测在时间上与当前chunk相隔的chunk，且不访问中间视觉上下文；监督信号直接取自训练视频，无需额外预训练视觉编码器，推理阶段保持自回归方式不变。

Result: 相比标准teacher-forced自回归训练，在更低的整体训练计算预算下取得更优的物理合理性；可视化显示FCP促成更结构化的表征空间。

Conclusion: Future-chunk监督是建模视觉演化的有效且高效手段，可为构建自回归世界模型提供更结构化的表征基础。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models generate videos chunk by chunk, yet producing plausible continuations does not guarantee that they capture how scenes evolve. Under teacher forcing, strong temporal redundancy may allow local visual continuity to serve as a shortcut for current-chunk denoising, reducing the pressure to model visual evolution. We introduce Future Chunk Prediction (FCP), an auxiliary objective jointly optimized with teacher-forced denoising that predicts temporally separated chunks without access to the intervening visual context. Drawing supervision directly from training videos, FCP requires no additional pretrained visual encoder and leaves autoregressive inference unchanged. Our method achieves superior physical plausibility over standard teacher-forced autoregressive training with a lower overall training compute budget, highlighting the effectiveness and efficiency of future-chunk supervision for modeling visual evolution. Intriguingly, our visualizations suggest that FCP fosters a more structured representation space, which may be valuable for building autoregressive world models.

</details>


### [113] [Expandable Modality Experts for Medical Vision-Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29881)
*DM BAPPY,Eunsu Kim,Hanjoo Lee,Sung Hak Lee,Sangjeong Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以可扩展"模态专家库"为核心的医学视觉-语言方法，通过多路Transformer（共享视觉计算+确定性路由的前馈专家+独立文本通路）在7种模态、1940万无配对图像上做掩码预训练，再以323万图文对做对比对齐，并以冻结主干、新增专家的方式扩展新模态，在9种模态25项基准中24项取得最佳或并列最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像跨模态学习面临两大需求：需利用无配对文本的图像，且需在吸收新模态的同时保留已有表征（避免灾难性遗忘）。现有视觉-语言模型难以同时满足扩展性与表征保持。

Method: 构建可扩展的模态专家库；Multiway Transformer将共享视觉计算与确定性路由的FFN专家结合，并设独立文本通路；模态感知视觉tokenizer为掩码预训练提供目标，使用7种模态19.4M无配对图像行；随后用3.23M图文对进行对比对齐；新增眼底与OCT专家时冻结原有图像、文本嵌入路径，仅训练新专家。主干为ViT-Large。

Result: 25项基准中24项最佳或并列最佳（9种模态）：零样本分类11项中10项、线性探针10项全部、检索4项全部。零样本平均平衡准确率0.674，线性探针0.856，平均双向检索召回20.70%。三随机种子顺序实验（适配数据匹配）显示眼底与OCT检索分别提升14.95与9.31个百分点，且在此前眼底/病理任务上无测得损失；特征恒等性检查验证了保留性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了模块化、可增量扩展的医学视觉-语言表征学习范式：表征保持依赖于不变的计算路径与推理条件，并通过特征恒等性检查验证；为多模态医学模型的持续扩展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Learning across medical imaging modalities requires making use of images without paired text and accommodating new domains while retaining existing representations. We propose a medical vision-language technique built around an expandable bank of modality experts. Its Multiway Transformer combines shared visual computation with deterministically routed feed-forward experts and a separate text pathway. A modality-aware visual tokenizer supplies targets for masked pretraining on 19.4 million unpaired image rows across seven modalities; contrastive alignment then uses 3.23 million image-text pairs. Additional fundus and optical coherence tomography experts learn from paired data while the original image and text embedding paths remain frozen. With a ViT-Large backbone, the proposed technique achieves the best or tied-best result in 24 of 25 benchmark settings across nine modalities: 10 of 11 zero-shot classification benchmarks, all 10 linear probes, and all four retrieval benchmarks. Mean balanced accuracy is 0.674 for zero-shot classification and 0.856 for linear probing, and mean bidirectional retrieval recall is 20.70%. A separate three-seed sequential experiment with matched adaptation data yields fundus and OCT retrieval gains of 14.95 and 9.31 percentage points, with no measured loss on preceding fundus or pathology tasks. The preservation argument requires unchanged existing computation paths and inference conditions, and feature-identity checks verify retention on evaluated inputs. The proposed technique provides a modular approach to learning and expanding medical vision-language representations.

</details>


### [114] [Stochastic Nonlinearities Improve Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29875)
*Mohammad Sarhangzadeh,Amirreza M. Shebly,Doruk Oner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将随机激活切换（stochastic activation switching）重新定位为一种轻量级不确定性估计机制：单次前向中在多个非线性函数间采样，产生预测多样性而不抑制中间特征，从而用单一训练模型获得不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的不确定性估计对视觉识别任务很重要。现有方法依赖抑制中间特征、扰动输入或大量额外计算（如集成、任务特定不确定性头），代价高或破坏信息通路。

Method: 在网络单元中于多个非线性激活函数之间随机采样（随机激活切换），保持中间特征通路激活，仅通过非线性选择引入预测多样性；无需集成训练、无需任务特定不确定性头、无需结构重设计。

Result: 在分类、语义分割和分布外检测任务上，使用卷积与Transformer架构及多数据集评估，不确定性质量提升，同时主任务性能保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 随机非线性提供了一种有效且信息保持的预测变异来源，可作为视觉识别中轻量、通用的不确定性估计手段。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimation is important across visual recognition tasks, providing information about the confidence and reliability of model predictions. Many uncertainty estimation methods obtain predictive diversity through mechanisms that can suppress intermediate features, perturb the input, or require substantial additional computation. Stochastic activation functions, where network units randomly select between multiple nonlinearities during a forward pass, have previously been used to improve optimization behavior, mitigate limitations of fixed ReLU activations, and increase output diversity. In this work, we reframe stochastic activation switching as a lightweight mechanism for uncertainty estimation. The proposed method samples between nonlinear activation functions, inducing predictive diversity while keeping intermediate feature pathways active. This enables uncertainty estimation from a single trained model without the training cost of ensembles, task-specific uncertainty heads, or architectural redesign. We evaluate the proposed approach across classification, semantic segmentation, and out-of-distribution detection using convolutional and transformer-based architectures on diverse datasets. Across these settings, stochastic activation switching improves uncertainty quality while preserving competitive primary-task performance, showing that stochastic nonlinearities provide an effective and information-preserving source of predictive variation for visual recognition.

</details>


### [115] [Rethinking Video Autoencoders for Interactive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30129)
*Adrien Ramanana Rahary,Yutong Bai,Nicolas Dufour,Patrick Pérez,David Picard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CADRE——一族可跨时空压缩率训练的因果扩散表示自编码器，系统研究世界模型"该在哪里、如何、压缩多少"，并给出四条实证发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型在自编码器潜空间生成，自编码器压缩率决定了单帧成本与动作生效延迟。但现有世界模型多沿用文本到视频生成器的自编码器，其为离线生成设计、无agent等待，且因缺少训练数据与代码无法在另一压缩率上重训。

Method: 构建CADRE：因果扩散表示自编码器开放族，覆盖多种时空压缩率；各成员仅在冻结特征上加一个小瓶颈，共享经自蒸馏压缩到一步或少步的扩散解码器。在每个自编码器上训练同一世界模型，覆盖操作与导航两域、各两个环境。

Result: CADRE占据延迟—质量前沿的大部分，移动相机场景尤甚。四项发现：(1)强压缩下扩散解码器比感知回归更锐利；(2)机器人操作中，时序压缩放在自编码器里比放在世界模型中更有效；(3)操作与导航的压缩需求相反——相机静止时时序压缩代价小，相机移动时空间压缩代价小；(4)相机静止时，更粗的空间token对运动的损害远大于对图像质量的影响。

Conclusion: 世界模型的压缩设计应域自适应：压缩位置与维度需依相机运动与任务类型取舍，而非沿用T2V自编码器默认配置。

Abstract: Video world models predict future frames from past frames and actions. They serve as simulators in which agents train, plan or are evaluated, and that humans can control interactively. They generate in the latent space of a video autoencoder, whose compression sets their cost per frame and how long each action waits to take effect. Yet world models often reuse the autoencoders of text-to-video generators, built for offline generation with no agent waiting and, without their training data and code, not retrainable at another compression. We therefore ask how, where and how much a world model should compress. We build CADRE, an open family of causal diffusion representation autoencoders spanning multiple spatio-temporal compressions. Its members differ only in a small bottleneck on frozen features and share a diffusion decoder self-distilled to one or a few steps. We train the same world model on each autoencoder in two domains, manipulation and navigation, with two environments each. CADRE forms most of the latency-quality frontier, especially with a moving camera. Our study also leads to four findings. At strong compression, a diffusion decoder is sharper than perceptual regression. In robot manipulation, temporal compression is more effective in the autoencoder than in the world model. Manipulation and navigation call for opposite compressions: temporal compression costs little when the camera is still, and spatial compression when it moves. Surprisingly, with a still camera, coarser spatial tokens hurt motion far more than image quality. Code, models and a live demo are at https://kyutai.org/blog/2026-10-21-cadre

</details>


### [116] [Training-free Acceleration for Blind Compressed Image Restoration via Activation-Calibrated Singular Value Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29959)
*Suhang Yao,Xin Li,Xijun Wang,Yufei Wang,Yeying Jin,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对基于Transformer的盲压缩图像复原（CIR）模型部署代价高的问题，提出免训练的激活校准奇异值分解（ASVD），对注意力、前馈、卷积与提示条件路径中的重复投影做低秩替换，在PromptCIR上rank=64时参数减少59%、计算减少16.1%，并在四个基准上稳定优于TSVD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer类盲CIR模型在未知压缩设置下性能强，但密集堆叠的投影算子使部署成本高昂；作者希望在不重训练、不改动推理图的前提下实现训练-free加速，并解释为何统一的低秩目标不适配压缩图像的通道不均衡特征分布。

Method: 采用算子级实证分析：剖析计算集中位置、刻画压缩输入下的通道级激活分布、考察代表性投影权重的奇异值衰减，据此锁定注意力/FFN/卷积/提示条件路径中的重复投影作为低秩替换目标；以TSVD作为免训练基线，进一步提出ASVD——仅用少量压缩校准图像收集的轻量激活统计，离线对预训练权重做通道级幅度加权的SVD分解，对高活跃通道误差施加更大惩罚。

Result: 在PromptCIR上以rank 64实例化，ASVD在相同参数与MAC预算下于四个标准基准上一致优于TSVD的PSNR-B；参数量由34.75M降至14.34M（-59%），256×256输入计算量由258.79降至217.16 GMACs（-16.1%）。

Conclusion: ASVD为盲压缩图像复原提供了一种无需重训练、无需在线校准、保持复原流水线完整的低秩加速方案，且通道校准的低秩目标比统一低秩目标更契合压缩图像的特征分布。

Abstract: Transformer-based models achieve strong performance on blind compressed image restoration (CIR) under unknown compression settings, but their densely stacked projection operators make deployment costly. We study training-free acceleration for such models through an empirical, operator-level analysis. Specifically, we profile where computation is concentrated, characterize channel-wise activation distributions under compressed inputs, and examine the singular-value decay of representative projection weights. These measurements identify the recurring projections in the attention, feed-forward, convolutional, and prompt-conditioning pathways as effective low-rank replacement targets that preserve the original inference graph. They further establish truncated SVD (TSVD) as a principled training-free baseline for blind CIR and reveal why a uniform low-rank objective is mismatched to the channel-imbalanced feature distributions of compressed images. Accordingly, we formulate Activation-Calibrated Singular Value Decomposition (ASVD) for this restoration setting, deriving low-rank factors entirely offline from pretrained weights and lightweight activation statistics gathered from only a few compressed calibration images. By weighting the decomposition with channel-wise activation magnitudes, our ASVD assigns larger penalties to errors on more active channels, requiring neither retraining nor online calibration and leaving the restoration pipeline intact. When instantiated on PromptCIR at rank 64, ASVD consistently improves PSNR-B over TSVD across four standard benchmarks under identical parameter and MAC budgets. At this operating point, the factorized model cuts parameters by 59% (34.75M to 14.34M) and computation by 16.1% (258.79 to 217.16 GMACs for a 256 x 256 input), enabling acceleration without retraining.

</details>


### [117] [CausalAlign: Causal Alignment of Video Generation Models for Consistent Ego-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29915)
*Kai Zhu,Yintianrun Zhang,Shuwei Jiang,Ruolin Zuo,Yixin Chen,Duomin Wang,Miao Liu,Yin Li,Daquan Zhou,Yiwu Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CausalAlign因果对齐框架，通过线性探针与因果干预诊断VGM中自运动表征的"先升后降"规律，并借助delta-token空间将指定层的表征对齐到3D基础模型，显著提升相机自运动与几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式视频生成模型（VGM）能生成视觉逼真视频，但相机自运动在长时序上易漂移。自运动决定帧间视角变化，是保持几何与轨迹一致性的关键，而现有VGM中其表征与保持机制尚不清晰。

Method: CausalAlign分两步：1）用线性探针与因果干预诊断精确自运动在VGM中被表征、使用与保留的方式，发现去噪过程中呈"先升后降"模式；2）在识别出的层上，通过delta-token空间（编码相邻帧间变化）将VGM表征对齐至3D基础模型，使对齐聚焦于自运动动态而非静态场景内容。基于Wan2.2在DL3DV与RealEstate10K上微调。

Result: 显著提升运动精度、几何一致性与时序平滑性，同时保持视觉质量；几何一致性较基座模型提升超20%。在RoboCasa365的移动操作任务中，微调后的VGM使世界动作模型（WAM）成功率提升10个百分点。

Conclusion: CausalAlign有效改善扩散式VGM的相机自运动一致性，并在具身模型中带来可观的性能增益。

Abstract: Diffusion-based video generation models (VGMs) can produce visually plausible videos, yet their camera ego-motion often drifts over long horizons. Camera ego-motion determines how the viewpoint changes between frames and is essential for maintaining consistent geometry and trajectories. To address this problem, we propose CausalAlign, a causal alignment framework with two components. First, we diagnose how precise ego motion is represented, used, and retained within VGMs using linear probes and causal interventions, revealing a rise-and-fall pattern during denoising. Second, we strengthen ego-motion state in VGM by aligning the representations at the identified layers to a 3D foundation model through delta-token space. This space encodes the changes between consecutive frames, allowing the alignment to focus on ego-motion dynamics rather than static scene content. With Wan2.2 fine-tuned on DL3DV and RealEstate10K, CausalAlign can largely improve motion accuracy, geometric consistency, and temporal smoothness, while preserving visual quality. For example, it improves geometric consistency by more than 20% relative to the base model. Moreover, with the fine-tuned VGM, CausalAlign improves the success rate of world action models (WAMs) by 10 points on mobile manipulation tasks of RoboCasa365 benchmark. Overall, CausalAlign improves ego-motion consistency in diffusion-based VGMs and yields noticeable gains in embodied models.

</details>


### [118] [Asking a Multimodal LLM Whether Someone Fell: Zero-Shot Screening of Low-Resolution Thermal Video in a Laboratory and an Occupied Home](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29913)
*Marcos Junca,Mauro Junca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估生产级多模态大模型（Gemini 3.8 Flash）作为事件触发式跌倒筛查器决策环节的性能：在公开热成像数据集上的留出受试者中召回率69%，499个非跌倒窗口零误报；76小时真实居家记录中395段零误报（误报率95%上界1.2次/天），但仅检出9次可见摆拍跌倒中的4次；提示词中一条保守性警告句是主要漏检来源，删除后灵敏度显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独居老人监护需在隐私与有效性间平衡：热成像相机不采集人脸，多模态大模型无需任务特定训练即可判断短视频。作者希望检验"现成部署形态"（每段短热成像片段一次请求、含音轨、温度0）下该方案的真实表现，并与在实验室数据上训练的传统检测器在真实居家场景中的误报率作对比。

Method: 将Gemini 3.8 Flash（temperature=0）置于事件触发跌倒筛查器的决策环节，按已部署原型的方式调用：每段短热成像片段一次请求，有音轨则一并输入；并在相同请求上与上一代Gemini 3.7 Flash对比。评测数据：公开32x24热成像数据集（人工标注438段摆拍跌倒、563个非跌倒窗口）；另有160x120带声音的闲置房间76小时真实生活记录（395段片段、9次可见摆拍跌倒）。分析提示词句子消融、分辨率降采样（8x6像素）、请求重复一致性、解释时间定位准确性，并与两个基于实验室数据训练的传统检测器对比。

Result: 留出受试者跌倒召回69%（全受试者67%，不含床上跌倒），499个非跌倒窗口零误报（含躺地、起身）；76小时居家记录中395段全部未误报（误报95%上界1.2次/天），但仅检出9次可见摆拍跌倒中的4次。删除提示词中"离开视野者不算跌倒"一句后，留出受试者灵敏度由69%升至83%（3.8 Flash）、由80%升至92%（3.7 Flash），居家检出由4/9升至9/9，且日常活动无误报，代价是把刻意躺下判为跌倒。分辨率降至8x6像素时灵敏度崩溃至34%；相同请求间4%判断不一致；判为跌倒时解释落点与标注落地时刻误差在1秒内的比例为99%。两个传统检测器几乎检出全部留出实验室跌倒，但居家误报分别达14.2与23.9次/天。

Conclusion: 生产级多模态大模型可在零样本、单次请求的部署形态下实现极低误报的居家跌倒筛查，但灵敏度受提示词措辞强烈影响，且模型对分辨率与请求随机性敏感；实验室训练的传统检测器在真实居家环境中误报不可接受。提示词保守表述是灵敏度与特异性之间的关键调节杠杆，需谨慎设计；作者同时公开该数据集的帧级跌倒标注及全部模型回答。

Abstract: Thermal cameras can watch over older people living alone without capturing faces, and multimodal large language models (LLMs) can judge a short video without task-specific training. We evaluate a production multimodal LLM, Gemini 3.8 Flash at temperature 0, as the decision stage of an event-triggered fall screener, used exactly as a deployed prototype uses it: one request per short thermal clip, with its sound track when there is one, and its predecessor Gemini 3.7 Flash on the same requests. On 438 staged falls and 563 non-fall windows that we located by hand in a public 32x24 thermal dataset, the deployed request called 69% of the held-out subjects' falls (67% over all subjects; bed falls excluded) and none of 499 non-fall windows, including people lying on the floor and getting up. Over 76 h of ordinary life in an occupied room, recorded at 160x120 with sound, it called none of 395 clips a fall (95% upper bound 1.2 false alarms per day) and caught 4 of the 9 staged falls the camera could see. One cautionary sentence in the prompt ("a person who goes out of view ... is not a fall") accounted for many of the misses: removing it raised sensitivity on the held-out subjects from 69% to 83% on 3.8 Flash and from 80% to 92% on Gemini 3.7 Flash, and in the home from 4 to 9 of the 9 visible falls, with no false call in ordinary living but at the price of calling deliberate lie-downs a fall. Sensitivity collapsed at 8x6 pixels (34%); identical requests disagreed on 4% of windows; and when the model called a fall, its explanation placed it within a second of the annotated landing 99% of the time. Two conventional detectors trained on the laboratory data caught nearly every held-out laboratory fall but raised 14.2 and 23.9 false alarms per day in the home. We release the frame-level fall annotations of the public dataset and every model answer on it.

</details>


### [119] [ALPS: Visual State Encoders for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30120)
*Yanqing Liu,Letian Zhang,Chengzhi Liu,Zijun Wang,Jieru Mei,Hongliang Fei,Yuyin Zhou,Cihang Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALPS：一个统一的视觉状态编码器，用单一 patch token 同时支撑物理状态预测、语言对齐与像素重建，冻结特征在 LIBERO 策略与多任务世界模型规划中表现领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的视觉基础应同时具备物理状态信息、原生语言对齐与像素重建能力，从而用同一表征支持预测、指令跟随与可视化检查；但现有编码器通常只擅长其中一到两项。

Method: 从零预训练 ViT-B/16，仅用静态图像与文本，联合语言、重建与空间对应（spatial correspondence）三类目标，再通过跨视角自蒸馏（cross-view self-distillation）精炼。

Result: 在十五个公开编码器的统一评测中，ALPS 在 ViT-B 模型中取得最低的 LIBERO 状态探针聚合误差（与体量大 3.5 倍的 V-JEPA 2-L 相当），同时保持较强语言对齐与准确像素重建；冻结 token 配合原生文本塔在 LIBERO 上达最高平均策略成功率 85.5%，在 PushT、Wall、Granular 上匹配或超越领先编码器，且预测的未来 token 可直接解码为像素。

Conclusion: 视觉预训练可作为世界模型中理解、预测与动作的共享基础，单一表征可同时服务三类下游需求。

Abstract: World models turn visual representations into predictions and actions. We argue that their visual foundation should combine physical-state information, native language alignment, and pixel reconstruction, so that a single representation can support prediction, instruction, and inspection. However, existing encoders typically specialize in only one or two of these capabilities; we therefore introduce ALPS, a pretrained visual state encoder that unifies all three in a single set of patch tokens. Using only still images and text, we pretrain a ViT-B/16 from scratch with language, reconstruction, and spatial correspondence objectives, and then refine it via cross-view self-distillation. In a unified evaluation across fifteen public encoders, ALPS achieves the lowest aggregate LIBERO state-probe error among ViT-B models (matching the 3.5x larger V-JEPA 2-L) while maintaining strong language alignment and accurate pixel reconstruction. Downstream, frozen ALPS tokens reach the highest mean policy success on LIBERO (85.5%) when paired with our native text tower, match or exceed leading encoders on world-model planning across PushT, Wall, and Granular, and allow predicted future tokens to be decoded directly into pixels for visual inspection. We hope this work helps establish visual pretraining as a shared foundation for understanding, prediction, and action in world models.

</details>


### [120] [ClunoGS: Compact Language 3D Gaussian Splatting via Unified Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30013)
*Jian Xu,Yi Li,Ying Huang,Siyi Wu,Bingzhe Li,Yuede Ji,Miao Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ClunoGS，将语义感知的高斯稀疏化与向量量化统一为一个带约束的目标，实现语言3DGS的紧凑压缩，在激进压缩下保持语义精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言3DGS需为高斯基元关联语言特征，显著增加存储；现有3DGS压缩方法主要为保持RGB渲染质量而设计。若简单采用"稀疏化+量化"顺序流程，仅按渲染质量剪枝会删除RGB贡献小但对语义预测重要（一旦删除其语义信息无法被后续量化恢复）的高斯。

Method: 提出统一约束目标，将语义监督引入稀疏化过程，目标同时考虑渲染质量、语义精度与紧凑性；采用交替优化：原始变量更新、稀疏化、量化与对偶变量更新的交替求解。

Result: 在LERF与ScanNet-10上的开放词汇定位与分割任务中，激进压缩下统一语义感知公式显著缓解了顺序压缩与语义无关稀疏化造成的语义表示退化；消融表明联合协调稀疏化与量化比独立执行更能保持语义精度。

Conclusion: 语义感知的联合稀疏化—量化框架可有效压缩语言3DGS并在高压缩率下保持开放词汇语义能力，说明压缩设计需兼顾语义而非仅渲染质量。

Abstract: Language 3D Gaussian Splatting enables open-vocabulary scene understanding by associating Gaussian primitives with language features. These semantic attributes increase model storage, while most existing 3DGS compression methods are designed mainly to preserve RGB rendering quality. One straightforward approach is to apply sparsification and quantization sequentially. However, pruning based only on rendering quality can remove Gaussians that have small RGB contributions but are important for semantic prediction. Once removed, their semantic information cannot be recovered by later quantization. We propose ClunoGS, a compact framework for Language 3DGS that combines semantics-aware Gaussian sparsification and vector quantization in a unified constrained objective. Semantic supervision is used during sparsification, and the objective jointly considers rendering quality, semantic accuracy, and compactness. We optimize the unified objective with alternating primal, sparsification, quantization, and dual updates. We evaluate ClunoGS on open-vocabulary localization and segmentation using the LERF and ScanNet-10 benchmarks. Under aggressive compression, our unified, semantics-aware formulation substantially alleviates the degradation of semantic representations caused by sequential compression and semantics-agnostic Gaussian sparsification. Ablations further show that jointly coordinating sparsification and quantization better preserves semantic accuracy than applying them independently.

</details>


### [121] [ViBMR: A Large-Scale Multi-Sequence Brain MRI Dataset and Comprehensive Evaluation for Vietnamese Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29905)
*Dai Lam Le,Minh Khoi Nguyen,Son Dinh Nguyen,Amir Reza Jafari,Viet Ha,Nguyen Son,Reza Farahbakhsh,Noel Crespi,Phi Le Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布 ViBMR——首个大规模多序列脑 MRI 与越南语常规临床报告配对数据集（3,649 名患者、11,875 个序列，覆盖 T1/T2/FLAIR/CE-T1），并建立脑 MRI 报告生成的综合评测框架，涵盖代表性医学视觉-语言模型、多序列输入与特征聚合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑 MRI 自动报告生成可缓解放射科医师负担，但面临三重挑战：脑 MRI 本质上是三维体数据、各脉冲序列携带互补诊断信息、低资源临床语言标注数据极度稀缺。现有多序列脑 MRI 报告资源以英文报告、翻译文本或由任务型影像基准改造的数据集为主，难以覆盖常规低资源临床报告流程与异构多序列协议，因此需要原生低资源临床数据集。

Method: 构建 ViBMR 数据集：3,649 名患者、11,875 个 MRI 序列，包含 T1、T2、FLAIR 与增强 T1 四种序列，并与常规越南语临床报告配对。基于该数据建立统一评测设置：对比代表性医学视觉-语言模型、不同多序列输入方式与特征聚合策略。

Result: 实验揭示了该数据集的关键特性与模型行为规律，并表明原生低资源临床数据集对于可靠的越南语脑 MRI 报告生成是必要的；翻译式或改造型数据难以替代真实临床分布。

Conclusion: ViBMR 填补了多序列脑 MRI 与低资源语言临床报告配对的空白，为多序列脑 MRI 报告生成提供了数据与评测基准。数据集与基线模型将面向非商业研究开放。

Abstract: Automated radiology report generation for multi-sequence brain MRI holds strong promise for alleviating radiologists' workload, yet it remains a demanding problem: brain MRI studies are inherently volumetric, each pulse sequence carries complementary diagnostic information, and annotated data are critically scarce for low-resource clinical languages. Existing brain MRI-report resources are still dominated by English reports, translated clinical text, or datasets adapted from task-specific imaging benchmarks, leaving routine low-resource clinical reporting and heterogeneous multi-sequence MRI protocols insufficiently represented. To bridge this gap, we introduce ViBMR, to the best of our knowledge, the first large-scale multi-sequence brain MRI dataset paired with routine Vietnamese clinical reports, comprising 3,649 patients and 11,875 MRI series across T1, T2, FLAIR, and contrast-enhanced T1 sequences. Building on ViBMR, we establish a comprehensive evaluation setup for brain MRI report generation, covering representative medical vision-language models, multi-sequence inputs, and feature aggregation strategies. The results provide key insights into dataset characteristics and model behavior, while demonstrating the necessity of native low-resource clinical datasets for reliable Vietnamese-origin brain MRI report generation. Our dataset and baseline models will be released for non-commercial research.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [How to Use Parallel Data for Multilingual Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28838)
*Zhihong Huang,Yao Lu,Derek F. Wong,Hamid Alinejad-Rokny,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出“平行预训练（PPT）”，将原文与译文拼接进同一训练序列，并与标准预训练（SPT）在相同token预算下从头训练对比；发现PPT单独使用并不能稳定提升通用语言理解，但能构建更强的跨语言基础。据此提出“两阶段”方案：先PPT构建跨语言基础，再用SPT适配标准语言建模，在高低资源场景下均优于单独SPT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量人工平行语料规模过小，难以支撑大规模预训练；机器翻译的网页平行语料虽可达到预训练规模，但标准多语言预训练独立采样原文与译文，未显式利用其平行结构。作者希望探究如何利用平行结构提升多语言预训练。

Method: 提出平行预训练（PPT）：把每篇文档与其译文拼接为同一训练序列，从而显式利用平行结构；与标准预训练（SPT）在匹配token预算下从零训练进行对照。进一步在“预训练—微调”范式下设计两阶段方案：第一阶段用PPT构建跨语言基础，第二阶段用SPT将其适配回标准语言建模。

Result: 与先前跨语言工作中平行数据带来收益的结论不同，PPT单独使用并不能稳定提升语言理解，甚至相对SPT无一致优势；分析表明PPT虽能更好利用跨语言共享信息、构建强跨语言基础，但平行训练上下文与单语言任务输入之间的不匹配限制了其在通用语言理解上的收益。两阶段方案在高低资源设置、相同训练预算下，均稳定优于单独SPT，提升通用语言理解与跨语言任务表现。

Conclusion: 平行结构本身不足以直接带来通用语言理解增益，关键在于训练上下文与下游输入形式的匹配；将PPT用于“打基础”、SPT用于“做适配”的两阶段策略，能有效兑现平行数据的跨语言价值。

Abstract: High-quality human-translated parallel corpora can improve cross-lingual transfer, but are typically too small for large-scale pretraining. Machine-translated web corpora help overcome this limitation, making parallel data available at pretraining scale. Standard multilingual pretraining, however, samples source documents and their translations independently, without explicitly using their parallel structure. We investigate how to use the parallel structure of the data to improve multilingual pretraining by concatenating each document with its translation in the same training sequence. We compare this parallel pretraining (PPT) approach with standard pretraining (SPT), training models from scratch at matched token budgets. Surprisingly, despite the benefits of parallel data reported in prior cross-lingual work, PPT alone does not consistently improve language understanding over SPT. Our further analysis suggests that although PPT builds a strong cross-lingual foundation, as shown by its better use of shared information across languages, the mismatch between parallel training contexts and single-language task inputs limits its gains in general language understanding. This finding motivates a two-stage approach following the pretraining-fine-tuning paradigm: we first use PPT to build a cross-lingual foundation, then continue with SPT to adapt it to standard language modeling. In both high-resource and low-resource settings, the two-stage approach consistently improves general language understanding and cross-lingual task performance over standard pretraining alone at matched training budgets.

</details>


### [123] [Scaling Therapy Supervision: Assessing Psychotherapy Fidelity Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28772)
*Arvind Pillai,Alexis Roach,Jason Holden,Varsha D Badal,Subigya Nepal,Yu Yvonne Wu,Yuliang Chen,Sudarshan Regmi,Sarah Masud Preum,Nicholas C Jacobson,Nikhil Singh,Eric Granholm,Colin Depp,Andrew Campbell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于大语言模型的集成应用，自动化心理治疗保真度评估流程并从真实长程临床转录文本生成可执行的督导反馈，实现可扩展的治疗督导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保真度评估是影响患者疗效的关键因素，但依赖专家人工审阅，耗时且难以规模化，造成社区心理卫生督导缺口。

Method: 在真实长程临床转录文本上，评估三种开放权重模型，采用多种任务分解配置与推理策略；提出"证据优先"（evidence-first）推理；在独立随机对照试验数据上检验外部构念效度；并通过专家用户研究评估生成反馈。

Result: 证据优先推理提升了保真度估计；框架在独立RCT上可靠区分CBT与非CBT会谈的核心治疗技能；心理健康专家认为该流程可生成可执行的临床反馈。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了实用、可扩展的解决方案，有助于弥合社区心理卫生督导缺口。

Abstract: Psychotherapy fidelity assessment measures how faithfully a therapist adheres to a treatment protocol, a critical determinant of positive patient outcomes. However, fidelity assessment is bottlenecked by manual expert review, rendering the process labor-intensive and unscalable. To scale therapy supervision and support clinician self-learning, we present an integrated application of large language models (LLMs) that automates psychotherapy fidelity workflows and generates actionable supervisory feedback from authentic, long-form clinical transcripts. We evaluate three open-weight models across multiple task decomposition configurations and reasoning strategies, demonstrating that evidence-first reasoning improves fidelity estimation. Furthermore, we establish the framework's external construct validity on an independent randomized controlled trial (RCT), where it reliably distinguishes Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) from non-CBT sessions across core therapeutic skills. Through a user study that evaluates the generated feedback, mental health experts indicate that the pipeline synthesizes actionable clinical feedback. Ultimately, our work offers a practical, scalable solution toward bridging the community mental health supervision gap.

</details>


### [124] [How Far Can the Gemma 4 Speech Encoder Go Without Changing Its Weights? Saudi and Gulf Arabic Speech Recognition with a Frozen and an Adapter-Augmented Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28673)
*Amr Abdelkhalik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究冻结的Gemma 4/EmbeddingGemma 2共享305M音频编码器在沙特与海湾阿拉伯语语音识别上的可用性：仅训练轻量CTC或低秩适配+混合CTC/注意力读出层，即可在多数条件下CER与2.07B的Cohere Transcribe相当甚至更优，且CPU速度快3.4倍、单GPU达139倍实时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估能否在不改动大模型编码器权重的前提下，以极小的可训练开销为沙特/海湾阿拉伯语构建高性价比ASR系统，并与大型商业模型在可比的同音频条件下做统计性对比。

Method: 不改动305M编码器权重。冻结系统仅训练22.6M的CTC读出层（混合多种编码器层特征）；适配系统额外加入3.1M低秩适配器、41.5M混合CTC/注意力读出层，并与子词语言模型做联合解码。对比基线为Cohere Transcribe Arabic(2.07B)、SADA微调的0.6B识别器；在六个人工标注条件及公开/盲测客服电话样本上评估WER与CER，并进行统计显著性检验与解码效率测试；同时考察"机器草稿+人工修正"参考文本对起草系统的偏好偏差。

Result: 冻结系统WER落后但六条件中五条CER与Cohere无显著差异，CPU贪心解码快3.4倍。适配系统在公开沙特客服通话(19.20% vs 19.27%)与干净阿联酋语音上WER无显著差异，在电话编码阿联酋语音上显著更优，六个条件CER均显著更低，并在全部六条件WER上优于SADA微调的0.6B模型；长时沙特语音WER仍落后Cohere。盲测人工校正客服样本中15系统排名第4，落后三个商业API（仅一个显著），以点估计领先其他可自托管系统。优化联合解码单GPU达139倍实时。

Conclusion: 以冻结大编码器+轻量读出/适配的可训练方案，可在海湾阿拉伯语ASR上接近或部分超越参数量大得多的商业模型，并具显著计算优势；同时提示基于机器草稿修正的参考文本存在偏向起草系统的评估偏差，故需谨慎解读此类基准结果。代码与公开集结果已发布。

Abstract: We study the 305M-parameter audio encoder shared by Gemma 4 and EmbeddingGemma 2 (identical except for its final projection) for Saudi and Gulf Arabic speech recognition, without changing any of its weights. A frozen system trains only a 22.6M-parameter CTC reader over a mix of encoder layers; an adapted system adds 3.1M low-rank adapter parameters, a 41.5M-parameter hybrid CTC/attention reader and joint decoding with a sub-word language model. Against Cohere Transcribe Arabic (2.07B parameters), re-run on identical audio, the frozen system trails in word error rate (WER) but is statistically indistinguishable in character error rate (CER) on five of six human-labelled conditions, while running 3.4 times faster on CPU with greedy decoding. The adapted system is statistically indistinguishable in WER on a public Saudi customer-service call (19.20% against 19.27%) and on clean Emirati speech, is significantly better on telephone-coded Emirati speech, has a significantly lower CER on all six conditions of these sets, and beats a 0.6B recogniser fine-tuned on SADA in WER on all six; it still trails Cohere in WER on long-form Saudi speech. On a blind, human-corrected public call-centre sample it ranks 4 of 15 systems, behind three commercial APIs (significantly only one) and ahead of the other self-hostable systems by point estimate. An optimised joint decoder runs it at 139 times real time on one GPU. We also show that references made by correcting machine drafts favour the drafting system. Code and public-set results are released.

</details>


### [125] [B-CARE: Blind Classwise Assessment of Relative Evidence for Stance Error Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29350)
*Meysam Sabbaghan,Doina Caragea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出B-CARE：仅基于目标与文本、由固定LLM验证器对所有候选标签给出序数证据分，通过对比最强替代类别证据与预测类证据构建比较风险分，实现预测盲的姿态错误排序，且证据画像可在多基模型间复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姿态分类器会出错，而人工复核预算有限，需优先排查潜在错误预测；在异构基模型间统一、可迁移地识别高风险预测仍具挑战，且训练专用错误检测器或校准风险分成本高。

Method: B-CARE为预测盲框架：给定目标与文本，固定LLM验证器在不知基模型预测与置信度的情况下，为所有候选姿态标签赋予序数证据分；再以"最强替代类别证据"对比"支持预测类别的证据"构造比较风险分；所得类别级证据画像无需额外训练检测器或校准即可跨多个基模型复用。评测覆盖4个姿态基准、3个异构基LLM，共12个数据集-模型设置、5206个测试实例，指标含AUROC/AUPRC、选择性预测E-AURC及固定复核预算下的ErrorCapture。

Result: 平均AUROC 0.735、平均AUPRC 0.524，在最高风险20%预测中捕获41.06%的错误；虽未全面优于强置信度基线，但提供了可复用、互补的证据信号。

Conclusion: 预测盲的类别级证据对比可作为跨异构基模型的通用错误排序信号，与置信度方法互补，适用于有限人工复核预算下的姿态错误优先排查。

Abstract: Stance classifiers make errors that can be costly to identify under limited human-review budgets. Prioritizing potentially incorrect predictions remains challenging, particularly across heterogeneous base models. We introduce B-CARE (Blind Classwise Assessment of Relative Evidence for Stance Error Ranking), a prediction-blind framework for identifying high-risk stance predictions. Given only a target and text, a fixed LLM verifier assigns ordinal evidence scores to all candidate stance labels without observing the base model's prediction or confidence. B-CARE constructs a comparative risk score by contrasting the strongest alternative-class evidence with the evidence supporting the predicted class. The resulting classwise evidence profile can be reused across multiple base models without training a separate error detector or calibrating risk scores. We evaluate B-CARE on four stance benchmarks and three heterogeneous base LLMs, covering 12 dataset-model settings and 5,206 unique test instances. Evaluation includes error-ranking discrimination (AUROC and AUPRC), selective prediction (E-AURC), and review effectiveness (ErrorCapture at fixed review budgets). B-CARE achieves a mean AUROC of 0.735 and a mean AUPRC of 0.524, capturing 41.06% of errors within the highest-risk 20% of predictions. Although B-CARE does not uniformly outperform strong confidence-based baselines, it provides a reusable, complementary evidence signal for prioritizing potentially incorrect stance predictions across heterogeneous models.

</details>


### [126] [Policy Central: Detecting Conflicts, Redundancy, and Similarity in Deployed Police Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29338)
*Holly Baker,Michael Kuo,Kyana Pollas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Policy Central 是一个已部署系统，用两种架构（Retrieve-Judge 检索-判定流水线、Screen-Confirm 筛选-确认级联）发现警务政策条文间的关系（冲突、冗余、相似、不相关），核心困难在于"找到候选对"而非"判定"，但在低成本和可重复运行上已改变政策审查的经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 警务部门政策文本量大（约84份文档、近2万条），目前靠人工通读全库、两两比对、且每次修订后重查来发现条文间冲突或冗余，成本极高。作者希望用自动化方式提供廉价的全库健康信号与按需的定向审计。

Method: 对比两种架构：Retrieve-Judge（先检索再分类的两阶段流水线，作为低成本全库扫描信号）与 Screen-Confirm（先粗筛再确认的级联，对审查者选定文档做穷尽式两两核对）。在来自某部门约84份真实政策文档的302对基准上评估，分别测量"给定配对"的判定召回与"端到端找对"的组合召回。

Result: 给定配对时 Screen-Confirm 聚合召回0.795，Conflict 类达0.885，说明现有模型判定能力较强；但组合召回（找到配对并正确判定）Retrieve-Judge 仅0.457，Screen-Confirm 为0.472（Conflict 0.615），瓶颈在检索/筛选阶段。

Conclusion: "找对"是主要瓶颈，端到端召回仍明显偏低。然而系统能在数分钟内以低成本返回约一半的相关配对，并可随时重跑，已显著改变政策审查的经济学，即便在召回进一步提升之前亦然。

Abstract: Policy Central is a deployed system that helps police departments find relationships between sections of their written policy: whether two sections conflict, are redundant, are merely similar, or are different. We describe the system and compare two architectures. Retrieve-Judge, a retrieve-then-classify pipeline, runs as a cheap library-wide health signal; Screen-Confirm, a screen-then-confirm cascade, is a selective audit of the documents a reviewer chooses, checking them exhaustively against one another. On a 302-pair benchmark drawn from one department's ~84 real policy documents, given each pair Screen-Confirm reaches 0.795 aggregate recall and 0.885 on Conflict, so current models judge well. Finding the pair is the hard part: Retrieve-Judge's composed recall falls to 0.457, and Screen-Confirm recovers 0.472 (0.615 on Conflict). Today these pairs are found by reading the whole library, nearly 20,000 sections, checked against one another and re-checked after every revision. A scan that returns half of them in minutes, at low cost, and can be re-run on demand changes the economics of policy review even before recall improves.

</details>


### [127] [Seeing the Forest for the Trees: Emergent Agreement from Individual Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29328)
*Adi Simhi,Yaniv Nikankin,Jonathan Herzig,Idan Szpektor,Yonatan Belinkov,Shay B. Cohen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究多轮对话中两个LLM就主观话题达成一致的动态，发现单条对话内部激活中的"一致"表征不稳定，但跨对话聚合后呈现马尔可夫结构与幂律耦合，且立场改变后外部表达先于内部表征更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在真实环境中相互交互并影响彼此决策，因此理解它们如何趋同于一致（agreement）具有重要性；但已有工作缺乏对一致性的内部表征与外部行为之间关系的刻画。

Method: 构建两个LLM就主观话题进行多轮讨论、可同意/不同意并改变立场的对话数据；在单条对话激活上训练线性分类器以识别"一致"的内部表征；再将一致表征跨对话聚合，并与外部一致行为（马尔可夫过程建模）对照，分析内外部耦合关系及立场转变的时间顺序。

Result: 单条对话层面的一致表征随轮次与对话大幅波动，无法可靠追踪外部一致；聚合后外部一致可由马尔可夫过程很好预测（当轮一致是上一轮一致的直接函数）；内部聚合一致与外部马尔可夫一致通过幂律映射耦合，显示内部表征的马尔可夫动力学；立场改变后，模型先在外表达一致，内部表征随后才反映该转变。

Conclusion: 内部与外部一致之间存在联合结构，但这种结构只有在内部特征跨对话聚合时才显现；单对话层面的内部表征不足以刻画一致动态，需采用分布/聚合视角。

Abstract: As LLM agents interact in the wild and shape each other's decisions, understanding how they converge toward agreement becomes important. We study this in multi-turn conversations where two LLMs discuss subjective matters, can agree or disagree, and may change their stances. Using a linear classifier on individual conversation activations, we identify an internal representation of agreement. We find that it varies substantially across turns and conversations and does not reliably track external agreement at the individual conversation level. We therefore move beyond the representation of agreement in individual conversations, by aggregating the agreement representation across conversations. Externally, this aggregate view reveals a simple structure: when pooled across conversations, agreement can be predicted well with a Markov process, with agreement at each turn being a direct function of agreement at the previous turn. Similarly, when internal agreement is aggregated, it couples with the external Markov agreement through a power-law mapping, exposing Markovian dynamics in internal representations. Additionally, we find that after a change of stance, models express agreement externally before their internal representations reflect the same shift. These results reveal a joint structure between internal and external agreement that emerges only when internal features are aggregated across conversations. Code available at https://github.com/technion-cs-nlp/SeeingTheForestForTheTrees.

</details>


### [128] [OSynth: A Longitudinal, Context-Sensitive Evaluation of Meeting Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29319)
*Ian Porada,Suraj Tripathi,Chaitanya Animesh,Leda Sari,Chaitanya Narisetty,Austin R. Matthews,Bohan Yu,Ramesh L. Thulasiram,Gregory Sell,Kean Chin,Dmitriy Genzel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 OSynth：一个大规模合成会议数据集，包含模拟组织内约 570 小时会议音频及配套工件（日历、历史会议、邮件、幻灯片、说话人注册信息）与潜在的真实状态标注，用于研究长时序上下文的会议理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会议记录助手已在实际场景中使用日历元数据、既往会议、邮件、幻灯片、说话人注册等长时序上下文，但现有会议语料在规模、领域覆盖及可用上下文方面均受限，难以支撑上下文感知的会议理解系统研发。

Method: 构建 OSynth 合成数据集：以模拟组织的潜在真实状态为基准，生成 570 小时会议音频并同步衍生多种配套工件与上下文信息；通过人类成对判别实验（150–300 词摘录）检验真实性，并在多个最先进 LLM 上评测额外上下文对实体敏感维度的影响。

Result: 人类评审仅以 56% 的准确率区分 OSynth 会议与真实会议转写，接近随机水平；在多个 SOTA LLM 上，引入额外上下文在若干实体敏感维度上带来显著提升。

Conclusion: OSynth 具有较高的真实性，可作为发展上下文增强型会议理解系统的大规模资源，同时暴露了现有方法在上下文利用上的局限性。

Abstract: Meeting notetakers are increasingly deployed in settings where longitudinal context is available including calendar metadata, prior meetings, emails, slides, and speaker enrollments; however, meeting corpora remain limited in terms of size, domain coverage, and available context. We present OSynth, a large-scale synthetic dataset of simulated organizations comprising 570 hours of meeting audio together with associated artifacts and a latent ground-truth state. In a pairwise comparison, human raters distinguish OSynth meetings from real meeting transcripts with only 56% accuracy based on excerpts of 150-300 words. Across state-of-the-art LLMs, additional context yields significant gains on several entity-sensitive dimensions, demonstrating limitations of contemporary approaches and the potential usefulness of OSynth in developing better-contextualized meeting-understanding systems.

</details>


### [129] [Credit Where Credit Is Due: Contrastive Attribution Topology Search for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29292)
*Fabian Tritthart,Tom Zehle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CATS 用 LLM 归因器对候选拓扑执行轨迹做对比式信用分配，据此更新并剪枝超图，得到紧凑高效的多智能体团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 多智能体系统的精度与成本取决于拓扑（包含哪些智能体、如何通信），但单一任务分数无法揭示哪些智能体有益或有害，缺少细粒度诊断信号指导团队设计。

Method: 在固定的专业智能体超图上搜索紧凑团队。LLM 归因器跨候选拓扑比较执行轨迹，为单个智能体分配信用；用这些信用更新超图边，并逐步剪除弱边与弱智能体，最终得到小规模部署团队。实验采用小规模开源权重模型与对齐的候选评估。

Result: BigCodeBench 上平均精度较仅用分数的启发式搜索提升 5.3 个百分点，部署 token 减少 54%；MBPP 上以少于其他搜索的 token 匹配最强基线；HotpotQA 提示投毒压力测试中，三次运行均排除全部被对抗性提示的智能体，而随机搜索在相近精度下仍保留部分此类智能体。

Conclusion: 逐智能体信用既能驱动团队优化，也能剪除潜在有害智能体，说明归因信号可同时服务于性能、成本与鲁棒性目标。

Abstract: An LLM multi-agent system (MAS) is only as good as the agents it chooses to trust: its accuracy and cost hinge on its topology, meaning which agents are included and how they communicate. A single task score, however, does not reveal which agents help or hinder performance. We propose Contrastive Attribution Topology Search (CATS), which searches for compact teams within a fixed supergraph of specialized agents. An LLM attributer compares execution traces across candidate topologies and assigns credit to individual agents. CATS uses these credits to update supergraph edges while progressively pruning weak edges and agents, yielding a small team for deployment. With small open-weight models and matched candidate evaluations, CATS improves mean accuracy on BigCodeBench by 5.3 percentage points over score-only heuristic search while using 54\% fewer deployment tokens. On MBPP, CATS matches the strongest baselines with fewer tokens than the other searches. In a HotpotQA prompt-poisoning stress test, CATS excludes all adversarially prompted agents in all three runs, whereas random search retains some at comparable accuracy. Per-agent credit thus supports both team optimization and the pruning of potentially harmful agents.

</details>


### [130] [A Large-Scale Evaluation of Role-Enhanced Zero-Shot Chain-of-Thought Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29073)
*Ibrahim Khan,Thai Van Nguyen,Yi Xia,Pratch Suntichaikul,Mustafa Can Gursesli,Daniel Fernandez Galeote,Juho Hamari,Ruck Thawonmas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出角色增强零样本思维链（RE-ZS-CoT）提示框架，将领域专家角色系统性地融入零样本CoT推理，通过静态与动态两种角色选择方式，在20个LLM和7个基准上实现最高44个百分点的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT提示通常依赖任务特定示例，成本高且泛化受限；同时角色扮演对LLM推理的系统性影响尚缺乏在零样本设置下的系统评估。作者希望在不使用任务示例的前提下，通过角色专门化与思维链推理的统一来增强LLM跨领域推理能力，并提供资源高效的实用方案。

Method: 提出RE-ZS-CoT框架。版本一为静态角色选择：在算术、常识与阅读理解基准上评估八种预定义专家角色对推理表现的影响；版本二为动态角色选择：由模型自主为每个基准挑选最合适的单一角色，实现无需人工指定的自适应任务—角色对齐。实验覆盖20个LLM、7个基准，并进行非常规角色的消融研究及2b–14b参数规模的扩展性验证。

Result: RE-ZS-CoT频繁超越零样本与Zero-Shot-CoT基线，性能提升最高达44个百分点；静态与动态版本结果高度一致——静态版本带来提升的基准几乎都是动态版本也优于基线的基准，但静态选择的峰值性能更高。消融显示非常规角色会降低性能，说明任务—角色对齐的必要性。扩展性方面，小模型（如Granite3.3-8b）在算术推理上表现突出。

Conclusion: RE-ZS-CoT将角色专门化与思维链推理统一于零样本提示，验证了任务—角色对齐的关键作用，为跨领域提升LLM能力提供了实用且资源高效的方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes Role-Enhanced Zero-Shot Chain-of-Thought (RE-ZS-CoT), a prompting framework that systematically integrates domain-specific expert roles to enhance reasoning in large language models (LLMs) without task-specific examples. We assess two versions of our approach. In the first version, static role selection, we evaluate eight predefined expert roles across arithmetic, commonsense, and reading comprehension benchmarks to determine their impact on reasoning performance. In the second version, dynamic role selection, each model autonomously selects the single most suitable role for each benchmark, enabling adaptive task-role alignment without manual specification. Across 20 LLMs and seven benchmarks, RE-ZS-CoT frequently surpasses zero-shot and zero-shot-CoT baselines, achieving performance improvements of up to 44 percentage points. The results reveal a strong correspondence between static and dynamic versions; the benchmarks where static roles yielded improvements were almost always those where dynamic roles also outperformed baselines, though static selection achieved peak performance. Ablation studies further show that unconventional roles degrade performance, underscoring the necessity of task-role alignment. The framework's scalability is validated across model sizes (2b-14b parameters), with smaller models like Granite3.3-8b achieving remarkable proficiency in arithmetic reasoning. RE-ZS-CoT advances zero-shot prompting by unifying role specialization with chain-of-thought reasoning, offering a practical, resource-efficient solution for improving LLM capabilities across diverse domains.

</details>


### [131] [Guilt by Association: Semantic Leakage Drives Associative Gender Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28944)
*Hazel Chen,Peter West,Hila Gonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语义泄漏不限于将概念直接归属于特定实体；即便移除归属关系（概念仅出现在情境中），模型输出仍会被该无关概念引导，且这一现象可扩展解释性别偏见中被现有评测忽略的偏差形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义泄漏研究多局限于"概念被直接归属于模型所询问的实体"这一设定，无法判定泄漏究竟是模型构建实体画像的缺陷启发式，还是更广泛的联想倾向；同时现有性别偏见评测同样只关注刻板概念被归属到实体的情形，可能遗漏仅凭概念在场即产生偏差的情况。

Method: 在移除概念对特定实体归属关系的条件下检验泄漏是否持续；并将该思路扩展至性别偏见，设置两种条件——主体与该概念发生交互、以及概念仅"存在于场景中"而不被主体涉及——比较二者使生成偏向刻板印象的程度。

Result: 在移除概念归属后语义泄漏依然存在；在性别偏见实验中，"主体参与概念"与"概念仅在场景中"两种条件均使模型生成向刻板印象偏移，且偏移程度相近。

Conclusion: 语义泄漏并非局限于实体画像式启发式，而是一种更广泛的联想倾向；它驱动了一种仅凭概念在场即可产生的偏见，该偏见被当前评测体系所忽视。

Abstract: Semantic leakage occurs when a concept in the prompt steers model generation about an entity, even though the concept is irrelevant to it (e.g., a person who likes yellow is said to work as a school bus driver). It has mostly been studied in settings where the concept is directly attributed to the entity the model is asked about. This leaves open whether leakage is a flawed heuristic, where the model builds a profile of that entity, or a much broader associative tendency, e.g., working across seemingly unrelated entities. We argue for the latter: even after removing the attribution of the concept to the specific entity, leakage persists. Next, we extend this idea to gender bias. While work on gender bias in LMs typically looks at cases where the stereotyped concept is attributed to an entity, our findings suggest that a concept may steer outputs with its mere presence in the scene. We show this using gendered concepts under two conditions: when the subject engages with the concept, and when the concept just sits in the scene. We find that both shift model generation toward the stereotype to a similar extent. Together, our results suggest that semantic leakage drives a form of bias that is overlooked by current evaluations.

</details>


### [132] [A Strong MoE Is All You Need for Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29498)
*Guanzhi Deng,Zengyan Liu,Haibo Wang,Kuan Wu,Shing Yin Wong,Sichun Luo,Zhuoran Wang,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现MoE模型内部的专家路由统计可作为内生的过程置信度信号，据此提出RoCo-Rethink（定位最不自信的推理步并选择性重生成后续轨迹）和RoCo-PRM（用强MoE自动标注步级分数并蒸馏为独立PRM），在多架构、多任务上稳定超越强基线且具备良好迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程奖励模型（PRM）对多步推理提供细粒度监督，但可靠的过程标签获取困难：现有方法依赖人工标注、rollout估计或辅助验证器，成本高或噪声大。作者希望寻找一种无需额外标注、由模型架构原生暴露的过程监督来源。

Method: 提出以MoE专家路由作为过程监督信号：先验证简单路由统计量与传统输出logit置信度相当；再构造"路由置信度"，综合整体路由质量（overall routing mass）、头级路由质量（head routing mass）与步内路由变化（within-step routing variation）。基于此设计两个方法：RoCo-Rethink在推理时识别置信度最低的步骤并仅重生成其后轨迹；RoCo-PRM用强MoE自动为推理轨迹标注路由导出的步级分数，并蒸馏为独立的PRM。

Result: 在多种MoE架构与多样化推理任务上，RoCo-Rethink与RoCo-PRM均一致优于强基线；RoCo-PRM在模型架构与任务间均表现出有效的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 专家路由不仅是计算调度机制，更是一种架构原生的过程监督来源，为PRM构建与推理时自我修正提供了免人工标注的新范式。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) provide fine-grained supervision for multi-step reasoning, yet obtaining reliable process labels remains a central bottleneck. Existing approaches typically rely on human annotation, rollout-based estimation, or auxiliary verification. In this work, we explore a different source of process supervision natively exposed by Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models: expert routing. We show that simple routing statistics provide an intrinsic process-confidence signal that is competitive with conventional output-logit confidence. Building on this observation, we construct routing confidence by combining overall routing mass, head routing mass, and within-step routing variation, and introduce RoCo-Rethink, which identifies the least confident reasoning step and selectively regenerates the subsequent trajectory. We further introduce RoCo-PRM, which uses a strong MoE to automatically annotate reasoning trajectories with routing-derived step scores and distills them into a standalone PRM. Across multiple MoE architectures and diverse reasoning tasks, both RoCo-Rethink and RoCo-PRM consistently outperform strong baselines, while RoCo-PRM transfers effectively across both model architectures and tasks. Our findings suggest that expert routing is more than a computational dispatch mechanism: it provides an architecture-native source of process supervision for reasoning.

</details>


### [133] [A KAN-based Non-linear Factorization for Embedding Layer Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29424)
*Oliver Giudice,Anna Russo Russo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在低秩嵌入分解中引入KAN风格的可学习非线性单变量投影，缓解线性瓶颈限制，并在等参数预算下提升压缩嵌入质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩分解虽数学简洁，但其线性瓶颈限制表示能力；同时KAN在NLP中多局限于输出/任务层，尚未用于嵌入层这类核心表示组件。

Method: 提出在压缩嵌入层内加入可学习非线性投影的增强分解方案，灵感来自Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks（用可学习单变量函数替代固定线性变换）；将其嵌入Skip-gram框架，与等参数预算的标准低秩基线对比；并设计从预训练未压缩嵌入层出发的初始化策略，分析初始化即时与短程精炼后的行为，结合标准Skip-gram目标及从原嵌入层蒸馏的变体。

Result: 在Word Analogy与Semantic Similarity基准上，非线性投影有效缓解线性瓶颈，在固定维度下得到更忠实的压缩嵌入。

Conclusion: 以可学习非线性投影增强低秩分解是超越纯线性嵌入压缩的有前景方向。

Abstract: Among existing embedding compression approaches, low-rank factorization provides a simple and mathematically principled solution by approximating the embedding matrix with a reduced-rank decomposition. However, its practical effectiveness is often limited, as the resulting linear bottleneck constrains representational capacity. To overcome these limitations, we propose an enhanced factorization scheme that increases expressive power by introducing learnable non-linear projections within the compressed embedding layer. Our method is inspired by the Kolmogorov Arnold Networks (KAN) paradigm, which replaces fixed linear transformations with learnable univariate functions. Despite promising results in other domains, applications of KANs in NLP remain scarce and have so far been largely limited to final or task specific layers, rather than core representational components such as embedding layers.
  We evaluate the proposed approach by integrating the compressed embedding layer into the Skip-gram framework and comparing it against a standard low-rank baseline under matched parameter budgets on the Word Analogy and Semantic Similarity benchmarks. We further introduce an effective initialization strategy from a pre-trained, uncompressed embedding layer. The behavior of the model is analyzed both immediately after initialization and after a brief post-initialization refinement phase, considering training with the standard Skip-gram objective as well as variants augmented with distillation from the original embedding layer. Overall, our results show that augmenting low-rank factorization with learnable non-linear projections effectively mitigates the limitations of linear bottlenecks, yielding more faithful compressed embeddings under fixed dimensionality and suggesting a promising direction for embedding layer compression beyond purely linear methods.

</details>


### [134] [Every Part Has Its Role: Part-of-Speech Effects in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29843)
*Laya Iyer,Chen Shani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文复现并扩展词性概念增强工作，将概念信号从名词拓展到动词和形容词，发现概念增强对困惑度的提升在新闻文本上最稳定且与词性无关，而下游任务收益以动词最为可靠；CKA与线性探针分析表明，下游收益最大的条件并非内部表征改变最大的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准下一词预测训练奖励预测特定token，未显式建模不同词或表达之间的语义相似性。已有工作通过用概念相关替代词构造最小变化句子来缓解该问题并取得提升，但其有效性是否依赖于概念在句中出现的位置/承载词性尚不清楚。

Method: 复现并扩展先前工作，将概念增强从名词推广到动词与形容词，考察承载概念信号的词性对困惑度和下游性能的影响；并结合CKA表征相似度分析与线性探针，比较不同条件下模型内部表征的变化。

Result: 不同评测设置下呈现不同模式：概念增强对困惑度的改善在新闻文本上最稳定且与所用词性无关；下游任务收益在动词条件下最可靠。表征分析显示，下游收益最大的条件并不一定是内部表征改变最大的条件。

Conclusion: 概念增强的有效性取决于训练中概念信息被引入的位置与方式，词性选择对困惑度与下游表现的影响并不一致，且行为层面的收益不能简单由表征变化幅度解释。

Abstract: Large language models are typically trained with next-token prediction (NTP), which rewards predicting specific tokens rather than explicitly accounting for the semantic similarities between different words or expressions. Prior work has addressed this limitation by augmenting training data with minimally varying sentences that replace a target word with conceptually related alternatives, e.g., "The tired coach ran home" $\rightarrow$ {"The tired trainer ran home", "The tired instructor ran home", ...}. Such concept augmentation improves performance, but it remains unclear whether its effectiveness depends on where in the sentence the concept is expressed. We reproduce and extend prior work $\textit{beyond nouns}$ to verbs and adjectives, asking whether the part of speech (POS) carrying the concept signal affects perplexity and downstream performance. We find different patterns across evaluation settings: concept augmentation improves perplexity most consistently on news, independent of the POS used, whereas verbs provide the most reliable downstream gains. Despite these behavioral differences, representational analyses using CKA and linear probing show that the conditions yielding the largest downstream gains are not necessarily those that most alter the model's internal representations. Together, these results suggest that the effectiveness of concept augmentation depends on where and how conceptual information is introduced during training.

</details>


### [135] [Did the Problem Actually Get Harder? Testing LLMs on Paired P and NP-Complete Problems Hardened by Adversarial Multi-Agent Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30103)
*Nurul Labib Sayeedi,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi,Zabir Al Nazi,Mahbub E Sobhani,Shubhashis Roy Dipta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出P2NPCBench基准：将12类NP完全问题与其"表面相同、仅一处结构差异"的多项式可解孪生问题配对，用对抗式多智能体框架AgentGAN改写题干以消除"背诵线索"，共2,114条硬化语句；评测12个模型发现NPC侧通过率显著低于P侧（35.4% vs 63.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么固定题干措辞、要么忽略问题所属复杂度类，因此无法区分"模型真正推理复杂度边界"与"模型凭记忆匹配教科书题面"。作者希望构建能识别问题落在P/NPC边界哪一侧的推理型基准。

Method: 构建P2NPCBench：每个NPC问题配一个相同表面的P孪生（如3-着色 vs 2-着色）；用AgentGAN对抗多智能体框架改写题干——生成器持续改写直到一组冻结代码模型集体失败，同时以可执行的有效性闸门保证改写不改变答案与复杂度类别；最终得到2,114条硬化语句，并配备人工核验的参考解法；在12个闭源与开源模型上评测。

Result: 所有模型在NPC语句上均低于其P孪生（pass@1 35.4% vs 63.8%）；95.1%的模型族配对在NPC侧失败更多；将时限放宽十倍仅使NPC准确率从28.5%升至31.9%，说明失败源于程序错误或崩溃而非运行缓慢；题干硬化使类别差距从24.8点收窄至14.6点。

Conclusion: 模型在复杂度边界上系统性失效，且部分表面差距源于对教科书措辞的熟悉而非复杂度类本身；基准为区分"回忆"与"推理"提供了可控诊断工具。

Abstract: Many NP-complete (NPC) problems have a polynomial-time (P) twin that differs in a single structural detail, such as 3- versus 2-colouring a graph. A model that reasons rather than recalls should notice which side of this boundary a problem falls on, yet existing benchmarks either fix the wording of each task or ignore its complexity class, so they cannot separate the two. We introduce P2NPCBench, a benchmark of 12 problem families in which every NPC problem is paired with a P twin posed over the same surface. To remove the textbook cues that enable recall, we build it with AgentGAN, an adversarial multi-agent framework in which a generator rewrites each statement until a panel of frozen code models fails, while an executable validity gate keeps every rewrite answer- and class-preserving. The result is 2,114 hardened statements with hand-verified reference human solvers. Evaluating 12 proprietary and open-source models, we find that every model scores lower on NPC statements than on their P twins (35.4% vs. 63.8% pass@1), and that 95.1% of model-family pairs fail more often on the NPC side. A tenfold time limit raises NPC accuracy only from 28.5% to 31.9%, so failures stem from wrong or crashing programs rather than slow ones. Hardening also narrows the class gap from 24.8 to 14.6 points, showing that part of the apparent gap reflects familiarity with textbook phrasing rather than complexity class.

</details>


### [136] [GoiBERT: Compact Vietnamese Language Models via Two-Stage Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30067)
*Quang Anh Kiet Tran,Nam Anh Tran,Manh Hung Trinh,Thanh Danh Le,Khang Nhat Hoang Vo,Huynh Dai Nhan Tran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GoiBERT：两个紧凑越南语编码器（26M/49M），直接处理原始文本，通过双教师蒸馏（同分词器越南语编码器+大模型软标签）训练，在八项基准上以不到一半参数、两倍吞吐超过最强紧凑基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最强的越南语预训练编码器要么体量大、使用多语种词表，要么依赖外部分词，部署成本高。作者希望构建可直接处理原始文本的紧凑越南语编码器，降低部署开销。

Method: 提出GoiBERT系列（base 26M、large 49M），直接在原始文本上运行，无需外部分词。采用双教师蒸馏：第一教师为同分词器的越南语编码器，传授通用表示；第二教师为规模更大的模型，通过软标签提供任务级指导，且生成软标签过程无需标注其自身训练数据。

Result: 在覆盖文本分类、方面级情感分析、自然语言推断和命名实体识别共八个基准上，GoiBERT-large在非教师模型中平均分最高，参数不到最强紧凑基线的一半，吞吐为其两倍；GoiBERT-base以四分之一规模保留该基线99%的性能。

Conclusion: 压缩在情感与分类任务上几乎无损，但在推断与实体识别任务上仍有明显损失，为后续工作指明方向；代码与模型已开源。
