Abstract: Vietnamese pre-trained encoders have improved rapidly, but the strongest ones are large, multilingual-vocabulary models or depend on external word segmentation, which makes them costly to deploy. We present GoiBERT, a family of two compact Vietnamese encoders with 26M and 49M parameters that operate directly on raw text. GoiBERT learns from two complementary teachers. A same-tokenizer Vietnamese encoder first teaches general-purpose representations. A much larger teacher then provides task-level guidance through soft labels that are generated without ever labeling its own training data. Across eight benchmarks covering text classification, aspect-based sentiment analysis, natural language inference, and named entity recognition, GoiBERT-large achieves the highest average score among non-teacher models. It does so with fewer than half the parameters of the strongest compact baseline and twice its throughput. GoiBERT-base retains 99% of that baseline's performance at a quarter of its size. Our analysis shows that compression is nearly lossless for sentiment and classification tasks but remains costly for inference and entity recognition, which points to clear directions for future work. Our code and models are available at: https://github.com/Nhantran65/GoiBERT.git

</details>


### [137] [Graph Topology Meets Energy-Driven Pathfinding: Preserving Contextual Consistency in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29933)
*Yufeng Li,Zaijing Li,Fuchen Zhang,Zongzong Wu,Ming Zhao,Fengxiao Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ThreadRAG：以语义拓扑图保留上下文完整性的 RAG 框架，通过能量扩散寻路并确定性线性化为文本证据路径，在多跳 QA 上优于基线且显著降低 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KG 增强的 RAG 在多跳问答中受限于知识结构化与推理过程的两大瓶颈：(i) 严重语义损失——连续文本被蒸馏为离散符号，丢失上下文细微语义与叙事连贯性；(ii) 明显模态错配——碎片化图结构与 LLM 预训练的连续文本分布不一致，而改写为自然语言又引入生成噪声并遗漏信息。

Method: ThreadRAG 由两部分组成：Semantic Topology Mapper（STM）将检索文档组织为上下文完整的 Semantic Nodes，并依据叙事、语义、词汇三类边构建 Semantic Topology Graph（STG）；Energy-Driven Pathfinding（EDP）通过能量扩散定位查询相关子图，并将其确定性线性化为连贯的文本证据路径。

Result: 在三个多跳问答基准上稳定优于强基线：HotpotQA 上相较文本中心方法 EM 提升 14.4%，2WikiMultihop 上超过最佳基线 5.8% EM，同时 token 成本降至最强基线的 23.3%。

Conclusion: 以语义拓扑保留上下文的图结构化方式配合模态原生（确定性线性化）的证据推理，可在提升多跳 QA 准确率的同时大幅压缩 token 开销，为 KG 增强 RAG 的语义损失与模态错配问题提供可行方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) frequently integrates knowledge graphs (KGs) for complex multi-hop question answering. However, their performance is increasingly bottlenecked by the knowledge structuring and reasoning processes. (i) Severe semantic loss: Distilling continuous texts into discrete symbols strips away contextual nuances and narrative coherence. (ii) Pronounced modality mismatch: Fragmented graph structures clash with the continuous texts LLMs are pre-trained on, while rewriting them into natural language introduces generative noise and omits information. To address these limitations, we propose ThreadRAG, a semantic-topology-based RAG framework for context-preserving knowledge structuring and modality-native evidence reasoning. ThreadRAG introduces a Semantic Topology Mapper (STM), which organizes retrieved documents into context-intact Semantic Nodes and constructs a Semantic Topology Graph (STG) through narrative, semantic, and lexical edges. It further incorporates Energy-Driven Pathfinding (EDP), which identifies query-relevant subgraphs via energy diffusion and deterministically linearizes them into coherent textual evidence paths. Experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that ThreadRAG consistently outperforms strong baselines. Notably, it achieves a 14.4% EM improvement over text-centric methods on HotpotQA and surpasses the best baseline by 5.8% EM on 2WikiMultihop, while reducing token cost to 23.3% of the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [138] [Unintended Side Effects of Value Training in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29922)
*Siddhesh Pawar,Chen Cecilia Liu,Anna Korhonen,Isabelle Augenstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过围绕Hofstede单个价值维度对四个指令微调LLM进行价值微调，评估跨价值迁移与更广泛行为，发现价值特定训练会对未训练价值、风险偏好、社会认知和安全特质产生非预期副作用，呼吁价值对齐后应纳入更广泛的行为评测并厘清价值训练的流水线位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益部署于价值敏感场景，价值对齐成为后训练目标之一，但现有评测范围狭窄；由于价值之间相互关联，针对特定价值的微调如何影响不相关的能力与行为尚不明确。

Method: 选取四个指令微调LLM，分别沿Hofstede文化价值维度中的单一维度进行微调，随后在跨价值迁移（未训练的价值维度）以及风险偏好、社会认知、安全特质等更广泛行为上进行评测。

Result: 价值特定训练对未训练的价值维度、风险偏好、社会认知与安全特质均产生了非预期的副作用（即存在跨维度的迁移与外溢效应）。

Conclusion: 后训练中的价值对齐应纳入更广泛的行为评测；同时需要进一步研究额外的价值训练在整体训练流水线中应置于何处最为合适。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed in value-sensitive contexts, aligning them with human values has emerged as a post-training objective in NLP, aimed at instilling additional values and guiding model behaviour. However, current evaluations remain narrow. Because values are interconnected, it is unclear how fine-tuning for specific values alters unrelated capabilities and behaviours. In this work, we adapt four instruction-tuned LLMs across individual Hofstede's value dimensions and evaluate them on both cross-value transfer and broader behaviours. We find that value-specific training produces unintended side effects on untrained values, risk preferences, social cognition, and safety traits. Consequently, we suggest that value alignment after post-training should incorporate broader behavioral evaluations, and we highlight the need to investigate where additional value training might best fit into the pipeline.

</details>


### [139] [Carving Radiology Reports at Their Evidential Joints: Anatomical Region Sets as Generation Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29165)
*Jinwei Yang,Junhao Chen,Jianwei Zhao,Zhonghan Xu,Hong Cheng,Ling Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 RadCarve，直接从已有 statement-region 对应关系中的解剖区域集合定义生成单元，实现单元划分与视觉证据选择的统一，在胸部X线报告生成中以更少参数取得领先效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将放射报告拆解为独立生成单元的方法常采用自然报告中未直接规定、也非标注给出的生成单元结构，从而引入额外的建模妥协；同时单元形成与视觉证据选择相互割裂。

Method: RadCarve 以现有 statement-region 对应关系中的解剖区域集合直接定义生成单元。其 planner 使用面向组成（composition-specialized）的查询库，从图像联合预测单元存在性与区域组成，并依据当前成员预测聚合的区域证据对两者迭代细化；generator 以其预测区域集合的联合视觉证据为条件，独立生成每个单元的文本。

Result: 相比强基线，在给定区域集合的单元生成任务上 CXRFEScore 提升 9.4%，端到端绑定 F1 达到约 2.4 倍；在 Chest ImaGenome 与 PadChest-GR 上取得领先的报告生成结果，且参数量不足大规模基线的六分之一。

Conclusion: 将生成单元定义为解剖区域集合并与视觉证据选择统一，是一种无需额外结构假设的有效建模范式，可在低参数代价下实现细粒度、可绑定视觉证据的报告生成。

Abstract: Organizing radiology reports into independently generated units offers a way to generate fine-grained clinical content and bind it to visual evidence. However, existing methods typically adopt generation-unit structures not directly specified in natural reports or their annotations, introducing additional modeling compromises. We propose RadCarve, which instead defines generation units directly from the anatomical region sets in existing statement-region correspondences, unifying unit formation with visual evidence selection. RadCarve's planner uses composition-specialized query banks to jointly predict unit existence and region composition from the image, iteratively refining both with regional evidence aggregated according to current membership predictions. The generator independently produces each unit's text conditioned on the joint visual evidence of its predicted region set. Compared with strong baselines, RadCarve improves CXRFEScore by 9.4% in unit generation given region sets and achieves 2.4 times the end-to-end binding F1. It also achieves leading report-generation results on Chest ImaGenome and PadChest-GR, using fewer than one-sixth as many parameters as large-scale baselines.

</details>


### [140] [GRAFT: Restoring the Missing Sampling Interface in Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29136)
*Haoning Yang,Lili Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 连续扩散语言模型因"无逐步离散化"导致词汇长尾塌陷，稀有词生成率仅为人类的0.30x；作者提出免训练的GRAFT方法，通过强制提交稀有词并重生成序列，将稀有词占比恢复至人类水平的0.82–1.10x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散语言模型（如ELF）以并行方式生成整段序列、仅在最后一步将嵌入转为文本，其"无逐步离散化"被自身消融实验视为性能优势来源。作者指出该原则存在隐藏代价：与留出的人类文本相比，ELF在常见词上与人类频率几乎一致，但最稀有词仅为人率的0.30x，且该差距在105M到652M参数间不收敛、任何采样设置都无法弥补；而同规模GPT-2仅凭解码规则即可在0到高于人类率之间调节。作者将成因归为：缺少离散采样步骤时，每步由候选的均值驱动，稀有候选与竞争同一位置的常见词平均后落到常见词上。

Method: 提出GRAFT——一种免训练方法，将缺失的"选择"嫁接回冻结模型：在模型仍未定稿的阶段，提交（commit）那些会被均值化丢弃的稀有词，随后在保持这些承诺的前提下从头重新生成整段序列。

Result: 在三个ELF规模及独立连续dLLM LangFlow上测试，GRAFT将生成文本的稀有词占比从人率的0.42–0.77x提升至0.82–1.10x（相对增益34–109%）；并在所有测试模型上使整体词频分布更接近人类，ELF-B上提升2.7x、LangFlow上提升12x。

Conclusion: 连续dLLM的"无逐步离散化"设计在获得并行生成效率的同时引入系统性长尾塌陷，其根源在于均值化而非采样选择；GRAFT作为一种即插即用的免训练干预，可在不改动模型权重的前提下有效恢复词汇多样性，表明这一缺陷是可修复的解码层问题。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion language models generate a whole sequence in parallel, never drawing a single word at any intermediate step; only the final step turns embeddings into text. Models such as ELF now report autoregressive-level quality at a fraction of the sampling steps of discrete-token diffusion models, and this design principle -- no step-wise discretization -- is what their own ablations credit for the gain. We show that this same principle carries a hidden cost: a collapse of the lexical long tail. Measured against held-out human text, ELF's output matches the human rate on common words almost exactly, yet its rarest words appear at only 0.30x the human rate, a gap that does not close from 105M to 652M parameters and that no sampler setting these models expose can reach. A same-size GPT-2, in contrast, spans zero to above the human rate by its decoding rule alone. We trace the difference to what continuous diffusion models lack: with no step that ever draws a discrete word, every step is instead driven by an average over the candidates still in play, and averaging a rare candidate together with the common word competing for its slot lands on the common word. An autoregressive model avoids this because its decoding rule samples one candidate rather than averaging over all of them. We propose GRAFT, a training-free method that grafts this missing choice back onto a frozen model: while the model is still undecided, GRAFT commits the rare words the average would otherwise discard, then regenerates the sequence from scratch with those commitments held in place. Across three ELF scales and on LangFlow, an independent continuous dLLM, GRAFT restores the rare-word share of generated text from 0.42-0.77x the human rate to 0.82-1.10x, a relative gain of 34-109%, and moves the entire word-frequency profile closer to human on every model tested, by 2.7x on ELF-B and 12x on LangFlow.

</details>


### [141] [Our Own Jev: Typed Decisions in One Forward Pass, from an In-House Judge to Open LoRA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29052)
*Yuchuan Weng,Chi Liu,Xin Chen,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了"类型化决策图"(typed decision map)这一System 1式接口的两种应用:Part I介绍用于约14.8万条agentic编码会话记录分诊的自研System 1评审器,以单个前向传播、单GPU约3分钟/千条、约$0.06的成本替代每轮约$1000的前沿LLM评审;Part II基于Eikos配方在1B/2B/35B三个骨干上验证冻结输出层读出(readout)的竞争力,35B在JevBench 111个困难项上得79.3%(与商业参考73.0%无显著差异),并发现训练输出层26个字母行的消融无可检测收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业系统Jev推广了"一次前向传播直接读取语言模型logits/隐藏状态得到选项概率"的System 1接口,但缺少开源复现与实证评估;同时前沿LLM作为评审器成本极高(约$1000/轮)且存储标签与新双评审共识仅26.9%一致,存在数据质量与隐私外泄问题,故需低成本、可本地部署、可靠的替代方案,并检验无参数读出是否具备竞争力。

Method: Part I:自托管冻结骨干+rank-16 LoRA适配器+指针头(pointer head),一次前向传播回答三个类型化问题,用于约148K条agentic编码会话的分诊。Part II:采用公开Eikos配方与约25K条决策语料,将方法迁移至1B、2B、35B三个骨干,读取LoRA适配解码器的冻结输出层;在JevBench 111个公开困难项上评测,并对输出层26个字母行进行单种子训练消融,以及在后续语料版本上重训以检验小规模评测的分辨力。

Result: Part I:处理1000条会话约需单GPU 3分钟,按参考费率约$0.06,约为前沿评审器成本的1%,且数据全程留在组织内部。Part II:单种子35B在JevBench困难项得79.3%,与商业参考(73.0%)无显著差异(未校正p=0.12),Holm–Bonferroni校正后优于五个开源系统;2B优于排行榜上全部四个kev检查点。消融显示训练输出层26个字母行无可检测收益;在后续语料版本重训使2B困难档准确率变动11.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 类型化决策图式System 1读出是以极低成本替代前沿LLM评审的有效方案,无参数冻结输出层读出已具竞争力,额外训练读出参数收益不明显;同时小规模评测(如111项、单种子)统计分辨力有限,结果对语料版本高度敏感,需谨慎解读。

Abstract: A typed decision maps evidence, a question and a fixed set of options to a probability for every option, computed in one forward pass, by reading a language model's logits or a small head over its hidden states, instead of generating and parsing text. The commercial system Jev popularised this System~1 interface, and open reproductions have followed. We report two uses of the pattern. \emph{Part I} describes an in-house System~1 judge that triages about 148K agentic coding session records for a training corpus. It replaces a frontier LLM judge that cost on the order of \$1,000 per pass and whose stored labels matched a fresh two-judge consensus on only 26.9\% of the sessions where the two judges agreed; a self-hosted frozen backbone with a rank-16 LoRA adapter and a pointer head answers three typed questions in one forward pass, judges 1,000 sessions in about three minutes of one GPU, about \$0.06 at a reference rate and roughly 1\% of the frontier judge's cost, and keeps every session inside the organisation. \emph{Part II} asks whether a readout that adds no parameters can be competitive and whether training one helps. Applying the public Eikos recipe and corpus of about 25K decisions to three further backbones (1B, 2B and 35B), we read the frozen output layer of a LoRA-adapted decoder. On the 111 public hard items of JevBench our single-seed 35B, developed with those items in view, scores 79.3\%, not significantly different from the commercial reference (73.0\%, unadjusted $p=0.12$) and ahead of five open systems after Holm--Bonferroni correction; our 2B is ahead of all four kev checkpoints on the leaderboard. A single-seed ablation finds no detectable benefit from training the 26 letter rows of the output layer, and a retrain on a later corpus release moves the 2B's hard-tier accuracy by 11.7 points, which shows how little small evaluations resolve.

</details>


### [142] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for Detecting Satirical Code-Mixed Sentences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29907)
*Răzvan-Alexandru Smădu,Ioana Buhnila,Daniela Tanase,Dumitru-Clementin Cercel,Florin Pop,Mihaela-Claudia Cercel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 构建含60万+语料的合成罗英/德/法/意语码转换讽刺检测数据集CS-SeLeRoSa及人工裁定金标CS-SeLeRoSa-Gold，系统比较多语言大模型的零样本/思维链/微调表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语码转换是自然语言中的常见现象，但其对罗姆语等低资源语言的高层推理任务（如依赖文化与会话细微线索的讽刺检测）的影响尚缺乏研究；同时缺乏大规模、可控的合成语码混合数据与可靠评测基准。

Method: 基于罗马尼亚语新闻句子级人工标注讽刺数据集，采用四种生成方法（随机替换、短语翻译、依存句法分析、大语言模型）生成与英语、德语、法语、意大利语的合成语码混合句子，扩增出超60万样本的CS-SeLeRoSa，并构建人工裁定的金标CS-SeLeRoSa-Gold；在零样本、思维链与微调三种设置下评测多个多语言大模型。

Result: 较早的开源模型性能显著下降（宏F1降至37%–55%）；较新模型Gemma 4 31B零样本达68.1%，接近前沿模型GPT-5 mini（69.5%）；微调显著减少格式错误并提升性能，Gemma 4 31B在CS-SeLeRoSa上达77.5%、在金标上达72.6%，超过前沿模型零样本基线。

Conclusion: 合成语码混合数据可有效支持低资源语言的高层语义任务研究；微调较零样本更能缓解语码转换带来的性能退化，并可在金标上超越前沿闭源模型的零样本表现。

Abstract: Code-switching is a natural linguistic phenomenon, yet its impact on high-level reasoning tasks remains largely unexplored in under-resourced languages like Romanian. Natural language processing tasks, such as satire detection, rely heavily on nuanced cultural and syntactic cues that large language models struggle to capture. In this paper, we investigate four methods (i.e., random replacement, phrase translation, dependency parsing, and large language models) for generating synthetic code-mixed Romanian sentences with English, German, French, and Italian. We start with an existing dataset for satire detection, compiled from Romanian news articles and annotated at the sentence level by humans, and augment it with over 600k samples, namely CS-SeLeRoSa. We provide an in-depth analysis of the dataset generation process. Then, we evaluate several multilingual large language models across zero-shot, chain-of-thought, and fine-tuning regimes on both the synthetic corpus and a human-adjudicated gold benchmark, namely CS-SeLeRoSa-Gold. While older open models suffer substantial degradation (dropping to 37%-55% macro-F1), newer models like Gemma 4 31B achieve 68.1% zero-shot, rivaling frontier models like GPT-5 mini (69.5%). Furthermore, fine-tuning significantly reduces formatting errors and improves the results, increasing Gemma 4 31B to 77.5% on CS-SeLeRoSa and 72.6% on the gold standard, outperforming frontier zero-shot baselines.

</details>


### [143] [OmniSimulator: Aligning Small Language Models for Authentic Heterogeneous Behavior Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29472)
*Jiawei Chen,Ruoxi Xu,Boxi Cao,Zhuoqun Li,Ruotong Pan,Yunfei Zhang,Zhirui Yang,Jingyan Chen,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Yaojie Lu,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Xiangyu Wu,Hongyu Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniSimulator 训练框架，通过五维结构化 schema 引导小模型学习行为导向推理，从而提升短视频平台用户模拟的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型作为交互系统的通用用户模拟器潜力巨大，但用户模拟的效果上限受限于模型的推理范式：原生思维链（CoT）易退化为浅层模式匹配，无法充分建模真实用户决策背后的行为因素。

Method: OmniSimulator 用五维度结构化 schema 组织行为导向推理；从真实用户行为场景中精选数据；引入教师模型按该 schema 生成高质量推理轨迹，使小模型学到与行为相关的推理模式而非表层行为相关性。基于 200 名真实用户三个月的历史交互轨迹构建 9,881 条高质量训练实例（人均约 50 条蒸馏样本）。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上平均得分提升 34.33%，性能与 Claude-Sonnet-4.5 相当，并支持更小规模的自托管部署。

Conclusion: 学习结构化的行为导向推理模式，是通向可扩展、高保真 LLM 用户模拟的有效方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown great potential as general-purpose user simulators for interactive systems. Prior studies have demonstrated that training on high-quality data can substantially improve model reasoning and downstream task performance. However, the upper bound of user simulation is still constrained by the model's reasoning paradigm: native chain-of-thought often degenerates into shallow pattern matching and fails to adequately model the behavioral factors underlying real user decisions. In this paper, we propose OmniSimulator, a novel training framework designed to improve the predictive ability of small LLMs for realistic user simulation on short-video platform environments. Specifically, OmniSimulator organizes behavior-oriented reasoning through a structured schema with five dimensions, carefully selects data from real user-behavior scenarios, and introduces a teacher model to generate high-quality reasoning traces under the proposed schema, helping small models learn behavior-relevant reasoning patterns rather than relying on superficial behavioral correlations. Based on this process, we construct 9,881 high-quality training instances from three months of historical interaction trajectories of 200 real users, averaging about 50 distilled examples per user. Experimental results show that OmniSimulator improves the average score of Qwen3-8B by 34.33%, achieves performance competitive with Claude-Sonnet-4.5, and supports smaller-scale, self-hosted deployment. These results suggest that learning structured behavior-oriented reasoning patterns is a promising direction towards scalable and high-fidelity LLM-based user simulation.

</details>


### [144] [Don't Stop Long-Context Continual Pre-Training Too Early: An Empirical Scaling Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29352)
*Ziqing Qiao,Xiaoyue Xu,Sikui Zhang,Yinuo Xu,Bingxiang He,Chaojun Xiao,Xu Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过建模、监测与验证三方面研究长上下文持续预训练（LCCP）收益随token预算的扩展规律：拟合出加性幂律并验证其外推能力，提出Constant-Last4监测协议，证实在约100B LCCP token规模内性能持续提升且可迁移至下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LCCP被广泛用于提升长上下文能力，但其收益如何随LCCP所用token数扩展尚不清楚，导致难以判断是否应继续扩大LCCP投入。

Method: 1) 建立长上下文性能与模型规模、短上下文预训练token预算及LCCP token预算之间的经验加性幂律；在5种模型规模、100余种短/长上下文预算配置上拟合与验证，并外推到留出模型规模。2) 提出Constant-Last4协议：在恒定学习率训练中对最近4个连续checkpoint取平均，实现单一可扩展训练轨迹上的稳定监测，并用同形式幂律预测1.22B模型约100B LCCP token的性能轨迹。3) 沿该轨迹开展下游评测（含128K上下文扩展与SFT后）。

Result: 拟合的幂律能复现观测趋势并良好外推至未见模型规模；Constant-Last4下同一幂律形式拟合良好，可准确预测1.22B模型约100B LCCP token的性能轨迹；下游评测显示长上下文性能持续提升，在评测范围内无明显饱和迹象，且在128K扩展与SFT后收益保持，并延伸至长上下文理解与agentic任务。

Conclusion: LCCP收益沿可扩展轨迹稳定可观测、可定量预测，并能迁移到下游任务，支持继续扩大LCCP规模而非过早停止。

Abstract: Long-context continual pre-training (LCCP) is widely used to improve long-context capability, but how its benefits scale with the number of tokens used for LCCP remains unclear. We investigate this question through modeling, monitoring, and validating the benefits of scaling LCCP. First, we establish an empirical additive power law relating long-context performance to model size and the respective token budgets of short-context pre-training and LCCP. Experiments across five model scales and more than one hundred short- and long-context budget configurations show that the fitted law reproduces the observed trends and extrapolates well to held-out model scales. Second, we introduce \textsc{Constant-Last4}, a training and observation protocol that averages the four most recent consecutive checkpoints from a constant-learning-rate run to enable stable monitoring along a single extensible training trajectory. Under this protocol, the same scaling-law form fits well and accurately predicts the LCCP performance trajectory of a 1.22B-parameter model trained on approximately 100B LCCP tokens. Finally, downstream evaluations along this trajectory show continued improvements in long-context performance, with no clear sign of saturation within the evaluated range. These gains persist after both 128K context extension and supervised fine-tuning, with benefits extending to long-context understanding and agentic tasks. Together, these results argue for continuing to scale LCCP rather than stopping early: its gains are stably observable along an extensible trajectory, quantitatively predictable, and transferable to downstream tasks.

</details>


### [145] [Overview of CHIP 2026 Shared Task 1: Patient-Specific Phenotype Concept Recognition in Full-Text Biomedical Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29606)
*Xuanlyu Zhu,Feng Tian,Yang Zhong,Pengqi Zheng,Xiwen Bi,Ling Luo,Yuanyuan Sun,Jian Wang,Lingfei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 CHIP 2026 Shared Task 1 的评测概览，任务是从生物医学全文文献中识别表型概念并判定其与具体患者的关联。数据为 PatientPheX 的 200 篇全文、506 名患者、15,193 个表型提及与 5,656 条患者-表型关联；455 队报名，最佳系统总体得分 0.7389，较基线高 8.37 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动表型信息抽取对罕见病诊断、基因型-表型分析和精准医学具有重要意义，但在包含多名患者与复杂临床描述的生物医学全文中，准确识别表型概念并确定其所属患者仍具挑战，因此设立该评测以推动患者级表型概念识别方法的发展。

Method: 构建基于 PatientPheX 数据集的评测，使用 200 篇经标注生物医学全文（506 名患者、15,193 个表型提及、5,656 条患者-表型关联）；设置两个子任务：全文表型概念识别与患者特定表型关联；采用 Phase A / Phase B 双阶段排行榜机制，共 455 支队伍注册，分别收到 189 与 103 份有效提交。

Result: 最佳系统在最终测试集上总体得分 0.7389，超出基线 8.37 个百分点；优胜队伍主要采用生物医学语言模型、大语言模型（LLM）与集成策略相结合的方法。

Conclusion: 评测结果既显示了上述方法的潜力，也揭示了全文生物医学文献中患者归属判定与表型归一化方面仍存在的挑战；评测后阶段持续开放。

Abstract: Automatic phenotype information extraction plays an important role in rare disease diagnosis, genotype-phenotype analysis, and precision medicine. However, accurately identifying phenotype concepts and determining their associations with specific patients remain challenging, particularly in full-text biomedical articles involving multiple patients and complex clinical descriptions. This paper presents an overview of CHIP 2026 Shared Task 1, which aims to promote the development of methods for patient-specific phenotype concept recognition from full-text biomedical literature. The task consists of two subtasks: full-text phenotype concept recognition and patient-specific phenotype association. For this evaluation, we used 200 annotated full-text biomedical articles from the PatientPheX dataset, containing 506 patients, 15,193 phenotype mentions, and 5,656 patient-phenotype associations. A total of 455 teams registered for the competition, with 189 and 103 valid submissions received on the Phase A and Phase B leaderboards, respectively. The best-performing system achieved an overall score of 0.7389 on the final test set, outperforming the baseline by 8.37 percentage points. The top-performing teams explored diverse approaches combining biomedical language models, large language models (LLMs), and ensemble strategies. The evaluation results highlight both the potential of these approaches and the remaining challenges in patient attribution and phenotype normalization from full-text biomedical literature. The post-evaluation phase remains open at https://tianchi.aliyun.com/competition/entrance/532512.

</details>


### [146] [MemoryTax: The Bitter Lesson of Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29823)
*Zedong Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该短篇实证研究在LoCoMo单段对话上比较四种记忆方法（全上下文、BM25、Mem0、Zep）与三种编码智能体（Pi、Codex、Claude Code），跨三种模型（GPT-4o-mini、GPT-6 Luna、Claude Opus 5.5）记录成本；结论是：设计式智能体记忆只是弱模型的高昂补丁，存在"写入模型"设定上限，而强模型自搜索（Pi）在成本-准确率前沿最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流智能体记忆系统（Mem0、Zep）在问题到来前预先决定保留什么，而编码式智能体保留原始历史并由模型自行用工具检索。作者质疑：当模型足够强、能自己检索时，人工设计的记忆是否仍有价值；同时现有工作很少系统记录每种方案的完整代价。

Method: 在单段LoCoMo对话（419轮、152个问题）上，对四类记忆方法（全上下文、BM25、Mem0、Zep）与三种编码智能体（Pi、Codex、Claude Code）进行交叉评测，每种组合分别配置GPT-4o-mini、GPT-6 Luna、Claude Opus 5.5三个模型，并记录每次rollout的成本；每个单元格仅运行一次，属小规模单例实证。另做消融：在88个证据不含图片的问题上比较Pi与全上下文。

Result: Mem0仅在GPT-4o-mini下优于全上下文，且仅其记忆构建成本就超过一次完整全上下文运行；记忆质量受写入模型能力封顶，更强的读取模型无法弥补弱写入模型的遗漏。使用Claude Opus 5.5时，Pi位于成本-准确率帕累托前沿，以不足全上下文一半的成本取得99%得分，唯一失误源于答案标注错误；其中部分领先来自仅智能体能读取的照片说明，在88个无图片证据问题上Pi与全上下文仅差一个答案。

Conclusion: 人工设计的智能体记忆并非普适收益，而是弱模型的高成本替代品，且其能力上限由写入端模型决定；强模型配合原生检索工具即可在成本-准确率上占优。由于样本仅一段对话、每个单元格单次运行，结论为初步证据，需更多对话、重复运行与统计检验验证，并应控制图片模态带来的混杂。

Abstract: Agent memory systems such as Mem0 and Zep decide what to keep before any question arrives; coding agents keep the raw history and let the model search it with its own tools. We ask whether designed memory still helps once the model is strong enough to search for itself. On one LoCoMo conversation (419 turns, 152 questions), we run four memory methods (full context, BM25, Mem0, Zep) and three coding agents (Pi, Codex, Claude Code), each with GPT-4o-mini, GPT-6 Luna and Claude Opus 5.5, and record what every rollout costs. This is a short empirical study, with one conversation and one run per cell. Agent memory is a costly patch for a weak model: Mem0 beats full context only with GPT-4o-mini, and its build alone costs more than a whole full-context rollout. Agent memory also has a ceiling, set by the model that writes it: a stronger reader does not recover what a weaker writer left out. With Claude Opus 5.5, Pi tops the cost-accuracy frontier, scoring 99% for less than half the cost of full context; its one miss is a wrong answer key. Part of that lead comes from photo captions that only the agents could read: on the 88 questions whose evidence has no photo, Pi and full context differ by one answer.

</details>


### [147] [Where Do LLMs Learn to Recommend AI?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29785)
*Benaya Trabelsi,Jonathan Shaki,Sarit Kraus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM 在职业、学业、产品建议中偏好推荐 AI 相关选项，该倾向起源于预训练，并由微调材料与提问措辞共同放大或削弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到 LLM 在被问及"学什么、做什么职业、做什么产品"时常给出 AI 相关推荐，希望定位这一倾向的来源：是预训练固有、还是指令微调引入，以及提问方式的影响。

Method: 采用对照实验：比较预训练模型与官方指令微调版本；自行进行多种微调（通用指令微调、删除 7% AI 相关指令样本、以有用性为奖励的数据、以多样模拟用户偏好排序的数据）；在模型固定下变换提问方式（"最佳"选项、"现在"该做什么、声明模型为 AI 顾问）。

Result: 预训练模型已有 27% 回答含明确 AI/ML 术语，官方指令微调版升至 59%；自建微调中通用指令微调不变，删除 7% AI 相关样本下降 14 个百分点（低于起点预训练模型）；有用性奖励数据提升 9 点，多样模拟用户偏好排序数据下降 9 点；提问层面，"最佳"提升 8 点、"现在"提升 18 点、自称 AI 顾问提升 4 点。

Conclusion: AI 偏好倾向始于预训练，其显现强度由后续学习内容（微调数据分布与偏好目标）及提问措辞共同决定，并非指令微调的必然结果。

Abstract: Asked what to study, which career to pursue, or what product to build, large language models (LLMs) often recommend AI-related options. We ask where this tendency comes from by comparing models before and after fine-tuning and by varying how questions are asked. First, pretrained models already include explicit AI/ML terms in the recommendations of 27% of their responses, and their released instruction-tuned versions do so in 59%. Second, fine-tuning models ourselves shows that this rise is not inevitable: general instruction tuning leaves the rate unchanged, but removing the 7% of instruction examples that concern AI cuts it by 14 percentage points, below the level of the pretrained models it starts from. Data rewarding helpfulness raise it by 9 points, while data ranked by the preferences of diverse simulated users lower it by 9. Finally, with models held fixed, asking for the "best" option or for what to do "right now" raises the rate by 8 and 18 points, whereas telling the model it is an AI advisor adds 4. Overall, the tendency starts in pretraining, and what models learn afterward, together with how a question is phrased, decides how strongly it appears.

</details>


### [148] [Bridging Foundational and Agentic Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29751)
*Wei Guo,Yuanjian Xu,Jianing Hao,Yongyao Duan,Ran Chen,Zhong Li,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出操作级测量框架，通过基础能力画像与交互轨迹操作画像，量化LLM基础能力向智能体能力转化中的“能力实现差距”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为智能体部署日益普遍，但基础能力如何转化为智能体能力尚不清晰；端到端得分无法区分失败源于基础能力不足还是部署执行薄弱，缺乏可操作的诊断手段。

Method: 构建操作级测量框架：由能力基准导出基础画像，由交互轨迹导出操作画像，并将观测到的操作表现相对基于基础能力的预测的偏差定义为能力实现差距；在τ²-Bench上对15个模型服务进行实验。

Result: 基础画像仅能部分预测智能体操作；画像相近的模型在执行上可差异显著，且该差距在不同任务子集间保持一致；操作画像提供额外信息，显著提升任务得分预测。

Conclusion: 该框架将执行偏离定位到操作层面，为模型开发与智能体评测提供可操作诊断依据；代码与分析产物将开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as agents, yet it remains unclear how their foundational capabilities translate into agentic capabilities. End-to-end scores leave unresolved whether low benchmark capability or weak deployment drives a failure, limiting actionable diagnosis. We bridge foundational and agentic capabilities through an operation-level measurement framework. We represent each model with a foundational profile derived from capability benchmarks and an operation profile derived from interaction trajectories, and define capability realization gaps as deviations of observed operation performance from predictions based on foundational capabilities. Experiments on fifteen model services in $τ^2$-Bench show that foundational profiles only partially predict agentic operations. Models with comparable profiles can differ substantially in execution, and these gaps remain consistent across task subsets. Operation profiles add information and substantially improve task-score prediction. The framework locates execution departures at the operation level to inform model development and agent evaluation. We will release our code and analysis artifacts.

</details>


### [149] [Effects of Model Scale and Quantization on Social Bias and Abstention in Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29657)
*Kuniko Paxton,Ifeoluwa Wuraola,Dhavalkumar Thakker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究小模型与量化模型在BBQ问答基准上"偏见下降"是否源于"更多弃答"，发现26个偏见减少的配置中20个同时出现过度弃答，故公平性评估需联合考察偏见、弃答、提示表述与答案选项设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限场景部署小型与量化语言模型时，压缩模型输出更少偏见回答究竟是"更公平"还是"更多弃答"，这一混淆问题缺乏系统研究。

Method: 在BBQ问答基准上，对Qwen3、Gemma-3、SmolLM2多个模型规模与量化配置进行评测；并检验不同提示表述、以及在强制选择设定下移除"Unknown"选项时趋势的稳定性。

Result: 更大模型问答准确率更高；偏见无简单缩放规律，量化效应随配置而异；26个偏见回答更少的配置中有20个同时过度弃答增加，说明低观测偏见部分反映更强弃答倾向。

Conclusion: 低观测偏见可部分归因于更多弃答，公平性评估应将偏见回答、弃答率、提示表述与答案选项设计共同纳入考量。

Abstract: The deployment of small and quantized language models in resource-constrained settings raises an important fairness question: when a compressed model produces fewer biased responses, does this reflect fairer behavior or greater abstention? We study this problem in Question Answering (QA) using the BBQ benchmark across multiple model sizes and quantization settings from Qwen3, Gemma-3, and SmolLM2. We also examine whether the observed trends remain stable across alternative prompt formulations and when the ``Unknown'' option is removed in a forced-choice setting. Our results show that larger models achieved higher QA accuracy, but bias showed no simple scaling trend and quantization effects varied across configurations. Notably, 20 of the 26 configurations with fewer biased responses also show increased over-abstention, indicating that lower observed bias can partly reflect greater abstention. These findings show that lower observed bias can partly reflect greater abstention, and that fairness evaluation should consider biased responses, abstention, prompt formulation, and answer-option design jointly.

</details>


### [150] [LongZero: Enhancing Long-Context Reasoning in LLMs from Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30132)
*Zhennan Wan,Jianfei Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LongZero，一种无需任何外部数据、让LLM自我提升长上下文推理能力的强化学习方法：先以紧凑结构化形式自拟问题、答案及支撑证据，再将证据扩展为长多文档上下文，并与自拟多轮检索任务结合，用GRPO训练，在五个长上下文基准上超越基于外部语料合成数据的RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习提升LLM长上下文推理依赖"长、多样、有挑战性且可验证"的训练数据，但人工标注昂贵、自然数据稀缺，现有合成方法又依赖外部语料或更强教师模型，而随着被训练模型变强，这些资源愈发难以获得；已有的"零数据RL"（模型自拟任务）仅面向短上下文任务，因此需要一种无需外部数据的长上下文自提升方法。

Method: LongZero不在现有文档上直接出题作答，而是先让模型以紧凑结构化形式合成问题、答案及其支撑证据，随后单独将证据扩展为长多文档上下文；并将这种自写的多跳推理任务与自写的多轮检索任务结合，采用GRPO对模型进行训练。

Result: 在两个LLM、五个长上下文推理基准上评测，与基于外部语料合成数据的RL方法对比，LongZero能够泛化到真实任务，且性能优于两种基线。

Conclusion: LongZero为在无外部数据条件下提升LLM长上下文推理能力提供了一条可行且有前景的路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is an effective way to strengthen the long-context reasoning of large language models (LLMs), but it needs training data that are long, diverse, challenging, and verifiable. Human annotation of such data is expensive, naturally occurring data are too scarce to sustain RL, and existing synthesis methods depend on external corpora or stronger teacher models, which become harder to obtain as the trained models get stronger. Recently, RL from zero data, in which a model learns from tasks it proposes itself, has achieved excellent performance, but existing methods target short-context tasks. Therefore, we propose $\textbf{LongZero}$, a method that lets an LLM improve its own long-context reasoning without any external data. Instead of writing questions and answers over existing documents, LongZero first has the model synthesize the question and answer with its supporting evidence in a compact structured form, and only then separately expands the evidence into a long multi-document context. LongZero combines these self-written multi-hop reasoning tasks with self-written multi-round retrieval tasks and trains the model with GRPO. We evaluate LongZero on two LLMs across five long-context reasoning benchmarks and compare it with RL on data synthesized from external corpora. Experimental results show that LongZero can generalize to real tasks, and outperforms both baselines, suggesting a viable and promising path to improving long-context reasoning of LLMs.

</details>


### [151] [Quant-Arena: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Quantitative Trading Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30113)
*Houde Qian,Zheng Jiang,Wenyi Li,Chenxi Xu,Xinjie Lin,Yuzhao Peng,Ruoyu Han,Youjie Zheng,Kaisen Yang,Qinhuai Na,You Fu,Calvin Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Quant-Arena——面向 A 股真实数据的端到端量化研究基准与公开排行榜，要求模型自行构造特征与打分代码、仅在 2022–2023 反馈下迭代并冻结策略，于封存的 2024 年按含交易约束与成本的现金账户评估。九个前沿模型参评，GPT-6 Astra 最高，但无一在 2024 年跑赢等权市场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化研究本质是"假设—实现—回测—取舍"的迭代闭环，而现有基准仅评测公式、代码或单笔交易决策，无法回答 LLM 能否完成完整闭环并产出样本外有效的策略，因此需要端到端、样本外隔离的评测框架。

Method: 构建 Quant-Arena 基准与公开排行榜：模型生成特征工程与股票评分代码，仅基于 2022–2023 数据获得反馈，最终冻结单一策略；评估器采用现金账户模拟，施加交易约束与成本（含基于模拟订单簿估计的市场冲击），并以封存年度 2024 作为样本外测试。

Result: 评分以已实现超额收益为主导，辅以信号质量与所用特征多样性。评测九个前沿模型，GPT-6 Astra 得分最高，但没有任何模型在 2024 年跑赢等权市场基准。

Conclusion: 现有 LLM 尚无法通过端到端量化研究闭环获得样本外真实超额收益，Quant-Arena 提供可复现、防过拟合的公开评测平台，并向任意模型或智能体框架开放提交。

Abstract: Quantitative research is an iterative loop: a researcher proposes a market hypothesis, implements it as an executable signal, tests it on historical data, and decides what to keep. Existing benchmarks score formulas, code, or individual trading decisions, leaving open whether large language models (LLMs) can complete this loop and produce strategies that work beyond the research period. We introduce Quant-Arena, a benchmark and public leaderboard for end-to-end quantitative research on real A-share market data. A model constructs features and stock-scoring code, receives feedback only on 2022--2023, and freezes one strategy for assessment on the sealed year 2024. A cash-account evaluator applies trading constraints and costs, including market impact estimated from a simulated order book. Scores are dominated by realized excess return and complemented by signal quality and the diversity of features used by the strategy. We evaluate nine frontier models, among which GPT-6 Astra scores highest; yet no model beats the equal-weight market in 2024. Quant-Arena is open to submissions from any model or agent harness.

</details>


### [152] [Keeping Time vs. Understanding Time: Neither Alone Explains Temporal Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29507)
*Auss Abbood,Fernando Martínez-Plumed,José Hernández-Orallo,Nigel Collier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文实证检验"时间推理=时间算术+时间语义"这一二分假设，发现基准中标注为算术/语义的任务只在各自数据集内带来增益，跨数据集迁移几乎为零（-3.2 至 +1.1 个百分点），且驱动性能的主要能力是知识而非定量或逻辑推理，说明现有基准并未实现算术-语义区分，而是主要奖励模型对时间常识的记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间推理被普遍假定可分解为时间算术（日期与时间计算）与时间语义（理解时间及因果关系）两种模式。该二分法影响了基准设计、模型训练与进展评估，但其经验有效性从未被直接检验。作者旨在直接检验这一二分是否成立。

Method: 两条证据线：（1）迁移检验——在基准中被标注为算术或语义的子集上微调，观察同一"模式"内部能否获得可迁移增益，并测试跨数据集（同模式）迁移；（2）基于认知需求的模型评估——考察哪些能力实际驱动这些基准上的表现，检验定量推理、逻辑推理作为算术/语义代理变量的有效性。

Result: 微调主要带来数据集特定的增益（+6.8 至 +23.1 个百分点），而同模式跨数据集迁移仅为 -3.2 至 +1.1 个百分点；在两个基准家族中均缺乏连贯、可迁移的时间能力的证据。认知需求评估显示，定量推理与逻辑推理（被假定为算术与语义的代理）并非主要驱动因素，知识才是。

Conclusion: 当前时间推理基准未能清晰实现算术-语义二分，其得分在很大程度上奖励的是模型对"时间通常如何运作"这一常识的记忆。这要求重新审视基准设计与能力归因方式。

Abstract: Temporal reasoning is widely assumed to decompose into two modes: temporal arithmetic (date and time calculations), and temporal semantics (understanding temporal and causal relations). This dichotomy shapes how benchmarks are designed, how models are trained, and how progress is measured, yet its empirical validity has never been directly tested. We provide two lines of evidence that challenge it. First, we test whether tasks labelled as arithmetic or semantic by the benchmarks support transferable gains within their purported mode. Finetuning on these subsets yields predominantly dataset-specific gains ($+6.8$ to $+23.1$ percentage points), while transfer to a different dataset in the same mode ranges from $-3.2$ to $+1.1$ percentage points. Across the two benchmark families used in the transfer analysis, this pattern provides little evidence of coherent, transferable temporal abilities. Second, using cognitive-demand-based model evaluation, we examine which capabilities actually drive performance on these benchmarks. We find that quantitative and logical reasoning, the assumed proxies for temporal arithmetic and temporal semantics, are not the primary drivers; knowledge is. Together, these findings show that current temporal reasoning benchmarks do not cleanly realise the arithmetic-semantics distinction and instead largely reward models for remembering how time typically works.

</details>


### [153] [Accept or Repair: Preserving Passage-Supported Answers in Contrastive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29727)
*Jungseob Lee,Seungyoon Lee,Hyeonseok Moon,Seongtae Hong,Suhyune Son,Sugyeong Eo,Minhyuk Kim,Chanjun Park,Jaehyung Seo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 Accept-or-Repair (AoR) 流程：用段落校验采样答案，仅当段落不支持时才用对比解码替换，从而在反事实段落上提升 12.5 分、普通段落上提升 2.3 分，并超越八种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型常依据训练知识作答，即使给定段落与之矛盾；而逐 token 的对比解码虽能纠正知识冲突，却也会破坏段落本已支持的正确答案，存在"纠错—保真"的权衡。

Method: 观察发现用段落校验答案可消除该权衡，据此设计 AoR：先采样答案，若段落支持则保留；否则进行至多两次对比解码替换，一旦通过校验即返回；若替换结果只是复现无段落时的答案、而段落支持另一答案，则拒绝该替换。全程无需训练。

Result: 在反事实段落上比采样提升 12.5 分，普通段落上提升 2.3 分；在两类设置下均优于八种对比解码与提示基线，且在各基线同样施加拒绝规则时依然领先。仅替换段落不支持的初始答案可提升每种解码器的平均得分。

Conclusion: 模型能够在遵循与自身知识相矛盾的段落的同时，保持在与知识一致的段落上的准确率；关键在于以段落为依据的条件化替换策略。

Abstract: Large language models often answer from knowledge learned during training, even when a provided passage contradicts that knowledge. To address this problem, contrastive decoding contrasts the predictions under the full and a modified prompt at every token, but it also alters correct answers that the passage supports. We show that checking answers against the passage removes this trade-off. Based on this observation, we propose Accept-or-Repair (AoR), a training-free procedure. AoR keeps a sampled answer if the passage supports it. Otherwise, it tries up to two replacements with contrastive decoding, returning one as soon as it passes this check. It rejects replacements that repeat the answer generated without the passage when the passage favors another answer. Our experiments show that AoR raises the score over sampling by 12.5 points on counterfactual passages and 2.3 on ordinary ones, surpassing eight contrastive and prompting baselines on both, including when each receives the same rejection rule. Replacing only initial answers that the passage does not support improves the average score of every decoder. These results suggest that models can follow passages that contradict their knowledge while preserving accuracy on passages that agree with it. Code will be released at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/AoR.

</details>


### [154] [Target Regeneration Shrinks the Gains of Draft-Training Objectives in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29013)
*Jungseob Lee,Hogun Seong,Euijun Choi,Sugyeong Eo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文质疑投机解码中草稿训练目标的增益评估方式：若目标模型会重写外部写作文本，则蒸馏与验证门控的增益大部分消失，应改用目标模型再生成的基线并在多种训练长度下评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有草稿模型训练目标（token级蒸馏、验证存活门控）的增益通常以交叉熵在"外部撰写回复"上的表现为基线来衡量；但许多流水线实际会让目标模型重写这些回复。作者因此想检验：在目标模型重写数据后，这些目标带来的接受率增益还有多少能保留。

Method: 以视觉语言目标模型加块状（block）草稿器为设置，在"同一语料的四个版本"上训练三种目标：语料token上的交叉熵、来自目标模型的token级蒸馏、以及仅在能通过验证的位置上加权的门控蒸馏。控制变量包括数据顺序、位置权重、验证器、解码温度，以及蒸馏的初始化与学习率；并做监督长度匹配、各目标最优训练长度、第二个视觉语料、纯文本复现（含自回归草稿器）等检验。

Result: 在外部回复上，蒸馏与门控带来大幅增益；但在贪心再生成（目标模型重写）数据上，蒸馏增益变小，门控几乎无增益。该衰减在数据顺序、位置权重、验证器、解码温度下稳定存在；对蒸馏还跨初始化与学习率稳定。衰减在长度匹配的监督数据上和各目标最佳训练长度上仍大部分保留；第二个视觉语料、纯文本复现以及自回归草稿器下的蒸馏均再现该现象。

Conclusion: 草稿训练目标的增益应以"目标模型再生成"的数据为基线，并跨多个训练长度进行测量，否则会高估实际收益；门控类目标在真实流水线中的边际价值可能极小。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language models by verifying drafted tokens in parallel. New draft-training objectives are usually credited with the acceptance they add over cross-entropy on externally written responses, yet many pipelines let the target rewrite them. We ask how much of that gain survives. With a vision-language target and a block drafter, we train three objectives on four versions of one corpus: cross-entropy on corpus tokens, token-level distillation from the target, and distillation gated to positions that would survive verification. Distillation and the gate add large gains on external responses, but after greedy regeneration, distillation adds a smaller gain and the gate little or none. The shrinkage persists across data orders, position weights, verifiers, decoding temperatures and, for distillation, initializations and learning rates. Most of it remains at matched supervised length and at each objective's best measured training length. A second vision corpus shows the shrinkage, as does a text-only replication, for distillation also with an autoregressive drafter. Objective gains should therefore be measured against a target-regenerated baseline and across training lengths. Our code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/TargetRegeneration.

</details>


### [155] [Let Agents Design How They Search: Automatic Interface Optimization for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29250)
*Sangjune An,Jaeyoon Jung,Yumin Choi,Jinheon Baek,Junmo Cho,Sung Ju Hwang,Hae Beom Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SearchWeaver 框架，用下游检索表现驱动自动优化 agentic search 的搜索接口，联合改写语料工件、可执行工具与使用说明，在 BrowseComp-Plus 与 BRIGHT 上显著优于 Direct Corpus Interaction。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic search 的效果高度依赖语言模型智能体访问与探索语料的接口，但现有接口均为人工固定设计并对所有语料一成不变，忽略了有效接口设计可能随语料而异。

Method: SearchWeaver 以执行轨迹与任务奖励为信号，由一个 proposer 联合修订三类接口要素：语料工件（预先准备的信息）、可执行工具（工具执行的操作）以及使用说明（留给搜索智能体的决策），从而共同优化“预置信息—工具操作—智能体决策”的分工。

Result: 配合 GPT-5.4-nano，优化后的接口相较 Direct Corpus Interaction 将 BrowseComp-Plus 答案准确率由 71.6% 提升至 78.4%，在四个 BRIGHT 子集上平均 nDCG@10 由 45.7 提升至 55.0。

Conclusion: 联合优化语料工件、可执行工具与使用说明，是构建高效搜索接口的有效途径，且接口设计应针对具体语料自动定制而非手工固定。

Abstract: Agentic search depends on the interface through which a language-model agent accesses and explores a corpus. Existing interface designs are fixed by hand and applied unchanged to every corpus, although effective designs may depend on the corpus. We introduce SearchWeaver, a framework for automatic search interface optimization guided by downstream search performance. A proposer uses execution trajectories and task rewards to jointly revise corpus artifacts, executable tools, and usage instructions. This pro cess optimizes what information is prepared in advance, which operations tools perform, and which decisions remain with the search agent. With GPT-5.4-nano, the optimized interfaces improve answer accuracy from 71.6% to 78.4% on BrowseComp-Plus and average nDCG@10 from 45.7 to 55.0 across four BRIGHT subsets over Direct Corpus Interaction. These findings highlight the value of jointly optimizing corpus artifacts, executable tools, and usage instructions to develop effective search interfaces.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Binary Labels: Diagnostic Evaluation of LLM Safety with Prompt-Level Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29225)
*Hongyu Li,Xinlong Huang,Zhan Yu,Jing Zhao,Zhe Zhao,Juntao Zhong,Xiong Kuang,Rui Yuan,ZhenXiang Yan,Huilin Xu,Haitao Yu,Lilin Wang,Rui Chen,Blaze Chen,Fengzong Lian,Renfen Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出诊断式安全评测框架 DiagSafe，用提示级双维度（安全性/有用性）评分量表替代二元标签，并构建中英双语基准（DiagBase、DiagAttack），使 LLM 评审与人工标注一致率达 95.34%、评审间一致性 κ=0.864。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 安全基准多采用“安全/不安全”二元标签，缺乏解释深度，且不同自动评审器之间一致性低，难以可靠审计模型对齐状况。

Method: 以全球适用的 AI 安全政策为依据，设计提示级评分量表（rubric），包含两个维度：安全性（针对具体危害，并设豁免条款以抑制假阳性）与有用性（独立评估指令完成度）；基于 84 标签安全分类体系构建中英双语基准，含 DiagBase（单/多轮直接有害查询与模板化越狱）与 DiagAttack（将有害意图嵌入复杂指令遵循任务形成指令攻击）。

Result: DiagSafe 显著提升 LLM-as-a-judge 可靠性：与人工标注一致率 95.34%，远高于基线的 69%–87%；评审间一致性 Cohen's κ=0.864。应用该框架发现主流 LLM 不安全率差异显著，各模型在不同维度上有独特脆弱点；模型在中文与多轮情境下尤为易受影响。

Conclusion: DiagSafe 为 LLM 对齐的审计与优化提供了可靠、可解释的评测工具。

Abstract: Current LLM safety benchmarks predominantly rely on binary safe or unsafe labels, lacking explanatory depth and suffering from low consistency across automated judges. To address this, we introduce DiagSafe, a diagnostic evaluation framework that moves beyond binary classification by employing prompt-level rubrics. Grounded in globally applicable AI safety policies, each rubric provides explicit criteria along two dimensions: safety, which targets concrete harms with specific exemptions to suppress false positives, and usefulness, which independently evaluates instruction fulfillment. To ensure a robust and realistic evaluation, we construct a bilingual (English-Chinese) benchmark grounded in a comprehensive 84-label safety taxonomy, consisting of two subsets: DiagBase, which covers single- and multi-turn prompts with direct harmful queries and template-based jailbreaks, and DiagAttack, which embeds harmful intents into complex instruction-following tasks to form challenging instruction attacks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DiagSafe significantly enhances the reliability of LLM-as-a-judge evaluation, achieving 95.34% agreement with human annotations -- substantially higher than the 69%-87% achieved by the baselines -- and high inter-judge consistency (Cohen's $κ$ = 0.864). Furthermore, applying our framework reveals that leading LLMs differ markedly in their unsafe rates, and each model also exhibits unique vulnerabilities along different dimensions. In addition, we observe that models are particularly susceptible in Chinese and multi-turn contexts. Ultimately, DiagSafe provides a reliable and explainable tool for auditing and optimizing LLM alignment.

</details>


### [157] [Scan or Index? large Evidence Coverage, Answer Quality, and Cost in Long-Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28957)
*Juan Terven,Diana-Margarita Córdova-Esparza,Julio-Alejandro Romero-González,Edgar Chavez-Urbiola,Juan Irving Vasquez,Julian Andres Ramirez-Bautista,Silvia L. Chaparro-Cárdenas,Gendry Alfonso-Francia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在396道QASPER问题与8本完整教科书80题上比较11种检索配置（词法/稠密检索、混合重排、Jev与Cloudflare Clef全文档扫描），以证据覆盖率、答案准确率和成本为互补指标评估长文档检索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全文档扫描看似最彻底，但是否能保留更多有用证据、产出更好答案尚不明确；需要区分"证据保留"与"答案质量"这两个常被混淆的评估维度。

Method: 构建11种检索配置：词法与稠密检索、混合重排（Hybrid）、基于Jev、OpenAI Decisions与Cloudflare Clef的全文档扫描；统一5段落选取上限、固定答案生成器，在QASPER 396题与8本教科书80题上评测，并分析延迟与累积查询下的API成本。

Result: 书籍数据上Hybrid+Jev证据覆盖率最高（94.1%），Jev全扫描88.7%，OpenAI稠密检索81.3%；全扫描虽初始查看所有段落，后续缩减步骤会丢弃相关证据。Hybrid+Decisions答案准确率最高（0.892），尽管其证据召回较低。全扫描在测试部署下延迟显著更高；可复用稠密检索在查询累积时预计成本低于付费决策类方法。多数QASPER文章已落入混合候选池，限制了扫描收益。

Conclusion: 证据保留、答案质量与复用成本是选择长文档检索策略的互补准则，高证据覆盖率并不必然带来更优答案，全扫描并不天然优于高效的混合检索。

Abstract: Scanning every passage in a long document seems thorough, but does it preserve more useful evidence or produce better answers? We compare 11 retrieval configurations spanning lexical and dense search, hybrid reranking, and full-document scanning with Jev, OpenAI Decisions, and Cloudflare Clef. Our evaluation uses 396 QASPER questions and 80 questions from eight complete textbooks, with common passages, a five-passage limit, and a fixed answer generator. On books, Hybrid + Jev achieves the highest observed evidence coverage (94.1%), compared with 88.7% for Jev full scan and 81.3\% for OpenAI dense retrieval. Full scans examine every passage initially, yet later reduction steps can discard relevant evidence. Higher evidence coverage also does not consistently produce better answers: Hybrid + Decisions achieves the highest observed book Answer Accuracy among retrieval methods (0.892), despite recovering less reference evidence. Full scanning incurs substantially higher latency on books under the tested deployments, while reusable dense retrieval has lower projected API cost than paid decision-based methods as queries accumulate. Most QASPER articles already fit within the hybrid candidate pool, limiting the benefit of scanning. These results highlight evidence retention, answer quality, and reuse cost as complementary criteria for choosing a long-document retrieval strategy.

</details>


### [158] [SANE: Automated Scorer-Specific Metamorphic Testing for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28886)
*Ethan Ludwick,Yu-Chung Hsiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SANE：一种基于智能体的框架，将随机评估器的行为需求编码为可复用的智能体指令，自动生成针对特定打分器的扰动配方与预期分数关系，并提供与打分器无关的验证框架；在五个领域中生成的可验证测试族数量为JRH的3.7倍，且与人类评判的排序一致性显著更高（ρ=0.852 vs 0.100）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判器广泛应用于基准评估、模型选择、数据筛选与训练，其可靠性至关重要。现有可靠性测试依赖人工撰写的扰动配方加合成测试生成，配方构思环节未被自动化，导致方法局限于固定扰动与预设的打分器类型，难以适配多样化的打分器。

Method: 提出SANE智能体框架：输入打分器规范、实现与代表性输入，先识别打分器特有的能力维度，再自动推导各维度对应的扰动配方及预期分数关系；同时定义与打分器无关的验证框架，检验每条配方是否符合打分器意图、每个实例化用例是否落实配方并支撑预期关系。由于配方库针对每个打分器定制生成，随打分器增加而累积扩展。

Result: 在五个领域中，SANE生成的有效验证测试族数量为Judge Reliability Harness（JRH）的3.7倍，端到端有效率相当，且在每个领域均能检出打分器缺陷（包括专业性与毒性维度，JRH在这些维度均未检出）。在忠实性任务上，SANE的测试套件得分对11个评判模型的排序与人工标注的FaithBench表现高度一致（Spearman ρ=0.852），JRH则不具此一致性（ρ=0.100）。

Conclusion: 通过将配方构思自动化并与验证解耦，SANE克服了固定扰动与预设打分器类型的局限，提升了LLM评判器可靠性测试的覆盖面与有效性，并在与人类判断的对齐上明显优于现有测试工具。

Abstract: LLM judges increasingly mediate benchmark evaluation, model selection, data curation, and training. Their decisions are useful only when each judge responds reliably to changes affecting its scoring criterion. Existing methods for reliability testing perturb inputs with known expected effects on judge scores. Prominent LLM-judge testing harnesses combine human-authored perturbation recipes with synthetic test generation, leaving scorer-specific recipe ideation outside the automated pipeline. This limits their use to fixed perturbations and pre-assumed scorer types. We introduce SANE, an agent-based framework that encodes behavioral requirements for stochastic evaluators as reusable agent instructions. Given a scorer specification, implementation, and representative inputs, the pipeline first identifies scorer-specific capabilities, then automatically derives perturbation recipes and expected score relations for each. SANE also defines a scorer-independent validation framework for generated judge tests. It determines whether each recipe agrees with scorer intent and whether each instantiated case realizes the recipe and supports its expected relation. Because SANE generates a recipe inventory tailored to each scorer, the number of aligned recipes grows as scorers are added. Across five domains, SANE produces 3.7 times as many validated test families as the Judge Reliability Harness (JRH) at comparable end-to-end validity and detects scorer deficiencies in every domain, including professionalism and toxicity, where JRH detects none. On faithfulness, SANE achieves strong human alignment in judge ranking: its test-suite scores rank 11 judge models consistently with human-labeled FaithBench performance (Spearman rho = 0.852), while JRH does not (rho = 0.100).

</details>


### [159] [KIRA: A Full-Stack Reading Screening System with Dual-Pathway Analysis for Children](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28840)
*Maggie Dong,Rohit Mekkoth,Zixuan Wang,Cheol Jun Cho,Jiachen Lian,Gopala Anumanchipalli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出KIRA，一个双通路早期阅读筛查系统，将词级流利度评分与音素级错误检测相结合，通过将预训练词/音素识别器适配儿童语音（WER 13.88%→8.84%，PER 41.66%→23.00%），实现Word Reading与Oral Reading Fluency两项K-2基础任务，并集成面向教师的Web界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期阅读筛查对识别阅读困难风险儿童至关重要，但现有AI筛查系统分析范围与评价指标有限，或为闭源专有系统、缺乏透明的模型规格与评估。

Method: 构建双通路筛查系统KIRA：将预训练的词识别器与音素识别器适配儿童语音；基于适配后的模型实现WRE与ORF两项任务；并将识别器与教师端Web界面集成，输出流利度分数与音素反馈以支持针对性干预。

Result: 儿童语音适配使词错误率由13.88%降至8.84%，音素错误率由41.66%降至23.00%；系统可同时提供流利度评分与细粒度音素错误信息。

Conclusion: KIRA将现代儿童语音识别技术整合为全栈、可部署、可直接用于课堂的阅读筛查工具，兼顾透明评估与细粒度诊断反馈。

Abstract: Early-grade reading screening is critical for identifying children at risk of reading difficulty. Existing AI-based screeners are often limited in the scope of their analyses and evaluation metrics, or are proprietary systems that lack transparent model specifications and evaluation. We present KIRA, a dual-pathway screening system that pairs word-level fluency scoring with detailed phoneme-level error detection. We adapt pretrained word and phoneme recognizers to children's speech, reducing word error rate from 13.88% to 8.84% and phoneme error rate from 41.66% to 23.00%. Powered by these child-adapted models, we implement Word Reading (WRE) and Oral Reading Fluency (ORF), two foundational tasks in K-2 screening. The system integrates these recognizers with a web-based teacher-facing interface presenting fluency scores alongside phoneme feedback for potential targeted intervention. KIRA brings modern child-speech recognition into a full-stack, deployable, classroom-ready reading screener.

</details>


### [160] [Is Anyone Enforcing the Cartel? What Reinforcement Learning Teaches LLM Agents in Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28787)
*Hieu Tran,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种"审计"(audit)方法，通过在反事实市场副本中强制某一成员作弊来检验合谋是否具备真正的"执行(enforcement)"机制，并据此分析强化学习在定价与交易中究竟教会了智能体什么。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有文献根据结果指标（如价格水平）判断LLM智能体"合谋"，但合谋的定义核心在于执行：卡特尔（成员共享垄断利润）只有在成员背叛时会受到惩罚才成立。仅看结果无法区分真正的可执行合谋、固定规则、以及对报复的恐惧等不同机制。

Method: 设计审计实验：在市场的反事实副本中强迫某个成员作弊，观察其他成员是否施加惩罚。将该审计用于小型LLM（经强化学习训练）以及Q-learner、提示型LLM，在定价与交易两类场景中检验RL实际学到什么、以及在对抗性对手（惩罚型、作弊型）下的行为。

Result: RL训练的小模型只学会其训练对手所奖励的行为，且仅限其"容易尝试"的行为：自博弈中学到忽视对手的固定规则；面对惩罚型对手学会被威慑；面对作弊型对手学会跟随降价，但从不学会能真正威慑对方的代价高昂的价格战（因其很少尝试）；强制在训练中引入价格战也仅在强制处被学到。面对自身副本时，被威慑型与跟随降价型智能体都能获得相同的卡特尔利润，但只有跟随型能（微弱地）执行合谋，且是副产品、仅针对大幅降价。Q-learner仅在定价场景中执行合谋。提示型LLM达成的类卡特尔结果大多源于忽视市场的规则。

Conclusion: 结果层面指标无法区分上述不同机制，而所提出的审计方法可以。因此评估LLM智能体的合谋风险须以检验执行机制为核心，而非仅依赖结果指标。

Abstract: Evaluations of large language model (LLM) agents report that pricing agents collude at others' expense. Yet collusion is defined by enforcement: a cartel, in which competitors share monopoly profits, holds only if a member who cheats is punished. We introduce an audit that tests enforcement by forcing a member to cheat in counterfactual copies of a market, and use it to study what reinforcement learning (RL) teaches agents in pricing and trading. Small LLMs trained by RL learn what their training rivals reward, but only what they readily try. In self-play, they learn fixed rules ignoring their rivals; against a punishing rival, to be deterred; against a cheating rival, to match its cuts, never the costly price war that would deter it, which they rarely try. A war forced into training is learned, but mostly only where it was forced. Against their own copies, deterred and matching agents can earn the same cartel profits, but only matching ones can enforce them, and then thinly: as a by-product and only against large cuts. Q-learners enforce collusion only in pricing. Prompted LLMs reach cartel-like outcomes mostly through rules that ignore the market. Outcome measures cannot tell these cases apart; the audit can.

</details>


### [161] [Understanding Target Probability Reuse in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29210)
*Jungseob Lee,Giyoon Jang,Chailin Nam,Jiwoo Kum,Sihyeon Lee,Kyojun Choo,Jihun Song,Eunseo Song,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究推测解码中目标模型验证产生的下一个token分布能否用于指导下一轮草稿树的选取，理论刻画了精确复用分布的条件，并提出基于当前上下文单独计算分布来重排首token分支、以及先抽取首token再分配树的策略，实验证明可提升期望接受率并加速解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码通常用草稿模型构建token树，再由目标模型并行验证，但验证过程产生的下一个token分布信息在传统方法中未被用于指导下一轮的树结构选择。作者希望探究这些分布能否用于改进树的分配与选取，从而进一步提升推测解码的效率。

Method: 1）理论分析：对无约束目标模型，证明保证精确复用需要下一轮完整上下文处计算的分布，并刻画保留该分布需牺牲已接受token的情形及损失跨轮持续的条件；2）方法：在六个目标模型上，用当前上下文单独计算的目标分布对首token分支进行重排（替代草稿排序），并进一步先按该分布抽取首token再分配剩余树，与预选树剪枝策略在等平均树规模下比较。

Result: 用单独计算的目标分布重排首token分支相比草稿排序提高了期望接受率，尤其在树预算较小时更明显；先抽取首token再分配树的策略在等平均树规模下优于剪枝预选树；当独立目标前向与独立草稿生成重叠时，两种选择规则均比草稿排序解码更快。

Conclusion: 在树分配之前获得目标概率可提升推测解码效率；理论揭示了精确分布复用的代价与限制，实践上则表明基于当前上下文的目标分布重排/抽样首token是一种简单有效的改进手段。

Abstract: Speculative decoding uses a target model to verify a tree of draft tokens in parallel. We study whether the resulting next-token distributions can help select the next tree. For unrestricted target models, we show that guaranteed exact reuse requires a distribution computed at the next round's complete context. We characterize when retaining this distribution requires sacrificing accepted tokens and establish conditions under which this loss persists across decoding rounds. Across six targets, we find that reranking first-token branches with a separately computed distribution at the current context improves expected acceptance over draft ranking, especially with small tree budgets. Drawing the first token from this distribution before allocating the tree further improves expected acceptance over pruning a preselected tree, even at equal mean tree size. In our experiments, both selection rules also decode faster than draft ranking when the separate target pass overlaps standalone drafting. Our findings show that making target probabilities available before tree allocation can improve the efficiency of speculative decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/TargetRows.

</details>


### [162] [Memory-Centric Continual Learning for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29357)
*Dongfang Li,Zhuoen Chen,Bonian Jia,Zixuan Liu,Zhengxuan Lu,Can Deng,Yukun Shi,Yuhang Meng,Xin Zhang,Jiahe Huang,Fuhao Li,Junmai Wang,Shunyao Wen,Jingjun Sun,Jiaxu Tian,Jiangling Zhang,Zihan Wang,Liangyou Li,Yitong Yao,Shuangyong Song,Beilun Wang,Pengxu Wei,Hanlin Xu,Chang Liu,Yongxiang Li,Baotian Hu,Xiangyang Ji,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出以记忆为中心的持续学习（MCCL）框架，主张记忆系统的评价标准应是能否改进未来行动，而非检索准确率，并给出三维分类学、双循环生命周期、评测协议与记忆缩放框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于基础模型的自主智能体权重固定、上下文受限，难以在长期部署中从经验持续学习；现有外部记忆系统多为被动检索缓存，主要按"能否召回已存事实或历史交互"来评估，记忆能否驱动真正的持续学习尚无定论。

Method: MCCL包含三部分：(1) 三轴分类学，按状态存储位置（substrates）、记忆形态（assets）、管理方式（policies）组织记忆，并界定双循环生命周期——内循环更新记忆状态，外循环依据任务反馈优化管理策略；(2) 评测协议，从干扰下的保持、对变化的适应、任务迁移三方面度量，以区分真实行为增益与单纯的上下文扩张；(3) 记忆缩放框架，建模智能体性能随经验量、存储容量与算力预算在非参数化与参数化记忆下的缩放关系。

Result: 摘要为立场/框架型论文，未报告具体实验数值；其"结果"在于提出统一的概念体系、可操作的评测维度与性能缩放模型，为记忆驱动的持续学习提供可检验的分析工具。

Conclusion: 该工作将智能体记忆从被动查找重构为持续学习机制，为构建可自我改进的自主智能体提供了原则性路径。

Abstract: Autonomous agents powered by foundation models can reason, plan, and use tools, but their fixed weights and context limits hinder sustained learning from experience over long-term deployment. While external memory systems attempt to bridge this gap, they mostly act as passive retrieval caches, evaluated primarily by whether they can recall stored facts or past interactions. Whether memory can drive genuine continual learning remains an open question. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Centric Continual Learning (MCCL), arguing that memory should be judged by whether it improves future actions, not merely what it retrieves. MCCL has three main parts. First, a three-axis taxonomy organizes memory by where state is stored (substrates), what form it takes (assets), and how it is managed (policies). This defines a two-loop lifecycle that updates memory state in an inner loop while refining management policies in an outer loop based on task feedback. Second, an evaluation protocol measures retention under interference, adaptation to change, and task transfer to isolate genuine behavioral gains from simple context expansion. Third, a memory scaling framework models how agent performance scales with experience, storage capacity, and compute budgets across non-parametric and parametric memory. Together, these components reframe agent memory from passive lookup to continuous learning, offering a principled route toward self-improving autonomous agents.

</details>


### [163] [Mitigating Query Shortcuts in Chain-of-Thought with Context-Aware Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29518)
*Shin'ya Yamaguchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将上下文感知解码（CAD）应用于CoT推理，提出Rationale Contrastive Decoding（RCD），通过对比"有/无推理链"条件下的下一词分布来放大真正受推理链影响的token，从而缓解查询捷径问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT提示虽提升LLM推理表现，但最终答案并不总依赖生成的推理步骤，模型可能依赖查询特定的捷径（query-specific shortcuts）而非推理链信息，削弱了CoT的可信度。

Method: 将CAD改造为RCD：对同一个查询，分别在有推理链与无推理链条件下计算下一词分布，对比两者并提升因推理链而概率上升的token权重；并从KL正则化的奖励最大化目标出发给出该解码规则的理论解释。在五个推理基准上评估，并通过ground-truth推理链与随机推理链的干预分析检验答案对推理链内容的行为依赖性。

Result: RCD在五个推理基准上相较标准CoT提升了答案准确率；干预分析显示RCD增强了最终答案对推理链内容的行为依赖，即答案更受推理链影响。

Conclusion: 对比解码可为缓解查询捷径提供一种简单、无需训练的方法，使LLM的最终答案更依赖推理链内容。

Abstract: While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting improves the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs), recent studies have shown that final answers do not always depend on the generated rationales. In particular, LLMs may rely on query-specific shortcuts rather than the information provided by their reasoning steps. In this paper, we investigate whether context-aware decoding (CAD), an existing contrastive decoding method, can mitigate such shortcut behavior when applied to CoT reasoning. We study this application as Rationale Contrastive Decoding (RCD), which contrasts the next-token distributions conditioned on the query with and without its rationale, amplifying tokens whose likelihood increases due to the rationale. We provide a theoretical interpretation of this decoding rule via a KL-regularized reward-maximization objective. Experiments across five reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RCD improves answer accuracy over standard CoT. Furthermore, intervention analyses using ground-truth and random rationales reveal that RCD increases the behavioral dependence of final answers on the rationale content. Our findings suggest that contrastive decoding offers a simple, training-free approach to mitigating query shortcuts.

</details>


### [164] [DynaWeave: Learning to Adapt through Dynamic Scenario Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29443)
*Xuan Yang,Chunxiao Liu,Hongsheng Xin,Hao Xu,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DynaWeave：将静态任务自动转化为可验证的动态场景，用于训练能应对外源事件的LLM智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体训练环境除智能体自身动作外基本静态，而真实环境中其他用户、服务或突发情况引入的外源事件会使进行中的计划失效，二者存在差距。

Method: 提出自动化合成框架DynaWeave：为每个任务生成相关外源事件，涵盖任务需求、环境状态与外部信息变化；用有向无环图（DAG）表示事件间依赖，并由执行引擎在交互中激活；动态验证器仅修订实际发生事件所影响的评估检查，保留其余检查。基于该框架收集36个环境中的2,095条已校验交互轨迹，对Qwen3.5进行监督微调。

Result: 在Gaia2、CostBench、ToolMaze上，各评估模型规模均获一致性能提升；CostBench任务完成率提升67.34个百分点，ToolMaze上甚至超过Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview。

Conclusion: 动态场景合成是训练智能体适应变化环境的可行且有效途径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use tools to complete tasks in stateful environments. However, many training environments remain largely static apart from changes caused by the agent's own actions, whereas external events introduced by other users, services, or sudden contingencies in real environments can invalidate ongoing plans. To address this gap, we introduce DynaWeave, an automated synthesis framework that transforms existing static tasks into verifiable dynamic scenarios. The framework generates exogenous events relevant to each task, covering changes in task requirements, environment state, and external information. It uses directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) to represent dependencies between events and an execution engine to activate them during agent interaction. A dynamic verifier revises only the evaluation checks affected by events that actually occur and retains all other checks. Using DynaWeave, we collect 2,095 verified interaction trajectories across 36 environments and use them for supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5 models. Experiments on Gaia2, CostBench, and ToolMaze show that DynaWeave achieves consistent improvements in overall performance across all three evaluated model sizes, increasing the task completion rate on CostBench by 67.34 percentage points and even exceeding the performance of Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview on ToolMaze. These results support dynamic scenario synthesis as a practical approach to training agents to adapt to changing environments. Our code is available at https://github.com/Miaow-Lab/DynaWeave.

</details>


### [165] [ChronoSynth: Constraint-Aware Question Synthesis over Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29339)
*Feng Liang,Weixin Zeng,Runhao Zhao,Mingjun Zhou,Shaoshi Yang,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究时序知识图谱问题生成（Temporal KGQG），将其形式化为"约束保持的图到问题生成"问题，提出记忆增强、约束感知的框架 ChronoSynth，通过显式建模时序意图状态、检索类型化问句模式并做验证-修订，缓解生成中的时序语义漂移，在 CRONQUESTIONS 与 MultiTQ 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图输入下的有效问题必须同时保持答案域、时间条件、参照事件与时序顺序，而现有 KGQG 方法与提示基线虽能生成流畅问句，却常改变这些条件，导致"时序语义漂移"（temporal semantic drift）。作者因此将时序 KGQG 重新界定为约束保持的生成问题。

Method: ChronoSynth 包含三个环节：(1) 构建 Temporal Intent State，显式化目标问句的时序语义；(2) 从带索引的模式记忆中检索兼容的类型化问句模式（typed question patterns），并适配到输入图；(3) 对生成问句进行验证，当检测到有依据的不一致（supported inconsistency）时，以验证反馈引导修订。

Result: 在 CRONQUESTIONS 和 MultiTQ 两个时序 KGQG 数据集上达到 state-of-the-art 生成性能；消融实验支持各组件（意图状态、模式记忆检索、验证反馈修订）的贡献。

Conclusion: 约束保持视角与"意图显式化 + 模式检索适配 + 验证修订"的框架能有效抑制时序语义漂移，提升时序 KGQG 的语义保真度；摘要未给出具体指标数值与误差分析，其泛化性与验证器覆盖范围仍待进一步评估。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question generation (KGQG) generates natural-language questions from structured graph evidence and a target answer. When the input graph is temporal, a valid question must also preserve the answer field, time condition, reference event, and temporal order that determine the answer set. Existing KGQG methods and prompting baselines can produce fluent questions that alter these conditions, causing temporal semantic drift. This paper studies temporal KGQG as a constraint-preserving graph-to-question problem and proposes ChronoSynth, a memory-augmented and constraint-aware generation framework. ChronoSynth first constructs a Temporal Intent State to make the intended question semantics explicit. It then retrieves compatible typed question patterns from an indexed pattern memory, adapts them to the input graph, and validates the generated question. Validation feedback guides revisions when a supported inconsistency is detected. Experiments on CRONQUESTIONS and MultiTQ demonstrate state-of-the-art generation performance, with ablations supporting the contributions of its components.

</details>


### [166] [Signals and Queries: Token-Efficient Agentic Context Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29326)
*Yam Marcovitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Signals and Queries (S&Q)，一种为 Parlant 4.0 设计的轻量级一阶段相关性过滤器：为每条策略离线生成激活示例与困难负例，训练独立的 logistic 分类器，在不进行逐轮 LLM 调用的情况下预测策略相关性，可在下游评估前剔除至少 30% 的候选策略并保持高召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向用户的 LLM 智能体需在每轮判断哪些行为策略适用，但用 LLM 逐一评估所有策略代价高昂，因此需要低成本的第一阶段过滤来缩减候选集。

Method: 对每条策略，由 LLM 离线生成激活示例与困难负例；在其嵌入上训练独立的 logistic 分类器，推理时仅依赖嵌入与向量点积预测相关性，无逐轮 LLM 调用；支持各策略分类器独立更新。

Result: 基于 254 个 τ²-bench 对话前缀与 36 条航空业策略构成的 9,144 个 LLM 判定相关性标签评估，S&Q 在每个召回水平上的精确率均高于 top-K 与余弦阈值检索，且在两种嵌入提供方下结果一致，可在下游评估前剔除至少 30% 候选策略并保持高召回。

Conclusion: 结果表明，低成本的逐策略分类可作为实用的第一阶段过滤手段，显著降低面向客户智能体的下游评估工作量。

Abstract: User-facing LLM agents must identify which behavioral policies apply at each turn, but evaluating every policy with an LLM is costly. We introduce Signals and Queries (S&Q), a lightweight first-stage relevance filter developed for Parlant 4.0 that removes at least 30% of candidate policies before downstream evaluation while preserving high recall. For each policy, an LLM generates activating examples and hard negatives offline. An independent logistic classifier trained on their embeddings then predicts relevance using embeddings and vector dot products, with no per-turn LLM calls. This shifts expensive relevance evaluation to a smaller candidate set, while allowing each policy's classifier to be updated independently. Evaluated against 9,144 LLM-judge relevance labels covering 254 $τ^2$-bench conversation prefixes and 36 airline policies, S&Q achieves higher precision than top-K and cosine-threshold retrieval at every evaluated recall level, with consistent results across two embedding providers. These findings support inexpensive, per-policy classification as a practical first stage for reducing downstream evaluation workload in customer-facing agents.

</details>


### [167] [Intergroup Bias Predicts Expressions of Social Judgment and Empathy in the r/AmITheAsshole Reddit Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29254)
*Yu Hou,Hal Daumé,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，在Reddit的r/AmItheAsshole社区中，当评论者与发帖者同属一个性别或年龄段时，评论者更倾向给出"Not the Asshole"判决并在文本中表现出更强的理解与共情；这种内群体效应还会被发帖者与冲突对方的群体关系所调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内群体共情差距（intergroup empathy gap）在社会心理学中已有大量研究，但其在自然发生的在线社交互动中如何体现尚不清晰。作者希望将这一实验室结论推广到真实网络话语场景，检验共享社会身份（性别、年龄）是否影响评论者对发帖者的理解与共情表达。

Method: 以r/AmItheAsshole为研究对象，选取发帖者自我报告性别与年龄的帖子，共36,163条帖子、18,111名评论者产生的126,716条评论。以评论者的社会判决（是否给出NTA判决）和基于LLM测量的文本共情/理解为结果变量；控制评论者与发帖者的群体身份、帖子特征（话题、长度、得分）以及评论者级和帖子级的方差。并在明确提及冲突另一方性别与年龄的子样本中，检验发帖者—对方群体关系对内群体效应的调节作用。

Result: 当评论者与发帖者在性别或年龄上属于内群体时，评论者更可能给出NTA判决，且LLM测得的文本理解程度更高；年长评论者的行为模式与年轻评论者不同；在提及冲突对方人口属性的子样本中，评论者—发帖者内群体效应对社会判决的影响受到发帖者与对方群体关系的调节。

Conclusion: 内群体共情差距在自然在线话语中确实存在，并会影响公开的社会判决与共情表达。研究强调理解在线讨论时需考虑群际情境与社会群体的细微差异，且LLM可作为测量文本层面共情的有效工具。

Abstract: People share and understand others' experiences differently based on their social identities; often, people empathize more when the other person belongs to their ingroup compared to an outgroup. While this intergroup empathy gap is well-studied in social psychology, it remains unclear how it manifests in naturally occurring online social interactions. To this end, we measure how commenters express social judgments, as an indirect outcome of their understanding towards the posters, and empathy in text, based on whether commenters share the poster's gender or are of similar ages. We focus on posts where the poster self-reports these demographics, and analyze 126,716 comments associated with 36,163 posts from 18,111 commenters in r/AmItheAsshole, while controlling for commenter and poster group identities, post features (topic, length, and score), and both commenter- and post-level variance. Our results show that when commenters and posters are ingroup, in either gender or age, commenters are more likely to provide a Not the Asshole verdict and show greater text-based understanding as measured by LLMs. We also observe that older commenters behave differently from younger ones. Furthermore, in a subset of posts where the gender and age of the other party in the conflict are explicitly mentioned, we find that commenter-poster ingroup effects are moderated by the group relationships between the poster and the other party when commenters make social judgments. Our work highlights the importance of intergroup context and social group nuances in understanding online discourse.

</details>


### [168] [norregel: a preregistered benchmark of dated numeric values in Norwegian law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29207)
*Eirik Botten Nicolaysen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了NorRegEL基准（916道挪威法规数值题，含带日期条文历史），以"正确/过时/错误/不可解释"四分法评估语言模型在给定日期下取值的准确性，并报告两次预注册运行：H1（过时答案在模型截止日后生效的数值上更频繁）在主检验中获支持（d=17.8pp，p=0.0001），但在唯一给出单一目标值的敏感性分析S8中不显著（d=3.9，区间含0），故作者判定H1支持不稳健；H2总体成立但区间含0.5，H3不确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法规中的数值（挪威克朗金额、百分比、G的倍数、年龄上限）随修订频繁变动，模型需按"指定日期"给出当时有效值，而训练截止导致的时间错位可能诱发"过时答案"。现有评测缺乏带条文修订史、可机械判定答案类别的题项来源，也缺乏对过时答案与截止时间关系的严格预注册检验。

Method: 从Lovdata开放数据机械抽取916道题项，每题附条文带日期的历史，从而把答案分为正确、过时、错误、不可解释四类。设置三项假设：H1为截止日后生效的数值上过时答案更多；H2为过时答案集中于"最近一次不同取值"；H3为尼诺斯克语（Nynorsk）正确率低于博克马尔语（Bokmål）。进行两次预注册运行：第一轮5个模型（模型名单仅本地分析后公开），880道主项上Opus 5.5与Sonnet 4.6正确率46.7%与40.2%；第二轮在调用前公开预注册，分析8个模型，采用汇总主检验、预注册决策规则及S8等敏感性分析。

Result: 第一轮H1实质不可检验，H2、H3不确定。第二轮汇总主检验支持H1（d=17.8个百分点，95%区间[7.7, 28.9]，p=0.0001）；但S8敏感性分析（仅把与键值不同的最近值计为过时）得d=3.9，区间[-3.6, 12.7]，p=0.1754，含0。H1在其余各预注册敏感性分析中均获支持。H2为56.1%[46.6, 65.0]，区间含0.5；H3汇总检验不确定。多个模型（含Haiku 4.5）多数回答不可解释。

Conclusion: 主检验显著但关键敏感性分析S8不显著，而S8是唯一使两组共享同一目标值的设定，故作者不认为H1的成立具有稳健性；H2、H3亦未获确证。基准本身可行（机械抽取、日期化历史、四分类判定），但模型按日期取值的能力仍需更强设计与更多可解释回答来验证。题项与键值保密，题项ID、逐答分类、代码与结果发布于Zenodo。

Abstract: norregel asks language models for numeric values in Norwegian statutes, regulations and parliamentary decisions (NOK amounts, percentages, multiples of the National Insurance basic amount (G) and age limits), each at a stated date. The 916 items were extracted mechanically from Lovdata's open data, and each carries the dated history of its provision, so an answer can be classed as correct, outdated, wrong or uninterpretable. We report a first and a second preregistered run on the same items. In the first run (five models, listed publicly only after local analysis), H1, that outdated answers are more frequent for values that took effect after a model's cutoff, could not be tested in substance, and H2 and H3 were inconclusive; Opus 5.5 and Sonnet 4.6 answered 46.7% and 40.2% of the 880 primary items correctly. The second run was preregistered and published before any of its calls, and eight models were analysed. Its pooled primary test supported H1 under the preregistered decision rule (d = 17.8 percentage points, 95% interval [7.7, 28.9], p = 0.0001). In the preregistered sensitivity analysis S8, which counts as outdated only the most recent value that differs from the key, d was 3.9 [-3.6, 12.7] (p = 0.1754), an interval that contains 0. H1 was supported in every other preregistered sensitivity analysis, but S8 is the only one that gives both groups one target value, so we do not regard the support for H1 as robust. The share of outdated answers equal to that most recent value (H2) was 56.1% [46.6, 65.0], an interval that contains 0.5, and the pooled test of a lower share correct in Nynorsk than in Bokmål (H3) was inconclusive. Several models, among them Haiku 4.5, gave mostly uninterpretable answers. Items and keys are withheld; item IDs, per-answer classes, code and results are on Zenodo.

</details>


### [169] [Language Impairment as a Lens onto Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28992)
*Muxuan He,Wang Bill Zhu,Yue Chen,Elsi Kaiser,Weizhi Du,Yao Du,Samuel Nastase,Yanjun Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 受失语症启发，提出分类器引导损伤与连续激活引导两种机制可解释性框架，系统诱导LLM的类失语症功能崩溃，发现语言功能可定位于离散电路与连续向量，但彼此高度纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM以高度分布式方式编码语言却易受"焦点性破坏"，这一现象与局灶性脑损伤导致人类临床失语症高度相似。作者借此理论张力追问：LLM是否具有类似人类语言系统的功能结构。

Method: 提出受失语症启发的机制可解释性框架，采用两条互补路径：(1) 分类器引导的病灶化（classifier-guided lesioning，结构性干预）；(2) 连续激活引导（continuous activation steering，表征性干预），系统诱导类失语症的功能崩溃。

Result: 语言功能可成功分离到离散的结构性电路和连续的表示向量上；但不同失语症剖面之间存在显著的电路重叠与向量相似性，表明功能高度纠缠。此外，激活引导在下游任务上泛化稳健，而结构性病灶化则呈非对称约束。

Conclusion: LLM在功能上类似于生物大脑，依赖一种密集纠缠的架构，并可通过临床启发的干预手段被系统性地映射；工作桥接了神经心理学与机制可解释性。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) encode language in highly distributed spaces, they remain vulnerable to focal disruptions---a phenomenon strikingly similar to how localized brain damage causes clinical aphasia in humans. To investigate this theoretical tension, we ask whether LLMs possess a functional structure analogous to the human language system, by introducing a novel mechanistic interpretability framework inspired by aphasia. Through two complementary approaches, classifier-guided lesioning and continuous activation steering, we systematically induce aphasia-like breakdowns in LLMs. We find that language functions can be successfully isolated to discrete structural circuits and continuous representational vectors, yet these functions remain densely entangled with significant circuit overlap and vector similarities across distinct aphasic profiles. Furthermore, while steering generalizes robustly across downstream tasks, structural lesioning remains asymmetrically constrained. By bridging neuropsychology and mechanistic interpretability, we demonstrate that LLMs, functionally analogous to biological brains, rely on a densely entangled architecture that can be systematically mapped through clinically-informed interventions.

</details>


### [170] [Is Refusal Direction Universal across Low-Resourced Languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28897)
*Anastasiia Ivanova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文在Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct上复现Wang等(2025)的PolyRefuse流程，新增白俄罗斯语、巴什基尔语、塔吉克语三种低资源语言，发现拒绝方向的跨语言可迁移性是"分级"而非"二元"的：安全对齐与部分对齐语言的拒绝方向可广泛迁移，而强失配语言的拒绝方向即便在扣除天花板效应后也无法可靠调控任何目标语言的拒绝行为，且几何对齐与行为迁移存在部分解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wang等(2025)声称拒绝方向可跨语言近乎完美迁移且与资源水平无关，但其唯一真正低资源语言（约鲁巴语）呈强失配、迁移有限。原研究的"普适性"结论缺乏足量低资源语言证据，因此需要扩展语言样本以检验该主张是否成立。

Method: 将PolyRefuse数据集扩展至白俄罗斯语、巴什基尔语、塔吉克语三种低资源语言，在Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct上严格复现Wang等(2025)的拒绝方向提取与跨语言迁移管线，评估源语言拒绝方向对各目标语言拒绝行为的调控效果，并在控制天花板效应后比较几何对齐度与行为迁移效果之间的关系。

Result: 拒绝方向的普适性是分级的而非二元的：来自安全对齐语言和部分对齐语言的拒绝方向可广泛迁移；来自强失配语言的拒绝方向在控制天花板效应后，无法对任何被测目标语言（包括其他失配语言）可靠地调控拒绝行为。几何对齐与行为迁移之间呈现部分解耦。

Conclusion: 研究结果对"拒绝方向跨语言普适迁移"的主张构成挑战，表明迁移能力取决于源语言的对齐状态而非仅资源水平，使用资源最少、失配最严重语言的用户可能面临更弱的安全保障。

Abstract: Refusal directions have been shown to transfer cross-lingually with near-perfect effectiveness across safety-aligned languages (Wang et al., 2025), reportedly regardless of resource level. However, the only truly low-resourced language evaluated is Yoruba, which is strongly misaligned and shows limited transfer, leaving open how refusal directions behave for other low-resourced languages. We extend PolyRefuse to three additional low-resourced languages (Belarusian, Bashkir, and Tajik) and replicate the pipeline of (Wang et al., 2025) on Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct. We find that universality is graded rather than binary: directions from safety-aligned and partially-aligned languages transfer broadly, while those from strongly misaligned languages fail to reliably modulate refusal behavior on any target tested, including other misaligned languages, once ceiling effects are accounted for. Geometric alignment and behavioral transfer are further shown to be partially dissociated. These findings complicate the universality claim and suggest that users of the least-resourced, most strongly misaligned languages may face weaker safety guarantees.

</details>


### [171] [Looped Uniform Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30066)
*Saiteja Utpala,Chieh-Hsin Lai,Yuki Mitsufuji,Praneeth Vepakomma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在保持训练目标和采样器不变的前提下，将selective looping引入均匀扩散语言模型（UDLMs），显著提升训练效率与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UDLMs需在可能不可靠的上下文上做去噪训练，FLOP开销高于MDLMs但推理性能更强；已有工作表明selective looping可在iso-FLOP下提升MDLM训练效率，但looping在UDLMs中的作用尚未被研究。
