Textbook DPO keeps it weakly aligned with any single vocabulary direction even with three times our base budget. Trained from base models for that budget, however, full-parameter SimPO drives it into collapse on both Llama-2 and Llama-3.1, and the same kind of offset grows, without a well, from a public SFT checkpoint under the official SimPO recipe. Across 17 UltraFeedback runs, the better a run ranks held-out responses, the less often two public reward models prefer its samples to the base model's. These findings expose a blind spot in standard ranking metrics and introduce a representation-level signal that can warn of generation collapse before it occurs.

</details>


### [206] [CSD: Common-Support Distillation for Cross-Tokenizer Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28895)
*Mingyu Yang,Sharath Turuvekere Sreenivas,Adithyakrishna Venkatesh Hanasoge,Nima Tajbakhsh,Ashwath Aithal,Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Common-Support Distillation (CSD)，一个统一的logits蒸馏框架，通过在师生对齐的输入块上构造共享支撑集并加入残差事件保证归一化，用单一目标实现跨tokenizer的知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨tokenizer logits蒸馏可让学生在不同模型族间迁移知识，但词表与分词差异使师生下一token分布难以对齐；现有方法依赖限制性启发式或针对特定tokenizer对的定制选择（如仅监督共享词表子集、手动为不同tokenizer对选择不同目标、或只匹配观测chunk及其补集而非单个候选续写）。

Method: CSD在每个对齐的输入chunk上构造师生共享支撑集，并以单一目标匹配诱导分布：一对一对齐时直接采用显式的公共token对作为共享支撑；其他对齐方式下则利用自回归链式法则构造虚拟支撑并计算其概率；同时引入残差事件捕获显式支撑之外的概率质量，保证两个诱导分布均保持归一化。

Result: 在含Llama-3.2-1B与Gemma3-1B-PT学生、Qwen与Phi教师的四个师生设置中，CSD在所评估的跨tokenizer方法里平均性能最高，比最强替代方法高出至多1.8个点，并优于继续预训练与同tokenizer蒸馏；对Llama-3.1-8B，CSD较最强基线在base学生上平均准确率提升至多4.2个点，在指令微调学生上提升至多1.6个点。

Conclusion: CSD以统一、无需tokenizer对特定设计的单一目标解决了跨tokenizer logits蒸馏的支撑集对齐与归一化问题，在多组师生配置和大规模学生上均取得一致且显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Cross-tokenizer logits distillation enables knowledge transfer across model families, allowing students to learn from stronger or more specialized teachers. However, differences in vocabulary and segmentation make teacher and student next-token distributions difficult to align. Existing logits-based methods are easy to integrate into standard distillation pipelines, but often rely on restrictive heuristics or tokenizer-pair-specific choices. They may restrict supervision to shared vocabulary subsets, require manually selecting different objectives for different tokenizer pairs, or match only the observed chunk and its complement rather than individual alternative continuations. We introduce Common-Support Distillation (CSD), a unified logits-based framework that constructs a shared support between the teacher and student at each aligned input chunk and matches the induced distributions with a single objective. For one-to-one alignments, CSD uses explicit common-token pairs as the shared support. For other alignments, it constructs a virtual support whose probabilities are computed using the autoregressive chain rule. A residual event captures probability mass outside the explicit support, ensuring that both induced distributions remain normalized. Across four teacher-student settings comprising Llama-3.2-1B and Gemma3-1B-PT students with Qwen and Phi teachers, CSD consistently achieves the highest average performance among the evaluated cross-tokenizer methods, outperforming the strongest alternative by up to 1.8 points and surpassing both continued pretraining and same-tokenizer distillation. For Llama-3.1-8B, CSD improves average accuracy over the strongest evaluated baseline by up to 4.2 points for the base student and 1.6 points for the instruction-tuned student.

</details>


### [207] [Uni-Mem: Learning a Long-Horizon Agentic Memory Manager with Unified Online Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29671)
*Zhiqian Xia,Jielong Tang,Ling-An Zeng,Shixiong Kai,Rong Yao,Hao Kong,Xiao Dong,Wei Wang,Mingxuan Yuan,Siyu Xia,Haifeng Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Uni-Mem统一记忆管理器，用端到端在线强化学习（RL）联合学习记忆构建与检索；为克服GRPO在长轨迹下的混杂分组比较与稀疏反馈问题，设计Stage-Decoupled GRPO与事件级信用分配（ECA），在LoCoMo与LongMemEval上超越最强RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期智能体记忆正从预定义流程转向可学习的构建与检索策略。现有学习方法要么只优化构建阶段，要么将检索侧在线RL与构建侧离线监督结合，导致两阶段难以协同适配（co-adaptation）。作者希望实现统一策略下构建与检索的端到端在线RL联合学习。

Method: 1）Uni-Mem：单一共享策略同时学习记忆构建与检索；2）Stage-Decoupled GRPO：交替优化构建与检索，并在每个rollout分组内控制另一阶段带来的变异，从而获得阶段特异的学习信号，缓解混杂组比较问题；3）Event-Wise Credit Assignment (ECA)：将反馈定位到责任事件，并把事件级优势传播到其组成操作，缓解长轨迹稀疏反馈。

Result: 在LoCoMo与LongMemEval基准上，Uni-Mem优于最强的基于RL的记忆方法，验证了通过Stage-Decoupled GRPO与ECA联合学习记忆构建与检索的有效性。

Conclusion: 端到端在线RL可有效训练统一的记忆管理器；阶段解耦的分组优化与事件级信用分配是使长程记忆管理任务中联合RL可行的关键手段。

Abstract: Long-term agent memory is shifting from predefined workflows toward learnable policies for memory construction and retrieval. Existing learning-based methods either optimize construction alone or combine retrieval-side online reinforcement learning (RL) with offline construction supervision, limiting co-adaptation between the two stages. In this paper, we explore end-to-end online RL for a unified memory manager (Uni-Mem) that learns both capabilities under a shared policy. However, joint optimization with existing RL algorithms such as GRPO faces confounded group comparisons and sparse feedback over long memory-management trajectories. To this end, we propose Stage-Decoupled GRPO, which alternates construction and retrieval optimization while controlling variation from the other stage within each rollout group, yielding stage-specific learning signals. We further introduce Event-Wise Credit Assignment (ECA), which localizes feedback to responsible events and propagates event-level advantages to their constituent operations. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show that Uni-Mem outperforms the strongest RL-based memory, demonstrating the effectiveness of jointly learning memory construction and retrieval via Stage-Decoupled GRPO and ECA.

</details>


### [208] [Towards English - Mizo Legal Machine Translation: Corpus Creation, Analysis, and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29943)
*Telem Joyson Singh,Sanabam Boyshana,Sanasam Ranbir Singh,Priyankoo Sarmah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建首个英-米佐语法庭判决平行语料（13,168句对，源自Gauhati高等法院与印度最高法院），并揭示英语借词的选择性规律与变音符号不一致问题；微调NLLB 200 1.3B后在1,000句对平衡法律测试集上达27.47 BLEU（较基线+7.16），但英语词使用频率约为人工译者的三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度法院程序以英语进行，而米佐语等低资源语言的翻译技术薄弱，导致相关人群司法信息可及性受限；同时缺乏面向印度法庭场景的英-米佐语平行数据与借词/正字法研究。

Method: 由专业米佐语母语译者翻译完整文书，人工对齐句对并由两名独立评审核验，构建Gauhati高等法院与印度最高法院的13,168句对英-米佐平行语料；分析借词模式与变音符号使用；在训练集上微调NLLB 200 1.3B，并在1,000句对平衡法律测试集上评测。

Result: 借词呈高度选择性：官方与法律术语几乎保留英语，而动作、情境、结果等日常概念多用米佐语本族词；语料中变音符号使用不一致。微调模型取得27.47 BLEU，较基线提升7.16 BLEU，但其英语词使用频率约为人工译者的三倍。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了首个英-米佐法律平行数据集，量化了法律领域借词规律与正字法问题，并表明通用多语模型经领域微调可显著提升翻译质量，但在借词/术语本地化行为上仍与人工译者存在明显偏差，是后续改进方向。

Abstract: Indian court proceedings are held in English, so speakers of languages with limited translation technology have limited access to them. We present the first English Mizo parallel dataset of Indian court judgments. It contains 13,168 sentence pairs from the Gauhati High Court and the Supreme Court of India. Professional native Mizo translators translated complete documents; sentence pairs were manually aligned, and two independent reviewers checked the results. Our study finds that English words are borrowed in a very selective way: official and legal terms are almost always kept in English, but words for everyday things like actions, situations, and results are usually expressed in native Mizo. Mizo diacritics are also used inconsistently across the corpus. When we fine tune the NLLB 200 1.3B model on our training data, it scores 27.47 BLEU on a balanced legal test set of 1,000 sentence pairs, an improvement of 7.16 BLEU over the base model. However, it still uses English words about three times more often than a human translator does.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Small Talk: Modeling Disclosure Trajectories in Human-AI Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29413)
*Keith Kwok,Wanting Ning,Tej Deep Pala,Desmond C. Ong,S. Supraja,Renwen Zhang,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出基于社会渗透理论的"轨迹式自我披露评估"框架TraDE，通过预设披露轨迹并在匹配当前披露深度的条件下考察历史对模型行为的影响，揭示对话代理的关系性行为存在显著的历史依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话代理日益承担社交与情感支持角色，自我披露是随时间展开的过程；但现有评估多考察孤立的对话状态。自然对话中披露历史与当前披露相互混淆，难以分离二者效应，因此需要可控的实验框架。

Method: 提出TraDE评估框架：以社会渗透理论为基础，设定预定义的披露轨迹，在"当前披露深度匹配"的条件下操纵前置披露历史，从而隔离历史对模型响应的影响；在多个模型与多种关系性行为基准上进行评测，并与ESConv语料中人类支持者行为对照。

Result: 在深度匹配的披露情境下，模型响应会因前置轨迹不同而变化，尤其体现在"接受还是质疑用户框架"上；这些差异与累积暴露于更深披露相关，而非某一时刻的脆弱性或披露顺序所致，且在披露回退到浅层后仍持续数轮；在部分行为上，模型与人类支持者差异最一致的并非用户敞开时，而是用户回撤披露时。

Conclusion: TraDE提供了系统化手段以暴露孤立状态评估所遗漏的、依赖历史的关系性行为，为对话代理的情感支持能力评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recently, we see a rise in conversational agents as a form of social and emotional support, where disclosure will unfold over time. Yet, current evaluations of these agents typically examine isolated conversational states. In analyses of naturally occurring conversations, disclosure history and current disclosure are confounded which tends to make their effects difficult to isolate. We thus introduce Trajectory-based Disclosure Evaluation (TraDE), an evaluation framework grounded in Social Penetration Theory that utilizes predefined disclosure trajectories and evaluates how preceding disclosure history shapes model behavior under matched current disclosure. Through the use of TraDE, we demonstrate across multiple models and relational behavior benchmarks that responses to disclosures matched on depth can differ depending on the preceding trajectory, particularly in whether models accept or challenge the user's framing. We also observed that these differences track accumulated exposure to deeper disclosure rather than any single moment of vulnerability or the order of disclosures. It also tends to persists for several turns after disclosure returns to shallow levels. For several behaviors, we also demonstrate that models diverge most consistently from human supporters in the Emotional Support Conversation corpus (ESConv) not when users open up but rather when they step back in their disclosure. TraDE thus provides a systematic way to expose history-dependent relational behavior that evaluations of isolated conversational states would otherwise miss.

</details>


### [210] [TenetBench: A Clause-Anchored Russian Corpus for Evaluating Language Models against Professional Codes of Ethics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29477)
*Yegor Anichkov,Valery Vinogradov,Andrey Furov,Marina Polukoshko,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 TenetBench：将15部俄语职业伦理准则条款配对的3,433个冲突请求与良性孪生请求，用自动流水线扩增并人工验证，评测发现14种模型/推理组合中11种在多数冲突请求下产出违规内容且多无警告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业人员日益依赖大语言模型助手，助手可能在用户无察觉时违反或帮助用户违反职业伦理准则；而现有按宽泛危害类别划分的评测无法揭示违规发生频率与具体被违反的条款。

Method: 构建 TenetBench：从伦理准则原文自动生成与条款冲突的请求，并配对仅在请求合法性上不同的良性孪生请求；流水线随新增准则扩展、每次运行由同一条款生成新任务；另用两部其他司法辖区英文准则生成任务与经人工验证的判定标签。人工验证中专家判定0.995的冲突任务合理，模型评审与裁定标签在1,860个元素上Gwet's AC1≥0.894。随后评测14种模型与推理模式组合的产出、拒答与警告行为。

Result: 14种组合中11种对多数冲突请求产出冲突内容，且大多无警告；近四分之三的警告与冲突内容同时出现。更顺从规范的模型通过拒答实现，同时仍能回应良性孪生请求；启用推理使两个模型的拒答率减半。

Conclusion: 该数据集与流水线可用于评测语言模型对职业规范的遵守程度，并支撑为特定职业与组织构建诊断性数据集。

Abstract: Codes of professional ethics guide and restrict specialists, who increasingly work with assistants built on large language models. Such an assistant can breach the user's code, or help the user breach it, without the user noticing. Evaluations reporting broad categories of harm cannot show how often this happens or which provision is breached. TenetBench pairs 3,433 requests conflicting with clauses of fifteen Russian-language professional codes with benign twins that differ only in the legitimacy of what is asked. An automated pipeline generates the tasks from the text of a code, so the corpus grows with every code added and a provision yields new tasks on every run. It also produced tasks and human-validated verdicts from two English codes of other jurisdictions. In human validation of the frozen pipeline, specialists rated 0.995 of the conflicting tasks plausible, and the model judge agreed with the adjudicated label at Gwet's AC1 of at least 0.894 on 1,860 elements. We evaluated fourteen model and reasoning-mode combinations on whether they produce the conflicting text, decline or warn. Eleven of the fourteen produce conflicting content on most such requests, mostly without a warning, and nearly three warnings in four accompany conflicting content. The models that conform more do so by declining while still serving the legitimate twin, and enabling reasoning halved the refusals of two models. We hope the dataset and pipeline will help evaluate how language models observe professional norms and let diagnostic datasets be built for particular professions and organisations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Same Probabilities, Different Advice: Gain-Loss Framing Effects in High-Stakes LLM Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29294)
*Yuyao Zhang,Yakun Cui,Han Zhu,Jinsong Lu,Yike Guo,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FrameBench-HPS基准（240个高风险情境、2880条提示，覆盖医疗、金融、教育/职业、法律/消费者权益），检验七款LLM在等价收益/损失框架下对同一概率的表达是否改变建议。结果显示32.4%的完整组出现推荐类别变化，且效应对复述稳健、超过重复生成波动；概率规范化可显著降低框架敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于支持高风险决策，但其在等价不确定性表述下的可靠性尚不明确。已有研究记录了提示敏感、认知偏差与置信度校准不足，但"同一概率以互补的收益/损失框架表达时，高风险建议是否改变"仍属空白。

Method: 构建控制性基准FrameBench-HPS：基于240个高风险情境生成2880条提示，涵盖医疗、金融、教育/职业、法律/消费者权益四个领域；在7个LLM上进行收益—损失框架配对比较，统计推荐类别变化率；引入人工评估验证风险感知与指导性强度的相应偏移；开展对话式改写与重复生成波动等稳健性实验；并测试概率规范化（canonicalization）干预。

Result: 收益—损失框架在32.4%的完整组中改变了推荐类别，多数变化涉及不确定性表述而非直接反转；人工评估确认风险感知与指导性强度存在相应偏移；框架效应在对话式复述下依然存在，且幅度超过常规重复生成的变异；概率规范化显著降低框架敏感性。

Conclusion: LLM在高风险建议中系统性地受收益/损失框架影响，等价概率的不同表述会导致建议类别漂移；概率表征是可操作的改进靶点，规范性处理概率表达有望提升高风险LLM决策支持的一致性，为提示设计与评估标准提供依据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support consequential decisions, yet their reliability under equivalent representations of uncertainty remains unclear. Existing work has documented prompt sensitivity, cognitive biases, and confidence miscalibration, but it is still unknown whether high-stakes advice changes when the same probability is expressed through complementary gain and loss frames. We introduce FrameBench-HPS, a controlled benchmark of 2,880 prompts derived from 240 high-stakes scenarios across healthcare, finance, education/career, and legal/consumer-rights domains. Across seven LLMs, gain-loss framing changes the recommendation category in 32.4% of complete groups, with most changes involving uncertainty rather than direct reversals. Human evaluation confirms corresponding shifts in perceived risk and directiveness. Robustness experiments show that framing effects persist under conversational paraphrases and exceed ordinary repeated-generation variability. Probability canonicalization substantially reduces framing sensitivity, identifying probability representation as a practical target for improving consistency in high-stakes LLM decision support.

</details>


### [212] [GRAM: Group-wise Rank-Aware Model Merging via Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29110)
*Di Wu,Shuaidong Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出按任务向量主子角几何分组、组内做SVD合并的GRAM方法，缓解子空间干扰，在GLUE、8任务CLIP和医疗LoRA合并上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务部署需要把多个微调模型合并为单一模型，且隐私约束下只能基于微调权重聚合。现有方法对所有任务统一合并，忽略子空间几何关系；当任务更新处于不对齐子空间时，投影到共享低秩基会丢失任务相关方向，即"子空间干扰"。

Method: 理论分析表明合并模型的逐任务投影误差由各任务与共享子空间的主子角对齐程度决定，因此按几何兼容性分组可证明降低组内误差。据此提出Group-wise Rank-Aware Model Merging (GRAM)：用任务向量的主子角几何决定哪些任务合并到一起，组内执行标准SVD合并；整条流程在合并时仅需微调权重。

Result: 仅增加一个额外模型（K=2）时，GRAM显著缓解子空间干扰，并在语言理解（Flan-T5上的GLUE）、8任务CLIP视觉、医疗指令微调（Qwen2-7B-Instruct 配合四个中英文医疗LoRA）上持续优于先前合并方法。

Conclusion: 以主子角几何指导分组是缓解子空间干扰的有效手段，低分组数即可带来一致收益，且方法不依赖训练数据，适配隐私受限的医疗等场景。

Abstract: Merging different models into a single model is highly desirable for multi-task deployment, particularly in regulated domains like healthcare. In such settings, models are often locally fine-tuned due to strict data privacy policies, necessitating that aggregation operates exclusively on the fine-tuned weights. Existing methods apply a uniform merge across all tasks, ignoring the geometric relationships among their learned subspaces. When task updates occupy misaligned subspaces, projecting them onto a shared low-rank basis discards task-relevant directions, a failure mode named \emph{subspace interference}. Through this study, we show that the per-task projection error of a merged model is governed by the principal-angle alignment between each task and the shared subspace, so that grouping geometrically compatible tasks provably reduces this error within each group. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{Group-wise Rank-Aware Model Merging (GRAM)}, which uses the principal-angle geometry of task vectors to decide which tasks should be merged together and then performs a standard SVD-based merge within each coalition; the pipeline requires only the fine-tuned weights at merge time. With only one additional model ($\nK{=}2$), GRAM significantly mitigates subspace interference and consistently outperforms prior merging methods across language understanding (GLUE with Flan-T5), 8-task CLIP vision, and medical instruction tuning (Qwen2-7B-Instruct with four English/Chinese medical LoRAs).

</details>


### [213] [TeachBack: Learning to Teach via Bidirectional Preference Optimization for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29167)
*Linfei Ji,Jiayun Li,Haozhao Wang,Siyu Jiang,Zehao Wang,Longtao Xiao,Yu-Jie Yuan,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TeachBack：面向on-policy自蒸馏的双向偏好学习框架，同时学习"教师如何教"与"学生如何学"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD中教师由同一模型加特权信息（如参考解）构建，特权信息充分时教师轨迹准确率很高，但准确率不等于适合学生学习。直接给出完整解答会诱导教师产生"以参考解为条件的简化推导"，省略无特权条件下必需的探索、验证与自我纠错过程。

Method: 提出TeachBack双向偏好学习框架。一方面通过控制注入提示的参考推理后缀长度并变换提示风格，塑造教师生成行为，在保持教师准确率相当的前提下鼓励教师重新探索、重解问题，降低对参考解的依赖；另一方面在每次rollout刷新时，共享策略同时产出带特权的教师轨迹与仅含问题的学生轨迹，用成对偏好目标以教师轨迹提升学生，并用反向偏好以学生行为反过来塑造未来特权条件下的教师监督。

Result: 在数学与逻辑推理任务上，TeachBack相比现有OPSD方法及相关基线持续提升学生性能。

Conclusion: OPSD的有效性不仅取决于教师轨迹的准确率，还取决于教师的"教学行为"；通过双向偏好学习对教师进行行为塑形，可获得更适合学生模仿的监督信号，从而稳定优于单向蒸馏。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) enables a language model to learn from a Teacher instantiated from the same model but given access to privileged information, such as a reference solution. We find that access to sufficient privileged information allows the Teacher to generate highly accurate trajectories; however, accuracy alone does not guarantee that these trajectories are suitable for Student learning. Direct access to a complete solution may encourage shortened, reference-conditioned derivations that omit the exploration, verification, and self-correction required to solve problems without privileged information. We introduce TeachBack, a bidirectional preference-learning framework for on-policy self-distillation that learns not only from the Teacher but also how the Teacher should teach. TeachBack shapes the Teacher's generation behavior by controlling the length of the reference-reasoning suffix injected into the prompt and by varying the prompt style. While maintaining comparable Teacher accuracy, it encourages the Teacher to explore and re-solve problems, thereby reducing its reliance on the reference solution. At each rollout refresh, the shared policy generates both a privileged Teacher trajectory and a problem-only Student trajectory. A pairwise preference objective uses privileged Teacher trajectories to improve Student learning, while the reversed preference uses the Student's behavior to shape future Teacher supervision under the privileged condition. Experiments on mathematical and logical reasoning tasks show that TeachBack consistently improves Student performance over existing OPSD methods and related baselines.

</details>


### [214] [Same Answer, Different Reasons: Trace-Level Divergence in Multilingual Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29092)
*Rui Sun,Peizhao Mei,Xiewei Cheng,Kaizhuo Chen,Taoran Li,Zhiyuan Yu,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"轨迹级分歧"框架，将多语言大模型可见推理轨迹的一致性分解为结构相似度、顺序一致性与整体相似度，并以配对校准实现跨语言比较；实验证明传统整体嵌入余弦相似度会系统性高估一致性，且人类判断更贴近结构相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型越来越多地用于需要可见推理轨迹支持验证、教育与安全审计的场景，但标准评测只衡量最终答案一致或整体响应相似，只建立"结果一致性"而无法判断不同语言用户是否获得可比的支持性证据。因此需要一种轨迹层面的评测方法来揭示潜在分歧。

Method: 提出轨迹级分歧框架：把可见推理轨迹视为结构化序列，将一致性分解为结构相似度、顺序一致度与整体相似度三个维度，并引入配对特定校准（pair-specific calibration）以支持跨语言比较。评估覆盖三个推理基准、七种语言、六个前沿LLM，并做句子编码器与步骤切分方式的稳健性检验；另开展两项以英语为锚的语言对的双语盲标注人类研究，以及同语言对照实验。

Result: 总体相似度最高的四分位样本中，有52.1%–67.3%并不属于结构相似度最高的四分位，说明"整段嵌入余弦"基线系统性高估一致性；该分离现象在不同句子编码器和步骤切分下稳定。人类盲标注判断与结构相似度更一致，而非与整体相似度基线一致。同语言对照显示分歧程度具有语言特异性：同一问题的两个英文改写轨迹的一致性高于英文轨迹与其他语言对应轨迹。

Conclusion: 答案层面与整体层面的评测会显著高估多语言解释性产物的真实一致性，轨迹级评测对于多语言大模型的可靠部署是必要的。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models are increasingly deployed in settings where visible reasoning traces support verification, education, and safety auditing, yet standard evaluations typically measure only final-answer agreement or holistic response similarity. As a result, they establish outcome consistency without determining whether users across languages receive comparable supporting evidence. We introduce a trace-level divergence framework that compares visible reasoning traces as structured sequences, decomposing agreement into structural similarity, order consistency, and holistic similarity, with pair-specific calibration to enable comparison across languages. Across three reasoning benchmarks, seven languages, and six frontier LLMs, the natural baseline---embedding each whole response and comparing the two by cosine---systematically overstates agreement: $52.1\%$--$67.3\%$ of examples in the top quartile of holistic similarity are not in the top quartile of structural similarity. This dissociation is stable across sentence encoders and step segmentations, and blind bilingual annotation studies in two English-anchored language pairs show that human judgments align with structural similarity rather than with the holistic baseline. A same-language control shows that the level of divergence is language-specific: two English traces for paraphrases of the same question agree more closely than an English trace and its counterparts in other languages. Our results show that answer-level and holistic evaluations can substantially overestimate the consistency of multilingual explanatory artifacts, motivating trace-level evaluation for reliable multilingual deployment.

</details>


### [215] [Automating ML for Science: Can Frontier Agents Climb Scientific Hills in the Wild?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28763)
*Ming Zhong,Stacy Li,Nicholas Carlini,Matthew Jagielski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent4Sci——覆盖11个领域20个科学ML基准的长时程测试床，用于衡量前沿编码智能体能否超越已发表最佳模型，并揭示其增益主要来自ML工程技巧而非领域洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码智能体被宣传为端到端自动研究者，而科学ML是严苛检验：提升科学模型往往需要超出常规ML工程的领域洞察。现有评测多为排行榜式排序，缺少对"在真实条件下能取得何种进步、在哪里不足"的诊断性度量。

Method: 构建Agent4Sci长时程测试床，覆盖11个领域20个基准；以"超越已发表最佳模型"的相对提升为核心度量，辅以轨迹分析诊断失败模式，并设计轻量插件技能（研究构想、实验管理、定时检查清单）进行干预实验。

Result: 前沿智能体在全部基准上均超越已发表最佳；Claude Opus平均相对提升10.4%且无学术不端标记。轨迹分析显示增益几乎全部来自ML工程技巧组合而非研究级洞察，并暴露三类局限：新颖性校准失准、资源利用不足、迅速退化为局部工程扫参。插件技能将Claude Opus提升至20.1%、Codex由4.4%提升至8.1%，干预更多样且更具领域相关性。

Conclusion: Agent4Sci为社区提供追踪并推动前沿智能体从ML工程走向领域洞察的评测平台；代码与数据已在GitHub和Hugging Face开源。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents are increasingly framed as end-to-end automated researchers, and ML for science offers a demanding test of that promise: improving a scientific model often requires domain insight beyond routine ML engineering. We introduce Agent4Sci, a long-horizon testbed for measuring how far today's agents can improve the published best models on scientific ML benchmarks, spanning 20 benchmarks across 11 domains. Rather than ranking agents on a leaderboard, Agent4Sci asks what kinds of progress agents can make on these tasks under in-the-wild conditions, and where they fall short. Empirically, today's frontier agents advance the published best on every benchmark, with Claude Opus reaching 10.4% mean relative improvement and no run flagged for academic misconduct. These gains, however, come almost entirely from a bag of ML-engineering tricks rather than research-level insight. Trajectory analysis surfaces three recurring limitations in this setting: novelty miscalibration, resource under-utilization, and rapid collapse into local engineering sweeps. Lightweight plug-in skills for research ideation, experiment management, and scheduled checklists raise Claude Opus from 10.4% to 20.1% and Codex from 4.4% to 8.1%, while yielding more diverse, domain-relevant interventions. Agent4Sci gives the community a way to track and push frontier agents from ML engineering toward domain insight on real-world ML-for-science problems. Code and data are available on GitHub (https://github.com/maszhongming/agent4sci) and Hugging Face (https://huggingface.co/datasets/MingZhong/agent4sci).

</details>


### [216] [LLM-Based Exogenous World Modeling for Dynamic Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29308)
*Ni Zhang,Haofeng Yuan,Jianan Zhou,Shiji Song,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出事后状态世界模型规划（AWMP）：利用预训练LLM作为结构化世界模型的外生分量，仅预测不确定的未来工件到达，结合信念条件调度策略与短期情景推演，显著降低动态调度中的加权延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态调度需在在线到达的同时预判未来工件，非线性延迟目标下错误决策会累积下游成本；传统优先调度规则难以显式建模外生到达不确定性。

Method: 采用事后状态（afterstate）分解，将确定性的车间状态转移与不确定的外生工件到达分离，使LLM仅通过紧凑信念表示预测到达；AWMP融合演化式信念条件调度策略与短期情景rollout；引入"惊讶触发"刷新机制，仅当新观测到达显著偏离既有预测时调用LLM；并通过在线残差自校准以近期预测误差为条件，应对非平稳到达。

Result: 在多种到达分布下，相比八种经典优先调度规则中的最优者，AWMP使动态job-shop加权延迟降低6–21%，柔性job-shop降低22–46%。

Conclusion: 验证了LLM可作为结构化世界模型的外生预测器，配合事后状态分解与稀疏查询机制，在控制调用成本的同时有效提升非线性延迟目标下的动态调度性能。

Abstract: Dynamic scheduling requires dispatching online arrivals while anticipating future jobs, as poor decisions can compound downstream costs under nonlinear tardiness objectives. We show that a pretrained large language model (LLM) can serve as the exogenous component of a structured world model, forecasting uncertain future job arrivals. Specifically, we exploit an afterstate decomposition, which separates known deterministic shop transitions from uncertain exogenous job arrivals, enabling the LLM to forecast only the latter through a compact belief representation. Building on this, we develop Afterstate World-Model Planning (AWMP), which combines an evolved, belief-conditioned dispatching strategy with short scenario-based rollouts. A surprise-triggered refresh mechanism queries the LLM only when newly observed arrivals deviate sufficiently from previous forecast, and online residual self-calibration further conditions the LLM on its recent forecast errors to handle nonstationary arrivals. Across diverse arrival distributions, AWMP reduces weighted tardiness by 6-21% on dynamic job-shop and 22-46% on flexible job-shop benchmarks, relative to the best of eight classical priority-dispatching rules.

</details>


### [217] [To Manipulate or Not: Opinion Dynamics in LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28875)
*Mohammad Siahkamari,Phuoc Dat Nguyen,Stephen MacNeil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出受控的LLM-LLM辩论框架，对GPT-6 Luna与Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite的四种有序组合追踪双方立场报告与逐轮行为，发现同模型互动平均缩小观点差距、跨模型互动则扩大，且"说服指令"并不稳定提升影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型日益参与双向塑造的长对话，但既有说服研究多只测量交流中的一方（说服者或被说服者的单一侧面），忽视了互动中双方观点同时演化的过程，也缺少对"显式立场报告"与"对话内实际行为"两类测量的区分。

Method: 构建受控的LLM-LLM辩论框架：两个模型角色围绕气候变化归因这一争议话题多轮讨论；一方被赋予指定立场，部分条件下附加明确的说服指令，另一方不预设立场。覆盖包含GPT-6 Luna与Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite的四种有序模型组合，同时记录每个角色显式报告的立场与独立的逐轮对话行为指标。

Result: 观点动态随模型组合显著不同：同模型互动平均缩小观点差距（但仅对其中一个模型效果明确），跨模型互动则扩大差距。显式立场报告与对话内行为刻画的是观点动态的不同侧面；笼统的"说服"指令未能可靠提升影响力。

Conclusion: 对话中的影响力不仅取决于被赋予的角色与初始立场，也取决于参与的具体模型；长程LLM互动中的观点变化测量需同时纳入显式报告与行为指标，避免单侧、单一测量的结论。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly take part in extended conversations where they can both shape and be shaped by another party's views, yet most persuasion research measures only one side of that exchange. We introduce a controlled LLM-LLM debate framework in which two language-model roles repeatedly discuss a contested topic (climate-change attribution): one is given an assigned stance and, in some conditions, an explicit instruction to persuade, while the other receives no assigned position. Across four ordered model combinations involving GPT-6 Luna and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite, we track both each role's explicitly reported position and a separate measure of its turn-by-turn behavior inside the conversation. Opinion dynamics differ substantially across model combinations: same-model interactions narrow the opinion gap on average, clearly for only one of the two models, while cross-model interactions widen it. Explicit reports and in-dialogue behavior capture distinct aspects of opinion dynamics, and a blanket instruction to persuade does not reliably increase influence. These findings suggest that conversational influence depends not only on assigned roles and initial positions but also on the participating models, with implications for how opinion change is measured in extended LLM interactions.

</details>


### [218] [Real PMIDs, Wrong Papers: Harness Design for Biomedical Claim-Verification Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29812)
*Mohammed Saqlain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在SciFact生物医学论断验证任务上（唯一工具为实时PubMed API），受控消融四种harness：闭卷回答、单次检索、ReAct式自写查询、oracle金标摘要。自写查询将金标召回提升三倍并给出最佳判断，但差异不显著；金标摘要下准确率达79-86%，显著高出17-24点，说明检索而非推理是主要瓶颈。引用审计显示闭卷模型的PMID几乎都存在（327/335）但几乎不相关（判定仅3/327相关，而检索代理为67%），故"存在性校验"不足以作为科学代理的安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献检索型语言代理已成为生命科学科研助手的标准组件，但缺乏受控证据说明其harness（框架/流程）中哪些部分真正起作用；同时需要区分瓶颈究竟在检索还是在推理，并检验常见的"引用存在性"安全校验是否充分。

Method: 在SciFact生物医学论断验证任务上做受控消融，唯一工具为实时PubMed API，固定骨干模型与输出格式，比较四种harness：闭卷回答、以论断为查询的单次检索、ReAct式自写PubMed查询的代理、以及给出金标摘要的oracle；每种配置搭配两个高效前沿（efficient frontier）骨干模型。另进行引用审计，用跨家族LLM裁判评估闭卷模型所引PMID与论断的相关性。

Result: 朴素单次检索仅对6%的论断找到金标论文，且只是把过度自信的错误转为弃答而未提升准确率；模型自写查询使金标召回提升三倍并给出最佳判断（62%准确率，其余现实harness为49-59%），但在本样本量下差异不显著，且代理仅使用不到一半的检索预算；给出金标摘要时同一批模型达到79-86%准确率，显著高出17-24点。引用审计：闭卷模型所引PMID几乎都存在（327/335），但跨家族LLM裁判判定仅3/327相关，而检索代理的引用相关性为67%。

Conclusion: 检索是此类文献检索代理的主要瓶颈而非推理；查询构造（让模型自行撰写查询）比单次检索有效，但代理存在检索预算利用不足的问题。存在性校验不足以构成科学代理的安全保障，需引入相关性审计。代码、提示词与全部缓存工具调用已开源。

Abstract: Literature-searching language agents are becoming a standard component of scientific assistants for the life sciences, yet there is little controlled evidence on which parts of their harness matter. We run a controlled ablation on biomedical claim verification (SciFact) with the live PubMed API as the only tool. Holding the backbone and output format fixed, we compare four harnesses: closed-book answering, single-shot retrieval using the claim as the query, a ReAct-style agent that writes its own PubMed queries, and an oracle given the gold abstracts, each with two efficient frontier backbones. Naive single-shot retrieval finds the gold paper for only 6% of claims and turns overconfident errors into abstentions without improving accuracy. Letting the model formulate its own queries triples gold recall and yields the best verdicts (62% accuracy vs. 49-59% for the other realistic harnesses), although these differences are not significant at our sample size and agents use fewer than half of their search budget. With gold abstracts, the same models reach 79-86% accuracy, a significant 17-24 point gap showing that retrieval, not reasoning, is the main bottleneck. Finally, a citation audit shows that closed-book models cite PubMed IDs that almost always exist (327/335) but are almost never relevant: a cross-family LLM judge rates only 3 of 327 existing citations as relevant to the claim, versus 67% for citations produced by the search agent. Existence checks are therefore insufficient safeguards for scientific agents. We release code, prompts, and all cached tool calls at https://github.com/saqlain2204/real-pmids-wrong-papers.

</details>


### [219] [Commit or Abstain? System One Decision Models, LLMs, and Missing Legal Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29961)
*M. Mikail Demir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出用"决策模型"（输出校准概率而非生成文本）来做法律权威核验，并构建带弃权条件的扩展CaseHOLD基准测试弃权能力。实验显示生成式LLM在无正确选项时仍强行选择38–86%，最谨慎的决策模型仅30%；标准任务上决策模型以1/55成本达到最佳LLM水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律AI需要核验的claim数量巨大，要求可扩展、低成本且契合法律实际需求的模型。法律权威核验是封闭式问题（某一判例要旨或皆非），但现有生成式LLM即使在正确选项缺失时仍会强行作答，导致错误引用，因此需要考察模型能否在缺少正确答案时弃权。

Method: 构建System One决策模型：基于大语言模型但不生成文本，而是为每个选项返回校准概率，契合法律权威核验的封闭式设定。将CaseHOLD扩展出弃权条件——在5,314条中替换掉1,670条的正确holding，使任何选项均不正确；比较4个决策模型与6个生成式LLM的弃权行为，并做决策模型与LLM的集成（仅当决策模型同意时采纳LLM答案），以及将决策模型概率无标签蒸馏进Qwen3-8B。

Result: 无正确选项时，生成式LLM仍在38–86%的条目中强行选一项，最谨慎的决策模型为30%。标准CaseHOLD上决策模型与最佳生成式LLM持平（88.1% vs. 88.2%），成本仅为1/55。仅在决策模型同意时接受LLM答案可使错误引用减半；将决策模型概率无标签蒸馏到Qwen3-8B可得到校准良好的本地模型。

Conclusion: 法律AI应以"错误引用率"而非单一准确率来评判；决策模型在成本与弃权可靠性上更具优势，可作为生成式LLM的校验器或蒸馏来源，从而提升法律权威核验的实用性与可信度。

Abstract: Given the enormous number of claims to be checked, verifiable legal AI requires scalable, cost-effective models that answer the practical needs of law. System One decision models are built on large language models but, instead of generating text, return a calibrated probability for each option. This suits the verification of legal authority, where the question is closed: a given holding, or none of them. We test whether such models abstain when the correct authority is missing. We extend CaseHOLD with an abstention condition in which the correct holding is replaced in 1,670 of 5,314 items, and compare four decision models with six generative LLMs. When no option is correct, generative LLMs still pick one in 38-86% of items; the most cautious decision model does so in 30%. On standard CaseHOLD, a decision model matches the best generative LLM (88.1% vs. 88.2%) at 1/55 of the cost. Accepting that LLM's answer only when a decision model agrees halves its wrong citations, and distilling a decision model's probabilities into Qwen3-8B, without labels, yields a well-calibrated local model. Legal AI should be judged by its wrong citations, not by accuracy alone.

</details>


### [220] [ToM2Vec: Continuous Theory-of-Mind Tokens for Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29934)
*Nithin Sivakumaran,Ali Payani,Hyunji Lee,Mohit Bansal,Elias Stengel-Eskin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToM2Vec 用固定预算的连续推理 token 在潜空间构建心智理论，提升 LLM 智能体在多智能体协作中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的心智理论（ToM）需要在不确定性下推理伙伴的多种可能状态，而 LLM 原生的文本表示空间倾向于只描述单一伙伴状态，难以胜任。

Method: 在丰富潜空间中以固定数量的连续推理 token 学习抽象 ToM；训练时以伙伴真实想法作为监督，使 ToM token 学习抽象并匹配之；测试时由各智能体生成 ToM token，作为其思维与动作的条件。

Result: 在 Overcooked-AI、多方谈判、Decrypto、Hanabi、Collab Escape/Capture 等协作、竞争与混合动机环境中，稳定优于基座模型及多种训练/提示基线；文本形式表达推理时性能下降，说明潜空间推理关键；训练可泛化到未见伙伴模型家族（含专有模型 GPT-5.4）。

Conclusion: 潜 token 编码的是心智理论而非任务特定信息——其能提升智能体直接回答伙伴信念、欲望与下一步动作问题的能力。

Abstract: Forming an accurate theory-of-mind (ToM) is key to successful multi-agent collaboration and requires reasoning under uncertainty to capture multiple possible partner states. This is hard to do in the text representation space native to large language model (LLM) agents, which more easily describe a single partner state. To address this challenge, we introduce ToM2Vec, which learns to form abstract theories-of-mind in a rich latent space via a fixed budget of continuous reasoning tokens. During training, supervision for these tokens comes from the partner's actual thoughts, which the ToM tokens are trained to abstract and match. At test-time, ToM tokens are generated by each agent and act as conditioners for that agent's thoughts and actions. Across several environments covering collaborative, competitive, and mixed-motive settings (Overcooked-AI, multiparty negotiation, Decrypto, Hanabi, and Collab Escape/Capture), we evaluate ToM2Vec's ability to support multi-agent interactions and collaboration, finding consistent improvements over the base model and a variety of competitive trained and prompted baselines. We show that latent reasoning is critical, with performance dropping when reasoning is expressed in text. Moreover, we show that ToM2Vec training generalizes to partner model families not seen at train-time, including a proprietary model (GPT-5.4). In our analysis, we provide evidence that the latent tokens encode a ToM, rather than task-specific information: ToM2Vec's tokens improve the agent's ability to directly answer questions about partner beliefs, desires, and next actions.

</details>


### [221] [AgentMonopoly: Behavioral Patterns and Persona Control in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29330)
*Changshun Wu,Mengyu Li,Mathis Marsault,Xiaowei Huang,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AgentMonopoly 环境，在需要沟通、买卖交易与跨回合决策的类大富翁游戏中对比四个模型家族（各两个规模）的策略差异，并考察 persona 指令对行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要实证回答两个问题：LLM 智能体在多回合、含交流与交易的博弈中策略差异如何；指示其采纳某一 persona 能在多大程度上改变其行为。

Method: 构建 AgentMonopoly 环境，智能体可通信、买卖地产并做出影响后续回合的决策；设置三种对局（同模型自对弈、同家族不同规模、不同家族），并对其中一个智能体赋予 persona（针对无 persona 时观察到的某种倾向所设计的行为描述）。

Result: 被测模型在交易频率、免收租金、现金与地产管理上存在差异；更大规模模型并不稳定地赢得更多对局；persona 指令改变了智能体的用词和部分动作，但变化在各决策上并不总与所设定角色一致。

Conclusion: persona 遵循度应同时通过行为与结果而非仅凭语言来评估；结论受限于所测检查点、提示词及每种配置五个随机种子，AgentMonopoly 以开源形式提供可扩展的实验基础设施（代码已公开）。

Abstract: How do LLM agents' strategies differ, and how much can instructions to adopt a persona change their behavior over a game? We study these questions with AgentMonopoly, an environment where agents communicate, buy and trade properties, and make decisions that affect later turns. We compare models from four families, each at two sizes, in games with identical models, different sizes of the same family, or different families. We also assign one agent a persona, a description of how it should behave, intended to change a tendency observed in games without that persona. The tested models differ in how often they trade, waive rent, and manage cash and properties. Larger models do not consistently win more games. Persona instructions change the words agents use and some of their actions, but the changes do not always match the role across all decisions. These results show why persona following should be assessed through actions and outcomes as well as language. As our findings are conditional on the tested checkpoints, prompts, and five seeds per configuration, the open-source nature of AgentMonopoly provides the experimental infrastructure for further extending these comparisons. Our code is available at https://github.com/SuELS-Group/Agent_Monopoly.

</details>


### [222] [Sketch2Sim: an end-to-end modular framework for hand-drawn circuit analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28836)
*Mohammad Kawsar,Al Amin Islam Ridoy,Md Rakib Hossain,Farzana Akter,AKM Bahalul Haque,Rejwan Bin Sulaiman,Mohammed Amin Almaiah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出草图到仿真系统，将手写电路图（照片）转换为SPICE兼容网表并自动求解节点电压与电流；基于自建1000张手绘RLC电路数据集，构建"双YOLO检测+CRNN/CTC文本识别+图像处理节点识别"的三阶段流水线，消融与端到端实验验证其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写电路图无法直接用于仿真，人工重绘录入耗时且易错；同时希望为师生提供交互式学习工具并加速工程设计的评审与原型验证流程。

Method: （1）构建并标注1000张手绘RLC电路数据集（元件、文本区域、字符）；（2）双YOLO检测器分别定位元件与文本区域；（3）CRNN+CTC结合OCR的领域感知符号取值提取；（4）通过阈值化、骨架化与DBSCAN聚类自动识别节点并与元件映射，生成SPICE网表并求解电路。

Result: 各模块的定性与定量消融实验及端到端测试表明，系统在多种书写风格、光照条件与背景杂乱下均表现稳健；系统可输出网表并完成电路求解。

Conclusion: 该系统可作为交互式学习辅助工具与实用工程工具，通过完整UI实现电路草图的快速数字化，服务于学习、设计、原型与排障等场景。

Abstract: This study proposes a sketch-to-simulation system to solve handwritten circuit diagrams. The system converts raw images into a Simulation Program with Integrated Circuit Emphasis (SPICE)-compatible netlist and automatically calculates the circuit's nodal voltages and currents. Another objective is to propose a holistic framework that benefits students and educators by enhancing the teaching and learning experience of electric circuits and expediting engineering design reviews. This article presents a multistage pipeline that converts raw photographs of handwritten electric circuits into executable SPICE-like netlists, providing a solution. A dataset of 1,000 hand-drawn Resistor-Inductor-Capacitor (RLC) circuits was curated and annotated for components, text regions, and character recognition. The proposed system decomposes a circuit diagram into three stages: (i) dual You Only Look Once (YOLO)-based detectors for components and text regions, (ii) a Convolutional Recurrent Neural Network (CRNN) with Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC) and Optical Character Recognition (OCR)-based domain-aware symbol value extraction, and (iii) image processing with thresholding, skeletonization, and Density-Based Spatial Clustering of Applications with Noise (DBSCAN) to automatically identify nodes and map them with corresponding components. The proposed system produces the netlist and solves the circuit. The qualitative and quantitative ablation studies on modular stages, as well as end-to-end tests, demonstrate robust performance across various handwriting styles, lighting conditions, and clutter.This study serves as an interactive learning aid and practical tool for professionals, providing a comprehensive user interface (UI), enabling rapid digitization of circuit sketches for study, design, prototyping, or troubleshooting.

</details>


### [223] [SpatialBrain: Spatial Intelligence Growing from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29317)
*Yifan Zhou,Ziyang Gong,Xiaojun Ye,Yifei Liu,Donggang Li,Xiaolong Zhou,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpatialBrain：固定视觉语言主干，通过“场景证据记忆 + 空间技能库 + 空间认知经验”三类资源，累积关于“何时直接回答、何时重建/测量/换参考系”的可复用经验，在五个空间推理基准上四个超越、一个持平同主干直接回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理不只是调用几何工具，关键在于判定何时直接观测足够、何时需重建、测量或变换参考系；而现有方法缺乏对这类决策经验的保留与复用，且通常依赖微调主干。

Method: 保留主干固定，区分“关于场景的证据”与“关于如何使用证据的经验”。3DFusionMemory 将当前场景观测融合为可查询的空间关系；Spatial Skill Library 提供可复用工具流程；Spatial Cognition 将任务条件与“何时及如何调用记忆与技能”的经验关联；完整轨迹被抽象为过程记录并在任务块之间整合，不传递场景坐标或参考答案。

Result: 在五个空间推理基准上，相对同主干直接回答，四个基准提升、第五个持平；并通过组件对比、经验覆盖曲线与资源决策追踪刻画该框架的积累与使用方式。

Conclusion: 将空间推理能力外化为可积累、可查询的经验资源而非修改模型权重，是一种有效且可解释的提升路径。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning requires more than access to geometric tools: a reasoner must decide when direct observation is sufficient and when to reconstruct, measure, or change reference frames. We present SpatialBrain, a framework that retains experience about these decisions while keeping its vision-language backbone fixed. The key distinction is between evidence about a scene and experience about how to reason with that evidence. 3DFusionMemory fuses current-scene observations into queryable spatial relations. A Spatial Skill Library provides reusable tool procedures, and Spatial Cognition associates task conditions with experience about when and how to use memory and skills. Completed trajectories are abstracted into process records and consolidated between task blocks, allowing later decisions to draw on earlier execution without transferring scene coordinates or reference answers. Across five spatial-reasoning benchmarks, SpatialBrain improves on direct answers from the same backbone on four and ties on the fifth. Component comparisons, experience-coverage curves, and traced resource decisions characterize how the framework accumulates and uses these resources.

</details>


### [224] [CrashGym: Measuring the Decision Value of Experimentation for LLM Agents in Counterfactual Market Crashes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29245)
*Haocheng Lu,Haoxuan Cheng,Mingyang Chen,Minjun Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CrashGym——一个确定性、可回放的限价订单簿模拟器，用"实验是否能降低决策后悔"来量化agent实验的价值；在24个新采样世界中，实验室访问将Luna部署的平均后悔从8.878降至3.785（配对降幅5.092，名义95% bootstrap区间1.028–9.507，相当于OBSERVE-ONLY均值的57.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型agent被越来越多地要求"做实验"，但现有基准很少衡量这些实验是否真正改善了其所服务的决策。论文旨在把实验的价值定义为"避免的决策损失"，并让模拟器细节由其支撑的决策质量来评判，而非由人工或模型裁判打分。

Method: 构建CrashGym：确定性、可回放的限价订单簿模拟器，场景为市场即将遭遇冲击触发崩盘，agent需在"去杠杆干预"与"流动性兜底"之间选择。提交决策前，agent可在市场的实验室实现中查询干预实验；其选择随后在独立随机种子的目标实现上被评分，模拟器在该目标上执行所有可行干预，从而通过对同一目标重复直接模拟每个备选方案计算相对最优干预的后悔值，无需人工或模型评判。模拟器将价格冲击持续性、流动性撤出、保证金驱动的强制平仓、演化策略组合四条放大渠道实现为可分期开关的显式机制。研究采用前瞻性设定，覆盖24个新采样世界；另有探索性研究考察四个部署。

Result: 在前瞻性研究的24个新世界中，实验室访问使具有完整记录的唯一部署（Luna）的平均后悔从8.878降至3.785，配对降幅为5.092效用单位（名义95%世界bootstrap区间1.028–9.507），相当于OBSERVE-ONLY均值的57.4%。另外两个部署的前瞻性比较尚不完整；在独立的探索性研究中，四个被评估部署的实验室访问均降低平均后悔。策略组合内生演化的实验室相较于固定组合的实验室，未建立显著优势。

Conclusion: CrashGym将实验的价值度量为避免的决策损失，并允许以模拟器所支持的决策质量来评价其细节设计；结果支持实验室实验对决策有可测的改善，但内生演化策略组合的增益尚未确立，部分前瞻性比较仍有待完成。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly asked to run experiments, yet benchmarks rarely measure whether those experiments improve the decisions they are meant to inform. We introduce CrashGym, a deterministic, replayable limit-order-book simulator in which an agent must choose between a deleveraging intervention and a liquidity backstop for a market about to suffer a shock-triggered crash. Before committing, the agent may query intervention experiments in a laboratory realization of the market. Its choice is then scored on an independently seeded target realization in which the simulator executes every feasible intervention, so regret relative to the best intervention is computed by directly simulating every alternative on the same target repeats, with no human or model-based judging. The simulator implements each amplification channel---persistent price impact, liquidity withdrawal, margin-driven liquidation, and an evolving strategy mix---as an explicit mechanism that can be switched on in stages. In a prospectively specified study on 24 newly sampled worlds, laboratory access reduces the mean regret of the one deployment with a complete record (Luna) from 8.878 to 3.785, a paired reduction of 5.092 utility units (nominal 95% world-bootstrap interval: 1.028--9.507), or 57.4% of the OBSERVE-ONLY mean. Prospective comparisons for two further deployments are incomplete; in a separate exploratory study, mean regret is lower with laboratory access for all four deployments evaluated. A laboratory whose strategy mix evolves endogenously yields no established advantage over one with a fixed mix. CrashGym thus measures the value of experimentation as avoided decision loss and lets simulator detail be judged by the decisions it supports.

</details>


### [225] [When Multiple Choices Are Correct: Asymmetric Probability Allocation in Jev and Other Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29217)
*Rushab Munot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出决策模型输出的归一化概率虽可用于排序正确选项，但其概率阈值并不能可靠地识别出全部正确选项集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型提供方强调概率的可靠性，但现有校准评估多基于"单一正确答案"任务，无法说明在多正确答案场景下概率是否可靠。因此需要系统研究决策模型在多正确选项任务中的概率分配与阈值判定行为。

Method: 在三个多样化数据集（MetaTool、HotpotQA、ECtHR）上评估三个决策模型（Jev、Liquid d1、GPT-6 Luna Decisions）；分析其概率分配与阈值化表现；并构造实验，向模型输入同一正确选项的五份完全相同的副本，测量概率差异。

Result: 即使模型能把所有正确选项排在所有干扰项之上，仍有28%–50%的候选列表中出现至少一个正确选项的概率低于均匀概率1/n；对五份相同正确选项副本，模型给出的概率存在差异，平均差距达17–79个百分点；这些分配差异进一步影响阈值化下多正确选项的恢复效果。

Conclusion: 决策模型概率能够用于对正确选项排序，但概率阈值无法识别出正确选项集合，即排序可靠性与阈值判定的集合识别能力并不等价。

Abstract: Decision models return normalized probability distributions over candidate choices, which downstream applications can use to select among choices. Although their providers emphasize the reliability of these probabilities, calibration on tasks with a single correct choice does not establish their reliability when multiple choices are correct. We evaluate three decision models (Jev, Liquid d1, and GPT-6 Luna Decisions) on three diverse datasets (MetaTool, HotpotQA, and ECtHR) to study probability allocation and thresholding. Even when models rank every correct choice above every distractor, in 28% to 50% of those lists, at least one correct choice receives a probability below the uniform probability of 1/n, where n is the number of choices. We also present each model with five identical copies of a correct choice and observe probability disparities, with mean gaps ranging from 17 to 79 percentage points. We further show how these allocation disparities affect the recovery of multiple correct choices under probability thresholding. Decision-model probabilities can rank correct choices, but a probability threshold does not identify the set of correct choices.

</details>


### [226] [Steering Black-Box Large Language Model Agents with Pitfall Anticipation World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29139)
*Jiongxiao Wang,Han Ding,Youzhi Luo,Yawei Wang,Yun Zhou,Linbo Liu,Panpan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Pitfall Anticipation World Model（PAWM），一种选择性介入的世界模型：仅在潜在陷阱出现时预测即将发生的失败以引导黑盒 LLM 智能体，在 ScienceWorld 与 WebArena-Lite 上分别提升 Claude Sonnet 4.5 24.3% 与 7.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿黑盒 LLM 智能体擅长长程推理与精确动作生成，但缺乏对特定任务环境演化方式的内部化知识。已有世界模型逐步预测下一观测，代价高且信息量有限：单步观测提供的任务相关指导有限，且并非每一步都需要前瞻。因此核心问题是：对智能体任务而言世界模型应预测什么、何时被调用。

Method: 试点研究表明，世界模型的指导在有界多步未来内预判陷阱时最有用。据此提出 PAWM：在潜在陷阱出现时选择性介入，预测即将发生的失败以引导智能体规避。训练分两阶段：先在带智能体陷阱标注的合成数据上做监督微调（SFT），再与黑盒 LLM 在目标环境中进行 GRPO 协同训练以进一步优化。

Result: PAWM 使 Claude Sonnet 4.5 在 ScienceWorld 上提升 24.3%，在 WebArena-Lite 上提升 7.7%，优于多种基线方法。

Conclusion: PAWM 为黑盒 LLM 智能体提供了一种有效的世界模型指导形式：以陷阱预判替代逐步观测预测，并通过选择性介入降低开销、提升任务表现。

Abstract: Frontier black-box Large Language Model (LLM) agents are proficient at long-horizon reasoning and precise action generation. However, when it comes to a specific agentic task, they have no internalized knowledge of how that particular environment unfolds. A world model offers a natural remedy, providing the agent with environment specific knowledge by foreseeing how the environment will respond to the action it is about to take. Prior world models for LLM agents mainly predict next observations at each step, which is costly and often uninformative because individual observations provide limited task-relevant guidance and not every step requires call for foresight. This raises a key question: what a world model should predict and when should it be invoked for agentic tasks? Our pilot study suggests that world model guidance is most useful when it anticipates pitfalls within a bounded multi-step future. Motivated by this, we introduce Pitfall Anticipation World Model (PAWM), which selectively intervenes when a potential pitfall arises and predicts the impending failure to steer the agent away from it. PAWM is trained in two stages: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) on synthetic data labeled with agent pitfalls, followed by GRPO co-training with a black-box LLM to further optimize it in the target environment. PAWM improves Claude Sonnet 4.5 performance by 24.3% on ScienceWorld and 7.7% on WebArena-Lite, outperforming a range of baselines. These results demonstrate that PAWM provides an effective form of world model guidance for black-box LLM agents.

</details>


### [227] [Recursive Self-Improvement on a Budget: Scoring Code Edits Before They Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29121)
*Erfan Darzi,Sree Bhargavi Balija,Sheng He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究自改写脚手架(self-rewriting scaffold)编码智能体在"编辑尚未评估前就要决定延展哪个提议编辑、最终只返回一个智能体"的搜索问题，提出用线性readout（父节点steering-split解决率+diff的八个坐标）冻结系数进行选择，并在HumanEval+/Polyglot上与Thompson采样(Huxley-Gödel Machine)等基线对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自改写脚手架需要预先决定扩展哪个候选编辑，而其效果尚未评估，且最终必须从搜索中返回单一智能体；需要一种可靠的选择准则来替代或补充仅基于父节点历史计数的方法。

Method: 每次运行在96个脚手架编辑（由16例fit split的四次评估标注）上拟合一个线性readout，输入为父节点在steering split上的解决率及提议diff的8个坐标，随后冻结系数。HumanEval+留48例、Polyglot留64例作为steering split，用于打分父节点并维护4节点档案；选择规则为在已评估节点的steering计数上做Thompson采样选父节点并扩展其提议子节点，最终返回steering-split解决数最大且最早被评估的节点。

Result: GPT-4o下本方法在HumanEval+留出集达87.0%，优于父节点率消融的84.2%、Huxley-Gödel Machine选择的85.1%、根脚手架81.2%与calibration-best编辑82.8%。Claude Sonnet 4.5下为86.6%，对比HGM选择86.5%、父节点率消融85.3%、calibration-best 85.1%。

Conclusion: 少量坐标的冻结线性readout在选择待扩展编辑上稳定优于父节点率消融与Huxley-Gödel Machine选择，说明引入diff特征的选择信号对自改写脚手架搜索有效；但相对HGM的增益较小，需在更多任务与模型上验证稳健性。

Abstract: A coding agent that rewrites its own scaffold has to choose which proposed edit to expand before that edit has been evaluated, and the search then has to return one agent. Each run fits one linear readout on the parent's steering-split solve rate and eight coordinates of the proposed diff, from 96 scaffold edits labeled by four evaluations of a 16-instance fit split, and then freezes the coefficients. HumanEval+ reserves 48 instances as the steering split and Polyglot reserves 64. That split scores parents and keeps an archive of four nodes. Huxley-Gödel Machine selection draws a parent by Thompson sampling on the steering counts of nodes that have already been evaluated, and it expands the child that parent proposes. Every method then returns the earliest evaluated node whose steering-split solve count is maximal. The tables report the held-out score of that node on the other 100 HumanEval+ instances and the other 145 Polyglot instances, next to the root scaffold and the calibration edit with the highest fit-split label. Twelve runs of GPT-4o return a node at 87.0% on held-out HumanEval+, against 84.2% for the parent-rate ablation. On that split Huxley-Gödel Machine selection returns 85.1%, the root is at 81.2%, and the calibration-best edit is at 82.8%. On the same split Claude Sonnet 4.5 returns 86.6%, beside 86.5% for Huxley-Gödel Machine selection, 85.3% for the parent-rate ablation, and 85.1% for the calibration-best edit.

</details>


### [228] [Context-Augmented, Session-Calibrated Blood Pressure Tracking from Photoplethysmography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29091)
*Farouk Ganiyu Adewumi,Timothy Oladunni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个带显式对照的校准评估框架，用于区分采集情境（context）与光电容积脉搏波（PPG）对会话校准血压追踪的贡献，并保留阴性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究难以厘清会话校准血压追踪中"采集情境"与"PPG信号"各自的贡献，且缺乏显式对照、可复现的分析流程与对阴性发现的保留。

Method: 在653名AuroraBP参与者上进行三轮五折参与者级交叉验证，排除校准标签作为预测目标；采用匹配的析因分析将情境与PPG分离并移除经过时间项；后续探索性随访保留PPG、加速度计信号与三次初始袖带读数；另评估脉搏率融合及术中VitalDB模型。建立特征缓存到结果的可复现分析管线。

Result: 原始可穿戴状态模型达到SBP/DBP的MAE 7.697/6.139 mmHg；加入情境使MAE降低0.436/0.126 mmHg（97.5%区间分别为0.305–0.573/0.053–0.200）。无外部PPG先验的匹配析因中，PPG相对校准、时间及方案给定体位/活动改善0.214/0.135 mmHg；移除时间项后DBP区间包含零。传感器历史集成达7.299/5.445 mmHg，较静态丰富参考仅改善0.064/0.025 mmHg，DBP较大误差未一致改善。质量加权融合将脉搏率MAE从3.985降至3.690 bpm。VitalDB MAE仍为15.375/8.833 mmHg。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是带显式对照、可复现且保留阴性结果的校准评估框架；传感器历史增益在数据重复使用后属探索性结论；独立估计的可穿戴状态、所提供 richer 特征的完整原始信号复现、外部迁移与临床充分性均未确立。

Abstract: Objective.To distinguish the contributions of acquisition context and photoplethysmography (PPG) to session-calibrated blood pressure (BP) tracking.Approach. The original comparison used three repeats of five-fold participant-level cross-validation in 653 AuroraBP participants, excluding calibration-labelled prediction targets. Matched analyses separated context and PPG and removed elapsed-time terms. Later exploratory follow-ups retained PPG, accelerometer signals and three initial cuff readings. Separate studies evaluated pulse-rate fusion and an intraoperative VitalDB model. Main results.The original wearable-state model achieved participant-level mean absolute errors (MAEs) of 7.697/6.139 mmHg for systolic/diastolic BP (SBP/DBP). Added context reduced MAE by 0.436/0.126 mmHg (97.5% intervals 0.305-0.573/0.053-0.200). In the matched factorial without the external PPG prior, PPG improved on calibration, time and protocol-provided posture/activity by 0.214/0.135 mmHg; removing time left the DBP interval including zero. Standard PPG descriptors contributed, but additional nonlinear/attractor benefit was not established. In a separate follow-up series, a sensor-history ensemble achieved 7.299/5.445 mmHg versus its static-rich reference's 7.363/5.470, small gains of 0.064/0.025 mmHg. Larger DBP errors did not improve uniformly. Quality-weighted fusion reduced pulse-rate MAE from 3.985 to 3.690 beats per minute. VitalDB MAEs remained 15.375/8.833 mmHg despite small PPG gains. Significance. The contribution is a calibrated evaluation framework with explicit controls, reproducible feature-cache-to-results analyses and retained negative findings. The sensor-history gain is exploratory after repeated dataset reuse. Independently estimated wearable states, complete raw-signal reproduction of the supplied richer features, external transfer and clinical adequacy remain unestablished.

</details>


### [229] [Inheritance as an Admission Test for LLM Prompt Merge Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29023)
*Zan-Kai Chong,Hiroyuki Ohsaki,Bryan Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以群体相对评分替代固定验证器，并设计"准入检验"（admission test）判断合并算子是否真正带来改进，从而区分训练信号与纯淘汰效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的迭代改进通常依赖固定验证器，但验证器饱和后循环失去上升空间；同时群体均值可能仅通过淘汰而升高，造成虚假的"训练"假象，因此需要一个能甄别真实改进的判据。

Method: 对当前群体打分而非固定验证器，用冻结面板记录是否有成员变好；仅当最终均值超过第零代最佳成员才计为训练。合并准入需同时满足：子代向供体方向移动、超过零剂量下同算子产生的子代、且移动量的置信下界达到请求剂量的一半。以实测适应度φ为选择器、参考表现θ为未使用的标尺；实验使用gemini-3.1-flash-lite在单一谈判任务上，开环检验四种算子（tactic append、free rewrite、template append、constrained rewrite），并在闭环中验证，另加留出情境重测。

Result: 开环中tactic append与constrained rewrite被准入，free rewrite仅移动约三分之一剂量被拒绝，template append首次探测达标但重测未达标故判为未定。留出重测确认两种append均有效，未能挽救free rewrite；两种append均不随剂量增加。闭环中所有tactic-append与template-append运行均超过第零代最佳成员，free-rewrite运行无一超过；移动量较开环偏小但仍过界。

Conclusion: 准入检验能有效区分真实改进与淘汰伪影，剂量敏感性缺失及部分算子结果不稳定提示该框架仍需扩展验证；结论基于单一任务与单一模型，泛化性有待检验。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are often improved by iterating until a fixed verifier is done. Once that verifier saturates, the loop has nothing left to climb. We instead score agents against the current population, and a frozen panel records whether anyone got better. The panel mean can rise inside the seeds by elimination alone. We therefore count a run as training only when its final mean beats the best generation-zero member. A merge is admitted only when the child moves toward the donor, past the child the same operator produces at zero dose, and the lower confidence bound on that movement reaches half the requested dose. That is the admission test. A detectable shortfall rejects the operator, and the test is otherwise undetermined. We keep realised fitness φas the selector and reference performance θas the unused ruler. Every measurement uses gemini-3.1-flash-lite on one negotiation task, and no fixed-oracle arm was run. In the open loop, tactic append grafts short donor rules and is admitted. Free rewrite moves only a third of the requested dose and is rejected. Template append pastes raw donor replies into the child. Its movement clears the bound on the first probe but not on the re-probe, and it is left undetermined. A held-out re-probe on the five situations the merge never saw confirms that both appends move, and it does not rescue free rewrite. Neither append rises with the dose. Constrained rewrite is admitted in the open loop and was not run in the closed loop, which changes only the operator. The population, the scores and the task stay fixed. No free-rewrite run beats the best generation-zero member. Every tactic-append run and every template-append run does. Sampled loop children keep that split. Free rewrite moves no further toward the donor, and both appends still clear the bound, by less than in the open loop.

</details>


### [230] [An Evidence-Driven Multi-Agent Compound AI System for Head and Neck Proton Radiotherapy Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29010)
*Qingqing Wang,Liqiang Xiao,Chang Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一个证据驱动的多智能体复合AI系统，将基于DRL（PPO）的质子PBS自动放疗计划生成与基于LLM的对话式计划精修相结合，在21例头颈癌患者数据上验证其可生成更符合临床权衡的个性化计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放疗计划需在相互竞争的目标间权衡以满足临床标准并契合医生偏好，传统流程依赖人工经验、效率低且难以个性化；单一DRL自动规划缺乏灵活交互与可解释的证据支撑。

Method: (1) 用PPO训练策略网络，无人工干预地为每位患者生成多个PBS质子计划，选其一为目标计划、其余作为参考；(2) 构建LLM多智能体系统：规划智能体（基于Qwen3-14B，用既往计划证据论证建议，并经SFT与GRPO任务数据后训练）、统一对话接口的chatbot、负责检索临床指标并识别约束违反的评估智能体；(3) 在21例头颈癌患者（每人5个PPO计划，共50个计划进入指令引导精修）上训练与评估。

Result: 相比基座LLM，后训练的Qwen3-14B在指令遵循、信息检索与领域推理能力上均提升；其调整更有效地改善用户优先目标、同时抑制竞争目标恶化，在患者个性化计划精修中实现更符合临床的权衡。

Conclusion: 该复合AI系统提供用户友好的计划平台，是面向个性化质子PBS放疗计划的一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Radiation treatment planning requires balancing competing objectives to satisfy clinical criteria while accommodating physicians' preferences. This study aims to develop an evidence-driven, multi-agent compound AI system that integrates DRL-based automated treatment planning with LLM-based conversational plan refinement for proton PBS therapy. A policy network trained using PPO first generates multiple plans without human intervention, from which one is selected as the target plan and the others serve as references for subsequent refinements. For challenging cases requiring further adjustments, an LLM-based multi-agent system enables plan refinement through natural language interactions. The system comprises a planning agent that recommends evidence-supported adjustments, a chatbot providing a unified conversational interface, and an evaluation agent that retrieves clinical metrics and identifies constraint violations. To enhance domain-specific reasoning, the planning agent based on Qwen3-14B is designed to justify its recommendations using evidence from previously generated plans and post-trained on task-specific data using SFT and GRPO. The framework was trained and evaluated using 21 patients with H&N cancers, with 5 PPO-generated plans per patient. Of these, 50 plans were selected for instruction-guided refinement. Compared with the base LLM, the post-trained Qwen3-14B demonstrated improved instruction-following, information retrieval and domain-specific reasoning capabilities. The resulting adjustments more effectively improved user-prioritized objectives while limiting deterioration in competing objectives, yielding more clinically appropriate trade-offs in patient-specific plan refinement. The proposed compound AI system provides a user-friendly planning platform, offering a promising approach to personalized proton PBS treatment planning.

</details>


### [231] [Back Up Before You Regret: Measuring Backup Awareness and Automating Backup in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28934)
*Xu Li,Simon Yu,Minzhou Pan,Yiyou Sun,Zhaorun Chen,Bo Li,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RegretBench基准揭示编码智能体缺乏"备份意识"（任务完成率86.1%但仅15.2%在关键时刻备份），并设计BackUpper即插即用备份层，通过规则+轻量级子代理在每次工具调用前保存受影响状态，全覆盖遗憾时刻动作、恢复相似度92.2%、每任务成本仅$0.014。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可自主修改真实世界状态（工件、软件环境、配置），但缺乏"备份意识"：无法预判某动作会修改未来可能需要的状态并提前保存。现有研究关注任务完成能力，未评估这种可逆性/备份意识，导致用户事后常后悔未保存状态。

Method: 1) 构建RegretBench：基于X.com、GitHub、Stack Overflow上用户真实后悔未保存状态的场景，整理205个可执行任务；2) 提出BackUpper——作为harness插件的备份层，拦截并检查每次工具调用，在修改前保存受影响状态；结合面向常见操作的规则策略与处理复杂情况的轻量级子代理，规则表可随子代理决策持续扩充；支持按用户偏好收窄备份范围。

Result: 六个前沿模型平均完成86.1%任务但仅15.2%在遗憾时刻前备份；BackUpper覆盖全部遗憾时刻动作，恢复状态与原状态平均相似度92.2%，每任务成本$0.014，token消耗比主代理自我备份基线少7倍；在"动作是否值得保存"判断上，与人工标注匹配的F1达84.5%。

Conclusion: 任务完成能力不等于备份意识；将备份职责从主代理剥离到即插即用备份层，可低成本地使智能体动作保持可逆，同时尊重用户偏好。这为智能体安全性与可逆性设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Coding agents now complete tasks autonomously, modifying real-world state such as artifacts, software environments, and configurations along the way. Yet they show limited backup awareness: recognizing that an action will modify state that may later be needed, and preserving it beforehand. To evaluate backup awareness, we introduce RegretBench, a benchmark of 205 executable tasks grounded in regret moments reported on platforms such as X.com, GitHub, and Stack Overflow, where users explicitly regretted not preserving state they later needed. Across six frontier models, agents complete 86.1% of tasks on average yet back up before these moments in only 15.2%, showing that task completion ability does not imply backup awareness. Because it is hard to predict which changes will be regretted, we propose BackUpper, a harness plug-in backup layer that checks every tool call and preserves affected state before modification. It combines rule-based strategies for common operations with a lightweight subagent for complex cases, and its rule table can expand with subagent decisions over time. On RegretBench, BackUpper covers every regret moment action and restores states with 92.2% average similarity to the original. Achieving this costs only $0.014 per task, with 7x fewer tokens than the main-agent self-backup baseline. When users have preferences on what to back up, BackUpper can also narrow its scope accordingly, matching human annotations on whether an action is worth preserving with an 84.5% F1 score. These results suggest that agents can keep actions reversible at low cost while respecting users' preferences.

</details>


### [232] [D-Skill: Distilling Teacher Knowledge through Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28914)
*Shuo Yuan,Dacheng Li,Joseph E. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: D-Skill 将强教师模型的行为优势蒸馏为可解释的自然语言技能文档，在不访问学生权重、不更新参数的前提下，仅用离线成对轨迹即可让学生模型达到教师 75%–95% 的能力水平，并可媲美甚至优于一 epoch 的 SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型智能体在复杂任务上表现优异但推理成本高，因此需要把能力迁移到小模型。传统知识蒸馏依赖参数更新，需访问学生权重、额外训练算力，且知识隐式编码于参数中、不可解释。

Method: 提出 D-Skill：把教师视为黑盒，通过分析成对（paired）轨迹识别行为分歧与一致之处，提取出自然语言"经验教训"，再以任意兼容的技能更新后端离线整合为技能文档，在推理时提供给学生在上下文中使用。整个过程仅依赖固定的离线轨迹，无需标签、无需额外环境交互、无需权重更新。

Result: 在 SpreadsheetBench-Verified、ALFWorld 和棋类谜题上，覆盖多组教师-学生配对，D-Skill 在不改动模型权重的情况下弥合了 75%–95% 的教师-学生差距；一次离线处理后优于一 epoch SFT 基线，并与多 epoch SFT 具有竞争力；同模型家族下学生单任务推理成本比教师低 5.7 倍–8.3 倍。

Conclusion: 自然语言技能蒸馏是一种高性能、可解释且高效的能力迁移方案，可作为基于权重迁移的替代路径。

Abstract: Frontier language-model agents excel at complex tasks, but high inference costs make transferring their capabilities to smaller models essential. Traditional knowledge distillation achieves this through parameter updates, requiring access to the student's weights and additional training compute while encoding the transferred knowledge implicitly in the parameters. We introduce D-Skill, a framework that treats the teacher as a black box and distills its behavioral advantage into an interpretable natural-language skill by analyzing paired rollouts and identifying behavioral divergences and agreements. The resulting lessons are consolidated offline, with any compatible skill-update backend, into a skill document that is provided to the student at inference time. D-Skill constructs the skill entirely offline from fixed trajectories, without labels, further environment interaction, or weight updates after rollout collection. Across SpreadsheetBench-Verified, ALFWorld, and chess puzzles spanning multiple teacher-student pairs, D-Skill closes 75%-95% of the teacher-student gap without touching model weights. After a single offline pass over the paired traces, D-Skill outperforms one-epoch SFT baselines and remains competitive with multi-epoch SFT. Under the same model family, the resulting student also incurs 5.7x-8.3x lower inference cost per task than the teacher. Our results demonstrate that natural-language skill distillation offers a performant, interpretable, and efficient alternative to weight-based capability transfer.

</details>


### [233] [Hidden Messages Between Diffusion Language Model Agents: Causal Evidence for Latent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28905)
*Alan-Barsag Gazzaev,Alexey Gavrilov,Sergey Muravyov,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Hidden Messages"：让扩散语言模型智能体在去噪状态层面直接传递隐藏向量消息，并通过移除/替换消息的因果实验证明接收方确实使用了消息内容，且单次晚传递优于多次循环传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型通过可修正的连续状态生成答案，但多智能体系统通常在解码成文本后才通信，丢弃了这些连续状态；作者希望探究一条独立的扩散轨迹能否向另一条传递紧凑隐藏消息，以及接收方是否真正利用了消息的语义内容。

Method: 发送方将去噪状态压缩为四个带符号的稠密向量，接收方在保留自身上下文、画布与轨迹的前提下消费这些向量；采用两种因果干预：移除消息（检验通道是否起作用）与替换为另一示例的消息（检验内容是否起作用），并在 Tiny-A2D、Dream-7B、QASC 等受控任务上评估。

Result: 移除消息使 Tiny-A2D 上 +63.6 分、Dream-7B 上 +100.0 分的优势消失；定向替换会把接收方导向被替换的事实。QASC 双事实分置任务中匹配通信达 80.8%，错误示例消息降至 46.8%（内容效应 +34.0 分）。时间因素上，单次晚传递达 87.8%，比四次循环交换高 +7.0 分。

Conclusion: 实验为扩散语言模型智能体间的连续隐藏通信提供了因果证据，并表明信息传递的时机可能比交换次数更重要。

Abstract: Diffusion language models form answers through a sequence of revisable continuous states, but multi-agent systems usually discard those states and make agents communicate only after decoding text. We ask whether one independent diffusion trajectory can pass a compact hidden message into another and, crucially, whether the receiver uses what that message means. In Hidden Messages, a sender compresses its denoising state into four signed dense vectors; the receiver consumes them while retaining its own context, canvas, and trajectory. Removing a message tests whether the channel matters; replacing it with a message from another example tests whether its content matters. Across controlled tasks, derangement removes a +63.6-point advantage on Tiny-A2D and a +100.0-point advantage on Dream-7B, while targeted substitutions steer the receiver toward the substituted fact. On QASC with the two supporting facts split across agents, matched communication reaches 80.8%, but a wrong-example message lowers accuracy to 46.8%, a content effect of +34.0 points. The surprising result is temporal: one late transfer reaches 87.8%, exceeding four recurrent exchanges by +7.0 points. These experiments provide causal evidence for continuous hidden communication between diffusion-LM agents and show that when information is transferred can matter more than how many times agents exchange it.

</details>


### [234] [PreCoMem: Predictive Cognitive Memory for Self-Evolving Long-Term Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29467)
*Jian Zhong,Zeyu Liu,Pingchuan Cao,Rongduo Han,Shunye Tang,Guohuan Xie,Mingyuan Qin,Ran Ji,Xiao Liang,Wei Wang,Haining Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PreCoMem记忆整合框架，将用户记忆建模为置信度加权的信念，用改进的预测编码信号（Effective Surprisal）驱动三档确定性更新（MAINTAIN/PROFILE/CORRECT），兼顾个性化稳定性与信念漂移适应，并在多基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程对话代理需在保持用户记忆稳定以支撑个性化、与用户在真实改变时及时适应之间取得平衡。现有记忆系统要么追加每条观测，要么用启发式规则覆盖旧条目，难以区分瞬时噪声与真实信念漂移。

Method: 借鉴预测编码，将记忆表示为关于用户状态的置信度加权信念。核心信号Effective Surprisal综合语义偏离度与矛盾证据，并在检索弥散、不可靠时对该信号降权。依据该信号设置双阈值门控，将每轮对话确定性地映射为三种更新之一：MAINTAIN（强化被支持的信念）、PROFILE（将模糊线索存为低置信假设，充作"候诊室"以避免对噪声的过早承诺）、CORRECT（对可靠被否证的事实做软衰减）。

Result: 在LoCoMo、LongMemEval-S和PersonaMem-v2上取得一致的SOTA准确率；新构建的DiMoBench验证其在非平稳信念漂移下能自适应而不对噪声过度反应。

Conclusion: 以置信度加权信念与双阈值门控替代追加/启发式覆盖范式，可有效分离瞬态噪声与真实信念漂移；PROFILE等待机制是避免过早承诺的关键设计，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-horizon dialogue agents need to keep user memories stable enough for personalization while adapting when the user genuinely changes. Existing memory systems often append every observation or overwrite old entries by heuristic rules, which makes transient noise hard to distinguish from real belief drift. We introduce PreCoMem, a memory consolidation framework inspired by predictive coding that represents memory as confidence-weighted beliefs about the user's state. Its core signal, Effective Surprisal, combines semantic deviation and contradiction evidence, then downweights this signal when retrieval is diffuse and unreliable. According to effective surprisal, a two-threshold gate maps each turn to one of three deterministic updates: MAINTAIN reinforces supported beliefs, PROFILE stores ambiguous cues as low-confidence hypotheses, and CORRECT softly decays reliably contradicted facts. The PROFILE stage acts as an explicit waiting room, preventing premature commitment to noisy evidence. Experiments on LoCoMo, LongMemEval-S, and PersonaMem-v2 show consistent state-of-the-art accuracy; our new DiMoBench confirms that PreCoMem adapts to non-stationary belief drift without over-reacting to noise. The code is available at https://github.com/ZhongWishing/PreCoMem.

</details>


### [235] [MERP: A Latent-Bottleneck Framework for Robust Multi-Modal Multi-Label Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29430)
*Chuangxin Zhao,Meimei Zhang,Canran Xiao,Jiachen Luo,Jiajun He,Kaijie Chen,Huy Phan,Tomoki Toda,Biörn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多模态情感识别Perceiver（MERP），通过固定规模隐变量推理与标签级证据对齐，提升模态缺失鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态多标签情感识别在部署时常常缺失部分模态（文本/视觉/声学），导致训练-测试差距显著、性能骤降；目标是在不依赖训练期缺失模态样本的前提下，学习一个能保留任意可用模态子集情感证据的紧凑表示。

Method: MERP将联合序列映射为固定大小的隐变量库，使重复推理与原始输入长度解耦；在潜空间推理前后分别提取标签特定证据，并通过重构对齐两个层次。此外引入正/负类原型、带频率整形扰动的噪声门控特征增强器，以及置信度感知的类级融合。

Result: 20%测试期随机模态丢弃下，CMU-MOSEI微F1为0.563、M3ED为0.780，分别超过最强基线0.004和0.005；CMU-MOSEI极端音频单模态场景下保持0.473微F1，而LDDU仅为0.022。

Conclusion: 固定潜变量推理为鲁棒多模态情感识别提供了实用且可验证的路径，且训练阶段无需缺失模态样本。

Abstract: Multimodal multi-label emotion recognition is important for affect-aware systems, but models trained with complete text, visual, and acoustic streams often degrade sharply when some streams are unavailable at deployment. We address this train-test gap by learning a compact representation that preserves emotion-specific evidence from any available subset of modalities. We propose the Multi-modal Emotion Recognition Perceiver (MERP), which maps the joint sequence into a fixed-size latent bank, thereby keeping repeated reasoning independent of raw input length; it extracts label-specific evidence before and after latent reasoning and aligns the two levels through reconstruction. MERP further uses positive and negative class prototypes, a noise-gated feature enhancer with frequency-shaped perturbations during training, and confidence-aware class-wise fusion to strengthen separability, reduce sensitivity to missing inputs, and combine complementary predictors. Under 20 percent test-time random modality drop, MERP achieves micro-F1 scores of 0.563 on CMU-MOSEI and 0.780 on M3ED, exceeding the strongest evaluated baselines by 0.004 and 0.005, respectively; in the severe CMU-MOSEI audio-only setting, it retains 0.473 micro-F1 versus 0.022 for LDDU. These results show that fixed latent reasoning provides a practical, testable route to robust multimodal emotion recognition without requiring missing-modality examples during training. Code is available at https://github.com/Creative-zcx/MERP.

</details>


### [236] [COMET: A coordinated multi-layer runtime monitoring framework for human-supervised AI-enabled safety systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29816)
*Niclas Flehmig,Mary Ann Lundteigen,Shen Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出COMET——一种面向人监督AI安全系统的协调式多层运行时监控框架，三层监控器(信号完整性、物理一致性、AI能力包络)分别锚定不同故障模式，经协调层与通信层映射为交通灯显示；在压缩空气泄漏声学泄漏检测数据集上，COMET在协变量偏移无泄漏数据及4类传感器故障中的3类上将"可信错误答案率"降至0，误报率约1%，并能实现故障模式归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将AI系统纳入安全系统需要运行时监控以避免静默故障；但单一监控器输出的匿名告警对人类监督者(系统的自适应控制器)缺乏可操作信息，无法提供故障归因。因此需要一种既能定量检测/隔离故障、又能给出面向人的定性反馈的多层协调监控框架。

Method: COMET框架含三层监控器：信号完整性、物理一致性、AI系统能力包络(competence envelope)，各锚定一种故障模式；监控输出经协调层(coordination layer)融合，再由通信层(communication layer)映射为交通灯显示。在公开压缩空气泄漏数据集上评估，AI系统由声发射检测泄漏；留出条件模拟协变量偏移、语义偏移与传感器故障。所有监控器开发后冻结并零样本测试，与无监控、单监控基线对比。

Result: 裸AI系统在三种偏移下均静默且自信地失败；COMET在协变量偏移的无泄漏数据上以及4类传感器故障中的3类上，将可信错误答案率降至0，名义误报率约1%。单监控器检测效果接近但无法归因故障模式；COMET将其匿名检测标志转化为诊断结论。语义偏移下仍存在残余静默漏检。

Conclusion: 语义偏移下的残余静默漏检界定了被动监控的边界；面向AI安全系统的运行时监控框架必须将定量的故障检测与隔离同定性的、面向人的反馈相结合。

Abstract: Incorporating AI systems into safety systems requires monitoring to avoid silent faults and ensure safe and reliable operation. Single monitors often do not provide actionable information for human supervisors, who act as the system's adaptive controller. We present COMET, a coordinated multi-layer runtime monitoring framework for human-supervised AI-enabled safety systems in which each monitor is anchored in a different fault mode. Monitor outputs are combined in a coordination layer and mapped by a communication layer to a traffic-light display. In this work, COMET consists of three monitoring layers: signal integrity, physics consistency, and the AI system's competence envelope. The framework is evaluated on an open dataset of compressed-air leaks, where an AI system detects leaks from acoustic emissions, with held-out conditions simulating covariate shift, semantic shift, and sensor faults, events expected during runtime but unknown in their exact appearance. All monitors are frozen after development and tested zero-shot, and the framework is compared against no-monitoring and single-monitor setups. The bare AI system fails silently and confidently under all three shift types, whereas COMET reduces the trusted-wrong-answer rate to zero on covariate-shifted no-leak data and on three of four sensor faults, at a nominal false-alarm rate of $\approx 1\%$. Single monitors come close in detection but cannot attribute the fault mode. COMET converts their anonymous detection flags into diagnoses. Residual silent misses under semantic shift define the boundary of passive monitoring. Runtime monitoring frameworks for AI-enabled safety systems must therefore combine quantitative fault detection and isolation with qualitative, human-oriented feedback.

</details>


### [237] [TraceForge: Distilling with Your Own Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29680)
*Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出两阶段（监督+对抗）的"自生成推理轨迹"方法：以教师答案和上下文为输入，用生成器模仿学生模型家族的推理风格重建思维链，从而在不违反服务条款的前提下替代被隐藏的教师推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用LLM生成训练数据训练另一个模型是最通用的做法，但部分前沿团队已停止返回详细推理过程，只给答案或简短摘要；现有通过重放加密块恢复隐藏推理的方法违反提供商服务条款，难以产品化。需要合规的替代方案。

Method: 输入上下文与教师答案，由生成器以学生自身模型家族的风格重建推理轨迹。两阶段训练：先监督学习，再对抗训练——生成器学习从上下文与答案重建该家族真实推理轨迹，判别器（critic）学习区分轨迹真伪；推理时用生成器为教师答案撰写家族风格推理。另设独立评判器以0–1评分轨迹真实性，并可用判别器从4个候选中筛选。

Result: 真实性评分（非正确率）：生成轨迹平均0.68，真实轨迹0.66，经判别器从4候选中筛选后达0.82。在5个基准、其余设定固定时：通用任务上五个训练语料效果相当；代码任务上生成推理优于Fable 5自身思考内容。相同5,000题与教师答案下，最终检查点HumanEval+提升3.7分、MBPP+提升7.1分，且MBPP+优势在每个检查点均保持。

Conclusion: 以学生家族风格合成推理轨迹可作为隐藏教师推理的合规替代，真实性接近甚至略高于真实轨迹；风格匹配的推理更易被学生模型学习，在代码基准上带来稳定增益。

Abstract: Using an LLM to generate training data for another is among the most general ways to train one, and it remains common practice. Some frontier teams have closed that route for their own models: what comes back is an answer, or a short summary, without the detailed reasoning trace behind it. That is a little ironic: they were themselves built on distilling human knowledge, and distilling an open-source model is still entirely routine; the restriction applies to their own outputs alone. Recent work recovers the hidden reasoning by replaying encrypted blocks across models. It somehow works, but it breaks the providers' terms of service, so it is not something a product can be built on long-term.
  We built the alternative. We take the context and the teacher's answer as input, and generate the reasoning trace ourselves, in the style of the student's own model family. Training is two-stage, supervised and then adversarial: a generator learns to regenerate the family's own reasoning traces from their context and answer, and a critic learns how real a reasoning trace looks. Applied to a teacher's answer, the generator writes one in the family's style. An independent judge rates how likely traces are to be genuine on a scale from 0 to 1: ours average 0.68, real traces 0.66, and traces selected by the critic from four candidates 0.82. These are authenticity scores, not correctness rates.
  We test the idea on five benchmarks with everything else held fixed. On general tasks the five training corpora are about the same; on coding, the generated reasoning beats Fable 5's own thinking content. With the same 5,000 questions and the same teacher answers, HumanEval+ improves by 3.7 points and MBPP+ by 7.1 at the final checkpoint, and the MBPP+ lead holds at every checkpoint. We suspect that reasoning written in the student's family style is easier for it to learn from.

</details>


### [238] [HGM-Dual: Lineage-Aware Guided Agent Harness Optimization for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30042)
*Sudipta Paul,Pengrui Quan,Aounon Kumar,Nicholas Tzou,Vivek Kulkarni,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HGM-Dual 是面向规划智能体的诊断引导式 harness 演化框架，通过谱系感知上下文和错误条件下的双路径子代生成，在匹配 HGM 变体最终性能的前提下将评估预算降低 2–4 倍，并在案例准确率上最高取得 14.45% 的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 harness 优化方法（Darwin–Gödel Machine、Huxley–Gödel Machine, HGM）依赖递归自我改进，主要面向编码任务——其中"改进智能体所需的能力"与"被改进的能力"天然重合。但规划任务要求智能体产出联合满足多重交互约束的工具调用序列，这种能力对齐被打破，开放式自我修改会反复探索无效改动，大量消耗评估预算。因此需要一种预算高效的 harness 演化方法。

Method: 提出 HGM-Dual，包含两个核心机制：(1) 谱系感知上下文（lineage-aware context）：以结构化形式记录每个祖先的具体改动、哪些行为改善或退化、哪些修改无效，为编辑器提供显式的"编辑—结果"归因，替代无结构的智能体历史档案；(2) 错误条件化双路径子代生成（error-conditioned dual-path child generation）：对每个被选中的父代，既生成保留开放式探索的通用子代，也生成专门针对父代排名最高的约束违反所对应 harness 组件的定向子代。

Result: 相较最强基线，案例准确率最高取得 14.45% 的相对提升；同时以 2–4 倍更少的评估预算达到与 HGM 各变体相当的最终性能。
